# RBK Command Center — Project Context

## UI & Performance Standards

These are required patterns for **all new pages and components**. Do not ship a new page that doesn't follow them, and update existing pages to comply when you touch them.

### 1. Shimmer loading — required for every page that fetches

Use the helpers in `app/components/ui/Shimmer.tsx`:

- `ShimmerStatCards` — for top-of-page metric/stat cards (matches `bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-5` card style).
- `ShimmerTableRows` — for the table body of any data table.
- `ShimmerCards` — for vertical card lists (e.g. the Campaign Giving fund cards).

Never render a blank state, a raw `<svg className="animate-spin">` spinner, or `null` while data loads. Already in use: `GuardianCirclePage`, `IsraelFundTab`, `CampaignsTab`. Match that pattern when adding new pages.

### 2. Lazy loading — fetch only when the user navigates in

Heavy data fetches must not run on dashboard mount. Use the `activeNav + useRef` pattern in `Dashboard.tsx`:

```tsx
const loadedFooRef = useRef(false);
useEffect(() => {
  if (activeNav !== 'foo' || loadedFooRef.current) return;
  loadedFooRef.current = true;
  fetchFoo();
}, [activeNav]);
```

The fetch fires the first time `activeNav` flips to that section and never re-fires automatically. Add a manual refresh button (see #4) to reset the ref. This is how `lever`, `development`, `monday` already work — search the file for `loadedLeverRef`, `dataCacheRef` for examples.

### 3. Decoupled stat cards — summary first, detail second

Pages with stat cards + a detail table must fetch the stat-card numbers separately and render them as soon as they arrive, before the table loads. Pattern:

1. API route accepts `?view=summary` and returns only the totals (no constituent / row payload). Cheap query, fast response.
2. Client fires `GET /api/foo?view=summary` first, then `GET /api/foo` for the full payload.
3. Stat cards read from `summary ?? data` so they render from either source.
4. Table area shows `ShimmerTableRows` while the second fetch is in flight.

Reference implementation: `GuardianCirclePage.tsx` (`summary` state + `tableLoading` state + two-phase `fetchData`). The Guardian Circle route already implements `?view=summary`; mirror that on new routes.

### 4. Refresh button — every lazy page

Every page using the lazy pattern must include a small refresh icon button in the header that (a) resets the loaded ref and (b) re-fires the fetch. Pattern (from the existing Recruiting page, search Dashboard.tsx for `setLeverData(null); delete dataCacheRef.current.lever; setLeverRefreshKey(k => k + 1);`):

```tsx
<button
  onClick={() => { resetLoadedRef(); fetchData(); }}
  className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded hover:bg-slate-100"
  title="Refresh"
>
  <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15" />
  </svg>
</button>
```

Show a small inline pulse next to the page title while a background refresh is in flight (see the `backgroundRefreshing === 'lever'` pattern in Dashboard.tsx).

### 5. Mobile — responsive Tailwind, single column under 768px

All new UI must use responsive Tailwind prefixes and degrade to a single column on screens narrower than 768px:

- Stat-card grids: `grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4` (not `grid-cols-3`).
- Two-column layouts: `flex flex-col md:flex-row` or `grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2`.
- Side panels/drilldowns: ensure `max-w-[420px]` panels don't exceed `w-full` on mobile (`fixed top-0 right-0 bottom-0 w-full max-w-[420px]` is correct).
- Tables in scroll containers must have `overflow-x-auto` on the wrapper so column overflow scrolls horizontally on small screens rather than breaking layout.
- Hover-only affordances (e.g. the envelope icon in ConstituentTable's `group-hover:opacity-100`) need a tap-friendly equivalent on mobile — use `sm:opacity-0 sm:group-hover:opacity-100` so the icon stays visible on touch devices.

Test at 375px (iPhone SE), 768px (iPad portrait), and 1280px (laptop) before shipping.

## Key Files

### Core UI

- **`app/components/Dashboard.tsx`** — Main UI component (~9100 lines). Contains all views: Dashboard, Inbox, Agenda, Tasks, Gemara, Communications, Projects, Admissions, Emily's Queue. Handles state, data derivation, and interactions. RBK (kraussb@saracademy.org) greeting uses time-of-day format: "Good morning/afternoon/evening, Bini." instead of "Welcome back, Rabbi." **Gemara** (`activeNav === 'gemara'`): link cards page with Gemara Folder, Oral Test Sign-Up, Oral Test Rubric (placeholder URLs). **Communications** (`activeNav === 'communications'`): Monday.com approvals queue + social media links (see Communications section below). **Client-side caching**: `dataCacheRef` (useRef) caches page data for absences, admissions, lever with 5-min TTL. On nav, shows cached data immediately if available, background-refreshes if stale (>5 min). `backgroundRefreshing` state shows "Updating..." pulsing dot in page headers during background refresh. Refresh buttons clear cache before re-fetching.
- **`app/components/EmailDashboard.tsx`** — Email dashboard wrapper
- **`app/components/TodayAgenda.tsx`** — Today's agenda component
- **`app/components/AuthProvider.tsx`** — Auth context provider. Exports `useAuth()` (user, signOut, loading) and `useWorkspace()` (workspaceId, role, modules, moduleConfig, displayName, workspaces, switchWorkspace, impersonating, startImpersonation, stopImpersonation). `WorkspaceContext` populated on auth state change via `GET /api/auth/session/workspace`. Supports multi-workspace: `workspaces` is an array of `{ id, name, role }` for all workspaces the user belongs to. `switchWorkspace(id)` saves selection to localStorage `selectedWorkspaceId` and reloads the page. **Impersonation** (admin-only, client-side): `impersonating` state persisted in localStorage as `impersonation`. When active, context returns impersonated `workspaceId`, `role`, `displayName` and fetches impersonated member's `allowed_modules` for `getEffectiveModules()`. Firebase user stays unchanged. `startImpersonation(target)` sets localStorage and reloads. `stopImpersonation()` clears and reloads. Sign-out clears both `selectedWorkspaceId` and `impersonation`. Periodic ID token refresh every 10 min.
- **`app/components/TiptapEditor.tsx`** — Reusable rich text editor component (Tiptap). Toolbar: Bold, Italic, Underline, Strikethrough, Bullet list, Ordered list, Link. Props: `content`, `onUpdate`, `onBlur`, `placeholder`, `minHeight`, `onCtrlEnter`. Dynamically imported with `next/dynamic` (SSR disabled).
- **`app/components/ImpersonationBanner.tsx`** — Amber banner shown at top of every page when impersonation is active. Shows target name/email/role/workspace + "Exit impersonation" button. Rendered from layout.tsx.
- **`app/admin/permissions/page.tsx`** — Admin page (`/admin/permissions`). **Phase A2 + F restructure**: 3 tabs at the top — **Users** (workspace member table; per-member module toggles + sub-permissions, Title text input, Divisions checkbox group, Assistant picker pill, role dropdown, Add User form, Remove button), **School Settings** (workspace name inline edit, divisions-in-use derived from members, branding placeholder), **Integrations** (7 cards: Veracross / Gmail / Google Calendar / Slack / Lever / Anthropic / Rise Vision). Each integration card shows a status pill — emerald Connected / rose Disconnected / slate Not configured / blue Google OAuth — driven by `workspace_integrations`. 4 API-key cards (Veracross/Slack/Lever/Anthropic) have Configure (inline form) + Test (live connection test) + Disconnect buttons. Gmail / Google Calendar are OAuth-only with a Reconnect link to `/api/auth/gmail-consent`. Rise Vision is "Coming soon" until Phase G. "Seed from environment" button appears when nothing is configured and runs the migrate endpoint to copy env vars into DB rows. **Sub-permissions** (existing): modules with sub-permissions (lever, development, home) show a chevron expand button when enabled. Data format: `allowed_modules` JSONB supports both `"lever": true` (legacy) and `"lever": { "enabled": true, "offer_approvals": true }` (granular). Defined in `SUB_PERMISSIONS` map. `lib/modules.ts` exports `hasSubPermission()` and `isModuleEnabled()` helpers.

Props: user, activeNav, setActiveNav, role, allowedModules, effectiveModules, workspaceId, workspaces, switchWorkspace, signOut, unreadCount, emilyQueueCount, onCompose, mounted. Used by both Dashboard.tsx and home/page.tsx. **Sidebar sections:** Home (with sub-items: Daily Announcements → `/home?tab=announcements`, Today's Folder → external Google Drive link; sub-items visible to non-viewer users with `home` module), Daily (Inbox, Agenda, Tasks, Gemara, Communications, Compose), **Academics** (Student Absences, Admissions & Enrollment, Student Logs — each gated by module), **Community** (Simchas & Shivas — gated by simchas module), Operations (Projects, Recruiting, Development, Emily's Queue). Sections are collapsible with state persisted to localStorage as `sidebarCollapsed`.
