### API Routes (`app/api/`)

- **`agenda-items/route.ts`** — CRUD for agenda items (GET with batch email/topic lookup, POST supports email/topic/manual item_type with optional title — email_id and topic_id are explicitly nullable so manual items save without an email link, PATCH with bulk reorder + tags/title fields, DELETE). DB check constraint: `item_type IN ('email', 'topic', 'manual')`. **Phase 3:** All methods require auth + workspace_id scope.
- **`agenda-items/topics/route.ts`** — Recurring topics management
- **`agenda-notes/route.ts`** — CRUD for agenda notes (GET, POST, PATCH, DELETE). POST allows standalone action notes (no `email_id` or `agenda_item_id` required when `type='action'`). PATCH supports `completed` field.
- **`emails/status/route.ts`** — Email status updates (status, action_status, draft_status, priority, reminder_date, revision_comment, tbd_suggestion, tbd_notes, draft_reply, edited_draft, is_unread). Supports explicit `is_unread` field; if not provided, defaults to `false` on any PATCH (backward compatible). Used by mark-as-read on email open.
- **`emails/[id]/flag/route.ts`** — Flag emails for meeting, update meeting_notes
- **`emails/[id]/draft/route.ts`** — Draft editing and approval
- **`emails/[id]/send/route.ts`** — Send emails via Gmail API. No approval gate — sends as long as draft has content.
- **`emails/[id]/archive/route.ts`** — Archive emails in Gmail (removes INBOX label)
- **`emails/compose/route.ts`** — Compose and send a new email (not a reply). POST with `{ to, subject, body }`. Builds HTML MIME message with RBK's signature, sends via Gmail API `users/me/messages.send`. Returns `{ success, messageId }`. Used by the Compose modal in Dashboard.
- **`emails/send-batch/route.ts`** — Batch email sending. No approval gate — sends as long as drafts have content.
- **`gmail/process-email/route.ts`** — POST with `{ emailId }`. Adds "RBK/Done" label (creates it if needed) and removes INBOX label from the Gmail message. Called fire-and-forget from Dashboard on task completion and email send. Never blocks the caller on failure.
- **`gmail/thread/[threadId]/route.ts`** — GET fetches full raw Gmail thread on demand. Uses signed-in user's access token from session cookie (same auth pattern as `emails/[id]/send`). Accepts either a Gmail thread ID or message ID as the path param — tries thread fetch first, falls back to message metadata lookup to resolve the real thread ID, then fetches that thread. Returns `{ threadId, messages: GmailThreadMessage[] }` with each message containing id, threadId, from, to, subject, date (ISO), body (full decoded MIME — prefers text/html, falls back to text/plain, handles multipart recursively via base64url decode), bodyType ('html' | 'text'), snippet. Messages sorted by date ascending. Returns JSON error on failure (401/403/404/500) — never throws. Requires `gmail.readonly` scope.
- **`gmail/sync/route.ts`** — POST triggers on-demand Gmail triage. Calls the `manualTriage` HTTP Cloud Function (`https://us-central1-rbk-cmd-center.cloudfunctions.net/manualTriage`), which publishes to the `firebase-schedule-triageGmail-us-central1` Pub/Sub topic internally. Returns `{ success: true/false }`. Used by the "Sync Gmail" button on the All Emails page.
- **`gmail/sync-drafts/route.ts`** — POST triggers on-demand draft sync. Calls the `manualSyncDrafts` HTTP Cloud Function (`https://us-central1-rbk-cmd-center.cloudfunctions.net/manualSyncDrafts`), which publishes to the `firebase-schedule-syncDraftsReady-us-central1` Pub/Sub topic internally. Returns `{ success: true/false }`.
- **`gmail/sent/route.ts`** — GET endpoint fetches last 50 sent messages from Gmail API (`labelIds=SENT`). Uses workspace refresh token pattern: gets `workspaceId` from session, queries Supabase `workspaces` table for `gmail_refresh_token`, exchanges it for an access token via Google OAuth (`https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token`), then uses that token for all Gmail API calls (always fetches workspace owner's sent mail, not the logged-in user's). Fetches each message in parallel, extracts Subject/To/Date headers and body text (recursive MIME walk, prefers text/plain, falls back to stripped HTML). Returns `{ messages: SentMessage[] }` where each has: id, threadId, subject, to, date (ISO), snippet, body.
- **`gmail/trash-email/route.ts`** — POST with `{ emailId }`. Looks up `message_id` from Supabase, calls Gmail `messages.trash` (reversible). Returns `{ success: true }` even on Gmail failure (fire-and-forget). Used when marking emails as junk.
- **`calendar/today/route.ts`** — Fetch Google Calendar events for a given date. Uses `getGoogleAccessToken(workspaceId)` from `lib/googleToken.ts` (workspace refresh token → fresh access token), falls back to session token. Calendar ID: `primary`. Returns `{ authError: true }` on 401 for client-side "Calendar disconnected" UI.
- **`calendar/create/route.ts`** — Create calendar events. Uses `getGoogleAccessToken()` with session fallback. Calendar ID: `primary`.
- **`calendar/delete/route.ts`** — Delete calendar events. Uses `getGoogleAccessToken()` with session fallback. Calendar ID: `primary`.
- **`auth/session/route.ts`** — Session management. Preserves existing Google access token across periodic Firebase ID token refreshes. Cookie set to 24h. **Multi-workspace:** Fetches ALL `workspace_members` rows for the Firebase UID (not just one). Falls back to email lookup for placeholder user_ids and auto-replaces them. Fetches workspace details (name, modules, module_config) for all memberships in a single batch query. Builds a `workspaces` array in the session cookie: `[{ id, name, role, modules, module_config }]`. Default workspace prefers `owner` role over `assistant`. Session cookie shape: `{ idToken, accessToken, user, workspace_id (default), role, display_name, modules, module_config, workspaces[], current_member, assistant, principal, workspace_owner_email, workspace_brand }`. Falls back gracefully if workspace tables don't exist yet. **Phase B additions**: `current_member` is the active `workspace_members` row for this user (includes `assignee_key`, `divisions`, `title`, `email_identity_html`); `assistant` = "who assists me" (reverse lookup `WHERE assistant_to = me.id`); `principal` = "who I assist" (forward lookup `WHERE id = me.assistant_to`). **Tasks page second column = `assistant ?? principal`** — principals see [me | assistant], assistants see [me | principal], standalone users see only their own column. **Phase E additions**: `workspace_owner_email` (from `workspaces.owner_email`) + `workspace_brand` (from `workspaces.brand` jsonb).
- **`auth/session/workspace/route.ts`** — Lightweight GET endpoint that reads workspace fields (`workspace_id`, `role`, `modules`, `module_config`, `display_name`, `workspaces[]`) from the httpOnly `__session` cookie and returns them as JSON. Used by `AuthProvider` to populate `WorkspaceContext` on the client. Returns the full `workspaces` array so the client can offer a workspace switcher.
- **`auth/switch-workspace/route.ts`** — POST with `{ workspaceId }`. Reads current `__session` cookie, verifies user is a member of the requested workspace (checks session `workspaces[]` first, falls back to Supabase `workspace_members` query as safety net), re-sets the `__session` cookie with the new active `workspace_id` and matching role/modules/module_config from the workspaces array. Returns `{ success: true }`. Called by `AuthProvider.switchWorkspace()` before page reload.
- **`auth/signout/route.ts`** — Sign out
- **`health/route.ts`** — Health check
- **`admin/workspace-members/route.ts`** — GET fetches all workspace members with workspace name join. Admin-only (checks email === rglassberg@saracademy.org, 403 otherwise). Returns `{ members: Array<{ id, email, display_name, role, workspace_id, workspace_name, allowed_modules }> }` sorted alphabetically.
- **`admin/workspace-members/[id]/route.ts`** — PATCH updates `allowed_modules` JSONB on a workspace_members row. Admin-only. Accepts `{ allowed_modules: Record<string, boolean> | null }`. Returns `{ success: true }`.
- **`home/week-events/route.ts`** — GET fetches SAR school calendar public iCal feed (`accalendars@saracademy.org`), parses VEVENT blocks, filters to current week (Mon–Sun), returns up to 8 events as `{ events: Array<{ id, title, startTime, isAllDay, location? }> }`. 5-min cache. Non-fatal (returns empty array on failure). No auth required.
- **`home/route.ts`** — GET endpoint powering the /home landing page. Requires auth. Returns `{ firstName, projects, dashboards }`. `firstName` derived from `workspace_members.display_name` (falls back to email prefix). `projects` fetched where `assignee_email = currentEmail OR currentEmail = ANY(team_emails)`, excludes archived, each row has `isMine: boolean`. `dashboards` built from effective modules via `getEffectiveModules()`, mapped to `DASHBOARD_META` (admissions, absences, simchas, lever, recruiting→lever alias, faculty_absences, development, projects). Each entry has optional `navId` for sidebar navigation override. Projects always included. Deduplicates by navId (lever/recruiting are one tile). Respects workspace `modules[key] === false` to hide disabled modules. Fallback: if a module is in `memberAllowed` but missing from workspace `modules`, it's still included (unless workspace explicitly disabled it).
- **`development/weekly-gifts/route.ts`** — GET fetches all gifts from Veracross `/v3/development/gifts` (OAuth2 via `VERACROSS_DEVELOPMENT_CLIENT_ID`/`SECRET`, scopes: `development.gifts:list development.gifts:read development.constituents:list development.constituents:read`). Paginated fetch (no server-side date filter). Client-side filters: past 7 days (ET), cash gift types only, excludes in-kind. Soft credit dedup: groups by date+amount+event/fund, keeps soft credit row. Fetches notes from `gift_notes` table. Returns `{ gifts: DisplayGift[], summary: { totalGifts, totalAmount, newGiftsAmount, pledgePaymentsAmount }, dateRange: { weekStart, weekEnd } }`. Module-gated to `development`.
- **`development/sync-gifts-internal/route.ts`** — POST for scheduled Cloud Function calls. Auth via `X-Internal-Secret` header (shared secret, no user session). Reads `X-Workspace-Id` header. Validates workspace has development module. Calls `syncGiftsForWorkspace()`.
- **`development/sync-gifts/route.ts`** — POST triggers on-demand gift sync. Auth + development module gated. Calls `syncGiftsForWorkspace()` from `lib/syncGifts.ts`. Returns `{ success, count, last_sync_at }`.
- **`development/gift-notes/route.ts`** — POST upserts a note for a gift (`gift_id + workspace_id` unique). Body: `{ gift_id, note }`. Author is always the real user email (not impersonated). DELETE removes by `?gift_id=` query param. Both methods require auth + development module.
- **`development/cooper-fund/route.ts`** — GET reads most recent `cooper_fund_categories` row for the current workspace, sums Veracross Cooper gifts by event for the current FY, AND reads live Column G disbursement totals from the Cooper Reconciliation Google Sheet (`COOPER_RECONCILIATION_SHEET_ID` env var, range `'Master Sheet'!A:H`). Sheet read uses the workspace `gmail_refresh_token` via `getGoogleAccessToken()` — requires the `spreadsheets.readonly` OAuth scope added in `gmail-consent/route.ts`. `fetchCooperDisbursementsByEvent()` filters rows: Column G must start with `APL:`, Column E credit > 0, date inside Sep 1 2025 – Aug 31 2026 (hardcoded FY26 window — bump for FY27), payee/check must not contain "VOID", payee must not start with Transfer/Wire/Void checks/Transfer to Operating. Returns `{ categories, donated_ytd, current_balance, as_of_date, fiscal_year, updated_by, moneyIn, moneyInFyLabel, disbursementsByEvent }`. `disbursementsByEvent` is `[]` on any Sheets failure (missing env var, missing/no-scope token, API non-2xx, network blip) — frontend degrades gracefully. Module-gated to `development`.
- **`development/israel-fund/route.ts`** — GET joins Money In + Money Out into per-initiative rows. **Money In (2026-06-08 rewrite)**: reads from `israel_fund_raised_cache` with `WHERE is_excluded = false` (added 2026-06-08 — see `raised-cache` endpoints below for the editor-only toggle). Cache is small (few dozen rows) — single `SELECT event_name, raised, gift_count ... ORDER BY raised DESC`. No ILIKE / no gift_type filter / no whitelist — the cache is already the canonical curated set (`normalizeIsraelFundEvent` drops Columbus Baseball Tournament and similar at sync time, and the sync's `APL: %` / `DEV: %` prefix gate keeps non-Israel events out). **Money Out**: paginated `israel_fund_grants` Supabase query, then in-route aggregation per initiative — `SUM(funding_amount) WHERE is_visible AND wire_was_sent AND NOT grant_not_given GROUP BY initiative`. Join: raised + disbursed by case-insensitive trimmed name. Every raised_cache row appears regardless of grants; grants-only initiatives still surface with `raised: 0`. `fetchIsraelGrantsPaidOut` (legacy Google Sheets helper) is fully removed from the file. Response shape unchanged: `{ total_raised, total_paid_out (= total_disbursed, kept for UI back-compat), total_disbursed, total_balance, initiatives: [{ name, raised, disbursed, paidOut, balance }], grants: GrantRow[], veracrossEvents: string[] (raised_cache event names for the initiative-picker dropdown), moneyIn: [{ event, total, pledgeBalance: 0, giftCount }], as_of }`. Module-gated to `development`.
- **`development/israel-fund/raised-cache/route.ts`** — GET editor-only list of every `israel_fund_raised_cache` row including `is_excluded = true`. Returns `{ rows: Array<{ id, event_name, raised, gift_count, seed_raised, is_excluded, last_updated }> }` ordered by `is_excluded ASC, raised DESC`. Same editor gate as the grants ACL (`hasSubPermission('development', 'israel_fund_editor')` OR Emily/Becca email OR `role === 'owner'`). Used by the "Show hidden" toggle in `IsraelFundTab.tsx`.
- **`development/israel-fund/raised-cache/[id]/route.ts`** — PATCH editor-only. Body `{ is_excluded: boolean }`, nothing else patchable here (raised / seed_raised / gift_count are owned by the sync + CSV seed). Verifies the row exists and belongs to the caller's workspace; updates `is_excluded` + `last_updated`. Returns `{ success: true }`.
- **`development/israel-fund/donors/route.ts`** — GET `?initiative=NAME`. Returns up to 100 most-recent donor gifts for a single Israel Fund initiative from `gifts_cache`. Match rule: APL:/DEV:-prefixed event match OR exact case-insensitive match on the un-prefixed event name (mirrors `normalizeIsraelFundEvent` + the sync's prefix gate). Anonymous gifts return `name: "Anonymous"` regardless of `constituent_name`. Response: `{ donors: Array<{ name, amount, date, anonymous }>, hasMore, totalMatched }`. Module-gated to `development`. Lazy-fetched by `IsraelFundTab.tsx` when an initiative row is first expanded; result cached per-initiative for the session. Empty result triggers the "Donor data available from May 2025 onward" notice (Veracross's API window is narrow).
- **`absences/historical/route.ts`** — GET `?date=YYYY-MM-DD`. Reads past-day attendance straight from `attendance_cache` (no Veracross hit) and returns the same shape as `/api/absences` so the Dashboard renderer needs no branching. Filters: workspace_id, the supplied date, HS grading periods dropped, HS grade ids dropped, present students (`attendance_category = 0 AND student_attendance_status = 0`) dropped. Module-gated to `absences`. Lives alongside the live route at `/api/absences`; only invoked when the user navigates the date picker to a past school day.
- **`slack/notify-emily/route.ts`** — POST quick-DM to Emily from the Today's Schedule cards. Body `{ eventTitle, eventTime? }`. Gated to `role === 'owner' || role === 'assistant'` so viewers can't spam. Looks up the workspace's Slack bot token via `getSlackCredentials`, posts to Emily's Slack ID (`U05M5KT86GK`) via `chat.postMessage` with text `👋 Rabbi Krauss wants to connect about *{eventTitle}*{at TIME}.`. Never throws — returns `{ ok: false, error }` on Slack failure.
- **`important-docs/route.ts`** — Important docs CRUD (GET, POST, PUT, DELETE)
- **`projects/route.ts`** — Projects CRUD (GET excludes archived, POST, PATCH multi-field update, DELETE soft-archives by setting `status='archived'`)
- **`gemara/route.ts`** — Gemara resources CRUD (GET ordered by created_at desc, POST, DELETE hard-delete by id query param). SQL for `gemara_items` table is in a comment at the top of the file.
- **`monday/communications/route.ts`** — Monday.com integration for the Communications page. GET fetches items from board 4035548140 ("Daily/Weekly Communications"), filters to `status_126.text === "Pending RBK Approval"`, maps column IDs to clean fields (commType from `dropdown_mkt3j6xf`, notes from `long_text_mkx4p0sh`, draftLink from `link_mkt2dyf7`, file from `file`, requester from `people5`, audience from `text`, dueDate from `date4`). Returns `{ items: CommunicationsItem[] }`. PATCH accepts `{ itemId, action, note? }`: action `"approve"` sets status to "Approved", action `"request_changes"` sets status to "To discuss" and optionally posts an update with `create_update`. Auth via `MONDAY_API_KEY` env var (Bearer token). Requires `getAuthSession()`.
- **`simchas/route.ts`** — GET fetches the SAR Bar/Bat Mitzvah public iCal feed (`barbatmitzvah@saracademy.org`), parses VEVENT blocks manually (no npm deps), filters to current week (Mon–Sun), returns `{ events: SimchaEvent[] }`. 5-min cache via `next.revalidate`.
- **`calendar/week/route.ts`** — GET fetches Google Calendar events for the full current week (Mon–Sun) using `getGoogleAccessToken()`. Calendar ID: `primary`. Used by the Simchas & Shivas page to find shiva/funeral events across the whole week.
- **`admissions/route.ts`** — GET fetches admission applications AND current students for re-enrollment from Veracross API. Two OAuth2 token requests: (1) admissions token with scopes `admission.applications:list admission.applications:read admission.applicants:list admission.applicants:read admission.households:list` — do NOT add `admission.households:read`. (2) students token with scopes `students:list students:read admission.applications:list admission.applicants:list admission.households:list`. Both use `VERACROSS_ADMISSIONS_CLIENT_ID`/`SECRET`. Paginates applications and students (1000/page). Applications filtered to `year_applying_for === 2026`, excludes HS grades (9-12) and unprocessed (application_status === 0). Students filtered to `grade_level` -3 to 7, `enrollment_status` in [2,3,4,5,6,8]. Returns `{ applications: AdmissionApplication[], reEnrollments: ReEnrollmentStudent[] }`. Students fetch is non-fatal (returns empty array on failure). **City data for re-enrollment students**: after fetching students, collects unique `household_id` values, fetches all admission households via `fetchAllPages()` (same admissions token, `/v3/admission/households`), builds `householdIdToCity` map, and sets `city` on each `ReEnrollmentStudent` from the household's city field. Household fetch is non-fatal. Logs `[HOUSEHOLD DEBUG]` fields on first run for field discovery. Refresh button resets admissionsData + admissionsCities + increments refreshKey. Page title: "Admissions & Enrollment". UI has three tabs: Admissions Overview (summary pills [Accepted, Waitlisted, Withdrawn — Denied pill removed], family response breakdown, by-grade bars, Two-column grid (items-start): left column has Family Response card (badge + bar scaled to max count + count) + City pie chart card (legend above, 280px pie below); right column has By Grade card (bars scale to max grade count). City pie: 15-color palette, hover tooltips, top 14 + "Other" slice.), Enrollment Projection (stat cards for Enrollment Complete/Accepted Offer/Considering/Pipeline Total, horizontal funnel bar, by-grade breakdown table with badges + mini progress bars, collapsible declined families section), and New Enrollment (confirmed/pending pills). Drill-down panel (420px) with real names.
- **`admissions/cities/route.ts`** — GET accepts `?applicantIds=1,2,3` query param. Scopes: `admission.applicants:list admission.applicants:read admission.households:list` (do NOT add `:read`). Uses paginated LIST endpoints (not individual fetches) for performance: fetches ALL applicants via `/v3/admission/applicants` (paginated, 1000/page) to get household_id, then ALL households via `/v3/admission/households` (paginated) to get city. Joins applicant→household→city, filters to requested IDs. Only 2-4 API calls total instead of 400+. Household city field is `data.city`. Returns `{ cities: Record<number, string> }`. Any failure returns `{ cities: {} }`. Called from Dashboard after main admissions data loads via `fetchAdmissionsCities()` with auto-retry (retries once after 2s on empty result or error). Loading spinner shown in pie chart area. Failed state shows "Retry" button. `admissionsCitiesFailed` state tracks failure.
- **`admissions/applicants/route.ts`** — POST accepts `{ applicantIds: number[] }`, fetches each applicant from Veracross `/v3/admission/applicants/{id}` in parallel (batches of 20), returns `{ applicantNames: Record<number, string> }` with "First Last" names. Called lazily from Dashboard when the drill-down panel opens — only fetches names for the currently filtered view, caches in state to avoid re-fetching.
- **`admissions/reenrollment/route.ts`** — GET fetches re-enrollment data from Veracross `/v3/academics/enrollments`. Authenticates with `academics.enrollments:list` scope. Paginates, filters to 2026 school year and excludes HS grades. Returns `{ enrollments: ReEnrollment[] }` with person_id, first_name, last_name, grade_level, enrollment_status, school_year. **ON HOLD** — route file exists but the Re-enrollment tab UI has been removed from Dashboard.tsx pending Veracross API endpoint confirmation.
- **`absences/route.ts`** — GET fetches today's student absences from Veracross API. Authenticates via OAuth2 client credentials (`VERACROSS_CLIENT_ID`, `VERACROSS_CLIENT_SECRET`) with scopes `master_attendance:list students:list`. Paginates through all master_attendance pages AND all student pages using `X-Page-Number` header (API caps at 1000/page, school has ~1731 students). Builds a `gradeMap` (Map<person_id, grade_level_id>) from the `/v3/students` endpoint (the master_attendance endpoint does NOT include grade_level). Converts numeric grade_level IDs to display labels via `GRADE_LABELS` map (40=Infant/Toddler, 35=2 Year Nursery, 30=3 Year Nursery, 25=4 Year Nursery, 20=Kindergarten, 1-8=1st-8th Grade). Excludes HS by `grading_period !== 29`, excludes present students (`attendance_category === 0`). After fetching live data, queries Supabase `attendance_cache` for YTD absence counts (`attendance_category = 1`) and consecutive absence streaks (walks backwards through cached school days). Returns `{ date, total, absences, tardies, earlyDismissals }` with each record containing `person_id, name, status_code, status_label, excused, attendance_category, notes, late_arrival_time, early_dismissal_time, grade_level, grade_level_id, ytd_absences, consecutive_absences`. Cache queries wrapped in try/catch — defaults to 0 if cache is empty.
- **`absences/sync/route.ts`** — GET syncs Veracross attendance to Supabase `attendance_cache` table. Protected by `Authorization: Bearer ${SYNC_SECRET}` header (401 without). Two modes via `?mode=` query param: `daily` (default) fetches today's attendance, filters out HS, upserts all records (including present) with `onConflict: 'person_id,attendance_date'`; `backfill` loops from 2025-09-02 to yesterday (skipping weekends), fetches each day, upserts, 300ms delay between requests. Both modes also fetch paginated students for grade_level_id lookup.
- **`lever/route.ts`** — GET fetches all Lever recruiting data in parallel: postings (published + internal), opportunities (limit 100, `expand=applications` so each application is an object with `posting` field), and stages. Auth via `LEVER_API_KEY` (Basic auth). Filters out SAR High School postings/teams. Returns `{ postings, opportunities, stages }`.
- **`webhook/lever/route.ts`** — POST webhook for Lever events. Processes `applicationCreated`: fetches opportunity + posting, skips HS, sends Slack DM to RBK (U04NBR22Y) with applicant name, role, and Lever link. Always returns 200. **To activate:** Lever Settings > Integrations & API > Webhooks, add URL `https://rbk-cmd-center.web.app/api/webhook/lever`, enable `applicationCreated`.
- **`webhook/email/route.ts`** — Inbound email webhook from Google Apps Script. Accepts `EmailWebhookPayload` with auth via `WEBHOOK_SECRET`. On insert, extracts from name/email, parses date, stores to `emails` table (including `draft_reply` when present). **Phase 2:** Stamps `workspace_id: 'a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890'` (RBK seed workspace) on every new email insert. Handles duplicates (23505) by updating `draft_status` AND `draft_reply` if provided — so draft content from Emily's "Drafts Ready" sync lands in the DB even when the email already exists from a prior triage sync. **Priority remapping:** When incoming `priority === 'drafts_ready'` (not a valid DB enum), remaps to `priority: 'owner_action'` and sets `draft_status: 'draft_ready'` so the email lands in the Drafts Ready section. **Priority enum (post-Phase E)**: `owner_action` (formerly `rbk_action`) = RBK's action required; `assistant_action` (formerly `eg_action`) = Emily's queue. Both renames are DB-level — UI label strings are configurable per workspace via `module_config.inbox.owner_label` / `assistant_label`.

### Config & Libs

- **`lib/auth.ts`** — Auth utilities and session handling. `getAuthSession()` returns `AuthSession` with `user`, `accessToken`, and (Phase 2) `workspaceId`, `role`, `modules`, `moduleConfig` — all read from the `__session` cookie. Phase B added `currentMember`, `assistant`, `principal`; Phase E added `workspaceOwnerEmail`, `workspaceBrand`. New fields are additive; existing callers that only use `accessToken`/`user` are unaffected.
- **`lib/googleToken.ts`** — Two token helpers: (1) `getValidGoogleToken(workspaceId, userEmail)`: reads per-user token from `user_google_tokens` table, auto-refreshes if expired (5-min buffer), saves new access_token + expiry. Deletes row on `invalid_grant`. Returns null if user hasn't connected. (2) `getGoogleAccessToken(workspaceId)`: workspace-level fallback using `workspaces.gmail_refresh_token`. Used for email sync. Calendar routes use per-user token ONLY (no workspace/session fallback). Returns `{ events: [], notConnected: true }` with status 200 if no token. During impersonation, calendar routes read `X-Impersonated-Email`/`X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` headers to use the impersonated user's token. Gmail routes keep using workspace-level token for email sync.
- **`lib/getIntegration.ts`** — Phase F per-workspace credential lookup. Reads `workspace_integrations` table first, falls back to env vars when no row (or empty field), 5-minute in-memory cache per `(workspaceId, integration_type)`. Typed helpers: `getVeracrossCredentials()`, `getSlackCredentials()`, `getLeverCredentials()`, `getAnthropicCredentials()`. `invalidateIntegrationCache(workspaceId, type)` clears cache on writes. Fall-back uses `||` (not `??`) so an empty DB string still picks up env.
- **`lib/divisions.ts`** — Constants: `DIVISION_ACADEMY = 'academy'`, `DIVISION_HS = 'hs'`, `ACADEMY_GRADE_NUMBERS = [40,35,30,25,20,1-8]`, `HS_GRADE_NUMBERS = [9,10,11,12]`, `HS_GRADING_PERIODS = [24, 29]` (24 = current; 29 reserved by Veracross for prior year). Helpers: `getGradeFilterForMember(divisions)` returns the correct Veracross grade-number filter for the caller (empty defaults to Academy-only), `memberCanSeeDivision(divisions, division)`, `shouldExcludeHs(divisions)`.
- **`lib/divisionParam.ts`** — `applyDivisionParam(raw, callerDivisions)` parses the optional `?division=academy|hs|both` query param and validates that the requested subset is actually a subset of the caller's divisions.
- **`lib/impersonate.ts`** — Server-side impersonation. `getEffectiveWorkspaceId(session)` honors `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` header but only for `rglassberg@saracademy.org` (the admin). `getEffectiveMembership(session)` returns the impersonated `workspace_members` row (including `divisions`). `getEffectiveDivisions(session)` returns the impersonated user's divisions when admin is impersonating, else falls back to `session.currentMember?.divisions ?? []`.
- **`lib/apiFetch.ts`** — Client-side fetch wrapper. Reads `localStorage.impersonation` (`{ workspace_id, email }`) and auto-attaches `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` + `X-Impersonated-Email` headers when impersonation is active. **Use this instead of plain `fetch()` for any Dashboard call that needs impersonation-awareness** — plain `fetch()` will silently leak the real admin's identity to the server.
- **`lib/emailIdentity.ts`** — Phase C. `getSenderIdentity(workspaceId, userEmail)` returns `{ fromEmail, fromName, signatureHtml }` from `workspace_members.email_identity_html`. Defensive `LEGACY_RBK_FROM_EMAIL` / `LEGACY_RBK_FROM_NAME` / `LEGACY_RBK_SIGNATURE` constants guard against null fields for the principal.
- **`lib/assignees.ts`** — `normalizeToCapitalized(v)` coerces any case of `'rbk'|'emily'|'sara'|'leora'|'becca'` to the Capitalized canonical form used by the CHECK constraints on `tasks.assigned_to` and `agenda_notes.assignee`. Used by every write path. `toLegacyLowercase(v)` is a transitional shim retired in Phase B.
- **`lib/getWorkspaceMemberByAssignee.ts`** — Replaces the old hardcoded Slack rosters. `getWorkspaceMembers(workspaceId)` returns all members with `assignee_key` set; `getMemberByAssigneeKey(workspaceId, key)` (case-insensitive) resolves to a single member row; `getMemberByEmail(email)` is the email-keyed lookup. Each row includes `id, email, display_name, assignee_key, slack_user_id, divisions, title, assistant_to`.
- **`lib/supabase.ts`** — Supabase client initialization
- **`lib/firebase-client.ts`** — Firebase client SDK setup
- **`lib/firebase-admin.ts`** — Firebase Admin SDK (verifyIdToken)
- **`types/index.ts`** — Shared TypeScript interfaces (Email, CalendarEvent, Doc, etc.)
- **`types/database.ts`** — Database type definitions
- **`hooks/useRealtimeEmails.ts`** — Real-time email subscription hook

## Current Features

### Email Inbox
- **Unified `ExpandedEmailPanel` component** — single shared expanded view used everywhere (EmailCard, all inbox sections, popup modal). Root div is `flex flex-col h-full`; inner two-column layout is `flex-1 min-h-0` with thread side (`flex-[4]`, `overflow-y-auto flex-1`) and draft side (`flex-[5]`, `flex-1 min-h-0`). Both sides fill available height and scroll independently. Draft textarea is **uncontrolled** (`defaultValue={draftValue}`) with a `draftTextRef` (useRef) for tracking edits — avoids Dashboard re-renders on every keystroke. `onChange` writes to `draftTextRef.current`; `onBlur` auto-saves to Supabase; "Mark Ready" reads from the ref. The `key={selectedEmailObj.id}` on ExpandedEmailPanel forces remount on email switch so `defaultValue` resets correctly. Left panel card clicks set `draftTextRef.current` to the selected email's draft content. Includes "Discard" button (red outline) alongside Send button in Drafts popup to reset draft content.
- **Thread support** — emails with matching `thread_id` are grouped and displayed chronologically in the left panel. Shows "Thread (N)" label for multi-message threads, "Original Email" for single emails. Each message shows sender name, timestamp, and cleaned body separated by dividers. Thread emails are fetched from Supabase if not all present in local state (handles threads beyond the 500-email fetch limit).
- **`linkifyText` helper** — converts URLs in email body text to clickable `<a>` tags (blue, underlined, opens in new tab). Applied in ExpandedEmailPanel thread view and task panel email body.
- **Three-zone Gmail-like layout** — Zone 1: Top bar with category `<select>` dropdown (RBK Action, Review, Important No Action, Invitations, FYI, Untagged, Emily Action, Sent), Drafts Ready button, TBD button, Sync Gmail button. Zone 2: Left panel (w-[380px]) with search input and scrollable compact email card list (priority dot, sender, subject, time ago). Zone 3: Right panel (flex-1, flex-col h-full) with sticky header (subject + sender on left, action icon row on right, border-b) above scrollable `ExpandedEmailPanel`. Action icons: Mark Done, Mark Urgent, TBD, Remind Me, Add to Agenda (star), Add to Calendar, Add Task — same handlers as EmailCard. Empty state shows envelope icon. `emailCategory` state (line 268) controls which derived array is shown; `expandedEmail` state selects the email for the right panel. Category dropdown shows live counts per category. `ExpandedEmailPanel` is rendered with `key={selectedEmailObj.id}` so it remounts on email switch (resets `defaultValue` in the draft textarea). Left panel card clicks set `draftTextRef.current` to the selected email's draft content. Separate `draftText` useState is kept for the Drafts Ready popup modal textarea (different context, no focus loss issue there).
- **Sent category** — "Sent" option in category dropdown fetches last 50 sent messages from Gmail API via `GET /api/gmail/sent` on first selection. State: `sentEmails` (array), `sentLoading` (boolean), `sentLoadedRef` (useRef, guards single fetch). Left panel cards show "To: recipient" instead of sender, no priority dot. Right panel shows a simple header (subject + "To:" + date) and full-width body text (`whitespace-pre-wrap`) — no ExpandedEmailPanel, no draft textarea, no action icons. Search filters sent emails by subject or recipient.
- **Mark as read/unread** — clicking an email card optimistically sets `is_unread: false` and fires a PATCH to `/api/emails/status`. "Mark Unread" envelope icon appears in the right panel action row when `is_unread === false`; clicking it sets `is_unread: true`.
- Priority color coding with dots from `priorityConfig` map
- Draft editing with approve/revision workflow (Request Revision sends to Emily's queue with comment)
- Send via Gmail API with batch support — no approval gate, sendable as long as draft has content
- **Gmail processing on completion/send** — when a task is completed or an email is sent, the connected email is: (1) marked 'done' in Supabase (optimistic update), (2) labeled "RBK/Done" in Gmail, (3) archived (INBOX label removed). Only fires for email-connected tasks, not standalone/agenda-note-only tasks. Gmail calls are fire-and-forget (never block the action).
- Archive functionality (marks done in Supabase + archives in Gmail)
- Snooze with reminder dates (hides email until date)
- Search across all fields (subject, from, summary, body) — filters the active category
- Bulk selection: floating action bar for selected emails (mark done, clear)
- Shiva emails (`action_status === 'shiva'`) are excluded from all inbox priority sections but are **not** shown in the inbox — they appear on the Simchas & Shivas page instead.
- Snoozed emails filtered out of main lists

### Meeting Agenda
- Left sidebar showing today's calendar schedule with date navigation
- Agenda items from `agenda_items` table — supports emails, topics, and manual items
- Auto-syncs flagged emails into agenda_items on mount
- **"+ Add Item" button** at top of agenda list — opens inline form with title input and optional email search/link. Creates `item_type='manual'` items (or `'email'` if linked).
- Drag-and-drop reorder of agenda items (bulk PATCH sort_order)
- Threaded notes per agenda item saved to Supabase `agenda_notes` table — single chronological stream (no tab filter)
- Inline note adding — all new notes save as `type='note'` by default
- Existing notes still display type indicator dots (slate=note, blue=decision, amber=action)
- **View Email button** on email-type agenda items — opens the email popup modal (`popupEmailId`) with `ExpandedEmailPanel`
- **"+ project" button** on each agenda item — inline dropdown with title (pre-filled) + department selector, creates project via POST `/api/projects`
- **Topic tags system** — topics are tags applied to any agenda item, not separate agenda items
  - Each item shows applied tags as small chips (bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 rounded-full)
  - "+ tag" button opens dropdown of available topics to apply
  - Tags stored in `tags text[]` column on `agenda_items` table
  - Applied tags are removable (x button)
  - "Available Tags" panel at bottom for managing tag library (add/remove)
- "Set as Current" indicator for active discussion item
- Mark as discussed checkbox per item
- Delete note and remove from agenda controls
- Create task from email modal (sets meeting_notes with `[@RBK]` or `[@EMILY]` prefix)

### Tasks
- **Data sources:**
  1. Email-derived tasks: emails with `meeting_notes` containing `[@RBK]` or `[@EMILY]` markers
  2. Agenda note-derived tasks: agenda notes with `type='action'` and an assignee (from Supabase)
  3. Standalone tasks: created via Add Task button, saved directly to Supabase `agenda_notes` table as `type='action'`
- **Visual design (Option B — purposeful color coding):**
  - Floating card UI with 3D hover effect (custom `boxShadow` inline styles, `translateY(-2px)` on hover)
  - Color-coded left borders: blue=RBK, teal=Emily, amber=urgent, green=completed
  - `bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100` card style
  - Rest shadow: `0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.06), 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)`
  - Hover shadow: `0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.10), 0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)`
- **Urgent visual treatment:**
  - Tasks marked urgent (via toggle, `priority === 'owner_action'`, or past-due date) get `bg-rose-50` background + `border-l-amber-400` left border
  - Red "Urgent" badge appears inline next to task text
  - Urgent toggle button (exclamation triangle icon) appears on hover for all cards, always visible when urgent
  - Urgent state persisted to localStorage as `taskUrgent` (`Record<string, boolean>`)
- **Layout:**
  - Two-column layout split by assignee (RBK / Emily) with pending count badges
  - RBK column header: blue-500 badge. Emily column header: teal-500 badge
  - No filter pills — all tasks shown, urgent tasks visually distinguished
  - Cards show: completion circle, task text, urgent badge (if urgent), urgent toggle (on hover), source badge pill (blue=email, violet=agenda, slate=manual), date, due date (red if overdue), assignee avatar circle
  - **"Drafts to Approve" collapsible section** pinned to top of RBK column (collapsed by default, persisted as `draftsToApproveCollapsed`). Shows emails where `draft_status === 'draft_ready' && status !== 'done' && status !== 'junk'`. Card click expands `ExpandedEmailPanel` inline with Send/Discard/Request Revision buttons. Auto-collapses after action.
  - Collapsible "X completed" section per column (green-400 left border, opacity-50)
- **Interactions:**
  - Drag-and-drop reordering within columns (persisted to localStorage as `taskOrder`)
  - Double-click to inline-edit agenda note task text on cards
  - Click card → opens side panel
  - Click circle → toggles completion (PATCHes `completed` field to Supabase for agenda notes, toggles `[DONE]` in meeting_notes for email tasks)
- **Floating Add Task button:**
  - Fixed bottom-right (`fixed bottom-8 right-8`), dark slate circle, `+` icon
  - Opens inline form at top of RBK column
  - Form has: text input, RBK/Emily assignee toggle (blue/teal pills), Cancel/Add buttons
  - POSTs to `/api/agenda-notes` with `type='action'`, optimistically updates `actionNotes` state
  - Supports Enter key to submit, Escape to cancel
- **Slide-in side panel** (420px right drawer, z-50, semi-transparent backdrop):
  - **Header:** Editable task title (click to edit for agenda tasks, textarea with auto-height), assignee badge, source badge (blue=email, violet=agenda, slate=manual), urgent badge + toggle button, complete badge
  - **Due Date:** Date + time inputs side by side (persisted to localStorage as `taskDueDates` with `{date, time}` shape). Shows "Slack reminder" hint when both set.
  - **Source (agenda tasks):** Clickable link to parent agenda item (navigates to Agenda view), AI summary/context from parent email
  - **Source (email tasks):** Sender name, relative time, email summary or action_needed
  - **Full Email (email tasks):** Scrollable body text preview (max-h-56), editable draft reply textarea (saves on blur)
  - **Assignee + Created/Updated row:** Assignee toggle button (click to swap RBK↔Emily for agenda tasks, read-only for email tasks), created date, last updated date (persisted to localStorage as `taskLastUpdated`)
  - **Notes:** Textarea for free-form notes (persisted to localStorage as `taskNotes`, saves on blur)
  - **Footer:** "Mark Complete" (green) / "Mark Incomplete" button + Close button

### Calendar
- Google Calendar API integration
- Fetches events on mount with date navigation (prev/next day buttons)
- **Auth prompts:** Login page uses `prompt: 'select_account'` (account picker). Token refresh `refreshGoogleToken()` uses `prompt: 'none'` (silent) with ALL scopes (calendar + gmail.send + gmail.modify). Gmail/Calendar OAuth consent flow (`/api/auth/gmail-consent`) uses `prompt: 'consent'` + `access_type: 'offline'` (required for refresh token). Scopes: `gmail.readonly`, `gmail.send`, `gmail.modify`, `calendar.readonly`, `calendar.events`. State encodes `{ workspaceId, userEmail }` as base64url JSON. Callback saves per-user tokens to `user_google_tokens` table (upsert on `workspace_id, user_email`). Also saves workspace-level `gmail_refresh_token` if user is the workspace owner (kraussb@saracademy.org) for email sync compatibility. **`user_google_tokens` table**: `id`, `workspace_id` (FK), `user_email`, `access_token`, `refresh_token`, `token_expiry`, `scopes`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. UNIQUE on `(workspace_id, user_email)`. RLS enabled with service role full access. Used on 401 for both calendar fetches and email sends.
- Create and delete events (with confirmation dialog for delete)
- Create event from email (pre-fills title, description with summary)
- Meeting countdown alert (checks every 30 seconds, shows banner when meeting within 5 minutes with join link)
- Shows in agenda sidebar and dashboard sidebar
- Filters past events on today view

### Projects (Kanban Board)
- **Nav:** "Projects" item in sidebar with kanban columns icon
- **Layout:** Horizontal-scrolling kanban board with 11 department columns (min/max 280px each)
  - Departments: Communications, Technology, Development, Finance, HR, Media, Building & Facilities, Admissions, Admin Team, Pisgah, Student Activities
  - Each column has colored top border via `departmentColors` map (blue, slate, violet, emerald, rose, cyan, amber, teal, indigo, orange, pink)
- **Project cards:** Priority badge pill on view, inline priority toggle on hover (High/Medium/Low pill row, same style as add modal), title, description (line-clamp-2), department tag chips (max 2 shown, +N overflow), link pills (max 3, link-chain icon, clickable, `e.stopPropagation`), progress bar (h-1.5, uses department accent color from `departmentColors[dept].fill`), assignee avatar (RBK blue / EG teal), due date (red if overdue)
  - Same floating card hover effect as Tasks (translateY(-2pm), elevated shadow)
  - **Card drag-and-drop:** HTML5 native drag, cards draggable between any department column. Dragging card: opacity-50/cursor-grabbing. Drop target column: bg-blue-50 + ring-1 ring-blue-200. Blue insertion line above hovered card. `projectDragRef` ref prevents drag from triggering click-to-open-panel. On drop to different column: PATCHes `department` field.
- **Column drag-and-drop:** Reorder department columns by dragging the grip icon (⠿) in column headers (opacity-0, visible on hover). Column order persisted in localStorage as `projectColumnOrder`. Blue vertical insertion line between columns. Falls back to default `DEPARTMENTS` order.
- **Side panel** (420px right drawer, same pattern as Tasks panel):
  - Header: department badge, editable title (auto-resize textarea), close button
  - Status: 3-way toggle (Active=blue / On Hold=amber / Complete=green)
  - Priority: 3-way pill toggle (High=rose / Medium=amber / Low=slate) — immediately saves on click
  - Progress: range slider 0-100 with percentage label + visual progress bar below using department accent color
  - Description: Tiptap rich text editor (bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, bullet/numbered lists, links). Saves HTML to Supabase on blur. Keyed by `project.id` to remount on project change.
  - Also involves (tags): removable department chips + "+" dropdown to add from remaining departments, PATCHes `tags` array
  - Assignee + Due Date: side-by-side, assignee is clickable toggle, due date is date input
  - Updates: Tiptap rich text editor + Post button. Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to post. Editor remounts (via `updateEditorKey`) after posting to clear content. Saved updates stored as HTML strings in localStorage `projectUpdates`, rendered with `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` and `prose prose-sm` styling. Reverse-chronological feed in bg-slate-50 cards with relative timestamps.
  - Links section removed — links are now inserted inline via Tiptap's link extension in description/updates
  - Footer: "Mark Complete" (green) / "Reopen" + "Archive" (red text, confirms)
- **Add Project modal** (centered, max-w-md): title input, department `<select>`, priority pills (3-way toggle), assignee toggle (RBK/Emily), Cancel + Create buttons
- **Floating + button:** same as Tasks (fixed bottom-8 right-8, slate-900 circle)
- **API:** `/api/projects` — GET (excludes `status='archived'`), POST (requires title + department), PATCH (accepts id + any subset of fields including `links`, `tags`, `department`), DELETE (soft-archives via `status='archived'` update)
- **Data:** Supabase `projects` table

### Gemara Card (Dashboard)
- Replaces the old "Meeting Agenda" summary card in the dashboard's top 4-card grid
- Lightweight resource board for Emily to drop links, notes, and reminders for RBK's Gemara class
- **Card styling:** matches other summary cards (white bg, rounded-xl, emerald top border, shadow-sm)
- **Items list:** compact scrollable list (max-h-280px), each item has type-specific styling:
  - **Link items:** blue-50/60 background, link chain icon, clickable title (opens in new tab)
  - **Note items:** amber-50/60 background, edit/pencil icon, title + body text (line-clamp-2)
  - Trash icon on hover per item (opacity-0 → opacity-100, text-slate-300 hover:text-red-400)
- **Inline add form:** "+ Add" button in card header toggles form at top of list
  - Type toggle: Link / Note (pill toggle, blue-500 / amber-500)
  - Title input (required), URL input (for links) or body textarea (for notes)
  - Save/Cancel buttons
  - On save: POST to `/api/gemara`, optimistically prepend to list, collapse form
- **API:** `/api/gemara` — GET, POST, DELETE (hard delete by id query param)
- **Data:** Supabase `gemara_items` table (id uuid, type text, title text, url text nullable, body text nullable, created_at timestamp)

### Communications (`activeNav === 'communications'`)
- **Nav:** "Communications" item in sidebar under Daily section
- **Section 1 — Pending Approvals:** Fetches from `GET /api/monday/communications` on mount. Shows cards for each item pending RBK approval from Monday.com board 4035548140. Each card: item name (bold), comm type colored pill (Email=blue, Chadashot=amber, LinkedIn=blue-dark, Today@SAR=teal, Jewish Link=purple, Digest=gray, other=slate), requester/audience/dueDate metadata, notes (truncated 2 lines). Actions: "View draft" (external link if draftLink exists), "View attachment" (if file exists), "Approve" (green, optimistic removal, PATCH approve), "Request changes" (inline textarea expansion with Send/Cancel). Toast notifications on actions. Empty state with checkmark. "Open board in Monday" header link.
- **Section 2 — Social Media:** 4-column grid of link cards: SAR Instagram, RBK Instagram, RBK LinkedIn, RBK X. Each opens in new tab with brand icon + name + handle.
- **State:** `mondayCommsItems`, `commsLoading`, `commsError`, `expandedRequestId`, `requestNoteText`, `commsToast`
- **API:** `/api/monday/communications` — GET (pending items), PATCH (approve/request_changes). Requires `MONDAY_API_KEY` env var.

### Simchas & Shivas (`activeNav === 'simchas'`)
- **Nav:** "Simchas & Shivas" item in sidebar under **Community** section (star icon)
- **Data sources:**
  1. Bar/Bat Mitzvahs: fetched from `/api/simchas` which parses the SAR public iCal feed (`barbatmitzvah@saracademy.org`) for current week events
  2. Shivas/Funerals: fetched from `/api/calendar/week` (full week of Google Calendar events), filtered where title contains "shiva", "funeral", or "levaya" (case-insensitive)
- **Two design prototypes** displayed vertically for RBK to compare:
  - **Design A — Week View:** 7-column grid (Mon–Sun), today highlighted blue-50. Events as colored pills (blue for B'nei Mitzvah with star icon, slate for shivas/funerals with dot icon). Click pill to expand details.
  - **Design B — Card View:** Two side-by-side cards (blue top border for B'nei Mitzvah, slate for Shivas). Each lists events with day/date, title, time, description/location.
- Empty state shows "None this week" per section when no events found
- **RSVP toggle** — Each Bar/Bat Mitzvah event has Yes/No buttons. Yes = green, No = blue (not red). Click active button to deselect (reset to null). State persisted to localStorage as `simchasAttending` (`Record<string, 'yes' | 'no'>` keyed by event UID, migrates legacy boolean format). Not yet wired to Google Calendar.
- **B'nei Mitzvah email filter + dismiss** — `bneiMitzvahInvitations` excludes reminders, confirmations, and dismissed IDs. Dismiss: `dismissed_invitations` Supabase table (per-user, `workspace_id + email_id + dismissed_by` unique). API: `GET/POST /api/simchas/dismiss`. X button on each card (opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100). Optimistic removal from UI.
- **Email HTML sanitization** — `sanitizeEmailHtml()` strips MS Office conditional comments, `<o:p>`/`<w:>`/`<m:>` tags, `<style>`/`<script>` blocks, class/id attrs. Applied to all HTML email bodies in ExpandedEmailPanel via `dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: sanitizeEmailHtml(msg.body) }}`. Container: `prose prose-sm max-w-none text-sm overflow-hidden max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto`.
- **Shiva email filter** — `shivaEmails` only shows emails where subject contains "hamakom" (official SAR condolence notices).
- **Send Note button** — Each shiva email card has a "Send Note" button that creates an action task (`POST /api/agenda-notes`) assigned to Emily: "Draft condolence note for [family name]". Shows "Sent" after click. State: `shivaNoteSent`, `shivaNoteSending`.
- **Shiva Emails section** — Collapsible section at the bottom of the page showing emails with `action_status === 'shiva'`. Collapsed by default. Each card shows subject, sender, summary, relative time, and an "Add to Calendar" button that opens the Create Event modal pre-filled with the email subject as title and summary as description. Uses `expandedSections.shivaEmails` for collapse state.
- **State:** `bnbMitzvahs` (fetched from API), `weekCalendarEvents` (fetched from `/api/calendar/week`), `simchasLoading`, `expandedSimcha` (for pill click expansion), `simchasAttending` (localStorage-persisted attending toggles)

### Student Logs (`activeNav === 'student-logs'`)
- **Nav:** "Student Logs" item in sidebar under **Academics** section (document icon). Gated to `admissions` module.
- **Content:** Heading, description ("Weekly behavior log summary from Veracross, subtotaled by grade and student."), "Open in Veracross" button linking to Axiom query #1473847, and an iframe embedding the same URL.
- **Iframe fallback:** If Veracross blocks embedding (X-Frame-Options), the iframe `onError` hides it and shows a fallback message with the open button.

### Student Absences (`activeNav === 'absences'`)
- **Nav:** "Student Absences" item in sidebar under **Academics** section (users icon)
- **Data:** Fetched from `/api/absences` on mount (lazy — only when navigating to page). Veracross API integration with OAuth2 client credentials.
- **Page header:** Today's date (formatted from API response), refresh button with spin animation
- **Summary pills:** Four color-coded count pills at top: Absent (red), Tardy (amber), Early Dismissal (blue), Not Expected (purple — only shown when count > 0)
- **Four collapsible sections** — records grouped by `grade_level` (sorted lowest to highest, K treated as 0). Each grade is a self-contained block: full-width grade label header (`text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wide`) showing display names (e.g. "Kindergarten", "3rd Grade"), then a `grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-6` of students, with `mb-4` between grade blocks and `<hr>` separator lines between groups. Sorted by `grade_level_id` using custom order: [40, 35, 30, 25, 20, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8] (youngest first). Compact rows: py-1.5, text-sm font-medium text-slate-800 name, small badge. Unexcused rows have `border-l-2 border-red-400 pl-3` accent for quick visual distinction.
  1. **Absent** — sorted unexcused first, then excused within each grade group. Each row: name, status label badge (red for unexcused, amber for excused), notes if present
  2. **Tardy** — each row: name, status label badge, late arrival time if present
  3. **Early Dismissal** — each row: name, "Early Dismissal" badge, dismissal time if present
  4. **Not Expected** — `student_attendance_status === 32`, `attendance_category === 0`. Purple styling (bg-purple-100 text-purple-700). Each row: name, "Not Expected" badge, notes if present. Section only renders when count > 0.
- **"Needs Follow-Up" alert box** — shown at top (after summary pills, before Absent section) only when flagged students exist. `bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 rounded-xl`. Two sub-lists: (1) "Consecutive Absences (3+ days)" — students absent 3+ consecutive school days, shows name, grade, streak count. (2) "High YTD Absences (15+)" — students with 15+ absences this year who are absent today, shows name, grade, total count. A student can appear in both.
- **YTD badge** — each absent student row shows `(N this year)` in `text-xs text-slate-400` after the status badge, only if `ytd_absences > 0`.
- **Attendance cache** — Supabase `attendance_cache` table stores historical attendance data. Populated by `/api/absences/sync` route (daily mode via Firebase scheduled function at 6 PM ET weekdays, backfill mode for historical data from 2025-09-02). Table has unique constraint on `(person_id, attendance_date)`.
- **Loading:** Skeleton cards with pulse animation. Error state with retry button.
- **Filtering:** Excludes HS students (grades 9-12). Includes records where `attendance_category > 0` OR `student_attendance_status !== 0` (captures "Not Expected" which has `attendance_category === 0` but `student_attendance_status === 32`)
- **State:** `absencesData` (AbsencesData | null), `absencesLoading`, `absencesCollapsed` (per-section collapse state)
- **Env vars:** `VERACROSS_CLIENT_ID`, `VERACROSS_CLIENT_SECRET`, `VERACROSS_SCHOOL_ROUTE` (defaults to 'sar'), `VERACROSS_DEVELOPMENT_CLIENT_ID`, `VERACROSS_DEVELOPMENT_CLIENT_SECRET` (for development/gifts API), `SYNC_SECRET` (for sync endpoint auth), `LEVER_API_KEY` (Lever API JWT token for recruiting data), `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN` (for Lever webhook Slack notifications), `MONDAY_API_KEY` (Monday.com API token for Communications approvals queue, board 4035548140)

### Dashboard
- Welcome header reads `useWorkspace().displayName` (shows impersonated name during impersonation, falls back to email prefix)
- Calendar events sidebar (today's schedule)
- Summary cards row: Urgent (emails + manually marked urgent tasks), Quick Links, Important Docs, Gemara
- URL param support: reads `?nav=` and `?projectPanel=` on mount for cross-page navigation from `/home`
- **To-Do Today section** with subsections: Urgent emails, Review Drafts, Approved Drafts Ready to Send, Tasks
  - Tasks sorted with urgent first (tasks in `taskUrgent` state or past-due tasks sort to top)
  - Urgent tasks (manually marked, `owner_action` priority, or past-due) get `bg-rose-50` background + amber left border + red "Urgent" badge (matches Tasks page treatment)
  - **Task cards are clickable** — clicking opens the same task side panel used on the Tasks page (shared `taskPanelId` state)
  - Task side panel is rendered at top-level (outside any `activeNav` conditional) so it works from Dashboard, Tasks, or any view
  - Active/selected card shows blue ring highlight (`ring-2 ring-blue-200 ring-offset-1`)
  - Completion circles work for both email tasks (`toggleTaskComplete`) and agenda note tasks (`toggleNoteTaskComplete`) — unified button with hover states
- Meeting countdown banner
- Important Documents section (stored in Supabase, editable titles)
- Quick access to all views via nav

### Recruiting (`activeNav === 'lever'`)
- Powered by Lever API (`LEVER_API_KEY`). Sidebar nav: "Recruiting" under Operations group (briefcase icon).
- Summary pills row (Open Roles, New This Week, Active Pipeline, Total 1yr) + horizontal scrolling pipeline stage cards below (each stage as clickable card with name + count, selected state with blue ring; "All" card to reset; Hired=green, Offer=amber)
- Two-column layout: left = "Recent Applications" (last 30 opps within 1 year, sorted by createdAt desc; each row has colored left border by stage, name, role title or headline fallback, stage badge, origin badge, source, time ago, Lever link); right = "Open Roles" (w-96, grouped by `categories.team` — department is always "SAR Academy" so useless, falls back to commitment→"General"). `getOppPostingId()` helper handles both string and expanded object posting IDs. Roles open 2+ years hidden by default with toggle.
- API fetches only active candidates (`archived=false` query param) so all returned opportunities are active pipeline. No client-side archived filtering needed.
- Filters out SAR High School postings and their associated opportunities; opportunities older than 1 year excluded
- API uses `expand=applications` on opportunities fetch so `opp.applications[0].posting` is an object with posting ID for correct cross-referencing. Debug logging of first posting categories + first opportunity in server logs (temporary).
- Clicking a stage card filters candidates list to that stage (up to 50 results, no time limit); clicking "All" resets. Header updates to "Candidates — [Stage Name]".
- Clicking a candidate row opens 420px slide-in detail panel: name, stage badge, role, source, contact info (email/phone), tags, headline, days in pipeline, "View in Lever" button.
- State: `selectedLeverStage` (stage ID | null), `leverCandidatePanel` (opportunity object | null)
- Lazy-loaded on first nav, refresh button resets data + increments refreshKey
- Lever webhook (`/api/webhook/lever`): on `applicationCreated`, sends Slack DM to RBK with applicant name + role + Lever link (skips HS)

### Home Page (`/home`)
- Personal landing page for non-RBK users. Viewers auto-redirect here on login.
- **WelcomeHeader:** Time-of-day greeting (Source Serif 4) + long date
- **TodayHero:** Up to 2 due-this-week / recently-updated project cards with ProgressRing
- **MyProjectsList:** Grouped by status (In progress / On hold / Complete), collapsible sections persisted to localStorage
- **DashboardsGrid:** Tinted tile grid of available module dashboards, click navigates to `/?nav={id}`
- **WeekCalendarPeek:** SAR school calendar events for current week (from `/api/home/week-events`)
- Data fetched from `/api/home` + `/api/home/week-events` in parallel

### Admin Permissions (`/admin/permissions`)
- Admin-only page (Becca only). Client-side email check + API 403 for non-admin.
- Table of all workspace members with toggle checkboxes for 6 modules (admissions, absences, lever, faculty_absences, simchas, projects)
- Sorted by workspace name then display name. Workspace column shown.
- Owners/assistants: disabled grayed checkmarks. Viewers: interactive toggles updating `allowed_modules` JSONB.
- Optimistic updates with error revert + toast. No sidebar nav item — direct URL access. Admin sidebar section with key icon link.

### Impersonation (Admin-only)
- Client-side feature for `rglassberg@saracademy.org` only. No server-side auth changes.
- Sidebar shows impersonation dropdown (all workspace members) instead of workspace switcher.
- When active: `useWorkspace()` returns impersonated `workspaceId`, `role`, `displayName`, `allowedModules`. Firebase user unchanged.
- Amber `ImpersonationBanner` at top of every page (rendered from `layout.tsx`). Shows target info + "Exit impersonation" button.
- API routes honor `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` header (via `lib/impersonate.ts`) only for admin email.
- `lib/apiFetch.ts` wrapper auto-adds the header from localStorage.

### Emily's Queue
- Filtered view of Emily's action items, needs-revision emails, and TBD emails needing input

### TBD Feature
- **Holding area** for emails RBK is unsure about — parks them out of regular priority sections
- **TBD toggle icon** on EmailCard (question-mark-circle, amber when active) — toggles `action_status` between `'tbd'` and `null`
- **TBD button** in top action bar (amber, shows count badge + teal notification dot when Emily has added suggestions)
- **TBD popup modal** — shows all TBD emails with subject, sender, time, summary, action_needed. Shows "Emily suggests: [text]" in teal box when suggestion exists. "Move to Action" returns email to its priority section, "Mark Done" archives it.
- **Emily's Queue "Needs Your Input" section** — TBD emails appear at top of Emily's Queue with `TbdInputCard` component. Emily can type and save suggestions (teal-styled cards with textarea).
- **Suggestion flow:** Emily saves suggestion via PATCH `/api/emails/status` with `tbd_suggestion` field → teal notification dot appears on RBK's TBD button → RBK opens popup, sees suggestion, decides to act or mark done.
- **TBD Notes:** Each TBD email in the popup has a notes textarea saved to Supabase (`tbd_notes` column on emails table) on blur. Syncs across devices. SQL: `ALTER TABLE emails ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tbd_notes TEXT;`
- **TBD on Agenda page:** Collapsible "TBD" section at bottom of Agenda page (above Available Tags), expanded by default. Clicking any TBD item opens the same shared TBD popup modal used on All Emails page (with notes textarea, Emily's suggestion, Move to Action, Mark Done).
- **Filtering:** TBD emails are excluded from all 6 priority section lists (`e.action_status !== 'tbd'`) and included in `emilyQueue` count.
- **Database:** `tbd_suggestion TEXT` and `tbd_notes TEXT` columns on `emails` table. SQL: `ALTER TABLE emails ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tbd_suggestion TEXT; ALTER TABLE emails ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tbd_notes TEXT;`

## Supabase Tables

### `emails`
Main email storage with fields for priority, status, draft management, meeting flags, meeting notes, attachments, reminder_date, revision_comment, tbd_suggestion, message_id.

### `agenda_notes`
| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `id` | uuid | Primary key |
| `email_id` | uuid (nullable) | FK to emails table (nullable for standalone tasks) |
| `agenda_item_id` | uuid (nullable) | FK to agenda_items table |
| `text` | text | Note content |
| `type` | text | `'note'` \| `'decision'` \| `'action'` |
| `assignee` | text | `'rbk'` \| `'emily'` \| null |
| `completed` | boolean | Default false — task completion state |
| `meeting_date` | date | Auto-set to current date on insert |
| `created_at` | timestamp | Auto-set on insert |

### `agenda_items`
| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `id` | uuid | Primary key |
| `item_type` | text | `'email'` \| `'topic'` \| `'manual'` |
| `email_id` | uuid (nullable) | FK to emails table |
| `topic_id` | uuid (nullable) | FK to recurring_topics table |
| `title` | text (nullable) | Display title for manual items |
| `tags` | text[] | Topic tag names applied to this item (default `'{}'`) |
| `sort_order` | integer | Display order |
| `is_discussed` | boolean | Whether item has been discussed |

### `recurring_topics`
| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `id` | uuid | Primary key |
| `name` | text | Topic name |
| `description` | text | Topic description |
| `sort_order` | integer | Display order |

### `important_docs`
Stores important document links (id, title, url).

### `projects`
| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `id` | uuid | Primary key (gen_random_uuid) |
| `title` | text | Project name (required) |
| `description` | text | Project description |
| `department` | text | Department name (required) |
| `priority` | text | `'high'` \| `'medium'` \| `'low'` (default 'medium') |
| `status` | text | `'active'` \| `'on_hold'` \| `'complete'` \| `'archived'` (default 'active') |
| `progress` | integer | 0-100 (default 0) |
| `assignee` | text | `'rbk'` \| `'emily'` (default 'rbk') |
| `due_date` | date | Optional due date |
| `links` | jsonb | Array of `{title, url}` objects (default `'[]'`) |
| `tags` | text[] | Secondary department names (default `'{}'`) |
| `assignee_email` | text | User email for granular assignment (nullable) |
| `team_emails` | text[] | Additional team member emails (default `'{}'`) |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | Auto-set on insert |

### `gift_notes`
| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `id` | uuid | Primary key (gen_random_uuid) |
| `gift_id` | text | Veracross gift ID (NOT NULL) |
| `workspace_id` | uuid | FK to workspaces (NOT NULL) |
| `note` | text | Note text |
| `author_email` | text | Email of the note author (real user, not impersonated) |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | Last update timestamp |

**Unique constraint:** `(gift_id, workspace_id)` — used by upsert `onConflict`.

**SQL:** `CREATE TABLE gift_notes (id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), gift_id text NOT NULL, workspace_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(id), note text, author_email text, updated_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(), UNIQUE(gift_id, workspace_id));`

### `cooper_fund_categories`
Stores Cooper Fund disbursement snapshots. One row per fiscal year snapshot. Data seeded from spreadsheet.
- Columns: `id` (uuid PK), `workspace_id` (uuid FK), `fiscal_year` (text), `as_of_date` (date), `donated_ytd` (numeric), `current_balance` (numeric), `categories` (jsonb — array of `{name, amount}`), `updated_at` (timestamptz), `updated_by` (text)
- API reads most recent row per workspace. RLS enabled.

### `israel_fund_grants` *(new 2026-06-03)*
First-class Supabase table backing Emily Gray's Israel Grants tracker. Replaces the Google Sheets `'Master EG'!A:Q` pull that the route used before. CRUD API at `app/api/development/israel-fund/grants/route.ts` (GET, POST) and `…/grants/[id]/route.ts` (PATCH, DELETE); the main `/api/development/israel-fund` route aggregates per-initiative disbursed totals from this table directly.
- Columns:
  - `id` (uuid PK, default `gen_random_uuid()`)
  - `workspace_id` (uuid FK → workspaces.id)
  - `grant_number` (text — Emily's external tracking number; null when she hasn't assigned one)
  - `confirmed_payment` (text)
  - `date_received` (date — when SAR received the donor funds)
  - `initiative` (text — the category that joins to Veracross event names)
  - `category`, `organization_person`, `link`, `what_funding`, `wire_status`, `submitted_by`, `contact_info` (text — free-form)
  - `funding_amount` (numeric, default 0 — disbursed dollar amount)
  - `grant_not_given` (boolean, default false — flagged when something blocked the disbursement; excluded from totals when true)
  - `notes`, `submitted_to_procurify`, `date_wire_sent` (text/date — workflow tracking)
  - `procurify_number` (text — Procurify number for the disbursement; surfaced in the grant form + expanded row as the "Procurify #" column as of 2026-06-08)
  - `wire_was_sent` (boolean, default false — only `true` rows contribute to the per-initiative disbursed total)
  - `is_visible` (boolean, default true — soft-hide rows from the page without deleting)
  - **UI surface (2026-06-08)**: the grant add/edit form and the expanded sub-table no longer show `confirmed_payment`, `wire_status`, `date_wire_sent`, or `grant_not_given`. Columns remain in the DB so existing values are preserved; aggregation logic in `deriveInitiatives` still excludes rows where `grant_not_given === true` or `wire_was_sent === false`; the sort logic in `IsraelFundTab.tsx` still reads `date_wire_sent` for ordering. New grants can't set these fields via the UI.
  - `created_at` / `updated_at` (timestamptz, default `now()` — `updated_at` overwritten on every PATCH)
- Indexes: `(workspace_id)` and `(initiative)`.
- RLS enabled with `workspace_isolation` policy (`workspace_id = (SELECT workspace_id FROM workspace_members WHERE email = auth.jwt()->>'email' LIMIT 1)`); routes go through `supabaseAdmin` (service role bypasses).
- Aggregation rule used by the main route: `SUM(funding_amount) GROUP BY initiative WHERE workspace_id = ? AND is_visible = true AND wire_was_sent = true AND (grant_not_given = false OR grant_not_given IS NULL)`.
- Seed script: `npm run seed:israel-grants` runs `scripts/seed-israel-fund-grants.ts`. Reads `data/master-eg.csv` (CSV export of Emily's "Master EG" tab) and bulk-inserts in batches of 100 after deleting any existing SAR-workspace rows (safe to re-run; the table has no natural unique key for upsert).
- The CSV column → table column mapping is documented at the top of the seed script. Header row is detected by `col0 === '' AND col1 === 'Confirmed payment'` (case-insensitive); blank rows where initiative + organization_person + funding_amount are all empty/zero are skipped.

### `israel_fund_raised_cache` *(new 2026-06-08)*
Authoritative source for Israel Fund money-in. Seeded from Veracross query 1077990 (June 8 2026 export, 43 rows / $1,841,649.79 total). Replaces the live `gifts_cache` ILIKE pipeline so historical totals survive Veracross's narrow `/v3/development/gifts` API window.
- Columns:
  - `id` (uuid PK, default `gen_random_uuid()`)
  - `workspace_id` (uuid FK → workspaces.id, NOT NULL)
  - `event_name` (text NOT NULL — canonical normalized name from `normalizeIsraelFundEvent`)
  - `raised` (numeric, default 0 — displayed total; refreshed to `MAX(live, seed_raised)` by `syncIsraelFundRaisedCache`)
  - `seed_raised` (numeric, default 0 — frozen baseline from the initial seed; never decreased)
  - `gift_count` (integer, default 0)
  - `is_excluded` (boolean NOT NULL, default false — added 2026-06-08; when true the row is hidden from the public Israel Fund route. The sync only writes `false` on insert and **never** updates this column on existing rows, so editor toggles are durable.)
  - `manual_raised` (numeric NOT NULL, default 0 — **added 2026-06-10**; editor-entered top-up for money that never flows through Veracross, e.g. a Venmo fundraiser. The Israel Fund route displays **effective raised = `raised` + `manual_raised`** everywhere — per-initiative raised, balance, the Total Raised card, and the chart. Owned by the UI, never touched by the sync.)
  - `manual_raised_note` (text, nullable — **added 2026-06-10**; free-text context for the top-up, e.g. "Venmo fundraiser, not in Veracross". Surfaced as an info-icon tooltip next to the raised amount for **all** viewers so it's clear part of the total is manual.)
  - `last_updated` (timestamptz, default `now()`)
- Constraints: `UNIQUE (workspace_id, event_name)`.
- Index: `(workspace_id, event_name)`.
- RLS enabled with `workspace_isolation` policy (`workspace_id = (SELECT workspace_id FROM workspace_members WHERE email = auth.jwt()->>'email' LIMIT 1)`).
- Refreshed by `syncIsraelFundRaisedCache(workspaceId)` from `lib/syncIsraelFundRaised.ts`, called at the end of `syncGiftsForWorkspace` so every hourly/daily gifts sync rolls forward the Israel Fund cache too. Sync rule: only update existing rows when live total > current `raised` (seeded rows, `seed_raised > 0`, are never touched); new events insert with `seed_raised = 0`. **Gift-type filter (added 2026-06-11):** the `gifts_cache` aggregation counts **`gift_type = 1` only** (hard credits / real money received) — soft credits (3), pledges (2), and soft-credit pledges (5) are excluded, since Veracross writes a hard + soft row per gift and summing both double-counts. One-time `scripts/recompute-israel-fund-raised.ts` reset the historical inflation in `raised` for non-seeded rows.
- Read by `app/api/development/israel-fund/route.ts` (`SELECT event_name, raised, manual_raised, manual_raised_note, gift_count WHERE is_excluded = false ORDER BY raised DESC`; folds `manual_raised` into the effective raised). Editor-only `GET /api/development/israel-fund/raised-cache` returns ALL rows including hidden (and now `manual_raised` + `manual_raised_note`), for the "Show hidden" toggle in `IsraelFundTab.tsx`.
- Editor writes via `PATCH /api/development/israel-fund/raised-cache/[id]` — accepts any combination of `{ is_excluded?: boolean, manual_raised?: number, manual_raised_note?: string|null }`. `manual_raised` must be a non-negative number; an empty/whitespace note normalizes to null. Gated to the same editor set as the eye toggle (`israel_fund_editor` sub-permission, Emily, Becca, or workspace owner).
- **Pre-excluded events (SAR workspace, set 2026-06-08)**: `Cong. Sons of Israel - Purim 25`, `Cong. Sons of Israel - Purim 26`, `Faculty Mishloach Manot - Purim 25`, `Faculty Mishloach Manot - Purim 26`, `KCI Food Pantry`. These come through the gifts_cache sync (APL:/DEV: prefixed) but don't belong on the Israel Fund page. Hidden via the per-row eye toggle; `is_excluded` stays sticky across syncs.

Stale snapshot table — **no longer the source of truth for the Israel Fund page**. Superseded twice: (1) 2026-06-03 swap to live `gifts_cache` + Google Sheets, then (2) 2026-06-08 swap to `israel_fund_raised_cache`. The columns below are preserved only for historical reference; the table is not written to or read by any deployed route. Safe to drop in a future cleanup.

### `workspace_integrations`
Phase F per-workspace integration credentials. **Service-role only** — RLS denies every non-service-role read, so credentials never leave the server.
