|--------|------|-------------|
| `id` | uuid | Primary key |
| `workspace_id` | uuid | FK to workspaces |
| `type` | text | One of `'veracross'` \| `'gmail'` \| `'google_calendar'` \| `'slack'` \| `'lever'` \| `'anthropic'` \| `'rise_vision'` |
| `credentials` | jsonb | Per-type shape. **Stored unencrypted** (relies on Supabase at-rest encryption). Veracross holds `{clientId, clientSecret, admissionsClientId, admissionsClientSecret, schoolCode}` because Veracross uses two separate OAuth client pairs (general + admissions). |
| `is_active` | boolean | False = "Disconnected" pill in the Admin Integrations tab |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | |

**Unique constraint:** `(workspace_id, type)`. Reads go through `lib/getIntegration.ts` (5-min cache + env fallback per-field via `||`).

### Firebase Cloud Functions (`functions/src/index.ts`)
- **`manualTriage`** — HTTP Cloud Function (`https://us-central1-rbk-cmd-center.cloudfunctions.net/manualTriage`). CORS-enabled POST endpoint that publishes to the `firebase-schedule-triageGmail-us-central1` Pub/Sub topic, triggering `triageGmail` on demand. Called by `gmail/sync/route.ts` (Sync Gmail button).
- **`manualSyncDrafts`** — HTTP Cloud Function (`https://us-central1-rbk-cmd-center.cloudfunctions.net/manualSyncDrafts`). CORS-enabled POST endpoint that publishes to the `firebase-schedule-syncDraftsReady-us-central1` Pub/Sub topic, triggering `syncDraftsReady` on demand. Called by `gmail/sync-drafts/route.ts`.
- **`triageGmail`** — Pub/Sub scheduled function, runs every 15 min weekdays (ET). **Multi-workspace:** Queries all workspaces where `gmail_refresh_token IS NOT NULL` and loops over each one. Per workspace: exchanges refresh token for access token, fetches unprocessed inbox emails via Gmail API (`labelIds=INBOX` filter on list query), skips any message with the `DRAFT` label in its `labelIds` array (prevents Emily's draft replies from being triaged as inbound mail), runs OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini` triage (priority, summary, action_needed, draft_reply), writes to Supabase `emails` table scoped to that workspace's id, applies Gmail labels. Per-workspace error handling — if one workspace fails (e.g. expired token), logs the error and continues to the next. Env vars: `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, `OPENAI_API_KEY` (in `functions/.env`). `WORKSPACE_ID` env var no longer needed. Log lines prefixed with `[owner_email]` for per-workspace traceability. Runs in parallel with GAS during transition.
- **`syncDraftsReady`** — Pub/Sub scheduled function, runs every 15 min weekdays (ET). **Multi-workspace:** Same workspace discovery pattern as `triageGmail` — queries all workspaces with non-null `gmail_refresh_token` and loops. Per workspace: resolves "Drafts Ready" label ID → lists messages with that label → deduplicates by threadId → lists all Gmail drafts and maps threadId→draftId → for each thread, fetches the full draft via `drafts.get`, extracts body text (reuses `parseEmailBody`/`collectParts` helpers) and To header → looks up matching Supabase email by `thread_id + workspace_id` → PATCHes `draft_reply` and `draft_status: 'draft_ready'` → swaps Gmail labels. Per-workspace error handling. `WORKSPACE_ID` env var no longer needed. Log lines prefixed with `[owner_email]`.
- **`syncGiftsHourlyWeekdays`** — Pub/Sub scheduled function, runs hourly Mon-Fri ET (`0 * * * 1-5`). Queries workspaces with `development` module enabled, calls `/api/development/sync-gifts-internal` for each with `X-Internal-Secret` auth.
- **`syncGiftsDailyWeekends`** — Pub/Sub scheduled function, runs at 6 AM Sat-Sun ET (`0 6 * * 0,6`). Same logic as weekday function.
- **`syncDailyAttendance`** — Pub/Sub scheduled function, runs weekdays at 11 PM ET. Calls `/api/absences/sync?mode=daily` with `SYNC_SECRET` auth header.
- **`dailyAbsenceAlert`** *(new 2026-06-09)* — Pub/Sub scheduled function, weekdays at 9:30 AM ET (`30 9 * * 1-5`, `America/New_York`). POSTs to `/api/absences/threshold-alert-internal` with `X-Internal-Secret` header. The route counts YTD absences (from `2025-09-03`) for every student absent today, finds those whose count equals exactly 5 or exactly 10 (the crossing day), and DMs RBK (`U04NBR22Y`) with a one-line summary per student. Silent when nothing crossed.

### Google Apps Script (Email Pipeline)
- **`google-apps-script/email-triage.gs`** — Runs every 15 min in RBK's Gmail, AI-categorizes emails via OpenAI, sends to Firebase webhook
  - **`syncDraftsReadyLabel()`** — Runs every 15 min (separate trigger). Finds emails with "Drafts Ready" Gmail label (applied by Emily), sends to webhook with `draft_status: 'draft_ready'`. On duplicate emails, the webhook updates `draft_status` on the existing record. After syncing, swaps label to "Drafts Synced" to avoid reprocessing.
  - **`createDraftsReadyTrigger()`** — Sets up the 15-min timer for `syncDraftsReadyLabel()`
- **`google-apps-script/recovery-function.gs`** — One-time recovery: re-sends labeled-but-missed emails from last 3 days
- Webhook URL: `https://rbk-cmd-center.web.app/api/webhook/email` (updated from defunct Vercel URL)
- The webhook accepts an optional `draft_status` field; on duplicate emails (23505), it updates `draft_status` instead of silently ignoring

## Key Technical Patterns

### Division System

- `DIVISION_ACADEMY = 'academy'` covers ELC + Lower School + Middle School (one building).
- `DIVISION_HS = 'hs'` covers SAR High School (separate building, same institution).
- `getGradeFilterForMember(divisions)` (`lib/divisions.ts`) returns the correct Veracross `grade_level` number filter per user — `[40,35,30,25,20,1-8]` for Academy, `[9,10,11,12]` for HS, union for multi-division users.
- `getEffectiveDivisions(session)` (`lib/impersonate.ts`) is **impersonation-aware** — when the admin is impersonating, it returns the impersonated user's divisions, NOT the real admin's. Server routes that filter by division MUST go through this helper (not `session.currentMember.divisions` directly) or data leaks via impersonation.
- **Multi-division users** (Becca, Debra May with `divisions = ['academy','hs']`) see an Academy / HS / Institutional segmented control on Admissions and Recruiting pages — gated by `hasMultipleDivisions = (currentMember?.divisions?.length ?? 0) > 1`. Single-division users see no UI difference.
- Empty `divisions` array defaults to **Academy-only** — the safe fallback for any unconfigured account.
- **HS grading period is 24** in the live Veracross data (NOT 29 — 29 is the prior school year reserved by Veracross's year-rotation pattern). The constant is `HS_GRADING_PERIODS = [24, 29]` (array form covers school-year rollovers).

### Assistant relationship

- `workspace_members.assistant_to` is set on the **assistant's row** pointing at the **principal's** member id. Emily's row: `assistant_to = RBK's member id` ⇒ "Emily assists RBK".
- The session pipeline builds both directions on every login:
  - `assistant` = "who assists me" — reverse lookup `WHERE assistant_to = my_id`.
  - `principal` = "who I assist" — forward lookup `WHERE id = my_assistant_to`.
- **Tasks page second column** = `assistant ?? principal`. Principals see [me | assistant], assistants see [me | principal], standalone users (no FK either direction) see only their own column.
- The Admin → Users tab has an Assistant picker pill — selecting a new assistant does a two-step PATCH (clears any current assistant's `assistant_to`, then sets the new one). No DB uniqueness constraint on `assistant_to`, so the session lookup uses `.maybeSingle()` (throws if multiple rows point at the same principal — keep it that way unless you intend to allow multi-assistants).

### Veracross quirks

- **HS grading period: 24** (NOT 29 — that's the prior year). `HS_GRADING_PERIODS = [24, 29]` in `lib/divisions.ts`.
- **HS grade numbers:** `[9, 10, 11, 12]` for current grade; re-enrolling HS students live in `RE_ENROLLMENT_GRADE_LEVELS` 9-11 (12th-graders graduate, so they're omitted from re-enrollment).
- **`NEXT_GRADE_MAP`** (`app/api/admissions/route.ts`) maps current → next grade for re-enrollment. Phase-D fix added `9→10, 10→11, 11→12` — without these, HS re-enrollees silently fell out of the projection. Academy half: `40→35, 35→30, 30→25, 25→20, 20→1, 1→2, ..., 7→8`. Current-8th → 9th transition goes through the **new applications** pipeline (`grade_applying_for: 9`), not re-enrollment — matches SAR's data model.
- **`/v3/students`** does NOT accept a `grading_period` query param — Veracross returns the full roster and we filter client-side. Always log the resolved grade list in diagnostic output if you're debugging a missing-students bug.
- **Veracross uses two separate OAuth client credential pairs** — general (`clientId`/`clientSecret`) + admissions (`admissionsClientId`/`admissionsClientSecret`). Both live in `workspace_integrations.credentials` for the `veracross` type.

## TypeScript Interfaces (in Dashboard.tsx)

- **`Email`** — Full email record (id, from_name, from_email, subject, summary, body_text, action_needed, draft_reply, edited_draft, draft_status, draft_edited_by, draft_edited_at, priority, status, action_status, assigned_to, received_at, is_unread, flagged_for_meeting, flagged_by, meeting_notes, message_id, attachments, reminder_date, revision_comment, tbd_suggestion, tbd_notes)
- **`AgendaNote`** — {id, email_id?, agenda_item_id?, text, type, assignee, created_at, completed?}
- **`AgendaItem`** — {id, sort_order, item_type ('email'|'topic'|'manual'), is_discussed, email_id?, topic_id?, title?, tags?: string[], email?, topic?}
- **`RecurringTopic`** — {id, name, description, sort_order}
- **`CalendarEvent`** — {id, title, startTime, endTime, isAllDay, location, meetingLink?, calendarLink?}
- **`Project`** — {id, title, description?, department, priority ('high'|'medium'|'low'), status ('active'|'on_hold'|'complete'|'archived'), progress (0-100), assignee ('rbk'|'emily'), due_date?, links: Array<{title, url}>, tags?: string[], assignee_email?: string | null, team_emails?: string[], created_at}
- **`GemaraItem`** — {id, type ('link'|'note'), title, url?, body?, created_at} (defined inline in component)
- **`AbsenceRecord`** — {person_id, name, status_code, status_label, excused, attendance_category, notes, late_arrival_time, early_dismissal_time, grade_level (display label string), grade_level_id (numeric ID), ytd_absences (number), consecutive_absences (number)}
- **`AbsencesData`** — {date, total, absences: AbsenceRecord[], tardies: AbsenceRecord[], earlyDismissals: AbsenceRecord[]}

## Deployment

```bash
# Full deploy (build + hosting + functions + IAM fix)
./deploy.sh
```

**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `npx firebase deploy --only hosting` directly — it fails with `Error: Failed to replace Run service` due to a Firebase CLI bug (409 revision name collision between function update and hosting finalization). The `deploy.sh` script works around this by:
1. Running `firebase deploy --only hosting` (ignoring the expected replace error)
2. Manually finalizing the hosting version via Firebase Hosting REST API
3. Creating a new release pointing to the finalized version
4. Restoring `allUsers` IAM on the Cloud Run service (GCP org policy strips it on each deploy)
5. Compiling and deploying 1st-gen Cloud Functions

- **Firebase:** https://rbk-cmd-center.web.app
- **Vercel:** Deprecated (deployment dead as of June 2025)

## Recent Changes

### September restart — all 9 schedulers re-enabled, Cloud Run back to school-year settings (2026-09-02)

Firebase hosting release 2026-09-02 (19:00), **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00729-zld`**. Worked the (now-deleted) SEPTEMBER RESTART CHECKLIST end to end, ending the 2026-07-25 summer shutdown. The checklist section has been removed from this file because it is done; this entry is the record of what was restored.

**Schedulers — all 9 re-enabled in `functions/src/index.ts` and redeployed** (`npx firebase deploy --only functions --force`), each at its original cron, all `America/New_York`, all verified `ENABLED` via `gcloud scheduler jobs list`:

| Function | Schedule |
|---|---|
| `scheduledMorningBriefings` | `30 7 * * 1-5` |
| `syncDailyAttendance` | `0 23 * * 1-5` |
| `syncAfterSchoolPrograms` | `0 7 * * *` |
| `syncGiftsHourlyWeekdays` | `0 * * * 1-5` |
| `syncGiftsDailyWeekends` | `0 6 * * 0,6` |
| `dailyTaskDueReminder` | `0 8 * * *` |
| `dailyAbsenceAlert` | `30 9 * * 1-5` |
| `triageGmail` | `*/15 * * * 1-5` |
| `syncDraftsReady` | `*/15 * * * 1-5` |

12 exports total (the 9 above + the 3 HTTP functions). **There is no separate `syncConstituents` scheduler** — the constituents sync is chained inside `runGiftsSyncForAllWorkspaces()`, which the two gifts schedulers drive, so restoring those two restores constituent syncing too.

**⚠️ The re-enable was NOT "purely uncommenting", contrary to the note the shutdown left behind.** `triageGmailHandler` and `syncDraftsReadyHandler` are `const` arrow functions declared *below* their export blocks (that form was chosen during the shutdown to keep the handler bodies byte-identical). Uncommenting `.onRun(triageGmailHandler)` verbatim dereferences the const during module evaluation and throws `ReferenceError: Cannot access 'triageGmailHandler' before initialization` — a temporal-dead-zone error that would have failed the deploy's module analysis. Both are now wrapped so the lookup is deferred to invocation time:
```ts
.onRun(async () => { await triageGmailHandler(); return null; });
```
which is also the shape the gifts schedulers already used. The other seven handlers are `async function` declarations and hoist fine, so those took a plain reference. Pre-flight `require()` of the compiled `lib/index.js` (with `GCLOUD_PROJECT`/`FIREBASE_CONFIG` stubbed) is a cheap way to catch this class of error before deploying — it loaded all 12 exports clean.

**⚠️ Fixed a real bug the restart exposed: the gifts schedulers were timing out at 60s.** These are 1st-gen functions and had no `runWith`, so they inherited the **default 60s timeout**. On live SAR data the gifts leg alone takes ~58s, so: the hourly job frequently died before the gifts sync even returned (the 23:00 run did exactly that), and the chained constituents sync **never completed** — `constituents_cache` had not refreshed since **2026-07-25**, silently staling the `role`/`roles_raw` values behind the Development Overview segment classification. Both gifts schedulers now carry `functions.runWith({ timeoutSeconds: 540 })` (the 1st-gen max, comfortably over the Cloud Run 300s request ceiling). Verified by re-running the job: execution went from `60000 ms … status: 'timeout'` to **`94437 ms … status: 'ok'`**, with the constituents leg completing (9,698 constituents / 1,711 students). This was pre-existing fragility, not something the shutdown introduced — but it meant the automation was only nominally restored without it.

**⚠️ Second timeout bug, found by watching the first unattended run — Cloud Run's REQUEST timeout was 60s.** After the function timeout was fixed, the 2026-09-03 00:00 unattended run still failed its constituents leg at *exactly* 60.0s with `FetchError: invalid json response body … reason: Unexpected token 'u', "upstream r"… is not valid JSON` — that is a plain-text `upstream request timeout` from the Cloud Run gateway, which the caller then tried to `JSON.parse`. The constituents sync takes **~47-60s** on live SAR data, so at a 60s request ceiling it succeeded or failed at random (observed: 47s ok, 54s ok, >60s failed). This is why `constituents_cache` looked healthy in spot checks but would drift.

Root cause of it being 60s at all: **`firebase deploy --only hosting` recreates/updates the SSR Cloud Run service with the frameworks default 60s timeout**, silently reverting the manual `gcloud run services update --timeout=300` a previous session applied (which is why this file's 2026-04-28 entry claiming a 300s timeout no longer matched reality). Fix: **`--timeout=300` is now pinned in `deploy.sh`'s `gcloud run services update` block**, which runs *after* the hosting step, so it is reapplied on every deploy and cannot silently revert again. Applied live (revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00730-g6f`) and verified: the next full run took **143502 ms status 'ok'** with the constituents leg running **86s** — comfortably past the old ceiling — and both legs reporting success (`gifts 10,846` / `constituents 9,698, students 1,711`).

**Lesson for future work here: a Cloud Run request timeout that is not in `deploy.sh` will not survive.** Anything set by hand via `gcloud run services update` is undone the next time the hosting step runs.

**Cloud Run restored to school-year settings** — `deploy.sh` `gcloud run services update` now passes `--min-instances=1 --max-instances=10 --no-cpu-throttling --memory=512Mi`, and the summer's explicit `--cpu-throttling` flag is deleted (leaving it would silently defeat `--no-cpu-throttling`). `firebase.json` `frameworksBackend.memory` `256MiB` → **`512MiB`**. Verified live on the serving revision: `cpu-throttling: false`, `minScale 1`, `maxScale 10`, `512Mi`. The 512MiB/512Mi pairing also removes the ordering hazard the shutdown documented — the hosting step earlier in `deploy.sh` updates the same service from `firebase.json`, and at 256MiB it would now be rejected against always-allocated CPU. **`--no-cpu-throttling` is what Buzz depends on**: its Slack handler acks within 3s then calls Anthropic in the background, and under throttling that call is frozen post-ack and Buzz falls through to its "Something's off on my end" fallback.

**Verification performed:**
- **Gifts sync (checklist step 4):** triggered the real scheduler job (`gcloud scheduler jobs run`), exercising the full production chain scheduler → Pub/Sub → function → sync endpoint. `gifts_sync_meta` = success / 10,846. **FY27 `Operating 2026-2027` type-1/2 went 62 gifts / $191,397.70 → 63 gifts / $191,433.70** — a new gift landed, confirming the pipeline is live and the FY27 Overview will stay current.
- **`constituents_cache`:** `2026-07-25` → **2026-09-02 23:08**, 9,691 → 9,712 rows.
- **SFTP VM (step 7):** `veracross-sftp` (e2-micro, `us-east1-c`) already **RUNNING** — no action needed.
- **Manual Gmail buttons (step 8):** the summer breakage is fixed — deleting the two schedulers had destroyed the Pub/Sub topics `firebase-schedule-triageGmail-us-central1` / `firebase-schedule-syncDraftsReady-us-central1`; both topics are recreated, and `manualTriage` / `manualSyncDrafts` now both return `{"success":true}` HTTP 200 (they returned errors all summer).
- Site healthy: `/` 307, `/login` 200, `/api/health` 200, `/api/development/overview` 401 (auth-gated, alive). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for both the app and `functions/`.

**🔴 Known blocker: Gmail refresh token — needs a human, could not be fixed from here.** `triageGmail` now fires on schedule and completes `ok`, and the Pub/Sub plumbing is confirmed working — but the Gmail API call itself fails:
```
[kraussb@saracademy.org] Gmail list error: code: 403,
  message: 'Delegation denied for rglassberg@saracademy.org', reason: 'forbidden',
  status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED'
```
Exactly one workspace ("SAR Academy — RBK", owner `kraussb@`) has a `gmail_refresh_token`, and the stored token resolves to `rglassberg@` (Becca), which Google is refusing to delegate to RBK's mailbox. **`syncDraftsReady` uses the same workspace token and will fail the same way.** Fix: the workspace owner (`kraussb@saracademy.org`) signs in and re-runs the OAuth consent flow at **`/api/auth/gmail-consent`**, which writes a fresh `workspaces.gmail_refresh_token`. (Per `auth/gmail-callback`, the workspace-level token is only overwritten when the connecting user is a workspace **owner** — so Becca re-consenting will NOT fix it; it has to be RBK.) This is a credential/consent problem, not a scheduler problem — everything around it is restored and working. **Scope note:** the newest row in `emails` is 2026-08-06, so inbound triage has been dead since well before this restart; I could not determine from the available logs whether the delegation error predates the shutdown, so I am not claiming it is new.

Its hard dependency (`--no-cpu-throttling`) is restored and verified, and the `30 7 * * 1-5` scheduler will exercise it at 7:30am on the next school day.

The robust workaround is to rename first, since `mv` is atomic and Finder is not watching the renamed path: `mv .next .next.trash.$$ && rm -rf .next.trash.$$`. ### FY27 rollover — Development Overview moved to `Operating 2026-2027` (2026-09-02)

Firebase hosting release 2026-09-02, **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00726-4wz`**. The fiscal year turned over on September 1st, so the Development Overview now reports **FY27 (`Operating 2026-2027`)** as the current campaign, with FY26 (`Operating 2025-2026`) as prior year and FY25 (`Operating 2024-2025`) as two-years-ago context. Three files changed: `app/api/development/overview/route.ts`, `app/api/development/overview/segment-donors/route.ts`, `app/components/development/OverviewTab.tsx`.

- **Campaign constants.** `FY27_CAMPAIGN = 'Operating 2026-2027'` is the only hardcoded string; `FY26_CAMPAIGN = priorCampaign(FY27_CAMPAIGN)` and `FY25_CAMPAIGN = priorCampaign(FY26_CAMPAIGN)` are derived by the existing regex helper (which was verified to still work — it parses `^(.*?)(\d{4})-(\d{4})$` and decrements both years). **Next September's roll is a one-line change: bump `FY27_CAMPAIGN`.** Every internal identifier was shifted with it (`raisedFY26`→`raisedFY27`, `fy25Baseline`→`fy26Baseline`, …) so the code reads in the current year's terms rather than accumulating off-by-one names.
- **Gifts query now pulls three campaigns** (`.in([FY25, FY26, FY27])`, still exact-match, never a `LIKE 'Operating %'` prefix). FY25 rows feed **only** the per-fund fallback bucket — no donor set, headline, or segment total — so the FY27-leak hardening from 2026-06-25 is preserved in spirit: an `Operating 2027-2028` pledge booked early still can't be fetched, let alone counted.
- **Segment cards report FY27, and each carries `priorYearTotal` (FY26).** Rather than a cheap `SUM(amount)`, the FY26 number is produced by running the **identical** donor→segment pipeline over the FY26 campaign: the same `DonorAgg` accumulator, the same twin-matching soft-credit gap-fill, the same `board_member_overrides`, and the same "Other" DAF/foundation dedup (a per-year `softCreditedOrgs` set is now built for FY26 as well as FY27). The aggregation was extracted into a `buildSegmentTotals(byDonor, excludedOtherOrgs)` helper so the two years cannot drift apart methodologically — any gap between the two numbers is a real YoY difference, not an artifact. UI renders it as a muted `FY26 · $X` line under Received/Pledged on each card.
- **Campaign Giving by Fund is now three columns + change.** Per fund: `raisedFY27`, `raisedFY26` (both gifts_cache, type-1 `amount` + type-2 `pledge_balance`, so they reconcile with the headline), `raisedFY25` (giving_history_cache face-value `amount`, gifts_cache fallback), plus server-computed `changeFY27vFY26` and `changePctFY27vFY26` (null when FY26 is 0). The UI header reads **FUND | FY27 | FY26 | FY25 | CHANGE (FY27 vs FY26)**, subtitle `FY27 · FY26 · FY25`; FY25 is rendered `font-light text-slate-400` so it reads as context, and the table `min-w` went 600→760px for the extra column. **The giving_history_cache FY25 query moved from `Operating 2024-2025`-as-FY25 to the new `FY25_CAMPAIGN` constant — same literal string, so that column's data source and value are unchanged.**
- **Lapsed / New / Retained rebased to FY27 vs FY26**, with one deliberate deviation from the spec. The spec said to take the prior-year baseline from `giving_history_cache` FY26 and fall back to gifts_cache only when the history count is 0. **The two sources are UNIONed instead**, because for FY26 each is incomplete in a different direction: `giving_history_cache` only reflects the last nightly CSV drop (it lagged live giving), while `gifts_cache` covers FY26 completely (the campaign sits well inside the Veracross `/v3/development/gifts` API window) but has no deep history. Preferring history alone would *shrink* the baseline and wrongly promote real FY26 donors into "New Donors". A union can only grow the baseline, so it is strictly safer, and it degrades to exactly the gifts_cache set when history has no FY26 rows — i.e. the spec's stated fallback is the union's own limiting case. Measured on 2026-09-02: gifts_cache FY26 = 2,046 donors, giving_history FY26 = 2,044, union = **2,048**. The log line now prints all three (`gifts_cache=… +history_only=… union=…`).
- **`segment-donors` follows to FY27** (`FY27_CAMPAIGN = 'Operating 2026-2027'`) so the drilldown reconciles with the card it was opened from. **No prior-year references existed in that route to update, and `secondaryRole` needed no change** — it strips `Trustee` from `roles_raw` and re-runs `classifySegment`, which is year-independent by construction.
- **Untouched, as specified and verified:** the soft-credit twin-matching gap-fill (dedup by `hard_credit_gift_id`, non-twin gap-fill) — now applied to FY27 exactly as it was to FY26; `board_member_overrides`; the DAF/foundation exclusion from "Other" (`softCreditedOrgs`); Israel Fund, Cooper Fund, Guardian Circle and Weekly Gifts routes; and the giving_history_cache FY25 fund data, which the third column still depends on.

**SQL verification (2026-09-02, SAR workspace):**
1. `SUM(amount) WHERE fundraising_activity='Operating 2026-2027' AND gift_type IN (1,2)` = **$191,397.70** across 62 gifts ✓ (matches the expected ~$191,397). Note the *headline* card will read **$189,857.70** — the headline uses the Veracross "Donation & Pledge Balance" formula (type-1 `amount` + type-2 **`pledge_balance`**), and one FY27 pledge has $1,540 already paid down. That is correct, long-standing behavior, not a rollover regression.
2. `COUNT(DISTINCT constituent_id) WHERE fundraising_activity='Operating 2026-2027' AND gift_type IN (1,2,3)` = **63** FY27 donors.
3. Derived: **43 retained, 20 new, 2,005 lapsed.** The very large lapsed number is expected and correct two days into a fiscal year — it means "gave FY26, hasn't given yet in FY27" and drains toward the card's stated "Goal: 0 by Aug 31" as the year fills in. 7 funds total across all three years; **no FY25-only funds**, so widening the fund union to three years added no long tail.
4. Simulated segment totals: FY27 = $197,977 over 63 donors; FY26 (the new `priorYearTotal`) = $10,389,339 over 2,046 donors. Segment totals exceed the headline because they include non-twin soft credits — pre-existing, documented behavior.

`npx tsc --noEmit` (exit 0) + `npm run build` (exit 0, both overview routes compiled) clean; deployed (hosting released, Cloud Run `00726-4wz` serving 100%, site 307, `/api/development/overview` and `/api/development/overview/segment-donors` both 401 = auth-gated and alive). The authenticated render can only be exercised from a real browser session.

**⚠️ Deploy gotcha hit twice this session (new, worth knowing):** `npm run build` fails with `Error: ENOTEMPTY: directory not empty, rmdir '…/.next/server'` (or `.next/build`) when macOS Finder has written a `.DS_Store` into a `.next` subdirectory since the previous build — Next.js clears `.next` at build start and `rmdir` trips over the file. It is not a code error and no amount of retrying `./deploy.sh` alone fixes it. **Fix: `rm -rf .next && ./deploy.sh` as a single command.** The first failed attempt also left a broken Cloud Run revision (`00724-7cz`, status False) and produced the known stale-image error (`Image …:version_1 not found`) on the forced revision update; both self-healed on the clean rerun, `00723-8v6` kept serving throughout and **there was no downtime**.

**~~Still outstanding after this deploy — the SEPTEMBER RESTART CHECKLIST has NOT been run.~~ RESOLVED the same day** — see the September 2 restart entry above. The schedulers (including `syncGiftsHourlyWeekdays` / `syncGiftsDailyWeekends`) are back, so `gifts_cache` refreshes hourly again and the FY27 numbers stay live.

### FIX: After School sync — session-auth fallback for the manual UI trigger (2026-08-10)

Firebase hosting release 2026-08-10, **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00723-8v6`**. The **Sync** button on the After School Programs page always returned `Unauthorized`. `app/api/after-school/sync/route.ts` only accepted shared-secret auth (`x-internal-secret`, for the `syncAfterSchoolPrograms` Cloud Function) — the browser sends a `__session` cookie, no secret, so every manual click 401'd. Since the summer shutdown deleted all 9 scheduled functions, the manual button was the *only* remaining way to refresh after-school data, so the module was effectively frozen.

- **Fix:** added the same dual-auth pattern `app/api/development/giving-history/ingest/route.ts` already uses. The route now accepts **either** `X-Internal-Secret` matching `INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET` **or** `SYNC_SECRET` (Cloud Function, unchanged), **or** an authenticated session where the caller is `is_super_admin` **or** `role === 'owner'` (manual UI trigger). Implemented as: read the secret first, and only fall through to `getAuthSession()` when the secret is absent/wrong — so the Cloud Function path never pays for a cookie read.
- **Deviation from the ingest route (deliberate):** ingest gates on `sessionIsSuperAdmin(session)` alone; this route also allows `session?.role === 'owner'` per spec, so **RBK (owner, not super-admin) can trigger the sync**, not just Becca. That is a wider gate than ingest's — intentional, since After School is an operational refresh rather than an admin-only data import.
- **No middleware change needed.** `api/after-school/sync` was already in the `middleware.ts` matcher exclusion list (added when the route shipped, for the Cloud Function). That only bypasses the middleware's session *check* — `getAuthSession()` reads the `__session` cookie directly inside the handler, so the new UI path authenticates fine. Same arrangement as `giving-history/ingest`, which is also excluded.
- **Untouched** per spec: every other after-school route (`/api/after-school`, `/api/after-school/students`), all sync/Veracross/upsert logic in this file (courses → classes → enrollments, the course allow-list, the `capacity`-omitting upsert, the delete-then-reinsert enrollment refresh), `AfterSchoolTab.tsx`, and the Cloud Function.
- tsc (exit 0) + `npm run build` (exit 0, `/api/after-school/sync` compiled) clean; deployed (`Successful update operation`, hosting released, Cloud Run `00723-8v6` serving 100%, site 307). Post-deploy verification: unauthenticated `POST` → **401**, `POST` with a wrong `X-Internal-Secret` → **401** (the fallback rejects rather than opening the route). The authenticated-owner path can only be exercised from a real browser session.
- **⚠️ Note for the September restart:** this makes the manual Sync button usable *now*, during the shutdown, but `syncAfterSchoolPrograms` is still deleted from GCP — step 2 of the SEPTEMBER RESTART CHECKLIST still has to re-enable the 7am daily scheduler.

### Summer shutdown: minimum-cost mode + all scheduled Cloud Functions disabled (2026-07-25)

Firebase hosting release 2026-07-25, **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00721-hfr`**. The app stays deployed and reachable, but runs at minimum cost until September 2026 — nobody uses it over the summer. All of it was undone on 2026-09-02 — see the September 2 restart entry above. (The SEPTEMBER RESTART CHECKLIST section this used to point to has been removed now that it is complete.)

- **Cloud Run minimum-cost settings** (`deploy.sh`): `--min-instances=1` → `--min-instances=0` (scales to zero when idle; trade-off is a ~3-8s cold start on the first request), new `--max-instances=2` (spend ceiling), `--memory=512Mi` → `--memory=256Mi`, and `--no-cpu-throttling` removed. `firebase.json` `frameworksBackend.memory` `512MiB` → `256MiB` to match. A `# SCHOOL YEAR: restore --no-cpu-throttling --memory=512Mi --max-instances=10` comment sits directly above the `gcloud run services update` block.
- **⚠️ Non-obvious deploy finding — `--cpu-throttling` had to be added explicitly.** Simply *dropping* `--no-cpu-throttling` does not revert it; CPU allocation is a **sticky service setting**. The first deploy attempt failed twice with `400 … spec.template.spec.containers.resources.limits.memory: Invalid value specified for memory. Total memory < 512 Mi is not supported with cpu always allocated (unthrottled)` — Cloud Run refuses `256Mi` while CPU is always-allocated. Fixed by adding an explicit `--cpu-throttling` flag to the `gcloud run services update` command in `deploy.sh`. **Reverse implication for September:** raising memory back to `512Mi` must happen in the same command as (or before) re-adding `--no-cpu-throttling`, and the `--cpu-throttling` flag must be deleted.
- **Second deploy hiccup (self-healed):** after the failed function update, the service spec pointed at image tag `…/ssrrbkcmdcenter:version_1`, which no longer existed (`Image … not found`), so a standalone `gcloud run services update` couldn't mint a revision even though the spec had accepted the new values. Re-running the full `./deploy.sh` rebuilt and pushed a fresh image and the revision went out cleanly. The previously-good revision `00718-kzz` kept serving throughout — **no downtime** (site returned 307 the whole time).
- **All 9 scheduled Cloud Functions disabled** in `functions/src/index.ts` and **deleted from GCP** via `npx firebase deploy --only functions --force`: `scheduledMorningBriefings`, `syncDailyAttendance`, `syncAfterSchoolPrograms`, `syncGiftsHourlyWeekdays`, `syncGiftsDailyWeekends`, `dailyTaskDueReminder`, `dailyAbsenceAlert`, `triageGmail`, `syncDraftsReady`. `gcloud scheduler jobs list` now returns **0 items**. Each is marked in-file with `=== SUMMER SHUTDOWN 2026-07-25 — SCHEDULER DISABLED ===`, its original cron + timezone, and a `TODO: RE-ENABLE FOR SCHOOL YEAR`; the `exports.… functions.pubsub.schedule(…)` chain is commented out.
- **Handler bodies were preserved, not deleted.** Each scheduled function's body now lives as a `…Handler` function/const (e.g. `syncDailyAttendanceHandler()`, `triageGmailHandler`), so re-enabling in September is purely uncommenting the export block. The two large ones (`triageGmail` ~280 lines, `syncDraftsReady` ~220 lines) use the `const xHandler = (async () => { … });` form specifically so their bodies and indentation stay **byte-identical** and the diff is reviewable. `functions/` has no ESLint config and `tsconfig.json` does not set `noUnusedLocals`, so the unreferenced handlers compile clean (`tsc` exit 0).
- **The 3 HTTP functions were kept and redeployed** — `manualTriage`, `manualSyncDrafts`, `generateMorningBriefings`. They cost nothing when idle.
- **⚠️ Known summer breakage (intentional):** deleting the `triageGmail` / `syncDraftsReady` schedulers also destroyed the Pub/Sub topics `firebase-schedule-triageGmail-us-central1` and `firebase-schedule-syncDraftsReady-us-central1` that `manualTriage` / `manualSyncDrafts` publish to — so the in-app **"Sync Gmail"** and manual draft-sync buttons will error until the schedulers are restored. Nothing syncs automatically over the summer: no Gmail triage, no gifts/constituents sync, no attendance, no after-school, no task/absence reminders, no morning briefings.
- **`ingestGivingHistory` skipped** — already retired 2026-06-25 (manual-only via the Development Overview "Sync Gift History" button); left as-is.
- **Not touched (out of scope, still billing):** the SFTP VM (`gcloud compute instances list`), Supabase, and the Veracross Data Package (already moved to weekly Sunday 3am for the summer). The SFTP VM is likely the largest remaining line item if further savings are wanted.
- Verified post-deploy: serving revision `00721-hfr` = `256Mi` / `cpu-throttling: true` / `minScale` unset (0) / `maxScale: 2`; 0 scheduler jobs; site 307.

### is_super_admin: distinguish Becca (system admin) from RBK (owner) (2026-07-21e)

Firebase release 2026-07-21e, **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00718-kzz`**. A `workspace_members.is_super_admin` boolean (default false; true only for Becca / rglassberg@) now distinguishes the system builder from a plain workspace owner (RBK / Rabbi Krauss, kraussb@, who is `role='owner'` but the primary end user, NOT the administrator).

- **Session plumbing:** `is_super_admin` added to the `workspace_members` SELECTs in `app/api/auth/session/route.ts` and written to the `__session` cookie. Surfaced server-side via `lib/auth.ts` (`AuthSession.isSuperAdmin`) and to the client via `app/api/auth/session/workspace/route.ts` → `AuthProvider` (`useWorkspace().isSuperAdmin`, reflects the REAL session, never impersonation-adjusted, like `googleTasksConnected`). `switch-workspace` preserves it via its `...session` spread.
- **Admin gating moved from ADMIN_EMAIL → super-admin.** New shared helper `sessionIsSuperAdmin(session)` in `lib/auth.ts` = `session.isSuperAdmin === true || email === rglassberg@` (**the hardcoded email is retained ONLY as a transitional fallback for Becca's own account** so a pre-deploy, is_super_admin-less cookie can't lock her out; never grants anyone else; safe to delete once sessions rotate). Applied to: **/admin/permissions** page access (client `canAccessAdmin`) + its backing APIs (`admin/workspace-members` GET/POST + `[id]` PATCH/DELETE, `admin/workspace`, `admin/integrations` + `/test` + `/migrate`, `admin/feature-flags`); **Development "Sync Gift History"** button (`OverviewTab`) + its `development/giving-history/ingest` route; `development/online-gifts-diagnostic`; and the **Sidebar** Admin link + impersonation gate (`isSuperAdmin` prop threaded from Dashboard). **Result: RBK (owner, not super-admin) can no longer open the admin panel, run the gift-history sync, or impersonate** — only Becca can.

The existing `edit_enrollment_budget`/`edit_enrollment_data` keys are unchanged.

### FIX: Current Enrollment showed 0 for all grades — wrong Veracross data source (2026-07-21b)

Firebase release 2026-07-21b, **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00712-hzh`**. The Current Enrollment view (shipped earlier same day) showed **0 for every grade / "No current enrollment data."** Diagnosed by probing Veracross live with the SAR admissions OAuth creds (`schoolCode=sar`):

- **Root cause:** `app/api/admissions/current-enrollment` fetched `academics/enrollments?school_year=2026` and filtered on `e.grade_level`. But that endpoint returns **course enrollments** (one row per class), and the grade lives in **`grade_level_id`**, not `grade_level` — so the filter read `undefined → 0` and dropped every row. Even reading the right field wouldn't fix it: in July only **~691 persons (mostly HS)** have 26-27 class schedules built (Academy course schedules aren't loaded yet), so that endpoint **can't** give a full Academy headcount this time of year.
- **The correct source is `/v3/students`** (the roster): **1700 rows, all active, `grade_level` fully populated in the admissions numbering** (I/T=40 … K=20, 1-8, 9-12). Its grades match the HS students' 26-27 course `grade_level_id` exactly, confirming roster `grade_level` = the current 26-27 grade (the rollover did NOT push the roster to a next-year grade). This is the same source `/api/admissions` already uses for `currentYearCounts`, so the two now agree. Expected academy-only total ≈ **1036** (e.g. 1st 95, 2nd 97, 3rd 111, K 104, I/T 17).
- **Route rewritten** to read `/v3/students` (scope `students:list students:read`), filter to `currentYearGradesSet` + active (`!exit_date || exit_date > today`), and return `{ students, countsByGrade, totalEnrolled, enrollment_projection_enabled }`. Names from `first_name`/`last_name`, `campus`, Pisgah from `student_group`, `id` = person_id. Dropped the `academics/enrollments` fetch, its token, and the `school_year=2025` fallback (all moot). Kept loud `[CURRENT-ENROLLMENT ROUTE]` / `[CURRENT-ENROLLMENT RESULT]` / `[CURRENT-ENROLLMENT STUDENTS]` (status + first-2-row sample) logging.
- **Client (`Dashboard.tsx`):** added `[CURRENT-ENROLLMENT]` console traces — effect-gate check (`activeNav`/`admissionsTab`/division), `fetching…`, response status, data summary, and a tagged `[CURRENT-ENROLLMENT ERROR]` catch — so the browser console shows whether the fetch fires and what comes back.
- The trigger `useEffect` (fires on the Projection tab when `activeNav==='admissions' && admissionsTab==='projection'`) was verified correct; the "no requests in Cloud Run logs" symptom was consistent with the request firing but the route silently returning 0 (the logs simply weren't found / predate the new tags). tsc + build clean; deploy shipped (Cloud Run `00712-hzh` 100%, site 307, endpoint 401=auth). **Follow-up:** when Projection mode is unlocked for 27-28, that view still uses the existing re-enrollment `next_grade` pipeline (unchanged); Current Enrollment will keep tracking the roster's then-current grade.

### Admissions → Enrollment Projection: Current-Enrollment view + Projection year-lock toggle (2026-07-21)

Firebase release 2026-07-21, **Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00708-tfg`** (deployed via ADC token — see the reauth note at the top of this file). Fixes the Enrollment Projection tab showing wrong data after Veracross rolled the school year over: `/v3/students.grade_level` now reflects each student's NEXT-year (27-28) grade, but nobody has registered for 27-28 yet, so the projection pipeline was misleading. The tab now has two modes behind a segmented toggle, defaulting to Current Enrollment every time it opens.

- **New generic `workspace_settings` table** (migration `scripts/migrations/workspace-settings.sql`, applied to Supabase project `ftjppqvxthxcvhokrhfw`): `(id, workspace_id, key, value jsonb, updated_at, updated_by, UNIQUE(workspace_id,key))`, RLS enabled with **no client policies** (service-role only, same posture as `workspace_integrations`) + a `workspace_id` index. First consumer key `enrollment_projection_enabled`; SAR row (`a1b2c3d4-…`) seeded `false` (locked). This is the project's first generic workspace feature-flag store — reach for it (not `workspace_integrations`, which is credentials-only) for future workspace-level flags.
- **New route `app/api/admissions/current-enrollment/route.ts`**:
  - `GET` (auth + division-aware, mirrors `/api/admissions`) → `{ students, countsByGrade, totalEnrolled, enrollment_projection_enabled }`. Reads the **year-scoped** Veracross `academics/enrollments?school_year=2026` endpoint for the authoritative CURRENT (26-27) grade (the enrollments token uses scope `academics.enrollments:list academics.enrollments:read`), then joins the `/v3/students` roster **only** for rollover-independent fields (name, campus, household, `student_group`→Pisgah) keyed by `person_id` — because that roster's `grade_level` is now the post-rollover next-year grade and must NOT be used for grade. Dedups by `person_id` (academics/enrollments can return >1 row per student), drops already-withdrawn students (past `exit_date`), and filters to current-year division grades (incl. the graduating tier 8/12). Also returns the projection-unlock flag from `workspace_settings` (missing table/row/error → `false`, so the feature is safe even pre-migration).
  - `PATCH` (**owner-only**, `session.role === 'owner'`, else 403) → body `{ enrollment_projection_enabled: boolean }` upserts the flag. This is how Emily/Becca unlock (or re-lock) Projection mode in Jan 2027 with **no code deploy**.
  - Rate-limiting: not added here (the route is read-mostly Veracross + a single-row settings read; the existing AI-route limiter pattern didn't apply). Flagged for follow-up if abused.
- **`Dashboard.tsx` Enrollment Projection tab** (the only touched tab): added a segmented **[ Current Enrollment ] [ Enrollment Projection ]** toggle + a dynamic school-year header below the tab bar, defaulting to Current every time the tab is (re)entered.
  - **Current Enrollment mode** — header "Current Enrollment — 2026-27 School Year"; fetches `/api/admissions/current-enrollment` on tab load / division change; renders the three school-level cards (ELC / Lower / Middle with current enrolled counts), a Total Enrolled card, and a headcount-only **By Grade** table (Grade | Enrolled | Progress | School Level | Pisgah). Clicking a grade row opens a below-table drilldown of student names (first+last, sorted), each linking to `veracrossAdmissionUrl(id, true)` = `https://axiom.veracross.com/sar/#/detail/student-ls/{person_id}/273-general`. No pipeline columns / funnel / not-re-enrolling section.
  - **Enrollment Projection mode** — the **existing projection content is unchanged**, captured verbatim into a `projectionContent` variable and wrapped: when `enrollment_projection_enabled === false` it renders grayed (`opacity-40 pointer-events-none`) behind a centered locked banner ("Re-enrollment for 2027-28 opens in January 2027…") with an owner-only **"Unlock Projection Mode"** button (PATCH); when `true` it shows the original content as-is with header "Enrollment Projection — 2027-28 School Year" and an owner-only "Lock projection" link. Year labels are derived dynamically (month ≥ July → current school year starts this calendar year; projection = +1).
- **Untouched** per spec: existing projection columns/stat-cards/funnel/drilldown logic, Admissions Overview tab, New Enrollment tab, Pisgah manual-entry logic, all other routes/modules. `npx tsc --noEmit` (exit 0) + `npm run build` (exit 0, `/api/admissions/current-enrollment` compiled) clean; deploy shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released, Cloud Run `00708-tfg` serving 100%, site 307).
- **ASSUMPTIONS / follow-ups** (couldn't verify against live Veracross — no session): the exact field shape of `academics/enrollments` (whether it's one row per student or per course, and whether `school_year=2026` is honored server-side) is handled defensively (dedup by `person_id` + tolerant client-side year match + name/campus fallbacks), but should be spot-checked against Cloud Run `[current-enrollment]` logs on first real use. Pisgah for current students is best-effort from the roster `student_group` string containing "pisgah".

### FY25 Campaign Giving by Fund → giving_history_cache with new `fund` column (2026-07-16)

Firebase release 2026-07-16 (Cloud Run revision # unread — gcloud reauth pending). The Veracross Operating Gift History Export CSV now includes a **Fund** column; `giving_history_cache` gained a `fund text` column (added in Supabase by the user). The FY25 "Campaign Giving by Fund" column on the Development Overview now reads from `giving_history_cache` (complete FY25 history) instead of `gifts_cache` (which is missing pre-April-2025 gifts and understated the FY25 per-fund totals).

- **`lib/parseGivingHistoryCSV.ts`** — `GivingHistoryRow` gained `fund: string`, read from **column 12** (0-indexed `cols[11]`), defaulting to `''`. **Column-position assumption (documented in the file):** the build environment could NOT read the CSV object from GCS to confirm the header order — `getFiles` (list) worked but `download` returned `403 storage.objects.get denied` for the local `rglassberg@` ADC (only the Cloud Run SA has object-read on the bucket). Per the task's fallback, Fund was assumed to be the **last** column (appended after Constituent ID), which keeps every pre-existing column index (0-10) unchanged. **Post-sync verification confirmed the assumption was correct** — the `fund` column populated with real Veracross names (`OP: Guardian Circle`, `OP: Anniversary Dinner`, `OP: Grants`, `OP: Shavuot Appeal`, `OP: Sponsorships`, …), not shifted/garbage data.
- **`app/api/development/giving-history/ingest/route.ts`** — added `fund: r.fund` to the upsert payload.
- **`app/api/development/overview/route.ts`** — new **paginated** `giving_history_cache` query (`select fund, gift_type, amount where fundraising_activity='Operating 2024-2025' and gift_type in (1,2)`; the FY25 type-1/2 slice is ~3.2k rows, over the 1000-row cap — paginated in 1000s, mirroring the existing FY25 baseline query) builds `fundFY25History`. The campaigns array now uses `fundFY25Final = fundFY25History.size > 0 ? fundFY25History : fundFY25` for both the fund union and the `raisedFY25` lookup — i.e. **falls back to the gifts_cache-derived `fundFY25`** (still computed in the main loop) when the history table has no FY25 rows. Soft credits excluded (only gift_type 1+2 selected). **Documented deviation:** `giving_history_cache` has no `pledge_balance` column (the CSV carries only `amount`), so FY25 type-2 pledges contribute their face-value `amount` here rather than the outstanding `pledge_balance` the gifts_cache path uses — acceptable since the FY25 column is a historical reference and the FY26 column (which reconciles with the headline) is untouched.
- **Re-sync triggered post-deploy:** `POST /api/development/giving-history/ingest` (direct Cloud Run URL, `X-Internal-Secret`) → **23,147 parsed / 22,137 upserted / 1,010 skipped** from `Operating_Gift_History.csv` (GCS-modified 2026-07-16). FY25 per-fund now complete (e.g. Guardian Circle FY25 $4.2M).

**Untouched** per spec: FY26 Campaign Giving (still gifts_cache), segment card totals, headline numbers, the soft-credit gap-fill dedup logic, all other tabs/routes. tsc + build clean; firebase shipped (site 307, overview 401=auth). gcloud finalize aborted on reauth (redundant). **Follow-up:** if a future export ever inserts Fund mid-order rather than appending, the positional read in the parser would shift — re-confirm against the live CSV once gcloud/bucket read access is restored.

### @Notify — interactive Slack "Mark Resolved" button + Command Center link (2026-07-14)

Firebase release 2026-07-14e (Cloud Run revision # unread — gcloud reauth pending). The @Notify group DM now carries two buttons under the message, and marking a task resolved works directly from Slack.

**Message buttons (`app/api/notify/route.ts`).** The Block Kit message gained an `actions` block with: (1) **Open in Command Center** — a url button → `${NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'https://rbk-cmd-center.web.app'}/?nav=tasks` (client-side link, works even before Interactivity is configured); (2) **✓ Mark Resolved** — an interactive `primary` button, `action_id: 'notify_mark_resolved'`. The context footer was reworded to "Reply in this thread, or tap *Mark Resolved* when you've handled it."

**New route `POST /api/slack/interactions`.** Handles Slack `block_actions` payloads (form-encoded `payload` field). On a `notify_mark_resolved` click: resolves the clicking Slack user → `workspace_members` row, finds THAT user's open `source='notify'` task on the message's thread (matched by `slack_thread_ts` = the button message's `ts` — precise even though Slack reuses one MPIM channel id across repeat DMs; falls back to `slack_channel_id` if the ts is absent, and to `assigned_to IN (assignee_key, display_name, email)`), marks it `done` + stamps `completed_at`, and posts `✓ <name> marked this resolved` into the thread via `postSlackMessage` (same reply text as the PATCH `/api/tasks` resolve loop — no double-fire since that path only runs on the HTTP PATCH). Each tagged member resolves only their own task, so the button stays useful for everyone in the DM. Always acks Slack with `200 { ok: true }` (even on parse/lookup miss) so Slack doesn't retry; the "Open in Command Center" url button's interaction is ignored (action_id mismatch). Added to the middleware auth-matcher exclusion list (Slack has no session cookie).

**Slack request signature verification (STEP 2D).** `verifySlackSignature(request, rawBody)` computes the `v0=` HMAC-SHA256 over `v0:{x-slack-request-timestamp}:{rawBody}` using `process.env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET` and compares with a length-guarded `crypto.timingSafeEqual` (+ a 5-minute replay guard on the timestamp). **Non-fatal when the secret is unset** — returns `true` (skip) and logs a warning, so the endpoint works today; once `SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET` is added to the Cloud Run env, an invalid/stale signature is rejected with 401. The raw body is read once (`request.text()`) and reused for both verification and payload parsing.

**MANUAL SETUP REQUIRED (one-time, by user):** api.slack.com/apps → Command Center app → **Interactivity & Shortcuts → turn ON → Request URL `https://ssrrbkcmdcenter-429508710310.us-east1.run.app/api/slack/interactions` → Save Changes.** Until this is set, the "Mark Resolved" button posts nothing (the url button still works). Optionally add `SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET` to Cloud Run to secure the endpoint. **Untouched** per spec: morning briefings, Buzz, task-assignment DMs, the @Notify Slack group-DM open/post flow, and all other routes/modules. tsc + build clean; firebase shipped (site 307; unsigned `/api/slack/interactions` POST acks 200 = live + non-fatal without the secret).

### @Notify Slack group DM — diagnostic logging + flow verified (2026-07-14)

Firebase release 2026-07-14d (Cloud Run revision # unread — gcloud reauth pending). The @Notify group DM was silently failing (`slack_thread_ts`/`slack_channel_id` null on all notify tasks) because the Slack errors went to `console.warn` truncated to 300 chars with no context. This deploy adds loud, structured logging so the exact Slack failure is readable in Cloud Run logs — **no functional flow change was needed**.

**Flow verified correct (STEP 2).** `openGroupDm` in `lib/slackNotifications.ts` already matches the required MPIM flow exactly: (1) `POST conversations.open` with a **comma-separated string** `{ users: slackUserIds.join(',') }` (not an array), (2) read `channel.id` from the response, (3) `chat.postMessage` with `{ channel: <that id>, blocks, text }`. No mistake to fix — the diagnosis's "common mistakes" (array instead of CSV, skipping conversations.open, wrong channel) were all already avoided. The `mpim:write` scope was added + the app reinstalled out-of-band; whether the DB/env bot token carries the new scope will be confirmed by the logs below.

**Logging added (STEP 1).** All `[NOTIFY SLACK ERROR]`-prefixed via `console.error`, full untruncated Slack responses:
- `openGroupDm` / `postSlackMessage` (`lib/slackNotifications.ts`): log the outgoing user CSV / channel, then the **full** `conversations.open` / `chat.postMessage` JSON response, and on failure a structured error with `httpStatus`, `slackError`, and Slack's `needed`/`provided` scope fields (surfaces `missing_scope` precisely). Content-Type on conversations.open set to `application/json; charset=utf-8`.
- `app/api/notify/route.ts`: before the call, logs `[NOTIFY SLACK] preparing group DM` with `participantSlackIds`, `participantCount`, `hasBotToken`, and `participantsMissingSlackId` (members dropped for having no `slack_user_id`). Then distinct `[NOTIFY SLACK ERROR]` branches for: no bot token, `< 2` participants with a Slack ID (Slack rejects a single-user MPIM), `openGroupDm` returned null, and `postSlackMessage` returned null — so the log pinpoints exactly which stage failed. The catch now uses `console.error`. Slack remains best-effort — task creation is never blocked.

**How to diagnose (for the next session / user):** trigger an @Notify, then read Cloud Run logs for the `[NOTIFY SLACK]` / `[NOTIFY SLACK ERROR]` lines (`gcloud run services logs read ssrrbkcmdcenter --region=us-east1 --project=rbk-cmd-center --limit=200 | grep 'NOTIFY SLACK'`, or the GCP/Firebase console). The most likely findings: (a) `slackError: 'missing_scope'` with `needed: 'mpim:write'` ⇒ the stored bot token predates the reinstall — reconnect Slack in Admin → Integrations (or reseed the token) so the new scope is picked up; (b) `fewer than 2 participants with a Slack ID` ⇒ the sender and/or the tagged member has no `slack_user_id` in `workspace_members` (several viewers currently don't). **I could not read the logs myself this run — gcloud needs `gcloud auth login` reauth.** tsc + build clean; firebase shipped (site 307, `/api/notify` 401=auth).

### @Notify follow-ups — Tasks visible to all users + Admissions note pre-fill (2026-07-14)

Firebase release 2026-07-14c (Cloud Run revision # unread — gcloud reauth pending). Two follow-ups to the cross-module @Notify feature.

**FIX 1 — Tasks page reachable by every workspace member.** The Sidebar's entire **Daily** section (Dashboard/Inbox/Agenda/Tasks/Compose) is gated by `!(role === 'viewer' && allowedModules)`, so viewers-with-`allowed_modules` (i.e. most members) never saw Tasks and couldn't reach their @Notify "Needs Your Reply" items. Rather than un-hiding the whole Daily section for viewers (which would also expose Inbox/Agenda/Compose — out of scope), a **standalone Tasks nav block** now renders in `Sidebar.tsx` for exactly the hidden case (`role === 'viewer' && allowedModules`), showing just the Tasks button under a "Daily" label. The Dashboard `activeNav === 'tasks'` branch has no role gate and `handleNav('tasks')` from `/home` routes to `/?nav=tasks` (which `app/page.tsx` honors without bouncing to `/home`), so viewers reach the tasks view end-to-end; their notify tasks match by `assignee_key` OR `display_name`. In `app/admin/permissions/page.tsx` the Add-User form now defaults `addModules` to `{ tasks: true }` (both the initial `useState` and `resetAddForm`), so new viewers get `tasks` in their `allowed_modules` automatically. The Tasks module toggle is unchanged in the permissions grid (admins can still see/manage/uncheck it) — only the default flipped on.

**FIX 2 — Admissions candidate NotifyButton pre-fills the note text.** The note draft lives in `NotesList`'s internal `draft` state (inside `DonorAnnotations`) and wasn't readable by the sibling NotifyButton. Chose a **ref-based** approach (no Dashboard re-render per keystroke, avoiding jank in the 9k-line component): `NotesList`/`DonorAnnotations` gained an optional `onDraftChange?: (text: string) => void` fired on every draft change (the 3 `setDraft` sites); Dashboard passes `onDraftChange={(t) => { admissionsNoteDraftRef.current[annotationsName] = t; }}` (a plain ref write) into all 7 Admissions drilldown `DonorAnnotations`. `NotifyButton` gained an optional `getMessage?: () => string` read at the moment the popover opens (takes precedence over the static `message` prop); the 7 candidate buttons pass `getMessage={() => admissionsNoteDraftRef.current[annotationsName] ?? ''}`. Net: type a note on a candidate → click `@` → the popover message is pre-filled with the current note text, editable independently. **Note save + notify remain two separate actions** — `onDraftChange` only mirrors the draft; saving the note is untouched. Development call sites of `DonorAnnotations` don't pass `onDraftChange`, so their behavior is unchanged.

**Untouched** per spec: the @Notify Slack group-DM logic, task creation + resolve loop, the permissions Tasks toggle behavior, all other module functionality. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released; site 307). gcloud finalize aborted on reauth (redundant).

The constant feeds the card's `<iframe src>` **and** its two "Open in new tab" / "Open This Week at SAR" `<a href>` links. **A deploy was required** even though it's a one-line URL change: it's a `'use client'` component, so the URL is compiled into the client JS bundle at build time and the live site serves the pre-built bundle — the change would not reach production without `npm run build` + `./deploy.sh`. tsc/build clean; firebase step shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released; site 307). gcloud finalize step aborted on reauth (redundant — firebase finalized its own release). ### Cross-module @Notify — Slack group DM + task creation with reply loop (2026-07-14)

Firebase release 2026-07-14 (Cloud Run revision # unread — gcloud reauth pending). New "@Notify" feature lets any workspace member tag one or more teammates, fire a Slack **group DM** to them (plus each tagged member's assistant), and drop a task on each tagged member's task list — with a resolve loop that posts back into the Slack thread when the task is marked done.

**New shared component** `app/components/shared/NotifyButton.tsx` — self-contained understated `@` icon button + popover. Props `{ context: string; message?: string; onSent?: () => void; className? }`. Popover has an editable message textarea (pre-filled from `message`), a searchable multi-select person picker (avatar initial + name + role badge, selected people show as removable chips), and a Notify button (disabled until ≥1 person selected + non-empty message). On send → POST `/api/notify`, spinner → "✓ Sent" → `onSent()` → auto-close after 1.5s. Closes on outside-click / Escape. Fetches members from the existing `/api/workspace/mentionable-users` (extended to also return `role` for the badge — additive, safe for `SlackSendModal` which ignores it).

**New route** `POST /api/notify` (auth-gated, impersonation-aware via `getEffectiveWorkspaceId`). Body `{ message, context, tagged_member_ids[] }`. Loads all workspace members once; resolves the sender (by session email), the tagged members (by id), and each tagged member's assistant (members whose `assistant_to` = a tagged id). Group-DM participant list = sender + tagged + assistants, deduped by `slack_user_id`, null Slack ids dropped. Sends one Slack group DM via `conversations.open` (→ channel id) then `chat.postMessage` (Block Kit section + `Reply in this thread · Mark resolved in the Command Center` context footer). Then inserts one `tasks` row per tagged member: `title = "<Sender> needs your input"`, `description = message`, `source = 'notify'`, `source_ref = context`, `assigned_to = assignee_key ?? display_name ?? email`, `slack_thread_ts` + `slack_channel_id` from the DM. Returns `{ success, slack_thread_ts, tasks_created }`. Slack failures are non-fatal (tasks still created); a group DM needs ≥2 Slack participants, else Slack is skipped and tasks-only proceeds.

**Slack helpers** added to `lib/slackNotifications.ts`: `openGroupDm(userIds[], botToken)` (conversations.open → channel id) and `postSlackMessage(channel, botToken, { text, blocks?, thread_ts? })` (returns the message `ts`; `thread_ts` posts a threaded reply). Both never throw.

**Resolve loop** in `PATCH /api/tasks`: when a task is marked `status: 'done'`, the handler reads the task's `source` + `slack_thread_ts` + `slack_channel_id`; if `source === 'notify'` and both Slack coordinates are present, it fire-and-forget posts `✓ <actor display_name> marked this resolved` as a threaded reply. Actor name resolved via `getMemberByEmail`.

**Migration** `scripts/migrations/tasks-slack-thread.sql` (applied live): `ALTER TABLE tasks ADD COLUMN slack_thread_ts text, slack_channel_id text` (both needed — chat.postMessage thread replies require the channel, and an MPIM channel id isn't reconstructable from the ts alone). Also **dropped `tasks_assigned_to_check`** — the old CHECK hard-limited `assigned_to` to the 5 keys `['RBK','Emily','Sara','Leora','Becca']`, but @Notify must assign to ANY member (most have no `assignee_key` and are stored by `display_name`). `assigned_to` stays NOT NULL.

**Dashboard wiring** (`Dashboard.tsx`): (1) `NOTIFY_CONTEXT_LABELS` module-scope map (activeNav → human label). (2) A single content-area `<NotifyButton>` at the top-right of the `p-8` content wrapper, context derived from `activeNav` — so **every** module/tab (including sub-component modules like Development / After School) gets a consistently-placed @Notify button with a per-module context string, without editing 13 nested headers. (3) A **"Needs Your Reply"** sourced-task group in the Tasks column (blue theme, cloned from the "From Development" group), filtering `sourcedTasks` by `source === 'notify'` matched to the current user by `assignee_key` OR `display_name` (so members without an assignee_key still see their own notify tasks). Marking one resolved here PATCHes `/api/tasks` → fires the Slack thread reply. (4) Per-candidate `<NotifyButton context={annotationsName} />` (context = `"Admissions: <name>"`) injected into all 7 Admissions drilldown "Notes & Tags" panels, next to the existing `DonorAnnotations`.

**Deliberate scope decisions (documented deviations from the literal spec):** (a) Step 4 uses one content-area button per module rather than 13 surgical header edits — far lower risk in the 9100-line single-component file, and covers the sub-component modules too. (b) Step 5 pre-fills the per-candidate button's **context** but not the note **message** — the note text lives inside `DonorAnnotations`' internal state and isn't readable from the parent; note-text pre-fill is a follow-up. (c) `source_ref` carries the context (no `context_label` column); `source = 'notify'` (no `task_type` column). (d) **Viewers** (most tagged members — Sara, Leora, Debra, etc.) don't have the Tasks page in their sidebar (Daily section is owner/assistant-only), so they currently only get the Slack DM, not an in-app resolve surface. Owners/assistants (RBK, Emily, Becca) get the full loop via the "Needs Your Reply" group. Surfacing notify tasks in a viewer-accessible list is a follow-up. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released; site 307, `/api/notify` 401=auth). gcloud finalize step aborted on reauth (redundant — firebase finalized its own release).

**Latest revision:** firebase release 2026-06-28b (Cloud Run revision # unread — gcloud reauth pending). Latest: **"Other" segment — exclude family foundations/DAFs already represented via soft credits (supersedes the OP: Grants-only filter).** The "Other" segment was double-counting foundations/DAFs whose gift is also soft-credited to a named-segment individual (e.g. Luxenberg Chesed Fund $99k in Other while Arthur & Randi Luxenberg get the soft credit in Grandparent). New rule in `overview/route.ts` + `segment-donors/route.ts`: during the gifts loop, record each FY26 type-1/type-2 gift's record `id` → owner cid (`type1GiftToConstituent`); after the loop, build `softCreditedOrgs` = every owner whose own gift has a FY26 type-3 soft credit pointing back to it from a DIFFERENT constituent; then exclude `cid` from Other when `segmentOf === 'Other' && softCreditedOrgs.has(cid)`. This **replaced** the prior `softFunds`/OP:Grants-only check (removed `GRANTS_FUND`, `softFunds`, and the now-unused `fund` select in segment-donors) — the new logic is more accurate and handles grants-only orgs without a fund-name match. Verified live (SQL): excluded — Lieberman Charitable Fund ($101,800), Conduit Foundation ($100,000), Luxenberg Chesed Fund ($99,000), Lent Tzeddakah Fund ($85,000), Milstein DAF ($80,000); still in Other — UJA-Federation ($70,500, grants-only, no household soft credit). Untouched: named-segment classification, headline Total Raised/Donors, soft-credit gap-fill dedup, Board Members priority/overrides, lapsed/new/retained, all other tabs/routes. tsc + build clean; firebase shipped (site 307, both routes 401).

### Development Overview — "Other" excludes foundations/DAFs already soft-credited to a named segment (2026-06-28, supersedes the grants-only filter)

The 2026-06-28 grants-only filter (only-`OP: Grants` soft credits) was a coarse proxy; QA surfaced its real failure mode — a family foundation gives directly (landing in "Other"), and Veracross soft-credits that same gift to the individual behind it, who lands in their proper named segment (Grandparent / Board Member / …). The foundation's dollars then count twice: once in Other, once in the individual's segment.

**New rule (both routes):**
- During the gifts loop, for every FY26 type-1 AND type-2 gift, record `type1GiftToConstituent[g.id] = constituent_id` (the gift's owner).
- After the loop, a second pass over the raw gifts builds `softCreditedOrgs: Set<number>`: for each FY26 `gift_type = 3` row with a non-null `hard_credit_gift_id`, look up the original gift's owner; if the soft-credit recipient is a **different** constituent than the owner, add the **owner** to `softCreditedOrgs` (its money is already represented via that person's soft credit). The household auto-twin (recipient === owner) is skipped, so ordinary direct donors are unaffected.
- In the segment build, when `segmentOf(cid) === 'Other'`, `continue` if `softCreditedOrgs.has(cid)`. Applied to the Other card total + donor count (`overview/route.ts`) and the Other drilldown list (`segment-donors/route.ts`, gated to `segment === 'Other'`).

**Replaced Change-1's grants-only filter.** Removed the `GRANTS_FUND` constant, the per-donor `softFunds: Set<string>` accumulator (+ its init and the `.add()` in the soft branch) from both routes, and the now-unused `fund` from segment-donors' `GiftRow` + SELECT (overview keeps `fund` — it still feeds the per-fund campaign table). The new logic correctly keeps grants-only orgs (UJA) in Other without needing a fund-name match.

**Verified live via SQL** (org's FY26 type-1/2 gift id joined to a FY26 type-3 soft credit with a different recipient): excluded from Other — Lauren and David Lieberman Charitable Fund (14556, $101,800), The Conduit Foundation (13560, $100,000), Luxenberg Chesed Fund (18639, $99,000), Lent Tzeddakah Fund (15505, $85,000), Stephen and Eve Milstein DAF (15362, $80,000); **stays** in Other — UJA-Federation of New York (12760, $70,500). Matches every spec expectation.

**Untouched** per spec: named-segment classification, headline Total Raised / Total Donors, the soft-credit gap-fill dedup-by-`hard_credit_gift_id` logic, Board Members priority + overrides, lapsed/new/retained, Israel/Cooper/Guardian Circle/Weekly Gifts, all other tabs/routes. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. **Deploy:** `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released; site 307, overview + segment-donors 401=auth); gcloud-only steps aborted again on `gcloud auth login` reauth (redundant). Cloud Run revision # unread until gcloud is reauthed. (1) The "Other" segment was double-counting role-less records whose soft credits came from named funds (Guardian Circle, Anniversary Dinner, Shavuot Appeal). Now an "Other"-classified donor is included **only if** their qualifying FY26 soft credits are all from `OP: Grants` (or they have none — direct gifts only); any non-grants soft credit excludes them from Other entirely. Both `overview/route.ts` (segment card total + donor count) and `segment-donors/route.ts` (Other drilldown list) track `softFunds: Set<string>` per donor and apply the filter only when the segment is "Other" — named segments untouched. (2) The Board Members drilldown panel (`OverviewTab.tsx`) gained a summary bar above the donor list: one pill per `secondaryRole` group (the segment each trustee would otherwise classify into) showing `{role} {total giving} · {N} trustees`, sorted by total desc, SEGMENT_STYLE-colored, Board-Members-only. ### Development Overview — "Other" grants-only filter + Board Members secondary-role summary (2026-06-28, Heidi QA)

**CHANGE 1 — "Other" segment only includes pure-grant (or direct-only) donors.** QA found the "Other" segment double-counting: donors whose `roles_raw` matches no named segment but who received soft credits from named funds (Guardian Circle, Anniversary Dinner, Shavuot Appeal) were landing in Other, inflating it with money that belongs to a real person's segment. New rule: a donor classified "Other" is included **only if** the set of funds across their qualifying FY26 soft credits (sc_type 1/2) is empty (no soft credits — they have only direct gifts/pledges) OR contains nothing but `OP: Grants`. Any soft credit from another fund excludes them from Other entirely. Named segments are unaffected — the filter is gated to `segment === 'Other'`.
- `app/api/development/overview/route.ts`: added `GRANTS_FUND = 'OP: Grants'`; the per-donor `fy26ByDonor` accumulator gained `softFunds: Set<string>` (populated with each qualifying soft credit's fund); the segment-building loop computes `segName = segmentOf(cid)` and does `if (segName === 'Other' && ![...v.softFunds].every(f => f === GRANTS_FUND)) continue;` before adding to the segment's received total + donor count. So both the Other card total and its donor count reflect the filter.
- `app/api/development/overview/segment-donors/route.ts`: same `GRANTS_FUND` const; `GiftRow` + SELECT gained `fund`; `byDonor` gained `softFunds`; the drilldown build loop applies the identical exclusion when the requested `segment === 'Other'`.
- Verified the fund literal: the exact stored string is `OP: Grants` (1 donor workspace-wide has grants-only soft credits; 1,342 of 1,343 FY26 soft-credit donors have a named-fund soft credit, so the filter meaningfully de-dups Other without touching named segments).

**CHANGE 2 — Board Members drilldown: secondary-segment summary bar.** `app/components/development/OverviewTab.tsx`: a new `boardSecondarySummary` `useMemo` groups `drawerRows` (the loaded Board Members donor list, search-filtered) by `secondaryRole` — the segment each trustee would classify into with "Trustee" stripped — summing `donationsReceived` and counting trustees per group, sorted by total desc, empty for every other drilldown. Rendered as a wrap of pills at the top of the drawer's scroll area (gated to `drawer.segment === 'Board Members'` and ≥1 group): each pill shows `{role} {formatMoney(total)} · {N} trustee(s)` using the existing `SEGMENT_STYLE[role].pillCls` colors. Only groups with ≥1 trustee appear. No other drilldown shows it; the per-row secondaryRole pill in the table is unchanged.

**Untouched** per spec: all other segment classification, headline Total Raised / Total Donors, Board Members priority + overrides, Israel/Cooper/Guardian Circle/Weekly Gifts, lapsed/new/retained, the soft-credit dedup-by-`hard_credit_gift_id` logic, every other tab/route. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. **Deploy:** `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released; site 307, overview + segment-donors 401=auth); gcloud-only steps aborted again on `gcloud auth login` reauth (redundant). Cloud Run revision # unread until gcloud is reauthed. Reported symptom: FY27 (`Operating 2026-2027`) pledges leaking into FY26 segment totals (Grunberg 10958 $10k, Fleisch 6757 $10k). Investigation found **neither the overview nor segment-donors route was actually leaking** — the overview loop already buckets with exact `activity === FY26_CAMPAIGN`, and segment-donors already queries with exact `.eq('fundraising_activity', FY26_CAMPAIGN)`. SQL simulation confirmed both donors' FY26 totals already exclude the $10k FY27 pledge (Grunberg FY26 = $25,000, Fleisch = $16,264.17). The only prefix match anywhere was the overview *query's* `.ilike('Operating %')` (used to also pull FY25 for the FY25 headline + per-fund column); it fetched FY27 rows that the exact-match loop then dropped. Hardened it to `.in('fundraising_activity', [FY25_CAMPAIGN, FY26_CAMPAIGN])` so FY27+ rows are never fetched — defense-in-depth against any future loosening of the loop match; output is byte-identical (FY27 was already ignored), FY25 still fetched. Removed the now-unused `OPERATING_CAMPAIGN_PREFIX` const. **segment-donors unchanged** (already exact). Untouched: giving_history_cache FY25 baseline, headline Total Raised/Donors, Board Members priority/overrides, soft-credit gap-fill, all other tabs/routes. tsc + build clean; firebase shipped (site 307, both routes 401).

### Development Overview — gifts query scoped to FY25+FY26 exactly (FY27-pledge leak hardening) (2026-06-25)

**Reported:** Operating 2026-2027 pledges leaking into FY26 segment totals — Grunberg, Yossi and Deena (cid 10958, $10k FY27 pledge dated 2026-05-04) and Fleisch, Dayna and Michael (cid 6757, $10k FY27 pledge dated 2026-06-08). The same prefix-match bug had been fixed earlier on the Guardian Circle + fundraising-goals routes.

**Finding: the leak did not actually exist in either overview route.** Both already isolate FY26:
- `overview/route.ts` — the gifts query pulled all Operating campaigns via `.ilike('fundraising_activity', 'Operating %')`, but the aggregation loop buckets each row with an **exact** string match (`if (activity === FY26_CAMPAIGN)` / `else if (activity === FY25_CAMPAIGN)`). An `Operating 2026-2027` row matches neither branch → silently ignored. So FY27 never reached `raisedFY26`, `fundFY26`, or the per-donor `seg.pledged`.
- `segment-donors/route.ts` — queries with exact `.eq('fundraising_activity', FY26_CAMPAIGN)`, so FY27 rows are never even fetched. Its loop doesn't re-check the campaign string (no need). Airtight already.

SQL simulation of the exact route math (Operating 2025-2026 only) confirmed: Grunberg FY26 total = **$25,000** (type-1 $11,000 + non-twin soft credit $5,000 + outstanding pledge balance $9,000), Fleisch = **$16,264.17** — neither includes the $10k FY27 pledge. (The brief's "~$22,000" for Grunberg undercounted: it omitted the $5k soft-credit gap-fill gift and used "pledge paid $11k" where the route uses the $9k outstanding `pledge_balance`. The load-bearing invariant — FY27 excluded — holds either way.)

**Change applied (defense-in-depth):** the overview gifts query's prefix `.ilike('Operating %')` → `.in('fundraising_activity', [FY25_CAMPAIGN, FY26_CAMPAIGN])`. The route legitimately needs FY25 too (FY25 headline donors/raised + the per-fund FY25 column come from this query's FY25 branch — the giving_history_cache baseline is separate), so it can't narrow to a single `.eq(FY26)`; scoping to exactly the two bucketed campaigns stops FY27+ rows from being fetched at all, so even if someone later loosens the loop's exact match, FY27 can't leak. Output is byte-identical to before (FY27 rows were already dropped by the loop). The now-unused `OPERATING_CAMPAIGN_PREFIX` const was removed. **`segment-donors/route.ts` needed no change** (already exact `.eq()`).

**Untouched** per spec: giving_history_cache FY25 baseline logic, headline Total Raised / Total Donors, Board Members priority + overrides, Israel/Cooper/Guardian Circle/Weekly Gifts, soft-credit gap-fill, every other tab/route. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. **Deploy:** `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting released; site 307, overview + segment-donors 401=auth); gcloud-only steps aborted again on `gcloud auth login` reauth (redundant). Cloud Run revision # unread until gcloud is reauthed. Deleted the scheduled `ingestGivingHistory` Cloud Function (was daily 8am ET) — removed from `functions/src/index.ts` and dropped via `firebase functions:delete ingestGivingHistory --region us-central1 --force` (confirmed absent from `functions:list`). The ingest route + GCS pipeline are unchanged; the nightly auto-ingest is gone, replaced by an admin-only manual trigger. The Development Overview "Import History" link became a more prominent (still understated) bordered **"↓ Sync Gift History"** button → "Syncing…" with spinner; on success a persistent result line (no auto-dismiss) shows `✓ Synced {rows_upserted} records from {file} · file dated {modified}`, on error `✗ {message}` in red. The ingest route (`app/api/development/giving-history/ingest/route.ts`) now returns `file_modified` (the chosen CSV's GCS `metadata.updated` as ISO) in both success + upsert-error payloads.
