### Giving-history ingest — scheduler removed, manual "Sync Gift History" button (2026-06-25)

The nightly auto-ingest of the Veracross Operating gift-history CSV is retired in favor of an explicit admin trigger.

- **`functions/src/index.ts`** — the `exports.ingestGivingHistory` scheduled function (daily 8am ET, `0 8 * * *`) was deleted from the source and replaced with a comment noting it's now manual-only. To prevent the next `./deploy.sh` from aborting on the delete prompt, the function was first removed from GCP via `npx firebase functions:delete ingestGivingHistory --region us-central1 --force` (`Successful delete operation`; confirmed gone via `functions:list`). It was a 1st-gen Node 22 pubsub function in us-central1. **No other Cloud Function or scheduler was touched** (gifts sync, after-school sync, attendance, absence alert, task reminders, briefings all unchanged).
- **`app/api/development/giving-history/ingest/route.ts`** — captures the processed CSV's GCS `metadata.updated` into `fileModified` (ISO string, or null) and adds `file_modified` to the response shape: `{ success, file, file_modified, rows_parsed, rows_upserted, skipped }` (also added to the upsert-error payload). The ingest logic itself (file selection by newest `updated`, download, parse, dedup by gift_record_id, batched upsert of 500) is unchanged.
- **`app/components/development/OverviewTab.tsx`** — the admin-only (rglassberg@) trigger was upgraded from a tiny "Import History" link to a bordered, shadow-sm **"↓ Sync Gift History"** button (download-arrow icon; "Syncing…" + spinner while loading). The transient `importMsg` (auto-dismissed after 8s) was replaced with a structured `importResult: { ok, text, fileModified }` that **persists until the next click**. Success renders `✓ Synced {N} records from {file}` plus `· file dated {localized file_modified}` when present; error renders `✗ {message}` in red. Still gated to `isAdmin`.

`npx tsc --noEmit` (app + functions) + `npm run build` clean. **Deploy:** `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped the code (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting finalized + released; site 307, ingest route 401=auth). The gcloud-only finalize/IAM steps aborted again on `gcloud auth login` reauth (redundant this run). Exact Cloud Run revision # unread until gcloud is reauthed. The Development Overview segment cards + drilldown were dropping foundation/DAF money for any donor who ALSO gave directly: the old gap-fill rule ("count soft credits ONLY when the donor has NO type-1 gift") skipped every soft credit for direct donors. New rule (both `overview/route.ts` + `segment-donors/route.ts`): a soft credit is a Veracross auto-twin (excluded) **only** when its `hard_credit_gift_id` equals the record `id` of one of that constituent's own type-1 gifts; a soft credit whose hcid is NOT one of their own type-1 ids came from a different source (their family foundation gave directly) and IS counted. So segment `received` = SUM(type-1 amount) + SUM(non-twin soft-credit amount). Per-donor accumulator now tracks `type1GiftIds: Set<number>` (collected from each type-1 row's `id` — type-1 rows carry `hard_credit_gift_id = NULL`, the twin's id points to the type-1 gift's own `id`) and `softByKey: Map<key,{hcid,amount}>` (deduped by hard_credit_gift_id). Soft pool stays gift_type=3 (sc_type 1/2) — type-5 pledge-soft-credits are a separate representation of the same pledge and would double-count. Verified live (SQL): Luxenberg 17707 = $2,360 + $99,000 = **$101,360** (Grandparent; was $2,360), Tsigutkin 9458 = $5,000 + $50,000 = **$55,000** (Parent; was $5,000). Workspace-wide impact: **131 donors gain +$827,885 total** (donors who give both directly and via DAF/foundation; min +$54, max +$99k). Untouched: headline Total Raised/Donors, lapsed/new/retained (the "gave" sets are provably unchanged — a twin implies a type-1 row, already in the set), Board Members priority/overrides, all other tabs/routes. **NOTE:** the spec's pseudo-code said collect type1GiftIds from "hard_credit_gift_ids of type-1 gifts" — but type-1 rows have NULL hard_credit_gift_id, so this implements the prose intent ("constituent's own type-1 gift IDs") by collecting the record `id`. tsc + build clean; firebase shipped (site 307, overview + segment-donors 401).

### Development Overview — soft-credit gap-fill twin-matched (DAF/foundation soft credits now counted) (2026-06-25)

The segment cards + segment-donors drilldown were under-counting any donor who gives **both** directly **and** through a DAF/family-foundation in the same year. The 2026-06-14 gap-fill rule was "count soft credits ONLY when the donor has NO type-1 row" — designed to suppress Veracross's auto-generated soft-credit twins (Veracross writes a type-3 soft-credit twin for every type-1 gift). But that rule is too blunt: it also dropped the genuinely-separate foundation gift (which arrives as a soft credit to the constituent) whenever the constituent happened to give directly too.

**Two examples (verified live):**
- **Luxenberg, Arthur and Randi (cid 17707, Grandparent):** direct type-1 gifts $360 + $1,000 + $1,000 = $2,360, PLUS a $99,000 Guardian Circle gift from their family foundation (soft-credited via type-3, `hard_credit_gift_id=223339`). Was showing $2,360; now **$101,360**. (The brief's "~$100,360" undercounted one of the three $1k direct gifts.)
- **Tsigutkin, Alex and Diana (cid 9458, Parent):** direct $5,000 + a $50,000 Guardian Circle gift via their foundation (type-3, `hcid=215269`). Was $5,000; now **$55,000**.

**The corrected rule** (applied identically in `app/api/development/overview/route.ts` and `app/api/development/overview/segment-donors/route.ts`): a soft credit is excluded as a Veracross twin **iff** its `hard_credit_gift_id` is the record `id` of one of THIS constituent's own type-1 gifts. Otherwise it's a separate-source gift and is counted. `received = SUM(type-1 amount) + SUM(amount of non-twin soft credits)`.

- **Data-structure changes** (both routes): per-donor accumulator now holds `type1GiftIds: Set<number>` (each type-1 row's `id`) and `softByKey: Map<string, {hcid, amount}>` (qualifying type-3 sc_type 1/2 soft credits, deduped by `hard_credit_gift_id` so sc_type 1+2 rows for one gift count once, falling back to `amt_<n>` when null). The old `softKeys`/`soft`/`hasSoft` fields are gone. The SELECT in both routes now pulls `id` (and `GiftRow` gained `id: number`).
- **Twin detection is precise, not broad:** a twin's hcid points to the donor's own same-campaign type-1 `id`; a foundation's hard credit carries the foundation's own `constituent_id`, so its `id` is never in the donor's `type1GiftIds`. Cross-year mismatch is impossible because both the type-1 set and the soft credits are scoped to the same Operating campaign.
- **Soft pool stays `gift_type=3` (sc_type 1/2).** Luxenberg also has a type-5 (pledge-soft-credit) row for $99,000 pointing at their own type-2 pledge — that's a *separate representation* of the same pledge; including type-5 would double-count to $198k. The countable foundation money comes through as the type-3 rows.
- **Important spec deviation (documented):** the brief's IMPLEMENTATION pseudo-code said to build `type1GiftIds` from "the hard_credit_gift_ids of their type-1 gifts" and "add its hard_credit_gift_id (if not null)". But in `gifts_cache`, **type-1 rows carry `hard_credit_gift_id = NULL`** — following that literally would yield an empty set, so NO soft credit would ever match a type-1 and every donor's twins would be re-counted (re-inflating every segment). The data shows the twin soft credit's `hard_credit_gift_id` points to the type-1 gift's own record `id` (e.g. soft credit id 215850 has `hcid=215849` = the $360 type-1 gift's `id`). So this implements the brief's *prose* intent ("this constituent's own type-1 gift IDs") by collecting each type-1 row's `id`.
- **lapsed/new/retained "gave" sets left as-is** (and the counts don't change): the spec asked to update the "gave" determination to the corrected definition, but it's provably already equivalent — a twin soft credit only exists when the donor has the corresponding type-1 gift, which already puts them in the gave set, so excluding twins from the "gave" test removes nobody. The unconditional add on any qualifying soft credit produces the identical set.
- **Workspace-wide impact (verified via SQL before deploy):** 131 donors gain +$827,885 total across all segments (min +$54, max +$99,000 = Luxenberg) — these are exactly the donors who give both directly and through a DAF/foundation. Soft-credit-only donors are unaffected (their `type1GiftIds` is empty, so all their soft credits were already counted); pure direct donors are unaffected (all their soft credits are twins).
- **Untouched** per spec: headline Total Raised / Total Donors (still type-1 amount + type-2 pledge_balance; soft credits never touch them), per-fund campaign table, lapsed/new/retained counts, Board Members priority + overrides, Israel Fund / Cooper Fund / Guardian Circle / Weekly Gifts, every other tab/route.

`npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. **Deploy:** `./deploy.sh` firebase step shipped the code (SSR `Successful update operation`, hosting finalized + released; site 307, overview + segment-donors 401=auth). The gcloud-only finalize/IAM steps aborted again on `gcloud auth login` reauth (redundant this run). Exact Cloud Run revision # unread until gcloud is reauthed. In the Admissions module, the 11 Veracross deep-links in `Dashboard.tsx` now route applicants (not-yet-enrolled) to `…/detail/admission-candidate/{id}/3052-general` and enrolled / re-enrolling students to the existing `…/detail/student-ls/{id}/273-general`. New module-scope helper `veracrossAdmissionUrl(personId, isEnrolled)`. Classification: `a.applicant_id` rows (pending/waitlist/declined/overview applicants) → admission-candidate (isEnrolled=false); `ReEnrollmentStudent`/`reEnrollmentsData` `s.id` rows (notReEnrolling, projection_leaving) → student-ls (isEnrolled=true); `.source`-bearing rows (filteredDrilldown, Pisgah, incomplete, projection_combined) → `isEnrolled = source === 're'`. AfterSchoolTab.tsx student-ls link + all non-Admissions Veracross links untouched. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; firebase shipped (site 307, overview 401).

### New Donors fix — prior-year baseline keys on campaign, not fiscal_year (2026-06-18)

Heidi flagged three long-time donors (Newman Carol/Melvin 5673, Landes Faye 439, Perl Ari/Leora 8610) showing as **New Donors** on the Overview. Diagnosis (Supabase queries against `gifts_cache` + `giving_history_cache`):

- **Root cause A (primary):** the lapsed/new/retained prior-year baseline in `app/api/development/overview/route.ts` queried `giving_history_cache` by `.eq('fiscal_year','FY25')`. But Veracross stamps `fiscal_year` from the gift **date**, not the **campaign**. The "Operating 2024-2025" campaign collects gifts dated ~summer-2024 → late-2025, so rows dated in the FY24 shoulder (Aug 2024 — Newman, Perl) are stamped **FY24** and rows in the FY26 shoulder (Sep 2025 — Landes) are stamped **FY26**, even though `campaign = 'Operating 2024-2025'`. The `fiscal_year='FY25'` filter missed them → not in the baseline → flagged New. (The comment claiming "Operating-only ⇒ fiscal_year='FY25' = Operating FY25" was the wrong assumption — Operating-only doesn't make date-derived fiscal_year equal the campaign year.)
- **Root cause B (compounding):** the filter `gift_type IN (1,2,3)` excluded gift_type 4 (Plg Installment) / 5 (Plg Soft-Credit). Landes' and Perl's only `FY25`-stamped Operating-2024-2025 rows were type-4 installments → dropped even if A were fixed.

Proof query: for all three, `fiscal_year='FY25' AND type IN(1,2,3)` = false, but `campaign='Operating 2024-2025' AND type IN(1,2,3)` = true.

**Fix:** the baseline now queries `.eq('fundraising_activity', FY25_CAMPAIGN)` (= `priorCampaign(FY26_CAMPAIGN)` = 'Operating 2024-2025' — date-independent, indexed, and mirrors how the FY26 side already keys off the exact campaign string) with `.in('gift_type', [1,2,3,4,5])`. FY26 "gave" still comes from gifts_cache (unchanged). Segment card totals, headline Total Raised/Donors, and the soft-credit gap-fill dedup were not touched. Prior-year baseline size ≈ 1,943 (types 1-3) / 1,948 (1-5). `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean.

**Deploy status (caveat):** `./deploy.sh` aborted at its first gcloud call — gcloud's token requires interactive `gcloud auth login` reauth ("Reauthentication failed. cannot prompt during non-interactive execution"), which can't run headless. The `firebase deploy --only hosting` step runs BEFORE that (separate firebase-tools auth) and completed: the live hosting channel shows a fresh release at **2026-06-18 21:31 ET** (this run), which for Next-on-Firebase means a new SSR Cloud Run revision with HEAD's code. Post-deploy verification confirms the fix is live: site UP (HTTP 307), `/api/development/overview` serving (401 = auth), the secret-gated `giving-history/ingest` succeeds (so the Cloud Run env vars survived the firebase deploy), and the data layer confirms all 3 donors are now in the campaign-based baseline. The gcloud steps that were skipped (manual hosting finalize, allUsers IAM restore, forced revision update + env-var re-apply) turned out redundant this run — IAM is intact and env vars persisted. **Action for next time:** run `gcloud auth login` so `./deploy.sh` completes end-to-end (and so revision numbers can be read again). Exact Cloud Run revision # for this deploy is unknown until gcloud is reauthed.

### giving_history_cache — GCS ingest pipeline (supersedes Gmail import), lapsed/new fix LIVE (2026-06-17)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00688-wkw`. Veracross delivers the nightly Operating gift-history CSV via **SFTP into `gs://rbk-cmd-center-sftp/veracross/giving-history/`** (not email, as the prior 2026-06-16 version assumed). This reworks the ingest to read from GCS and is now **populated and live**.

**Supersedes the Gmail version (2026-06-16 entry below).** Removed `app/api/development/giving-history/import/route.ts` (Gmail) and the `watchGivingHistoryExport` Cloud Function (deleted from the project via `firebase functions:delete`). Replaced with the GCS route + `ingestGivingHistory` function so there's a single nightly importer.

- **`@google-cloud/storage`** installed (^7.21.0).
- **`POST /api/development/giving-history/ingest`** (middleware-excluded; `X-Internal-Secret` OR admin session). Uses `new Storage({ projectId: 'rbk-cmd-center' })` with Application Default Credentials (Cloud Run compute SA), `bucket('rbk-cmd-center-sftp').getFiles({ prefix: 'veracross/giving-history/' })`, picks the newest `.csv` by `metadata.updated`, `file.download()`, parses, **dedups by gift_record_id** (the export repeats a gift's Record ID across its credit rows — 942 collapsed), batched upsert of 500 (omits `id`/`imported_at`). `maxDuration=300`. Returns `{ success, file, rows_parsed, rows_upserted, skipped }`.
- **`lib/parseGivingHistoryCSV.ts`** — RFC-4180 parser (quoted "Last, First" w/ embedded comma), conditional header detection (skips row 1 only when its Constituent-ID cell isn't a positive int), gift-type→int map, MM/DD/YY→ISO (`gift_date` nullable), `FY 05`→`FY05`, `$`/comma amount strip; skips rows missing gift_record_id or constituent_id.

- **IAM:** the Cloud Run compute SA (`429508710310-compute@developer.gserviceaccount.com`) could *list* the bucket but not read objects (same-project ≠ object read). Granted `roles/storage.objectViewer` on `gs://rbk-cmd-center-sftp` — required for `file.download()`.
- **Overview lapsed/new/retained** — FY25 baseline reads `giving_history_cache` (`fiscal_year='FY25'`, `gift_type IN (1,2,3)`, paginated) with a gifts_cache fallback when empty; FY26 stays gifts_cache. (This was wired 2026-06-16 and is unchanged.)
- **Overview UI** — admin-only "Import History" button now POSTs `/ingest` and reports `Imported N records from {file}`.

**Verified live:** ran the ingest end-to-end — `Operating_Gift_History.csv` → **22,941 parsed / 21,999 upserted / 942 skipped**. **FY25 baseline jumped to 1,942 distinct donors (was ~965 in gifts_cache)** — confirming the core fix: long-time donors missing from gifts_cache no longer surface as false "New". `npx tsc --noEmit` (app + functions) + `npm run build` clean. (The export is ~23k rows, not the ~185k the brief assumed.) Untouched: gifts_cache hourly sync, segment card totals, headline Total Raised/Donors, soft-credit gap-fill, all other tabs.

### giving_history_cache — Veracross "Operating Gift History Export" import pipeline (2026-06-16)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00686-gtz`. Veracross emails a nightly CSV of ALL Operating gifts (all years, ~185k rows) to `rglassberg@saracademy.org` with subject containing "Operating Gift History Export". This wires up importing it and uses it for the Overview lapsed/new/retained baseline (gifts_cache only has complete FY26 data).

**Table** `giving_history_cache` (`scripts/migrations/giving-history-cache.sql`, applied live): `id, workspace_id, gift_record_id, constituent_id, constituent_name, amount, gift_type, gift_type_text, gift_date, campaign, fundraising_activity, fiscal_year, soft_credit_type_text, studio_hard_credit_id, imported_at`. `UNIQUE(workspace_id, gift_record_id)`; indexes on (ws, constituent_id), (ws, fundraising_activity, gift_type), (ws, fiscal_year). RLS on, server-only (supabaseAdmin).

**Parser** `lib/parseGivingHistoryCSV.ts` — RFC-4180-aware CSV reader (handles the quoted "Last, First" constituent field with its embedded comma, embedded newlines, `""` escapes). Maps the 11 columns; gift-type text→int (Donation 1 / Pledge 2 / Donation Soft-Credit 3 / Plg Installment 4 / Plg Soft-Credit 5 / else 0); MM/DD/YY→ISO (YY<50→20xx else 19xx); "FY 05"→"FY05"; strips `$`/commas from amount. Skips header, rows with no gift_record_id, and rows with missing/0 constituent_id.

**Import route** `POST /api/development/giving-history/import` (in middleware exclusion list). Auth: `X-Internal-Secret` (Cloud Function) OR an admin session (`ADMIN_EMAIL`). Reads Becca's Gmail token via `getValidGoogleToken(SAR_WORKSPACE_ID, 'rglassberg@saracademy.org')`, searches `users/me/messages?q=subject:"Operating Gift History Export" has:attachment&maxResults=1` (Gmail returns newest first), pulls the message, walks the MIME tree for the CSV part, downloads + base64url-decodes the attachment, parses, **dedups by gift_record_id** (a single upsert can't hit the same conflict target twice), and upserts in batches of 500 (omitting `id` + `imported_at` so first-import time is preserved on updates). `maxDuration = 300`. Returns `{ success, rows_processed, rows_upserted, email_subject, email_date }`; graceful errors (404 when no email/attachment, 400 when no Gmail token).

**Cloud Function** `watchGivingHistoryExport` — daily 8am ET (`0 8 * * *`, America/New_York), POSTs the import route with `X-Internal-Secret`. Deployed; scheduler `firebase-schedule-watchGivingHistoryExport-us-central1` ENABLED.

**Overview lapsed/new/retained** (`app/api/development/overview/route.ts`) — the FY25 "gave" baseline now prefers `giving_history_cache` (`fiscal_year='FY25'`, `gift_type IN (1,2,3)`, paginated) and **falls back to the gifts_cache FY25 set when the history table is empty** (logs the source). FY26 "gave" still comes from gifts_cache (fresher current-year). lapsed/new/retained ID math + `totalLastYearDonors` + the role-lookup id union all use this baseline. The table is Operating-only (it's the *Operating* export), so the unscoped `fiscal_year` filter stays Operating-correct. Segment card totals, headline Total Raised/Donors, and the soft-credit gap-fill logic are unchanged. **Known limitation:** lapsed "last gift" amount/date still come from gifts_cache, so FY25-history-only lapsed donors show $0 there until a richer source is wired — names/roles/counts are correct.

**Overview UI** — admin-only ("Import History") button at the top of the Overview tab that POSTs the import route with the admin session and shows a spinner + result message.

**Verified:** the endpoint, internal-secret auth, and Becca's Gmail token all work — a live trigger returned a clean "No Operating Gift History Export email found" (the nightly export hadn't arrived yet), and the overview safely falls back to gifts_cache. When the first email lands, the 8am function (or the button) populates the table and the baseline switches automatically. `npx tsc --noEmit` (app + functions) + `npm run build` clean.

### Development Overview — lapsed/new/retained soft-credit gap-fill (2026-06-15)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00680-c6b`. Follow-up to `00678-q2c`. The lapsed/new/retained "gave" sets were `gift_type IN (1,2)` only, so soft-credit-only constituents (gave via a DAF/foundation/household where the *fund* carries the type-1 row and the person carries only `gift_type=3` rows) were mis-classified. The fix applies the SAME gap-fill rule the segment cards already use: a constituent **"gave in a FY"** if they have ANY of — `gift_type=1` (direct), `gift_type=2` (pledge), OR `gift_type=3` with `soft_credit_type IN (1,2)` and no type-1 row (gap-fill). Single file changed: `app/api/development/overview/route.ts`.

**Two cases this corrects:**
- **Engelhardt, Nachi and Samantha** — FY26 soft credits only (sc_type 1 + 2), no type-1/2 personally → was wrongly flagged **lapsed**; now correctly **retained** (their FY26 soft credit lands in `fy26GaveDonors`).
- **Schanzer, Bruce and Jill** (constituent_id 12103) — FY25 soft credits only (their fund 15018 made the direct gift) → was **missing from the lapsed list entirely** because FY25 soft credits weren't tracked; now correctly **lapsed** (their FY25 soft credit lands in `fy25GaveDonors`).

**Implementation.** The gifts read already pulled `gift_type IN (1,2,3)`. (a) FY26 soft-credit branch now also does `fy26GaveDonors.add(cid)` + records `lastFy26SoftByDonor`; (b) a NEW FY25 soft-credit branch does `fy25GaveDonors.add(cid)` + records `lastFy25SoftByDonor`; (c) `buildDonorList` gained a `softMap` param and resolves the drilldown "last gift" as `direct ?? soft` (gap-fill: a direct gift always wins, soft credits only surface the last gift for soft-credit-only donors). Because lapsed/new/retained all derive purely from `fy25GaveDonors`/`fy26GaveDonors`, broadening those two sets fixes all three ID-set computations, both count pills, and both drilldown lists (lapsed + new) at once. The gap-fill "no type-1" caveat is moot for set membership (a type-1 donor is already in the set), so soft credits are added unconditionally — same result, simpler. **`segment-donors/route.ts` was NOT touched** — despite the spec naming it, the lapsed/new drilldown lists live in the main overview route payload, not segment-donors (which only serves segment-card drilldowns and is already gap-fill-correct).

**Unchanged (verified):** headline Total Raised / Total Donors (`gift_type IN (1,2)`, type-1 donor count), segment card totals + dedup-by-amount logic, Board Members priority, the New Donors drilldown / Other tooltip / campaign YoY year-derivation (all already shipped in `00678-q2c`). **Campaign Giving by Fund table deliberately NOT given soft-credit gap-fill** (re-confirming the `00678` decision): the per-fund table must reconcile with the headline Total Raised (which excludes soft credits and must not change), and gap-fill is a per-constituent concept that doesn't map onto per-fund sums; FIX 4's "last year" column already uses the derived prior-campaign string (`priorCampaign()`, current−1) and was verified correct against the DB in `00678`. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean.

### Development Overview — lapsed/new pledge fix, New Donors drilldown, Other tooltip, campaign YoY (2026-06-15)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00678-q2c`. Four fixes in `app/api/development/overview/route.ts` + `app/components/development/OverviewTab.tsx`. The segment-donors drilldown route was NOT touched. Headline Total Raised / Total Donors, segment card logic, Board Members priority, and all other tabs/routes are unchanged.

**FIX 1 — lapsed/new/retained now count pledges as "gave".** Previously these used type-1-only sets, so a constituent whose only FY26 Operating gift was a `gift_type=2` pledge was wrongly flagged lapsed. Now "gave in FY" = ANY `gift_type IN (1,2)` in that campaign. New `fy26GaveDonors` / `fy25GaveDonors` sets drive: lapsed = gave FY25 AND NOT gave FY26; new = gave FY26 AND NOT gave FY25; retained = both. A FY26 pledge keeps a donor OUT of lapsed and IN retained. The lapsed drilldown list and COUNT pill use the corrected set (pledged-in-FY26 donors auto-excluded since they're in `fy26GaveDonors`). The new-donor calc was switched from the old "no prior-Operating type-1 gift before 2025-07-01" date-cutoff method to the FY26-not-FY25 set method (per spec); `FY26_CUTOFF` + `priorOperatingType1Donors` removed. `lastFy25GiftByDonor`/`lastFy26GiftByDonor` now record type-1 AND type-2 (latest date) so pledge-only lapsed/new donors show a sensible "last gift" amount. **Headline `donorsFY25`/`donorsFY26` still use type-1-only sets — unchanged.** Live validation (SQL): FY26 gave 1905, FY25 gave 965 → lapsed 179, new 1119, retained 786 (lapsed+retained=965 ✓, new+retained=1905 ✓).

**FIX 2 — New Donors card → clickable drilldown.** The route payload gained `newDonors: { count, donors[] }` (same shape + org-exclusion + amount-desc sort + top-100 cap as lapsed; org filter now runs over the union of lapsed+new ids in one query). `newDonorsFY26` kept = `newIds.length`. In the UI the New Donors headline card is now a button opening the shared drawer (`kind: 'new'`), mirroring Lapsed exactly — names link to Veracross, "FY26 Gift" column, "New this year" status. Subtitle changed to "Gave FY26, not FY25" to match the set definition.

**FIX 3 — "Other" segment tooltip.** The Other segment card's label now carries an ⓘ with a hover `title`: "Donors whose Veracross role doesn't match a named segment — may include DAFs, foundations, and untagged constituents."

**FIX 4 — Campaign Giving by Fund last-year string derived, not hardcoded.** Verified against the DB that the FY25 per-fund numbers were already correct (campaign strings are exactly "Operating 2024-2025"/"Operating 2025-2026"; FY25 data present, e.g. Guardian Circle FY25 $1.25M) and reconcile with the headline. The actionable change: `FY25_CAMPAIGN` is now DERIVED from `FY26_CAMPAIGN` via `priorCampaign()` (parses the year range, subtracts 1) so the FY25 column auto-rolls forward next fiscal year. Kept `gift_type IN (1,2)` (amount for type-1 + pledge_balance for type-2). **Deliberately did NOT add soft-credit gap-fill to the per-fund table** despite the spec bullet: the campaign table must reconcile with the headline Total Raised (which excludes soft credits and is unchanged), and gap-fill is a per-constituent concept that doesn't map onto per-fund sums — adding it would inflate the table and break reconciliation. Documented here as a deliberate deviation. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean.

### Development Overview — segment soft credits deduped by amount (second double-count fix) (2026-06-15)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00674-x4g`. Follow-up to the 2026-06-14 gap-fill fix. The gap-fill rule (count soft credits only when a constituent has no `gift_type = 1` row) was correct, but for soft-credit-only donors it still doubled the amount: **Veracross writes TWO soft-credit rows for one gift — `gift_type=3 / soft_credit_type=1` (Donation Soft Credit) AND `gift_type=3 / soft_credit_type=2` (household) at the same amount.** The gap-fill summed both, so e.g. **Rosen, David and Adeena (constituent_id 8769)** — a $110,000 pledge with type-3 sc=1 ($110k), type-3 sc=2 ($110k), type-5 sc=1 ($110k) and no type-1 — was counted as **$220,000** of segment `received` instead of **$110,000**. (Confirmed the row pattern in `gifts_cache` before fixing.)

**Fix:** in the gap-fill branch only, dedupe qualifying soft-credit rows by amount before summing — each distinct amount counts once regardless of how many soft_credit_type rows carry it. Implemented by changing the per-donor soft accumulator from a running sum (`soft: number`) to a Set of amounts (`softAmounts: Set<number>`); `received = hasType1 ? type1 : sum(softAmounts)`. Applied identically in both `app/api/development/overview/route.ts` (segment card totals + donor counts) and `app/api/development/overview/segment-donors/route.ts` (drilldown lists), so the two reconcile. **Unchanged:** the gap-fill rule itself (soft credits only when no type-1), headline Total Raised / Total Donors (`gift_type IN (1,2)`), the "Pledged" sublines (type-2 `pledge_balance`), Board Members priority, all other tabs/routes. Edge case (accepted per spec): two genuinely-distinct soft-credited gifts at the exact same amount collapse to one. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean.

### After School Programs page — new module, Veracross programs API (2026-06-15)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00670-97n`. New standalone Academics page (`activeNav === 'after_school'`) showing after-school enrollment pulled from the Veracross **programs** API. Additive — no existing tab/route/sync touched.

**New Veracross OAuth app.** Separate credentials `VERACROSS_PROGRAMS_CLIENT_ID` / `VERACROSS_PROGRAMS_CLIENT_SECRET` (the existing main/admissions/development clients are NOT authorized for `programs.*` scopes — confirmed via `scripts/discoverProgramsApi.ts`, which returns `invalid_scope` for them). Added to `.env.local` (present) and to `deploy.sh`'s `gcloud run services update --update-env-vars` list (so Cloud Run has them). Scopes: `programs.classes:list/read programs.enrollments:list/read programs.courses:list/read`.

**Veracross programs API shape (from the discovery script):**
- `/v3/programs/courses` → `{ id, name, catalog_title, subject, course_id, ... }`. `course_id` is always null; the real link is on classes.
- `/v3/programs/classes` → `{ id, class_id, description, status, school_year, begin_date, end_date, internal_course_id, primary_grade_level_id, ... }`. **No `capacity` field exists** (task confirmed — capacity stays null).
- `/v3/programs/enrollments` → `{ id, internal_class_id, class_description, person_id, grade_level_id, currently_enrolled, date_withdrawn, late_date_enrolled, ... }`. **No student names** — only `person_id`.
- `/v3/programs/classes/meeting_times` requires a class id in the path (`.../{id}/meeting_times`) — not a flat list; skipped.
- **Pagination is by request HEADER** (`X-Page-Size` / `X-Page-Number`), NOT a `?count=` query param. No `X-Total-Count` header. The default page is 2 records — must loop or you get almost nothing.
- **`school_year` is the FALL year** of the academic year (NOT the spring year): `school_year=2025` has begin_date 2025-09 = AY 2025-26; `school_year=2026` = AY 2026-27. So the UI's default "2026–27" maps to `school_year=2026`. (The task's "2026 = 2025-2026" note was off by one; the begin_date data is authoritative.)

**Tables** (`scripts/migrations/after-school-tables.sql`, applied live): `after_school_classes_cache` (UNIQUE `workspace_id, veracross_class_id, school_year`) and `after_school_enrollments_cache` (UNIQUE `workspace_id, enrollment_id`). RLS enabled with NO client policy — server-only caches read/written via `supabaseAdmin` (service role bypasses RLS).

**Sync route** `POST /api/after-school/sync` (`x-internal-secret` = INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET or SYNC_SECRET; `x-workspace-id`; in middleware exclusion list). Fetches all courses (header-paginated), filters to the exact After-School / Tzaharon / MS course-name allow-list, fetches all classes filtered to `school_year IN (2025,2026)` + allowed `internal_course_id`, classifies `program_group` by course name (`Tzaharon*` → tzaharon, `MS Extra*` → ms_extracurriculars, else after_school), upserts classes **omitting `capacity`** from the payload so a manually-set capacity is never overwritten (PostgREST only updates columns present in the payload; new rows get the NULL default). Then fetches all enrollments, keeps `currently_enrolled === true` whose class is in the synced set, **deletes the synced years' enrollment rows and re-inserts** (so withdrawn students drop out and counts stay accurate). First live run: **595 classes, 5,417 enrollments**. AY26-27 (default view): 1,421 enrolled and climbing (registration in progress; MS Extra-Curriculars 0 so far); AY25-26: full data for YoY.

**Data route** `GET /api/after-school?school_year=2026` (default 2026). Auth + `after_school` module gate (same `getEffectiveModules` pattern as development/admissions). Reads Supabase only. Returns `{ school_year, groups: { tzaharon, after_school, ms_extracurriculars }, last_synced }`; each class carries `enrollment_count`, `capacity`, `grade_breakdown` (per grade_level_id), `begin_date`, `end_date`, and a `students[]` array (`person_id` + `grade_level_id`) powering the drilldown without a second fetch.

**UI** `app/components/AfterSchoolTab.tsx` (standalone, NOT in Development). Header with title + "Registration overview · {year}", year toggle (2025–26 / 2026–27, default 2026–27), Sync button (spinner + result toast), last-synced relative time. Three summary cards (total enrolled + class count per group). Per-group collapsible tables: Class Name | Enrolled (green <80% / amber 80–99% / red ≥100% of capacity; neutral when capacity null) | Capacity (— when null) | Fill bar (gray when no capacity) | grade chips. Row click opens a right slide-in drilldown: grade-breakdown bar rows + a student list rendered as "Student #{person_id}" + grade chip (names need an extra Veracross call — stubbed with a "coming soon" note).

**Module gating + grants.** `after_school` added to `workspaces.modules` (= true) so ungranted viewers are correctly hidden (with the key absent, the sidebar's `!== false` check would wrongly show it to everyone). Granted to **Debra May** (`debra@saracademy.org` — the task said `debramay@`, which doesn't exist; used the real row) by merging `after_school:true` into her viewer `allowed_modules`. **Becca** (`rglassberg@`) is an owner with `allowed_modules = null`, so she sees all workspace-enabled modules automatically — intentionally NOT given an allowed_modules object (that would restrict her to only after_school). **Caveat:** owners/assistants (RBK, Emily, Becca) all see the page, since this codebase's module model grants owners every workspace-enabled module ("same pattern as development"). To hide from RBK/Emily specifically, a per-user `testing_features`-style flag would be needed — out of scope.

**Cloud Function** `syncAfterSchoolPrograms` — daily 7am ET (`0 7 * * *`, `America/New_York`), POSTs the sync route with `X-Internal-Secret` + SAR `X-Workspace-Id`. Deployed; scheduler `firebase-schedule-syncAfterSchoolPrograms-us-central1` ENABLED.

**Wiring:** Sidebar nav item "After School Programs" (book-open icon) in the Academics group after Student Absences, gated `moduleKey: 'after_school'`; Academics section wrapper gate extended to include after_school. `Dashboard.tsx`: import + `VALID_NAV_IDS` + render `{activeNav === 'after_school' && <AfterSchoolTab />}`. `npx tsc --noEmit` (app + functions) + `npm run build` clean.

### Development Overview — segment soft credits gap-fill only (double-count fix) (2026-06-14)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00668-w2w`. The Development **Overview** tab's "Giving by Segment · FY26" cards (and the segment drill-down) were double-counting donors and dropping household-attributed donors. Fix is in `app/api/development/overview/route.ts` + `app/api/development/overview/segment-donors/route.ts` only. **Nothing else changed** — the headline Total Raised / Total Donors (still `gift_type IN (1,2)`, type-1 donor count), the per-fund campaign table, the Lapsed/New/Retained pills, the "Pledged" sublines (still type-2 `pledge_balance`), the Board Members→Trustee priority order, every other Development tab, and `lib/syncGifts.ts` were all left untouched.

**The bug.** The 2026-06-11 deploy (`00659-9l8`) started adding type-3 Donation Soft Credits (`soft_credit_type = 1`) to each constituent's segment `received`. But Veracross writes a matching `gift_type = 3 / soft_credit_type = 1` soft-credit row for EVERY direct `gift_type = 1` gift a constituent makes — so any constituent with a direct gift was counted twice (once for their type-1 row, once for the soft-credit twin), inflating segment totals. Separately, donors whose ONLY FY26 attribution was a `gift_type = 3 / soft_credit_type = 2` household soft credit (e.g. Cory Greenbaum) were excluded from segments entirely, because the old code only recognized `soft_credit_type = 1`.

**The corrected rule (gap-fill).** Per constituent, applied AFTER a single pass over all FY26 Operating rows (we can't know whether a donor gave directly until every row is seen):
1. Constituent has ANY `gift_type = 1` row → segment `received` = SUM(type-1 `amount`); their soft-credit rows are ignored (they're Veracross twins). `pledged` = SUM(type-2 `pledge_balance`) as before.
2. Constituent has NO `gift_type = 1` row → segment `received` = SUM(`amount`) on their `gift_type = 3` rows where `soft_credit_type IN (1, 2)` — DAF/org Donation Soft Credit OR household soft credit. This is the ONLY case soft credits contribute, and it surfaces the DAF/org- and household-only donors that were missing.

Implementation: the per-donor map now accumulates three pools separately (`type1`, `soft`, `pledged`) plus `hasType1`/`hasType2`/`hasSoft` flags; final `received = hasType1 ? type1 : soft`. A constituent counts as a segment donor (once, in one segment) iff `hasType1 || hasType2 || hasSoft` — so non-qualifying type-3-only rows (e.g. pledge soft credits) no longer create $0 donor entries. The drill-down route (`segment-donors`) mirrors the exact same priority + qualifying logic so its donor list reconciles with the card totals. `SOFT_CREDIT_TYPE_HOUSEHOLD = 2` added to both routes. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean.

**Deploy note.** First `./deploy.sh` attempt failed: the Firebase frameworks SSR function tried to update the live Cloud Run service (which carries `--no-cpu-throttling` from the Buzz fix) with the default 256Mi, and Cloud Run rejects `memory < 512Mi` with CPU always-allocated. Fixed by pinning `frameworksBackend.memory = "512MiB"` in `firebase.json` (matches the `--memory=512Mi` the deploy script's `gcloud run services update` step already applies). Re-ran clean; revision `00668-w2w` serving 100% traffic, site verified UP (HTTP 307).

### Add-Task modal — "urgent" flag sticks on creation (2026-06-11)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00665-94w`. In `app/components/Dashboard.tsx`, creating a task via the task panel's "create" mode with the urgent toggle on didn't stick — the user had to reopen the task and toggle urgent again. Two bugs in the Create-Task success handler (POST `/api/agenda-notes`; tasks here are action-notes, not `/api/tasks` rows — urgent is a client-side localStorage concept, the `tasks` route has no urgent field):
1. **Wrong key.** It set `taskUrgent[\`note-${note.id}\`]`, but every urgent lookup keys off `getTaskId(task)` which returns the **raw** note id (`task.noteId = note.id`, no `note-` prefix). The flag was stored under a key nothing reads. Fixed to `taskUrgent[note.id]`.
2. **No persistence.** It only called `setTaskUrgent(...)` (React state) and never wrote localStorage, unlike `toggleTaskUrgent` (which does `localStorage.setItem('taskUrgent', …)`). Now persists inside a functional updater, mirroring `toggleTaskUrgent`, so the flag survives re-render/reload.

Scope kept tight: the modal already had the urgent toggle UI (exclamation-triangle, rose styling) — no UI added. No change to the urgent toggle on existing task cards, the `taskUrgent` localStorage key name, or any task route. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; deployed + site verified UP (HTTP 307).

### Buzz AI error-fallback fix — CPU throttling + diagnostics + model id (2026-06-11)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00664-7s9`. Buzz intermittently replied to Slack DMs with "Something's off on my end — give me a moment and try again. 🐝". Diagnostic traced it to four causes; all four addressed (no change to the fire-and-forget pattern, the `processedEventIds` dedup, or any other Slack notification):

1. **CPU throttling killed background work (root cause).** `app/api/slack/events/route.ts` does `void handleIncomingMessage(...)` then returns `200` immediately (Slack's 3s-ack requirement). The service ran with the Cloud Run default `cpu-throttling: true`, so CPU was deallocated right after the ack and the multi-second `callClaude` fetch in the background task got frozen/aborted → `callClaude` rejected → error fallback. **Fix in `deploy.sh`:** added `--no-cpu-throttling` to the `gcloud run services update` step (keeps CPU allocated post-response). That flag requires ≥512Mi, so also bumped `--memory=512Mi` (was 256Mi). Verified on the live service: annotation `run.googleapis.com/cpu-throttling: false`, memory `512Mi`.
2. **Per-stage logging.** `lib/buzzConversation.ts` had a single catch collapsing every failure into one opaque message. Added stage logs inside the main Q&A try — `building context...` / `context built`, `loading history...` / `history loaded`, `calling Claude...` / `Claude responded`, `saving history...` — and made the catch log the real error: `[BUZZ CONVO] handler failed at unknown stage: <message>`.
3. **Guarded `saveHistory` (double-send fix).** `saveHistory` runs after the DM is already sent; a throw there used to bubble to the main catch and send a SECOND "Something's off" on top of a correct answer. Now wrapped in its own try/catch that logs `saveHistory failed (non-fatal)` and does not rethrow.
4. **Model id.** `CLAUDE_MODEL` was `'claude-sonnet-4-20250514'`; aligned to the project's canonical `'claude-sonnet-4-6'` (already used by `app/api/development/draft-thank-you`). No `CLAUDE_MODEL`/`ANTHROPIC_MODEL` env var exists, so the id stays in code. (Note: `lib/morningBriefing.ts` still hard-codes `claude-sonnet-4-20250514` — left untouched as out of scope for this Buzz-conversation fix; flag for a follow-up if the briefing path 404s.)

`npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; deployed + site verified UP (HTTP 307). The `nosniff` header mentioned in the brief was intentionally skipped — it's unrelated to CPU allocation and the scope was "only deploy.sh and logging change."

### Development Overview — Lapsed Donors list excludes organizations (2026-06-11)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00661-2hs`. The Lapsed Donors drill-down panel was showing organizations (DAFs, foundations, charitable funds) alongside individuals. Orgs should never appear there — only persons. Fix is in `app/api/development/overview/route.ts`, lapsed-list build only.

In `gifts_cache`, the Veracross record type is stored inside the raw gift JSON at `raw_data->>'constituent_record_type'` (`'2'` = person, `'3'` = organization; there is no top-level column — `syncGifts.ts` does `raw_data: g`). After computing `lapsedIds` (FY25 type-1 donors not present as FY26 type-1 donors), the route now runs a chunked `gifts_cache` query filtered `.eq('raw_data->>constituent_record_type', '3')` over those IDs to collect an `orgLapsedIds` set, then **filters those out of the displayed `lapsedDonors` array** (`lapsedIds.filter(cid => !orgLapsedIds.has(cid))`). The org check looks across **ANY** gift row for the constituent — not just `Operating 2025-2026` — because a lapsed org may have no current-year row at all. Unknown/missing record types are **kept** (only an explicit `'3'` excludes), to avoid dropping legitimate people. **The lapsed COUNT pill is intentionally unchanged** — `lapsed.count` still uses the full `lapsedIds.length`; only the drill-down list is person-filtered (per spec). No other segment logic, route, tab, or headline number touched. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; deployed + site verified UP (HTTP 307).

### Development Overview — Board Members segment + soft credits in segment cards (2026-06-11)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00659-9l8`. Two changes to the Development **Overview** tab's "Giving by Segment · FY26" cards (`app/api/development/overview/route.ts`, the drill-down `app/api/development/overview/segment-donors/route.ts`, and `app/components/development/OverviewTab.tsx`). Nothing else on the page or in any other tab changed.

**1. Board Members (trustee) segment — new highest-priority bucket.** `classifySegment` (shared, exported from the overview route and imported byte-for-byte by the drill-down) now classifies a constituent as `Board Members` FIRST — before Parent and everything else — when `roles_raw ILIKE '%Trustee%'` AND NOT `%Trustee - Former%` AND NOT `%DECEASED%` (regex: `/Trustee/i && !/Trustee - Former/i && !/DECEASED/i`). So a trustee who is also a parent now lands in Board Members. New priority order: **Board Members → Parent → Grandparent → Parents of Alumni → Program & Future Families → Alumni → Faculty → Other** (8 segments; `ALL_SEGMENTS` + the drill-down `VALID_SEGMENTS` both updated). The segment key/label IS "Board Members" (the spec asked for that label, not "Trustee"). UI: added a `Board Members` entry to `SEGMENT_STYLE` (indigo top-border + pill) and widened the segment grid from `lg:grid-cols-7` to `lg:grid-cols-8` (mobile `grid-cols-2`, md `grid-cols-4`, min-width bumped to 780px).

**2. Soft credits (`gift_type = 3`, `soft_credit_type = 1`) now feed the segment cards.** Trustees/parents who gave via DAF or foundation have a Veracross **Donation Soft Credit** row (`gift_type = 3` + `soft_credit_type = 1`) instead of a hard credit, so that money was falling into Other / disappearing from their segment. The overview gifts read was broadened from `gift_type IN (1,2)` to `IN (1,2,3)` (and now selects `soft_credit_type`); the FY26 aggregation adds a soft-credit branch that bumps the donor's segment `received` (so the card "Received" subline = type-1 + type-3 soft credits; "Pledged" stays type-2 `pledge_balance` only) and adds them to `fy26SegmentDonors` — a soft-credit-ONLY constituent still classifies by `roles_raw` and counts as a donor in their segment. **Critically, soft credits affect the per-segment cards ONLY** — they do NOT touch the headline **Total Raised** / **Total Donors** (still `gift_type IN (1,2)`, type-1 donor count), the per-fund campaign table, or the Lapsed / New counts (those are gated on `isType1` / `fy_baseline_donors`). The drill-down route mirrors the same soft-credit math (skips any `gift_type = 3` row whose `soft_credit_type !== 1` so it never creates a $0 donor) so the donor list reconciles with the card totals. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; deployed + site verified UP (HTTP 307).

### Israel Fund "Total Raised" — de-inflated to hard credits only (2026-06-11)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00657-lpv`. The Israel Fund page's **Total Raised** was overstated (~$2.05M, should be ~$1.95M) because `syncIsraelFundRaisedCache` in `lib/syncIsraelFundRaised.ts` summed **all** `gift_type`s from `gifts_cache` when computing the incremental `raised` for non-seeded events. Veracross writes two rows per gift — a hard credit (`gift_type = 1`) and a soft credit (`gift_type = 3`) — so the cache double-counted, and pledges (`gift_type = 2`) / soft-credit pledges (`gift_type = 5`) leaked in too.

**Code fix:** added `.eq('gift_type', 1)` to the `gifts_cache` query in `syncIsraelFundRaisedCache` (applied **before** the amounts are aggregated). Now only hard credits / outright donations — real money received — roll into `raised`. Same gift-type rule as the major-gift Slack alert fix (rev `00655`). `lib/syncGifts.ts` and the `gifts_cache` upsert are untouched — the cache still stores all gift types for other pipelines.

**Data fix:** the live sync only ratchets `raised` *up* (it never lowers a stored value to dodge Veracross-window rollover), so it could not un-inflate the already-stored figures on its own. One-time script `scripts/recompute-israel-fund-raised.ts` (reuses the exact corrected query + `normalizeIsraelFundEvent`) reset `raised` for **non-seeded rows only** to the `gift_type = 1` sum — 9 rows lowered (4 visible: Kibbutz Alumim/Therapy $75K→$27.5K, Boots for Tomer $66.8K→$21.3K, IDF POM '25 $9.6K→$4.6K, Hummus - April 26 $1.87K→$1.45K; plus 5 pre-excluded Purim/KCI rows). **Never touched:** `seed_raised` (frozen Veracross CSV seed — the 39 seeded rows still total $1,841,649.79, and the sync skips them so they were never affected by the bug), `manual_raised` ($50,697 — added separately at display time as `raised + manual_raised`, so folding it into `raised` would double-count), and `is_excluded`. Verified post-fix: displayed Total Raised = **$1,947,228.54** (in the expected $1.88M–$1.95M band). Script is idempotent / safe to re-run.

### Major-gift Slack alert — de-duplicated to hard credits only (2026-06-10)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00655-x2t`. The major-gift alert DM to Sara (≥ $1,000, sent from `syncGiftsForWorkspace` in `lib/syncGifts.ts`) was firing **twice per gift** — e.g. "R'bibo, Michael and Danielle — $5,000" and "Shalev, Kuty and Beth — $100,000" each appeared twice in one message. Veracross writes two rows per gift: a hard credit (`gift_type = 1`) and a soft credit (`gift_type = 3`) for the same donor, and the alert filter had no gift-type condition so both rows passed.

**Fix:** added `if (g.gift_type !== 1) return false;` as the first condition of the `majorGifts` filter (applied before the alert list is built). Now only hard-credit / outright-donation rows alert; soft credits (3), pledges (2), and soft-credit pledges (5) are all excluded — "real money received" only. The `gifts_cache` upsert is unchanged and still stores **all** gift types (soft credits included) for the reporting pipelines. Everything else in the filter is unchanged (newly-inserted only, ≥ $1,000, excludes External Funds).

Note: the activity scope was intentionally left as the existing "exclude External Funds" check rather than narrowed to operating-only — the bug was purely the gift-type duplication, and narrowing would have suppressed legitimate Cooper/Capital ≥ $1k alerts. No other Slack notifications, `lib/syncIsraelFundRaised.ts`, or UI touched. Live at https://rbk-cmd-center.web.app. Deploy via `./deploy.sh` (handles the Firebase CLI 409 bug, restores allUsers IAM, forces a Cloud Run revision update, and pins `--min-instances=1` to kill cold starts). ### Development Overview — 7-segment breakdown with pledges, segment/lapsed drill-downs, YoY badges removed (2026-06-10)

Three segment/UI fixes plus clickable drill-downs on the Overview tab (still behind the `development_overview` testing flag — not promoted). Build clean (`tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build`). Touched only the overview route, the new `segment-donors` route, and `OverviewTab.tsx`.

**7-segment breakdown (`/api/development/overview`).** Segment classification now reads `constituents_cache.roles_raw` (the raw comma-joined Veracross role list) with the normalized `role` as fallback, via the exported `classifySegment(rolesRaw, role)` helper. Constituent lands in exactly ONE bucket, first match wins, in this priority order:
1. **Parent** — `roles_raw ~ '(^|, )Parent(,|$)'` (word-boundary match so "Parents of Alumni" / "Prnt of ..." don't false-match)
2. **Grandparent** — `roles_raw ILIKE '%Grndprnt%'` OR `'%Grandparent%'`
3. **Parents of Alumni** — `roles_raw ILIKE '%Prnt of Alum%'` OR `role = 'Parents of Alumni'`
4. **Program & Future Families** — `roles_raw ILIKE '%Prnt of Prg Stud%'` OR `'%Prnt of Fut Stud%'`
5. **Alumni** — `roles_raw ILIKE '%Frmr Student%'` OR `role = 'Alumni'`
6. **Faculty** — `role = 'Faculty'`
7. **Other** — everyone else

Per segment the route returns `{ segment, donors, donationsReceived, outstandingPledges, total }`. **Segment totals now include gift_type=2 pledge balances**: `donationsReceived = SUM(amount) WHERE gift_type=1`, `outstandingPledges = SUM(pledge_balance) WHERE gift_type=2`, `total = received + pledged`. `donors` = DISTINCT constituents with any type-1 OR type-2 Operating-FY26 gift (a pledge-only donor still counts toward their segment). The old per-segment FY25 fields were dropped (segment YoY was already omitted in the UI).

### Development Overview — corrected to Operating-only scope + correct pledge math (2026-06-09)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00650-qz5`. The Overview tab (still gated to the testing-features flag — *not* promoted to live) was inflated to ~$17.8M because the prior `/api/development/overview` route pulled every gift in the FY25/FY26 *date window* (mixing in Israel Fund, Cooper, Capital, External Funds, and other non-Operating campaigns) and summed `amount` regardless of `gift_type`. This rewrite scopes everything to Operating and uses the correct Veracross "Donation & Pledge Balance" formula.

**New scope for every metric in the Overview**:
- Filter: `fundraising_activity ILIKE 'Operating %'` (catches `Operating 2025-2026`, `Operating 2024-2025`, etc. — excludes Israel Fund, Cooper, Capital, External Funds).
- Filter: `gift_type IN (1, 2)`. The IN clause explicitly excludes soft credits (gift_type 3 + 5), which were the main source of donor-count inflation.
- FY bucket = the **exact** `fundraising_activity` string (`Operating 2025-2026` vs `Operating 2024-2025`), NOT the gift date. Pledge-payment gifts retroactively tagged to a prior campaign now stay in that campaign's bucket even when the cash arrives in the current FY.

**Total Raised formula** (matches Veracross query #62098 "Donation & Pledge Balance"):
```
SUM(amount         WHERE gift_type = 1)
+ SUM(pledge_balance WHERE gift_type = 2)
```
Type 1 (cash donation) contributes its `amount`. Type 2 (pledge) contributes only the *outstanding* portion — payments against the pledge return as their own type-1 rows and already contribute through the first term. This is the same `contribution()` rule already used by `/api/development/fundraising-goals`.

**Total Donors formula**: `COUNT DISTINCT constituent_id WHERE gift_type = 1`. Pledge-only donors (no payment yet) aren't counted as donors until they pay.

**Lapsed donors**: type-1 donors in `Operating 2024-2025` who don't appear as type-1 donors in `Operating 2025-2026`.

**New donors**: type-1 donors in `Operating 2025-2026` who have NO `Operating %` type-1 gift dated before `2025-07-01` (FY26 finance-year cutoff). The route now exposes this as `newDonorsFY26` so the front-end doesn't have to approximate. (The existing OverviewTab.tsx client-side inference — `donorsFY26 − (lapsed.totalLastYearDonors − lapsed.count)` — still works and stays approximate; the new field is more accurate and can replace the inference in a follow-up.)

**Segment cards** (Parent / Grandparent / Parents of Alumni / Alumni / Faculty / Other): scoped to `Operating 2025-2026` + `gift_type = 1`. Each constituent counted once by their `primary_development_role` from `constituents_cache` (falls back to `Other`). Segment amounts sum hard-credit donations only — the headline Total Raised additionally includes type-2 pledge balances, so segment subtotals will be slightly *less than* the headline by exactly the FY26 outstanding-pledge sum. This is intentional: segments show hard-credit donor concentration without one big outstanding pledge dominating a single segment.

**Campaigns by fund**: per-fund breakdown within Operating (`OP:*`), per FY, using the same Total Raised formula. Excludes funds with zero raised in both years. Sorted by FY26 desc.

**Live SAR totals after fix (verified against Supabase 2026-06-09)**:
- `headline.raisedFY26` = **$6,297,616.75** (matches the dev team's expected $6,297,617 ✓)
- `headline.donorsFY26` = **1,738** unique constituents (down from the broken 2,159; the user's initial estimate of ~880-1000 was lower than the actual data — every type-1 row in Operating 2025-2026 has `soft_credit_type IS NULL` and a unique `constituent_id`, so there's no further dedup to do at this layer)

**Untouched**: `/api/development/fundraising-goals` (already correctly scoped — `fund LIKE 'OP:%'`, `gift_type IN (1, 2)`, `fundraising_activity = 'Operating 2025-2026'`, `contribution()` uses amount for type 1 + pledge_balance for type 2). Weekly Gifts tab. Guardian Circle. Campaign Giving by Fund. The Overview testing-feature flag is still in effect — the tab is hidden from anyone not in `workspace_members.testing_features`.

### Contextual "Send via Slack" modal — applied to Tasks + Agenda (2026-06-09)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00648-vz2`. New shared modal lets anyone in the workspace forward a snippet of context to any other workspace member via Slack DM, in two clicks.

**New component — `app/components/shared/SlackSendModal.tsx`**:
- Props: `{ contextText: string; onClose: () => void }`. Parent owns visibility via a single `slackSendContext: string | null` state — non-null renders the modal, `null` closes it.
- Pre-filled context rendered in a read-only `bg-slate-50` box (preserves newlines via `whitespace-pre-wrap`, scrolls past `max-h-40`).
- Recipient `<select>` fetches from the existing `/api/workspace/mentionable-users` endpoint (the spec named `/api/workspace/members` but that route doesn't exist — `mentionable-users` already returns `{ id, name, fullName, email, slackId }` for the active workspace and is auth-gated). Filters to members with a non-null `slackId`, sorts by `fullName ?? name`.
- Message textarea, Send button (disabled until recipient + message both set), Cancel, Esc-to-close, backdrop click closes.
- On success: green inline "Sent to {name}" + auto-close after 1.2s.

**New route — `app/api/slack/send-message/route.ts`**:
- POST, requires `getAuthSession()` (any role). Body: `{ toSlackUserId, message, context }`.
- Resolves the workspace's Slack bot token via `getSlackCredentials(session.workspaceId)`, then calls `sendSlackDM(toSlackUserId, "${context}\n\n${message}", botToken)`. Returns `{ ok: true }` on success or `{ ok: false, error }` (never throws to the client).
- Trusts the client-supplied `toSlackUserId` — recipients are pre-filtered by the modal which only renders members with a configured `slackId`. No second lookup on the server.

**Two call sites in `Dashboard.tsx`**:
- **Tasks**: each pending task card (both RBK column + Assistant column) gained a Slack-send icon button next to the existing urgent toggle, hidden by default and revealed on `group-hover` (matches the existing per-card affordance pattern). On click: `setSlackSendContext(\`Task: ${task.task}${notes}\`)`. `notes` is the `taskNotes[tid]` local-state value (the same string shown muted under the task title), prefixed with a newline when present.
- **Meeting Agenda header**: new "Send to Slack" outlined button next to "+ Add Item". Disabled when `agendaItemsList.length === 0`. On click: builds a multi-line context — `Meeting Agenda — {EEE, MMM d}` followed by `• {title} ({status})` per item (`status = is_discussed ? 'discussed' : 'pending'`), title prefers `item.title ?? item.email?.subject ?? item.topic?.name ?? '(untitled)'`.
- A single `slackSendContext: string | null` state covers both entry points; modal rendered once at the end of the component.

**Out of scope (still pending)**: grants, email cards, project cards, donor notes. Scope kept tight to Tasks + Agenda per spec.

### Four new Slack notification triggers + shared helpers (2026-06-09)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00646-wkt`. New scheduled Cloud Function `dailyAbsenceAlert` deployed alongside (`firebase-schedule-dailyAbsenceAlert-us-central1`, ENABLED, `30 9 * * 1-5 (America/New_York)`).

**Shared helpers in `lib/slackNotifications.ts`.** Two new exports plus a self-skip parameter on the existing `sendTaskSlack`:

- `sendSlackDM(slackUserId, message, botToken): Promise<void>` — generic Slack `chat.postMessage` DM. Bot token passed in so callers that already have it don't double-fetch. Logs + swallows failures (notifications are best-effort).
- `getSlackUserIdByEmail(email, workspaceId, supabase): Promise<string | null>` — `workspace_members.slack_user_id` lookup by email (case-insensitive via `ilike`). Returns null on no match or any error.
- `sendTaskSlack(workspaceId, assignedTo, task, actorEmail?)` — added optional `actorEmail` param. When the resolved assignee's `workspace_members.email` (case-insensitive) matches `actorEmail`, the DM is skipped — closes the "notify yourself" gap. Also gained a `notes?: string | null` field on `SlackTask` and a refreshed message body:
  ```
  📋 You've been assigned a task:
  *Title*
  Notes (truncated to 100 chars, if present)
  → rbk-cmd-center.web.app
  ```

**Trigger 1 — Task assignment notifications (existing route hardened).** `app/api/tasks/route.ts` POST + PATCH now pass `session.user.email` as `actorEmail` to `sendTaskSlack`. PATCH additionally selects `description` from the prior row and forwards it through `dmTaskShape.notes`. The self-skip via `actorEmail` automatically applies to every other caller of `sendTaskSlack` (agenda-notes, simchas/send-note, donor-notes) once their call sites pass an actor email — current Slack-noise baseline is preserved until those routes opt in.

**Trigger 2 — Grant wire-sent notification.** `app/api/development/israel-fund/grants/[id]/route.ts` PATCH reads the prior `wire_was_sent` value before the update. If the update flips it from `false`/`null` to `true`, fires `sendSlackDM` to RBK (`U04NBR22Y`) AND Emily (`U05M5KT86GK`) with:
```
💸 Wire sent: *{initiative}* grant
to {organization_person}
Amount: ${funding_amount}
Procurify #{procurify_number}   ← line shown only when set
```
Both recipients are hardcoded — they're role-specific regardless of who initiated the wire. Fire-and-forget via `void (async () => { … })()` so a Slack failure never breaks the PATCH response.

**Trigger 3 — Major gift notification (operating gifts only).** `lib/syncGifts.ts` now snapshots the existing `gifts_cache` IDs for the workspace BEFORE the upsert (paginated `.in('id', chunk)` lookup, 1000 IDs per chunk). After the upsert succeeds and the Israel raised_cache refresh completes, it filters incoming gifts to: NOT in pre-existing IDs (= genuinely new insert) AND `amount >= $1,000` AND `fundraising_activity NOT ILIKE '%External Funds%'` (Sara tracks Israel Fund separately). If any qualifying gifts found, fires a single batched DM to Sara Hasson (`U04NB3YP3`):
```
🎁 New major gift:                         ← 1-gift form
*{constituent_name}* gave *${amount}*
{event or fund}
{date}
```
```
🎁 N major gifts came in:                  ← batched form
• {name} — ${amount}
• ...
```
Currency formatting via `toLocaleString('en-US', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD', maximumFractionDigits: 0 })`. Failures are caught + warned — never break the gifts sync itself.

**Trigger 4 — Daily absence threshold alert.** New scheduled Cloud Function `dailyAbsenceAlert` (`30 9 * * 1-5 America/New_York`) POSTs to the new `/api/absences/threshold-alert-internal` route (auth: `X-Internal-Secret` shared secret). The route:
1. Queries `attendance_cache` for today's absentees in the SAR workspace (`attendance_category = 1`, `attendance_date = today_ET`).
2. For those students, fetches all YTD absences in chunked `.in('person_id', …)` queries from `2025-09-03` to today.
3. Aggregates per student. Identifies students whose YTD count is exactly 5 or exactly 10 — those are the *crossings* (today's absence ticked them up TO the threshold). Students at 5 absences but not absent today have been at 5 for ≥ 1 day and were already alerted on the day they crossed.
4. If any crossings, DMs RBK (`U04NBR22Y`) with one line per student: `• Name — N absences (Grade)`. If zero crossings, returns silently.

**Thresholds at a glance**:
- Major gift Slack alert: **`amount ≥ $1,000`** AND not External Funds.
- Absence Slack alert: YTD absence count **`= 5`** OR **`= 10`** on the day of the latest absence.

**Untouched**: every UI file, Dashboard.tsx, no other API routes. The existing `dailyTaskDueReminder` cron stays separate.

### Student Logs page + YTD attendance rate fix (2026-06-08)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00636-bws`. Two small but visible changes.

**Student Logs page (Axiom report #1473847).** Sidebar item `student-logs` was already present but module-gated on `admissions`; now role-gated to **owner + assistant** only and reordered to sit directly under Student Absences in the Academics group. The Dashboard view at `activeNav === 'student-logs'` updated: prominent "Open in Veracross →" button linking to `https://axiom.veracross.com/sar/reports/1473847`, plus an iframe attempt at the new `/sar/reports/1473847` URL inside a `calc(100vh - 220px)` container. A small slate-400 caption above the iframe warns the user that if the embed is blocked (Veracross sets `X-Frame-Options`, which it usually does — the browser-default "refused to connect" message replaces our content if so), they should use the button. We deliberately don't try to detect iframe failure via JS — X-Frame-Options blocking doesn't fire a reliable error event, so a permanent caption is more honest than a heuristic. Sidebar gained an optional `requiredRoles?: string[]` field on its nav-item shape, with a corresponding filter chained after the module filter — current only consumer is `student-logs`.

**YTD attendance rate denominator corrected.** RBK flagged that the per-grade attendance tier chart (Chronically Absent / At Risk / Satisfactory bands at <90% / 90-95% / ≥95%) on the Absences page was understating attendance. Root cause in `app/api/absences/route.ts` `getYtdView`: `totalSchoolDays` was inferred from the distinct dates among rows that ALREADY passed the `attendance_category IN (1, 2)` + `grade_level_id IN allowedGrades` filters. Days with all Academy students present (no absent/tardy events) silently dropped out of the denominator — same with days where only HS students had events. Smaller denominator → lower per-student rate → students misclassified into worse tiers. Fix: added module constant `ATTENDANCE_CACHE_FIRST_DATE = '2025-09-03'` (the actual first day with rows in `attendance_cache`, two days after the calendar school-year start) and a separate paginated query that scans `attendance_cache` projected to just `attendance_date` with `gte ATTENDANCE_CACHE_FIRST_DATE` and no category or grade filter. `distinctDates` is built from that scan instead; weekends and breaks fall out naturally because the sync only writes school days. Per-student rate calculation (`(totalSchoolDays - absent) / totalSchoolDays`) and tier bucketing logic unchanged. The extra ~140k-row scan adds a few seconds to the lazy YTD load; acceptable since the section is fetched once per session.

**Untouched**: live today's-attendance route, `/api/absences/historical`, all other tabs, gifts pipeline, Buzz.

### Attendance sync — SYNC_SECRET aligned + workspace_id stamped (2026-06-08)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00634-ksh`. Two root-cause fixes for the silent attendance sync failure that left `attendance_cache` stuck at 2026-03-09 for ~3 months.

**1. `SYNC_SECRET` value aligned across all three call sites.** The Cloud Function `syncDailyAttendance` (`functions/src/index.ts:113`) calls the sync route with `Authorization: 'Bearer rbk-sync-2026'`. `.env.local` agrees. But `deploy.sh:104` was hardcoding the long internal hex (`0395162bea09e4…`) for both `INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET` *and* `SYNC_SECRET`, so every `./deploy.sh` would flip Cloud Run's `SYNC_SECRET` to the wrong value and 401 the nightly cron. Fixed: `deploy.sh` now sets `SYNC_SECRET=rbk-sync-2026`. `INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET` stays on the long hex (used by Buzz + gifts internal endpoints). Cloud Run env on revision `00634-ksh` is now `SYNC_SECRET=rbk-sync-2026`, `INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET=0395…`.

**2. `app/api/absences/sync/route.ts` now writes `workspace_id`.** Every record going into `attendance_cache.upsert(...)` now carries `workspace_id: SAR_WORKSPACE_ID`. The route had been silently inserting NULL-workspace_id rows since the column was added — invisible to every workspace-scoped query (the post-2026-06-08 `/api/absences/historical` route most acutely). Fix is minimal: a module-level `const SAR_WORKSPACE_ID = 'a1b2c3d4-…'`, `workspace_id: string` added to the `AttendanceCacheRow` interface, and `workspace_id: SAR_WORKSPACE_ID` added to the `records.map(...)` output in `fetchAttendanceForDate`. All three upsert call sites (daily, foreground backfill, background `?days=N`) consume that mapped output, so a single change covers all three.

**3. Cache state after the 2026-06-08 backfill run.** `attendance_cache` now covers **2025-09-03 → 2026-06-05** (160 distinct school days, 138,216 rows) for the SAR workspace. Zero NULL-workspace_id rows remain. The `2026-03-10 → 2026-06-07` backfill was filled in by `scripts/backfill-attendance.ts` (paginated `master_attendance` fetch — the in-route helper only ever pulled page 1, which had silently truncated days >1000 records ever since the school grew past that count).

**4. Tonight's cron (Mon 11pm ET).** With the secret matched and the route stamping `workspace_id`, the `0 23 * * 1-5 (America/New_York)` `firebase-schedule-syncDailyAttendance-us-central1` should land 2026-06-08's data with `workspace_id = SAR` automatically. No further manual action required.

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00629-nk8`. Three independent features landed in one deploy.

**1. Slack ping Emily on calendar events.** New `POST /api/slack/notify-emily` route gated to owner + assistant. Looks up the workspace Slack bot token via `getSlackCredentials`, posts a templated DM to Emily's Slack ID (`U05M5KT86GK` — hardcoded for now, future generalization is role-driven lookup in `workspace_members`). Body `{ eventTitle, eventTime? }`; the route is fire-and-forget from the client side (returns `{ ok: true/false, error }`). `Dashboard.tsx` adds a small Slack-hash icon button on each Today's Schedule event row (only when `role === 'owner' || role === 'assistant'`). Click → POST + 2s checkmark on the button + bottom-right toast "Sent to Emily via Slack" (or a "Couldn't reach Emily" toast on failure). Toast state is module-local; no new global UI patterns.

**2. Attendance previous/next day nav.** New `GET /api/absences/historical?date=YYYY-MM-DD` route reads past-day attendance from `attendance_cache` (mirrors the live `/api/absences` response shape so the Dashboard renderer needs no branching). Filters: workspace, HS grading periods, HS grade ids, and present students. `Dashboard.tsx` Student Absences page now wraps its block in an IIFE that computes `prevDay`/`nextDay` from `absencesDate` (null = today). New `absencesDate` state drives `fetchAbsences` to choose between the live route and the historical route; today's data still caches in `dataCacheRef`, but historical days bypass that cache. Header gained ← / → arrow buttons around the date, disabled at the school-year boundary (2025-09-02) and at today; weekend days are skipped automatically by stepping the date forward/backward up to 14 times until a weekday is found. When the user lands on a past date, a small "Historical" label appears next to the date so it's obvious the numbers are cached.

**3. Israel Fund donor drill-down.** New `GET /api/development/israel-fund/donors?initiative=NAME` returns up to 100 most-recent donor gifts from `gifts_cache` for one initiative, matched by APL:/DEV: prefix OR un-prefixed exact match. Anonymous gifts return `name: "Anonymous"` regardless of `constituent_name`. Response includes `hasMore` so the UI can surface "Showing most recent 100 gifts." `IsraelFundTab.tsx` expanded-row block now ends with a "Donors" subsection below the grants sub-table — lazy-fetched on first expand (`useEffect` keyed off `expandedInitiative`), cached per-initiative in a `Map<string, { donors, hasMore }>` for the session, with a spinner during fetch, a "Donor data available from May 2025 onward." fallback when zero matches (Veracross's narrow API window means older-only initiatives won't surface donors here yet), and a compact 3-column table (Donor / Amount / Date).

**Untouched**: every other tab, every other route, Buzz, Admin, the existing Slack events webhook, the gifts sync.
