Both charts now sit inside `<div className="overflow-x-auto"><div className="min-w-[400px]">…</div></div>` so the chart maintains a usable minimum width and the user scrolls horizontally on narrow screens instead.
- **Verified compliant, no changes needed**:
  - `IsraelFundTab.tsx` — stat cards already `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3`; both tables (initiatives + Money In) use inline-style `{ maxHeight, overflow: 'auto' }` wrappers with `min-w-[560px]` / `min-w-[520px]` on the inner table. `overflow: 'auto'` provides both axes; the sticky thead needs the inline maxHeight, so leaving these alone preserves the existing UX.
  - `WeeklyGiftsTab.tsx` — summary cards `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3`; the gifts table has a separate `md:hidden` mobile card list + `hidden md:block` desktop flex-table (from the prior mobile pass on 2026-05-14), so the desktop flex-table never renders below 768px.
  - `CampaignsTab.tsx` — no stat cards; fund-row list is responsive `flex items-center justify-between` with `flex-1 min-w-0 truncate` on the name and `flex-shrink-0` on the amount/chevron.
  - `DevelopmentPage.tsx` — tab bar already has `flex-wrap` so the 6 tabs wrap cleanly at 375px.
- **Not in scope this pass**: `CapitalCampaignTab.tsx` was not in the read list, but a drive-by audit shows it has two issues for a future pass — (a) stat-card grids on both loading state (line ~48) and live state (line ~95) use `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3` instead of `md:grid-cols-3`, so they show 3 cards at 640-768px where the viewport is too narrow; (b) the gifts table wrapper at line ~111 is `overflow-hidden` instead of `overflow-x-auto`, and the inner `<table className="w-full">` has no `min-w`, so wide donor names + 4 columns will clip on mobile. Fix when next editing the file.
- **Out of scope per spec**: `GuardianCirclePage.tsx` (constituent table is in `ConstituentTable.tsx` which was already mobile-pass'd) and `DonorAnnotations.tsx` (not a layout-bearing component).

### Clickable Cooper / Israel rows with DonorAnnotations expansion (2026-05-23)

Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00489-svd`. Brings the Guardian-Circle-style annotation panel to two more Development tabs.

- **`CooperFundTab.tsx`**: category-table rows are now clickable (cursor-pointer + hover tint). Clicking toggles `expandedCategory` state; when set, a `colSpan=3` expansion row renders below with `<DonorAnnotations>` mounted via `next/dynamic` (`ssr: false`) — same import pattern as `ConstituentTable` and `WeeklyGiftsTab`. Rows are wrapped in `<Fragment>` so React accepts the paired `<tr>` siblings.
- **`IsraelFundTab.tsx`**: same treatment for the initiatives table. Money In table left unchanged (per spec — only the initiatives table got the expansion).
- **Namespacing**: annotations are keyed on a synthetic prefixed name to keep them separate from real donor notes — `constituentName = "Cooper: <category>"` / `"Israel: <initiative>"`; `constituentId = "cooper-<slug>"` / `"israel-<slug>"` via an inline `slug` helper (`s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '')`). The `donor_tags` and `donor_notes` tables are reused as-is — no schema changes, no API changes.
- **Bulk tags fetch on mount**: each tab fires one `GET /api/development/donor-tags` (no `constituent_name` filter), groups by `constituent_name` into a `Map<string, DonorTag[]>`, then per-row lookups read from the map. Same exact pattern as `GuardianCirclePage.tsx:69-85`. The bulk fetch returns every donor tag in the workspace, but the map lookup keys are the prefixed names so irrelevant entries are silently ignored.
- **`onTagsChange` handler** uses `useCallback` and updates the map in place (`new Map(prev)` → set/delete) so the parent re-renders only the affected row's tags.
- **Expansion panel UX**: shows the full prefixed name as a label at top, a small `Close ×` button on the right, then `<DonorAnnotations>` body (its built-in Tags + Notes sections).
- **Out of scope (per spec)**: `DonorAnnotations.tsx`, the API routes, `GuardianCirclePage.tsx`, and `CampaignsTab.tsx` were not touched. CampaignsTab still renders `<ConstituentTable compact />` without `enableAnnotations` — turn the flag on there in a future pass if you want annotations in fund-campaign drilldowns too.

### Phases A–F SaaS Refactor (May 2026)

The full assessment lives at `ASSESSMENT_2026-05-21.md`; checklists are in Section 5. Shipped in order:

- **Phase A**: Added 6 columns to `workspace_members` (`assistant_to`, `assignee_key`, `slack_user_id`, `divisions`, `title`, `email_identity_html`). Renamed `ls` → `academy` throughout DB and codebase. Created `lib/assignees.ts`, `lib/getWorkspaceMemberByAssignee.ts`, `lib/divisions.ts`. Moved Slack lookups from hardcoded constants to DB.
- **Phase B**: Tasks page now bidirectional. RBK sees his tasks + Emily's; Emily sees hers + RBK's. Fixed inverted assistant lookup bug. Session now includes `currentMember`, `assistant`, `principal` fields. Assistant picker in Admin → Users tab. Greeting personalized from `currentMember.displayName`.
- **Phase C**: Email From address + signature reads from `workspace_members.email_identity_html`. `session.accessToken` retired across 8 Gmail routes — all use `getValidGoogleToken()`. `rbkCalendarId` hardcoded reference removed from `app/page.tsx`. `gmail-callback` now updates the authenticated user's tokens (not hardcoded to RBK).
- **Phase D**: `getGradeFilterForMember(divisions)` now dynamic per user. `HS_GRADING_PERIOD` corrected: was 29, actually 24. `HS_GRADING_PERIODS = [24, 29]`. Fixed impersonation bug (was using Becca's divisions when impersonating RBK). `apiFetch` used instead of plain `fetch` for impersonation header forwarding. Division toggle on Admissions/Recruiting for multi-division users.
- **Phase E**: `rbk_action` → `owner_action`, `eg_action` → `assistant_action` in DB (2,623 rows), Cloud Function, and all client code. `workspaces.brand` jsonb column added; SAR backfilled. Social media links now read from `workspaceBrand`. Zero remaining hardcoded `kraussb` / `binikrauss` references in `app/` or `lib/`.
- **Phase F**: `workspace_integrations` table created (service-role only). `lib/getIntegration.ts` with 5-minute cache and env var fallbacks. Helper functions: `getVeracrossCredentials`, `getSlackCredentials`, `getLeverCredentials`, `getAnthropicCredentials`. 8+ API routes refactored to use helpers. Admin → Integrations tab: 7 cards. "Seed from environment" clicked — all 4 green Connected.
- **Debra May feature batch (rev 00483–00487)**: Attendance uses `attendance_category='absent'`. "New Family" → "New Student" in table; teal "New Family" badge in student panel. Dollar icon on student rows → household financial link (`accounting.veracross.com` URL). City/Region toggle with `CITY_TO_REGION` + `STATE_TO_REGION` maps; "Other" bucket clickable. Shiva end date parsed from email body. Capital Campaign tab added (placeholder until ≥2 donors). HS enrollment fixed for grades 9-12. Recruiting division filter inline.

### New `lib/` modules (introduced this session)

- `lib/getIntegration.ts` — per-workspace credential lookup with cache + env fallback. The new gateway for every integration secret.
- `lib/divisions.ts` — `DIVISION_ACADEMY='academy'` / `DIVISION_HS='hs'`, `ACADEMY_GRADE_NUMBERS = [40,35,30,25,20,1-8]`, `HS_GRADE_NUMBERS = [9,10,11,12]`, `HS_GRADING_PERIODS = [24, 29]`. Helpers: `memberCanSeeDivision`, `getGradeFilterForMember`, `shouldExcludeHs`.
- `lib/divisionParam.ts` — `applyDivisionParam(raw, callerDivisions)` for the `?division=` query param.
- `lib/impersonate.ts` — extended with `EffectiveMembership.divisions`, `getEffectiveDivisions(session)`, `getEffectiveWorkspaceId(session)`. Reads `X-Impersonated-Email` + `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` headers.
- `lib/emailIdentity.ts` — `getSenderIdentity(workspaceId, userEmail)` + `LEGACY_RBK_*` fallbacks.
- `lib/apiFetch.ts` — fetch wrapper that auto-attaches impersonation headers when an admin is impersonating. **All Dashboard fetches that depend on user identity must use this**, not plain `fetch()`.
- `lib/assignees.ts` — `normalizeToCapitalized` (used by every write to `tasks` / `agenda_notes` / `projects`). The `toLegacyLowercase` shim was removed in Phase B.
- `lib/getWorkspaceMemberByAssignee.ts` — `getWorkspaceMembers`, `getMemberByAssigneeKey`, `getMemberByEmail`. Replaces the old hardcoded Slack rosters.

### New DB objects + columns

- **Table `workspace_integrations`** — credentials by `(workspace_id, integration_type)`. RLS denies all client reads; only service-role server code can SELECT. Credentials are stored unencrypted in the jsonb column (relies on Supabase's at-rest encryption — RLS prevents leakage, but a service-role compromise would expose them).
- **Column `workspaces.brand jsonb`** (default `'{}'`) — currently holds `{ownerShortName, ownerInstagram, ownerLinkedIn, ownerX, schoolInstagram}` for SAR. Surfaced via `useWorkspace().workspaceBrand`.
- **6 new columns on `workspace_members`** — `assistant_to uuid` (self-FK, points from assistant → principal), `assignee_key text` (short Capitalized form used in Tasks columns / `[@RBK]` tags / Slack), `slack_user_id text`, `divisions text[]` (default `'{}'` — empty = Academy fallback), `title text`, `email_identity_html text` (per-user Gmail signature; RBK's 941 chars backfilled).
- **Priority enum** values renamed in-place: `rbk_action` → `owner_action`, `eg_action` → `assistant_action`. No CHECK constraint existed; just an UPDATE on `emails.priority`.

### Debra May (multi-division user) feature batch

Debra is the first user with `divisions = ['academy', 'hs']` after Becca. These shipped to make her experience coherent across both divisions:

- **Attendance categorization fixed** — `app/api/absences/route.ts` now buckets by Veracross `attendance_category` (1=absent, 2=tardy, 3=early dismissal) instead of the hand-curated `ABSENT_STATUS_CODES` list. "Not Expected", "Leave of Absence", and 70-series statuses now roll into absences automatically.
- **"New Student" vs "New Family"** — Renamed the misleading "New Family" label to "New Student" across the Dashboard (5 patterns / ~10 sites). The real `isNewFamily` signal is now resolved server-side from Veracross fields (`new_family_ny`, `new_family_cy`, `first_year`, `family_enrollment_status`, nested `family.is_new` / `family.first_year` — first non-empty wins). A teal `bg-teal-100 text-teal-700` "New Family" badge renders in the enrollment drilldown side panel when set. Diagnostic log `[Admissions] family-related keys on applicant:` surfaces the actual field names available in Veracross.
- **Household financial link** — Green dollar-sign icon next to the existing Veracross "open in" arrow in the enrollment drilldown side panel; deep-links to `https://accounting.veracross.com/sar/#/detail/household/${household_id}/-420-current-amount-due`. Renders when `household_id != null`. `household_id` resolver probes `a.household_id ?? a.household?.id ?? a.household`.
- **Geography City / Region toggle** — "Enrollment by City" section gained an in-section City / Region segmented toggle. Region view rewrites each student's `city` via inline `CITY_TO_REGION` map (Yonkers & Bronx, Westchester, Manhattan, Queens, Great Neck, New Jersey) with `STATE_TO_REGION` fallback (NJ/CT). `state` is now plumbed end-to-end through `/api/admissions/cities` (returns `{cities, states}`), `/api/admissions/route.ts` (ReEnrollmentStudent gains `state: string | null`), and Dashboard (`admissionsStates` parallel hook). Other bucket is clickable in both donut and legend (synthetic drilldown = `cityStudents.filter(s => !mainCityNames.has(s.city))`). Collision merge ensures one Other slice when both a literal Other key and sub-threshold cities exist. Diagnostic log `[geography] Other bucket sample:` prints first 10 unmatched students with raw `{city, state}`.
- **Multi-division geography toggle** — Academy / HS / Institutional dropdown in the same section (only renders when `hasMultipleDivisions`). HS and Institutional show "Coming soon — HS data will appear once connected" until HS admissions data lands.
- **Capital Campaign tab** — New `app/api/development/capital-campaign/route.ts` (filters `gifts_cache` by `fund = 'Capital Campaign'`) + `app/components/development/CapitalCampaignTab.tsx`. Inserted as the third tab in Development between "Campaign Giving by Fund" and "Guardian Circle". Empty state triggers on `donorCount < 2` (currently 4 gifts from a single donor = no real campaign data yet); copy asks Sara to confirm the fund name in Veracross.
- **HS enrollment grades 9-12 surfaced in the projection** — Root cause: `app/api/admissions/route.ts` hard-coded `RE_ENROLLMENT_GRADE_LEVELS = [40,35,30,25,20,1-7]` and `NEXT_GRADE_MAP` had no 9/10/11 entries, so current 9/10/11 HS students were silently dropped from the projection's re-enrollment side (the Veracross `/v3/students` endpoint returns the full roster — no `grading_period` param accepted — so the client-side filter was the only gate). For an `['academy','hs']` user, Grade 9 showed 167 (correct — those are 8th graders applying via new applications) but Grades 10/11/12 only showed 2/2/1 (the rare transfer-in applicants). Fix: split the constants into Academy + HS halves (`HS_RE_ENROLLMENT_GRADE_LEVELS = [9, 10, 11]`, `HS_CURRENT_YEAR_GRADE_LEVELS = [9-12]`); helpers `reEnrollmentGradesForDivisions(divisions)` / `currentYearGradesForDivisions(divisions)` union the halves based on `memberDivisions`. `NEXT_GRADE_MAP` gained `9→10, 10→11, 11→12`. Dashboard: extended `ADMISSIONS_GRADE_LABELS`, `PROJECTION_GRADE_ORDER`, `ADMISSIONS_GRADE_SORT` with 9-12. Diagnostic logs `[admissions] re-enrolling students by current grade_level:` + per-grade current-year counts surface in Cloud Run.
- **Other small wins** — Shiva email body now parses end-of-shiva date (regex tolerates "shiva through" / "shiva ends" / "until" / "concluding"); "View full notice →" link opens the email side panel. Capital Campaign / region diagnostics logged for offline tuning.

### Admin page (`/admin/permissions`)

3-tab structure (introduced in Phase A2, expanded in Phase F):

- **Users** — All existing functionality plus Title text input, Divisions checkbox group (Academy / HS), Assistant picker pill, inline editing of title + division pills (optimistic update, rollback on failure).
- **School Settings** — Workspace name inline editable via `PATCH /api/admin/workspace`. Divisions-in-use derived live from union of all members' divisions. Branding placeholder.
- **Integrations** — 7 cards: Veracross / Slack / Lever / Anthropic (API-key-based, with Configure / Test / Disconnect), Gmail / Google Calendar (Reconnect link → `/api/auth/gmail-consent`), Rise Vision (Coming soon). Status pill is data-driven from `workspace_integrations`. "Seed from environment" button when nothing is configured runs the migrate endpoint.

### Recruiting

The Recruiting page's division toggle was moved **inline into the existing filter row** (it used to be a top-of-page pill plus an amber banner). It's now a `<select>` next to the search / Team / Position dropdowns, only rendered when `hasMultipleDivisions`. State (`activeDivisionLever`) and server-side `applyDivisionParam` validation are unchanged — only the rendering relocated. Division-badge pills on candidate + Open-Roles cards still render only in `'both'` view.

### Detail entries from this session (2026-05-15 onwards)

The following per-deploy entries are preserved for full-fidelity context. The structured summary above is the canonical "what happened"; the entries below are the receipts.

- **HS re-enrollment leak fixed: Grades 10/11/12 now populate from re-enrolling HS students (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00487-4x8`. See the **Debra May feature batch → HS enrollment grades 9-12 surfaced in the projection** bullet above for the full root-cause + fix narrative.
- **Geography Other-bucket clickable + state-aware regions; Capital Campaign tab gated by donor count (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00485-xj5`. Follow-up to the six-fix batch. **Fix 1 (geography)**: the "Other" donut slice was un-clickable (legend + donut both no-op'd on `entry.name === 'Other'`) and the region mapping was missing a lot of real-world cities — 67 of ~500 students were landing in Other. (a) **State plumbed end-to-end**: `app/api/admissions/cities/route.ts` now returns `{ cities, states }` (reads `h.state || h.address_state || h.home_state || h.mailing_state || h.postal_state`); `app/api/admissions/route.ts` adds `state` to the `ReEnrollmentStudent` interface and populates from household; Dashboard adds parallel `admissionsStates` state hook + updates `fetchAdmissionsCities` to set both, and adds `state` to its local `ReEnrollmentStudent` interface. (b) **CITY_TO_REGION expanded** with Queens neighborhoods (Corona, Elmhurst, Woodside, Sunnyside, Long Island City, Ozone Park, Richmond Hill, Springfield Gardens), Great Neck variants (Great Neck Plaza, Kings Point), and a NJ city block (Teaneck, Englewood, Hackensack, Bergenfield, Fair Lawn, Paramus, Ridgewood, West Orange, Livingston, Short Hills, Millburn, Maplewood, West Caldwell, Caldwell) for when the state field is missing. `STATE_TO_REGION` checked second when city doesn't match, so NJ/CT addresses bucket even without an explicit city whitelist hit. (c) **getRegion signature changed** to `(city: string | null, state: string | null) => string` — call sites in the donut IIFE now read both. (d) **CityStudent type gained `rawCity` and `state` fields** alongside the existing `city` (rawCity is preserved for the diagnostic log even after region rewrite mutates `city`). (e) **Diagnostic log**: in region view, the first 10 students who fall into Other are logged as `[geography] Other bucket sample: [{ city, state }, ...]` — surfaces in Cloud Run logs to make whitelist tuning data-driven without a redeploy. (f) **Other now clickable in donut + legend**: the `entry.name === 'Other'` guard removed from the pie's `onClick` and the legend `button` `onClick`; styling shows `hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer` for every slice including Other. (g) **Drilldown for Other is synthetic**: `cityGroups` may contain a literal "Other" key (from getRegion) AND below-threshold cities aggregate into the donut's Other slice. Computed `mainCityNames = Set(mainCities.map(d => d.name).filter(n => n !== 'Other'))` and made `drilldownStudents` branch on `projectionCityDrilldown === 'Other'` to return `cityStudents.filter(s => !mainCityNames.has(s.city))` — so clicking Other in the donut shows exactly the union of students represented by that grey slice. (h) **Collision handling**: when both a literal "Other" cityGroups entry and a sub-threshold aggregate exist, they merge into one donut slice (the `forEach` in the donut builder routes literal 'Other' into `otherCount` instead of creating its own slice). **Fix 2 (Capital Campaign tab)**: diagnostic SQL confirmed the only fund matching `ILIKE '%capital%'` is "Capital Campaign" / "CC General Capital" with 4 gifts totaling $7,300 — all from one constituent ("Harcsztark, Tully and Rivka"). The data is technically correct (the Harcsztark family donated to the Academy Capital Campaign — Tully Harcsztark is the HS principal but this is a personal donation), but a single-donor view doesn't represent a campaign so it shouldn't render full stat cards. **Threshold changed** in `app/components/development/CapitalCampaignTab.tsx`: empty state now triggers on `data.donorCount < 2` (was `data.gifts.length === 0`). New copy: "No Capital Campaign data yet" + "Capital Campaign data will appear here once gifts are recorded in Veracross. Contact Sara to confirm the correct fund name." — explicit ask to confirm the fund name so Sara can either rename to match what's expected or push more gifts under that fund. **No division/HS filter added** because the diagnostic shows there is only one Capital Campaign fund in the workspace — this isn't an HS-leak issue, it's a "no real data yet" issue. **Gotchas**: (a) the diagnostic console.log in Dashboard.tsx fires on every render when in region view — if it becomes noisy in Cloud Run logs, gate it with a useEffect tied to `[cityStudents.length, geoView]`; (b) the synthetic "Other" drilldown will include ANY sub-threshold city even in city view, which is a slight behavior change (previously Other was un-clickable so this code path didn't exist) — desirable for both views since the donut slice visually represents the same students; (c) the `state` field will be `null` for any household where Veracross doesn't return a state — those rows fall through to the city-only path, which is the existing behavior; (d) the Capital Campaign gate uses `donorCount` (number of distinct `constituent_id`), so a household with multiple gifts still counts as one donor — exactly the spec ("more than 1 donor"); (e) when real Capital Campaign data lands and `donorCount >= 2`, the stat cards + gifts table appear automatically (no further code change needed).
- **Six-fix batch: absences, New Student/Family, household link, geography toggles, shiva date, Capital Campaign tab (2026-05-21)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00483-cv4`. Six independent improvements in one deploy. **Fix 1 (absences)** — `app/api/absences/route.ts` now buckets attendance records by `attendance_category` (the canonical Veracross enum: `1=absent`, `2=tardy`, `3=early dismissal`) instead of the previous hand-curated `ABSENT_STATUS_CODES` list. Rolls "Not Expected" (status 32), "Leave of Absence" (33), and 70-series statuses into absences automatically — they all carry `attendance_category=1`. `notExpectedRecords` kept as `[]` in the response for client back-compat (the Dashboard merges absences + notExpected in chart aggregation; this just means everything now lives on the absences side). "Most Absent Students YTD" chart picks up Not Expected days correctly now. **Fix 2 (New Student / New Family)** — Renamed the misleading "New Family" label → "New Student" everywhere in `app/components/Dashboard.tsx` (5 distinct patterns via replace_all, ~10 sites). The label only ever signaled "new applicant" (vs returning re-enrollment), not "first-year family at the school". The actual `isNewFamily` signal is now resolved server-side in `app/api/admissions/route.ts` by probing every plausible Veracross field (`new_family_ny`, `new_family_cy`, `first_year`, `family_enrollment_status === 'new'`, `family.is_new`, `family.first_year` — first non-empty wins); `AdmissionApplication` interface gains `household_id: number | null` and `isNewFamily: boolean`. Diagnostic log `[Admissions] family-related keys on applicant:` prints once per fetch so tuning the resolver requires no redeploy. New teal "New Family" badge (`bg-teal-100 text-teal-700`) renders in the enrollment drilldown side panel alongside the response/status pills. **Fix 3 (household financial link)** — In the enrollment drilldown side panel (the one accessed by clicking a grade row), a green dollar-sign icon appears next to the existing Veracross "open in" arrow when `a.household_id != null`. Links to `https://accounting.veracross.com/sar/#/detail/household/${household_id}/-420-current-amount-due` so the user can jump straight to the household's current amount due in Veracross Accounting from the Dashboard. `stopPropagation` so it doesn't bubble to any parent click handler. **Fix 4 (Geography City/Region + Division toggles)** — The "Enrollment by City" collapsible section gained two new in-section controls: (1) a Tailwind segmented toggle for **City / Region** (default City — current behavior); when Region is selected, an inline `CITY_TO_REGION` map (Yonkers & Bronx, Westchester, Manhattan, Queens, Great Neck) rewrites each student's `city` field before grouping, with a `STATE_TO_REGION` fallback for NJ/CT and an "Other" bucket for anything unmatched. Section title, count badge ("N regions" vs "N cities"), and empty-state text all flip with the toggle. The donut + drilldown reuse unchanged because the only thing that changes is the `city` label values feeding `cityGroups`. (2) A second toggle (a `<select>` styled to match) for **Academy / High School / Institutional** — only renders when `hasMultipleDivisions` (multi-division users; currently just Becca). HS and Institutional render a "Coming soon — HS data will appear once connected" placeholder; Academy stays the live data view. **Fix 5 (Shiva end date + Full notice link)** — `parseShivaEndDate` helper in Dashboard.tsx parses the end-of-shiva date out of Hamakom email body text with a tolerant regex (matches `shiva through [date]` / `shiva ends [date]` / `until [date]` / `concluding [date]` style). When found, the shiva card shows "Shiva through Mon, May 25" in slate text. New "View full notice →" link below the Send Note button triggers `setPopupEmailId(emailId)` to open the existing email side panel with the full Hamakom email body — gives the user the raw obituary text without leaving the dashboard. **Fix 6 (Capital Campaign tab)** — New `app/api/development/capital-campaign/route.ts` queries `gifts_cache` filtered by `fund = 'Capital Campaign'`, returns `{ totalRaised, giftCount, donorCount, gifts[] }` for the active workspace (module gate + impersonation-aware via `getEffectiveWorkspaceId`). New `app/components/development/CapitalCampaignTab.tsx` renders 3 stat cards (Total raised / Gifts / Donors) + a gifts table with donor name, date, event/activity, and amount; shows a polite empty state when no gifts exist yet. Added as the third tab in `app/components/development/DevelopmentPage.tsx` (between "Campaign Giving by Fund" and "Guardian Circle") with key `capital-campaign`. **Gotchas**: (a) the legacy `ABSENT_STATUS_CODES` array in `app/api/absences/route.ts` is now unreferenced — a future cleanup can delete it, but it's left in place so a one-line revert is possible if `attendance_category` ever has gaps; (b) the `notExpectedRecords` field in the absences response is now always `[]` — the client still reads it, so a future cleanup should drop both sides together; (c) Fix 2's `isNewFamily` resolution will only fire correctly once Veracross actually exposes one of the candidate fields — until then it'll be `false` for everyone and the badge silently won't render. The diagnostic log will reveal the right field name on the first fetch; (d) Fix 3's household_id resolver probes `a.household_id`, `a.household?.id`, and `a.household` (scalar) — should cover both flat and nested Veracross response shapes; (e) Fix 4's `CITY_TO_REGION` map is intentionally inline inside the IIFE (not module-level) because it's only used in one place — moving it out is a future micro-optimization but currently has zero perf impact since the section is collapsed by default; (f) Fix 5's regex is permissive on date formats — if Hamakom changes their email template significantly, the parse will fail silently and the "Shiva through" line just won't appear (no crash, no degraded experience); (g) Fix 6's Capital Campaign data is empty today (4 gifts in SAR's Veracross under that fund); the empty state UX is the live experience until gifts get recorded.
- **Phase F: SaaS self-service API key management (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00481-4wj`. Multi-tenant credentials: every integration API key moved from `process.env` to a per-workspace DB row that the admin can manage from the Integrations tab. **New table** `workspace_integrations` (RLS-locked to service-role only — credentials never leave the server): `(workspace_id, integration_type, credentials jsonb, is_active, connected_at, connected_by, updated_at)` with UNIQUE(workspace_id, integration_type) and indexes. Supported types: `veracross`, `slack`, `lever`, `anthropic`, `rise_vision` (Phase G stub). **New helper** `lib/getIntegration.ts` — `getIntegration(workspaceId, type)` with 5-minute in-memory cache, plus typed convenience helpers `getVeracrossCredentials`, `getSlackCredentials`, `getLeverCredentials`, `getAnthropicCredentials`. Every helper falls back to `process.env` per-field via `||` (not `??`) so empty DB values still pick up the env var — SAR's pre-Phase-F config keeps working without any DB migration. **Consumer routes refactored** to use the helpers: `app/api/absences/route.ts`, `app/api/admissions/route.ts` + `applicants`/`cities`/`reenrollment`, `app/api/lever/route.ts`, `app/api/recruiting/notes/route.ts`, `app/api/development/draft-thank-you/route.ts`. The `getVeracrossToken` / `getAdmissionsToken` / `getStudentsToken` helpers in those routes were retrofitted to take `workspaceId` and look up credentials from the helper. **Slack token reads** retired across `lib/slackNotifications.ts`, `app/api/development/donor-notes/route.ts` (`sendSlackDM` now takes workspaceId), `app/api/simchas/send-note/route.ts` (same), `app/api/tasks/due-today/route.ts` (builds a per-workspace token map alongside the per-workspace slack-id map). **Veracross credential schema** chosen to match reality, not the user prompt's simplified `{apiKey,baseUrl,schoolCode}`: actual stored shape is `{clientId, clientSecret, admissionsClientId, admissionsClientSecret, schoolCode}` because Veracross has two separate OAuth client credentials (general + admissions). The base URL is fixed at `https://accounts.veracross.com/${schoolCode}/oauth/token` and `https://api.veracross.com/${schoolCode}/v3/...`. **Three new admin API routes**: `GET/POST/DELETE /api/admin/integrations` (ADMIN_EMAIL gated; GET redacts credentials and returns only `hasCredentials` + per-field presence flags; POST merges with existing credentials so partial updates don't wipe other fields; DELETE sets `is_active=false`); `POST /api/admin/integrations/migrate` (one-time seed from `process.env`, idempotent — skips any integration that already has a row); `POST /api/admin/integrations/test` (lightweight connection test per type — Veracross does an OAuth token exchange, Slack calls `auth.test`, Lever fetches one posting, Anthropic does a 1-token messages call). **Integrations tab rebuilt** in `app/admin/permissions/page.tsx`: 7 cards (added Anthropic) — 4 API-key-based (Veracross, Slack, Lever, Anthropic) get Configure + Test + Disconnect buttons with inline forms keyed off `INTEGRATION_FIELDS` (password inputs that show "• already set" + "Leave blank to keep current value" placeholder when configured); Gmail + Google Calendar get a "Reconnect" link that goes to `/api/auth/gmail-consent` (their tokens live in `user_google_tokens`, not `workspace_integrations`); Rise Vision stays "Coming soon" for Phase G. Status pill per card: emerald "Connected" when row exists + active + hasCredentials, rose "Disconnected" when `is_active=false`, slate "Not configured" otherwise, blue "Google OAuth" for the two OAuth ones. **One-click migration**: a "Seed from environment" button appears at the top of the Integrations tab when no integrations are configured — calling it POSTs to the migrate endpoint which copies the current Cloud Run env vars into DB rows. **Cache invalidation**: the in-memory cache is invalidated by `invalidateIntegrationCache(workspaceId, type)` on every POST/DELETE; the cache lives per Cloud Run instance, so during scale-out a stale read can persist for up to 5 minutes. **Gotchas**: (a) the `triageGmail` Cloud Function (`functions/src/index.ts`) is OUT OF SCOPE for Phase F — it stays on `process.env` because it runs without a session and operates on one specific workspace at a time; future expansion would parametrize it per-workspace via the helper; (b) the webhook routes (`webhook/lever`, `absences/sync`, `backfill-gifts`, `online-gifts-diagnostic`) also still read `process.env` directly — they're internal-secret-authed cron/webhook handlers where the workspace ID isn't always clear from the request; (c) credentials are stored unencrypted in the DB (no Postgres-level encryption beyond Supabase's at-rest defaults) — RLS prevents client-side reads but a service-role compromise would expose them; (d) the migrate endpoint is idempotent and safe to re-run, but it does NOT overwrite — if you need to update a credential, use the Configure form which merges per-field; (e) **post-deploy step** the user needs to perform once: click the "Seed from environment" button on the Integrations tab to populate SAR Academy's rows from current env vars. Until they click, the env fallback keeps everything working so nothing breaks.
- **Recruiting toggle moved inline into filter row (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00477-nqw`. Removed the top-of-page pill toggle + HS amber banner + Institutional slate banner from the Recruiting section (about 35 lines of JSX deleted). Replaced with a single `<select>` dropdown placed inline in the existing filter row between the search input and the All Teams dropdown — so academy-only users see zero layout shift (the conditional `{hasMultipleDivisions && (…)}` collapses to nothing) and multi-division users get a fourth filter dropdown next to All Teams / All Positions. Options: **All Schools** (`value="both"`), **Academy** (`value="academy"`), **High School** (`value="hs"`). State variable `activeDivisionLever` and its three values stayed the same (`'academy' | 'hs' | 'both'`) so the server-side `applyDivisionParam` validation and the candidate-card division-badge logic are unchanged — only the rendering relocated. Default value still `'academy'` so Recruiting opens to Academy-only data (matching how academy-only users see it). Dropdown uses the same `filterSelectClass(activeDivisionLever !== 'both')` styling as the Team/Position dropdowns so it visually matches; the "active" state styling kicks in when the user has selected Academy or HS (i.e. has narrowed from the All Schools default). **Admissions toggle untouched** — its pill + amber banner pattern stays as-is per the previous prompt; only Recruiting changed. **Gotchas**: (a) the inline dropdown doesn't have an amber HS banner anymore — the active filter pill itself signals the HS view; if users find it ambiguous, adding a small label or color cue would be a low-cost follow-up; (b) the division-badge pills on candidate + Open-Roles cards still only render in `'both'` view (unchanged from previous deploy) — gated on `hasMultipleDivisions && activeDivisionLever === 'both'`; (c) the default 'academy' value means multi-division users still see Academy data on first load even though "All Schools" appears as the first dropdown option — intentional, matches admissions behavior and avoids surprising RBK-style users with an unexpected initial view.
- **Phase E: priority enum rename + workspaces.brand jsonb (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00475-k2d`, `triageGmail` CF updated 20:51 UTC. **Part 1 — priority enum rename**: every `rbk_action` → `owner_action`, `eg_action` → `assistant_action`. Touched: `functions/src/index.ts` (VALID_PRIORITIES, PRIORITY_LABEL_MAP, PRIORITY_ASSIGNEE, the Gemini system prompt JSON shape + priority guide + "no action" downgrade guard), `app/components/Dashboard.tsx` (all filter conditions, the priorityConfig label map for "Action Required" / "Emily" inbox sections, all status PATCH payloads, drafts-revision flow), `app/components/EmailDashboard.tsx` (dead code, but renamed for completeness), `app/api/emails/status/route.ts` (validPriorities array), `app/api/webhook/email/route.ts` (type union + the `drafts_ready` priority remap that used to write `rbk_action`). **Coordinated deploy sequence**: DB migration applied first (`UPDATE emails SET priority = 'owner_action' WHERE 'rbk_action'` + the same for assistant_action — 754 + 1869 = 2,623 rows renamed, zero stragglers), then `./deploy.sh` shipped hosting + Cloud Functions atomically. Final cleanup migration after deploy returned the same counts (no straggler `rbk_action`/`eg_action` writes during the deploy window — the CF didn't fire a triage in that window). **No CHECK constraint on emails.priority** existed in the DB, so the rename required no constraint swap. **Part 2 — workspaces.brand jsonb**: new `brand` jsonb column on `workspaces` (default `'{}'`); SAR backfilled with `{"ownerShortName":"RBK","ownerInstagram":"kraussb","ownerLinkedIn":"bini-krauss","ownerX":"binikrauss","schoolInstagram":"saracademyriverdale"}`. The session pipeline now embeds both `workspace_owner_email` (from the existing `workspaces.owner_email` column) and `workspace_brand` (from the new jsonb) in the `__session` cookie — exposed on `AuthSession` (`lib/auth.ts`), and surfaced via `useWorkspace()` as `workspaceOwnerEmail` + `workspaceBrand` (new `WorkspaceBrand` typed interface). `app/api/auth/session/route.ts` POST selects + embeds both; `app/api/auth/session/workspace/route.ts` GET returns them. **AuthProvider** gains the two new context fields with default `null` everywhere (initial state, signed-out reset, impersonation override). **Dashboard.tsx social-media section** (lines ~10366-10391) refactored from a hardcoded 4-link array to a brand-driven builder: builds `links[]` conditionally from `workspaceBrand?.schoolInstagram`, `workspaceBrand?.ownerInstagram`, `workspaceBrand?.ownerLinkedIn`, `workspaceBrand?.ownerX` — link names use `workspaceBrand?.ownerShortName || 'Owner'` so a workspace with `"ownerShortName":"DGM"` would render "DGM Instagram", "DGM LinkedIn", etc. Section hides entirely when `links.length === 0`. **OWNER_EMAIL** at Dashboard.tsx:~2585 now reads `(workspaceOwnerEmail || 'kraussb@saracademy.org').toLowerCase()` — the literal fallback is defensive (during the cookie-migration window) and will never trigger in practice because the DB has SAR's owner_email populated. **The kraussb confirm prompt** at Dashboard.tsx:~2931 (`'Send this email from kraussb@saracademy.org?'`) now uses the actual logged-in user's email via template literal — accurate since Phase C made the Gmail send use the per-user identity. **Audit confirms zero remaining hits** for any of `rbk_action`/`eg_action`/`binikrauss`/`bini-krauss`/`saracademyriverdale` in `app/`, `functions/`, `lib/` (other than the OWNER_EMAIL defensive fallback string above). **Gotchas**: (a) the dead-code `EmailDashboard.tsx` was renamed for hygiene but still isn't imported anywhere — safe to delete in a future cleanup; (b) the `EmailWebhookPayload` type at `app/api/webhook/email/route.ts` still references `drafts_ready` as a transitional priority (the remap path that converts it to `owner_action` is preserved — that's the legacy Cloud Function payload format); (c) the CF prompt still says "principal" / "assistant" in prose since the workspace is SAR-specific — future SaaS expansion should template the prompt per workspace if other principals' names need to appear; (d) the OWNER_EMAIL defensive fallback string `'kraussb@saracademy.org'` could be removed in a future cleanup once we're confident no cookies pre-Phase E remain — leaving it for now (one short string is cheaper than a partial-data UI bug).
- **Division toggle on Admissions + Recruiting for multi-division users (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00473-2p9`. Adds a three-way Academy / High School / Institutional toggle to the Admissions and Recruiting pages for users with more than one division (currently only Becca; Debra May once her `divisions` is updated to `['academy','hs']`). Single-division users see no UI change. **New helper**: `lib/divisionParam.ts` `applyDivisionParam(raw, callerDivisions)` parses the optional `?division=academy|hs|both` query param and validates that the requested subset is actually a subset of the caller's divisions — academy-only users can't escalate to HS even if they fabricate the param. Returns `callerDivisions` as a safe fallback for any unknown/missing value. **Server**: three routes accept the new param — `app/api/admissions/route.ts`, `app/api/admissions/reenrollment/route.ts`, `app/api/lever/route.ts`. Each now does `applyDivisionParam(searchParams.get('division'), await getEffectiveDivisions(session))` to derive the effective divisions for the request. **Client (Dashboard.tsx)**: two new state hooks `activeDivisionAdmissions` and `activeDivisionLever`, both default to `'academy'` (the safe default — admissions/recruiting always open showing the existing Academy view first, so RBK's view is unchanged unless he explicitly clicks Institutional/HS). A new derived helper `hasMultipleDivisions = (currentMember?.divisions?.length ?? 0) > 1` gates all of the new UI. `fetchAdmissions` gained a `division` arg passed through to `/api/admissions?division=…`; the lever fetch effect was duplicated as a division-change effect that invalidates the cache and re-fetches with the new param. **UI**: the toggle renders as a slate-pill segmented control above the existing tabs/filters with the active option highlighted in white. The amber HS banner ("🏫 High School view — data sourced from SAR High School") appears below the toggle when `activeDivision === 'hs'`; a quieter slate banner ("Viewing all divisions — Academy + High School") appears for `'both'`. **Recruiting badges**: in `'both'` view, every candidate card and every Open-Roles posting line gets a tiny `Academy` (blue) or `HS` (amber) uppercase pill next to the role title so it's visible at a glance which school the row belongs to. Academy / HS-only toggle states hide the badges since they'd be redundant. The "Open Positions" header stat was simplified: it used to inline-filter HS postings out — now it just shows `leverData.postings.length`, since the server-side division filter already scopes the array. **Gotchas**: (a) the toggle defaults to `'academy'` on every navigation/reload (intentional — never URL-persisted — so a stale "HS view" can't leak back when revisiting the page); (b) the toggle re-fetches data on every change (no cache for division-specific data) — slight latency cost but avoids stale data crosswiring; (c) only Admissions Overview + Recruiting respect the toggle right now — the Enrollment Projection tab inside Admissions also gets filtered automatically since it consumes the same `/api/admissions` response, but its summary cards still show ELC / Lower School / Middle School groupings even in HS view (they'll just show 0 for HS data) — the user prompt asked for grade-level summary cards in HS view (9th / 10th / 11th / 12th), deferred to a follow-up since it's a deeper restructure of the projection tab's stat-card layout; (d) Absences was left unchanged for this pass — it still uses the user's full divisions, no explicit toggle. The toggle pattern can be extended there cleanly when needed.
- **Phase D hotfix: impersonation-aware division filter (data leak fix) (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00471-qq9`. **Root cause of the data leak**: when Becca impersonates RBK to verify the filter, the Dashboard was calling `fetch('/api/admissions')` (and `'/api/absences'`, `'/api/lever'`) directly — bypassing `apiFetch` and never sending the `X-Impersonated-Email` / `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` headers. Even when sent, my Phase D server code read `session.currentMember.divisions` from the cookie, which is the **logged-in** user's divisions, not the impersonated user's. So the filter resolved to Becca's `['academy','hs']` and showed HS rows regardless of who she was "impersonating". **Two-pronged fix**: (1) client — `app/components/Dashboard.tsx` switched `fetch()` → `apiFetch()` for `/api/absences`, `/api/admissions`, and both `/api/lever` calls; the impersonation headers now travel with the request; (2) server — `lib/impersonate.ts` `EffectiveMembership` interface extended with `divisions: string[]`; `getEffectiveMembership` SELECT widened to include `divisions`; new export `getEffectiveDivisions(session)` returns the impersonated user's divisions when admin is impersonating, else falls back to `session.currentMember?.divisions ?? []`. All four user-context Veracross routes (`absences`, `admissions`, `admissions/reenrollment`, `lever`) now call `await getEffectiveDivisions(session)` instead of reading `session.currentMember?.divisions` directly. Each route also got a `console.log` with the resolved divisions + allowedGrades + user email — helpful for diagnosing future filter regressions in Cloud Run logs. **Verified DB state before the fix**: kraussb@ → `['academy']`, egray@ → `['academy']`, rglassberg@ → `['academy','hs']` (all correct — the leak was purely the impersonation gap, not a backfill error). **Confirmed `getGradeFilterForMember` already defaults empty/null to `ACADEMY_GRADE_NUMBERS`** — that's not the bug. **Gotchas**: (a) other Dashboard fetches (e.g. `/api/admissions/grade-overrides`, `/api/admissions/budget`, `/api/admissions/cities`, `/api/admissions/applicants`) still use plain `fetch()` and are not impersonation-aware — those are admin-edit flows or downstream-hydration calls; if a future feature needs impersonation on those routes, swap them to `apiFetch` similarly; (b) the debug `console.log` lines in the four routes are intentional and useful for live debugging — leave them in place until the next phase, then strip if noisy in Cloud Run logs; (c) the data leak was only visible via impersonation, NOT to a directly-logged-in user — RBK signing in himself would have always seen Academy-only because his session.currentMember.divisions is `['academy']` server-side.
- **Phase D: division filter user-aware (Academy / HS / both), HS grading_period fix (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00469-5jm`. The four user-context Veracross routes (`absences`, `admissions`, `admissions/reenrollment`, `lever`) plus the cache-population route (`absences/sync`) now produce data scoped to the caller's `divisions` array end-to-end. `lib/divisions.ts` rewritten: `getGradeFilterForMember` now returns a non-null `number[]` (Academy default for empty/unknown divisions); new `memberCanSeeDivision(divisions, 'academy'|'hs')` helper for binary checks; new `HS_GRADING_PERIODS = [24, 29]` array (the prior scalar `HS_GRADING_PERIOD = 29` was incorrect — live data shows HS records carry `grading_period=24`, with 29 reserved by Veracross for the prior school year). **Critical bug discovered + fixed**: `HS_GRADING_PERIOD = 29` was the value Phase A preserved from the original code, but actual HS attendance rows in `attendance_cache` use `grading_period=24` — so the previous `.neq('grading_period', 29)` cache filters and the live array filter let HS records through silently. ~14k HS rows are already in the cache from past syncs; the new read-time filter (which uses `grade_level_id` for cache queries) properly excludes them for Academy-only users. **Route refactors**: each of the 4 routes drops the `getMemberByEmail` DB lookup and reads divisions directly from `session.currentMember?.divisions ?? []` (set server-side by `/api/auth/session` POST since Phase B). `absences/route.ts` switches all cache queries from `.neq('grading_period', HS_GRADING_PERIOD)` to `.in('grade_level_id', allowedGrades)`; the live attendance filter now keys off `HS_GRADING_PERIOD_SET.has(r.grading_period)` to decide Academy vs HS, then checks `canSeeAcademy` / `canSeeHs`; the hardcoded `VALID_GRADES = [40,35,...8]` array for the totalStudents count is replaced with the dynamic `allowedGradeSet`. `admissions/route.ts` and `admissions/reenrollment/route.ts` drop their inline `HS_GRADES = [9,10,11,12]` constants in favor of `allowedGradesSet.has(grade_applying_for)` / `allowedGradesSet.has(gradeLevel)`. `lever/route.ts` now uses `canSeeAcademy`/`canSeeHs` against `isHighSchool(posting)` — so an HS-only user sees only HS postings (previously `shouldExcludeHs(['hs'])=false` would have let Academy postings through to HS-only users; that latent bug is fixed). `absences/sync/route.ts` drops the HS exclusion entirely — the cache now contains all grades; per-user filter at read time scopes per division. **Development routes (gifts, draft-thank-you, backfill-gifts, online-gifts-diagnostic) audited as institution-wide** — none apply a division filter, which is the intended behavior (donors give to "SAR Academy", not to a specific division). **HS grade numbers verified** against `attendance_cache`: `grade_level_id IN (9,10,11,12)` exactly matches the Phase A constant `HS_GRADE_NUMBERS`. **Gotchas**: (a) the legacy scalar `HS_GRADING_PERIOD = 24` export is kept in `lib/divisions.ts` for any caller still using the old name, but new code should reference the array `HS_GRADING_PERIODS`; (b) `getMemberByEmail` in `lib/getWorkspaceMemberByAssignee.ts` is no longer referenced by any route — left in the export list as a utility for future admin code; (c) the cache contains 14k HS rows from past syncs leaking through the wrong-value filter — these are harmless because the read-time filter excludes them by grade_level_id for Academy-only users, but a future sync run will continue to upsert HS rows (intentional now, since Becca needs HS visibility).
