- **Phase C: de-RBK-ified email send + retired session.accessToken + per-user calendar (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00467-l4t`. **Backfill first**: ran `phase_c_backfill_rbk_email_identity` migration to populate `workspace_members.email_identity_html` for kraussb@saracademy.org with his exact existing 941-char signature (Rabbi Binyamin Krauss, Principal, phone/email/LinkedIn, SAR logo image). This was done BEFORE code changes so the fallback path is never exercised in practice for RBK. **New helper**: `lib/emailIdentity.ts` exports `getSenderIdentity(workspaceId, userEmail)` → `{ fromEmail, fromName, signatureHtml }` with `LEGACY_RBK_FROM_EMAIL` / `LEGACY_RBK_FROM_NAME` / `LEGACY_RBK_SIGNATURE` constants as defense-in-depth fallbacks (RBK's flow can't break even if his `email_identity_html` somehow becomes NULL). **3 send routes refactored**: `app/api/emails/compose/route.ts`, `app/api/emails/send-batch/route.ts`, `app/api/emails/[id]/send/route.ts` — each one drops its inline `EMAIL_SIGNATURE` constant + hardcoded `fromEmail = 'kraussb@saracademy.org'` / `fromName = 'Rabbi Binyamin Krauss'`; `createMimeMessage` now takes a `SenderIdentity` arg; the POST handler calls `getSenderIdentity(workspaceId, session.user.email)` after auth. The 403 Send-As error message in `[id]/send/route.ts` now references the dynamic `identity.fromEmail` instead of hardcoded `kraussb@`. **Retired session.accessToken across 8 routes**: each Gmail-API-touching route now calls `getValidGoogleToken(workspaceId, session.user.email)` from `lib/googleToken.ts` (auto-refresh via user_google_tokens) and returns 401 "Google account not connected. Please sign in again." when null. Touched: `emails/compose`, `emails/send-batch`, `emails/[id]/send`, `emails/[id]/archive`, `gmail/process-email`, `gmail/trash-email`, `gmail/thread/[threadId]`, `development/draft-thank-you`. The draft-thank-you route's `createGmailDraft` helper was extended to take fromEmail/fromName params and now uses the sender's identity instead of hardcoded RBK. **app/page.tsx**: `rbkCalendarId = 'kraussb@saracademy.org'` removed; calendar URL now hits `/calendars/primary/events` (the authenticated user's default calendar — same pattern as `/api/calendar/today` and `/api/calendar/week`). Calendar token also routed through `getValidGoogleToken(workspaceId, session.user.email)` instead of session.accessToken. The "skip if not present" guard changed from `session.accessToken ? …` to `session.workspaceId && session.user?.email ? …`. The Dashboard component still consumes the `calendarEvents` prop (as initial state for `scheduleEvents` on the home Schedule card), so removing the SSR fetch wasn't an option — fixing it in place was. **gmail-callback de-hardcoded**: removed the `WORKSPACE_OWNER_EMAIL = 'kraussb@…'` constant. The workspace-level `workspaces.gmail_refresh_token` (still used by the email-sync Cloud Function) now only gets overwritten when the connecting user is verified as a **workspace owner** via `workspace_members.role = 'owner'` lookup — so a non-owner re-consenting can't clobber the principal's sync token. The per-user `user_google_tokens` upsert (already happens for any authenticated user) is unchanged. **Gotchas**: (a) the `session.accessToken` field is still stored in the `__session` cookie at login (it's the popup-flow token); it's just no longer read by any Gmail/Calendar route. The session refresh logic and existing cookie shape are unchanged. Could be removed entirely in a future cleanup since nothing reads it. (b) Per-user token availability gates Gmail features — if a member hasn't connected Google via `/api/auth/gmail-consent`, they now get a 401 with a clear "sign in again" message instead of cryptic 401s from Gmail API directly. (c) `app/api/development/draft-thank-you` has a vestigial `EMAIL_SIGNATURE` constant in scope but its `createGmailDraft` no longer references it — that route uses just text + line breaks; future cleanup could drop the constant. (d) RBK's signature is now in DB (941 chars), so subsequent visual edits go via SQL or admin UI rather than code change. **Test plan**: log in as Becca, impersonate RBK, send a test email (compose) — confirm it arrives with the same visual From/signature as before. If somehow the DB row regresses, the LEGACY_* fallbacks ensure the email still goes out correctly.
- **Tasks page now bidirectional (assistant ↔ principal) (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00465-jb4`. Previously the Tasks page's second column only resolved for principals (the assistant column was the reverse-lookup result). Emily, who is an assistant, was missing the corresponding view of RBK's tasks. Added a `principal` payload alongside `assistant` to the session pipeline: `app/api/auth/session/route.ts` POST now does both queries — the reverse lookup `WHERE assistant_to = me.id` for `assistant`, and the forward lookup `WHERE id = me.assistant_to` for `principal` — and embeds both into the `__session` cookie. `app/api/auth/session/workspace/route.ts` GET returns both. `lib/auth.ts` adds `SessionPrincipal` (type alias for `SessionAssistant`) and `AuthSession.principal`. **AuthProvider** exposes `principal` via `WorkspaceContext`; the impersonation override fetches the impersonated user's principal by `assistant_to` forward-lookup against `allMembers`. **Dashboard.tsx** introduces a `secondColumnMember = assistant ?? principal` resolution and rebinds the existing `theirAssigneeKey` / `theirAssigneeKeyLower` / `theirDisplayName` / `hasAssistant` helpers to use it. A `hasSecondColumn` alias is the canonical new name; `hasAssistant` is kept as a backward-compat alias resolving to the same value (so no downstream rename needed). Result: principals see [me | assistant], assistants see [me | principal], standalone users see only their own column. **Layout**: the `grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2` wrapper now collapses to a single column when `!hasSecondColumn`, so the Tasks page is a single full-width column for standalone users instead of a half-width column with empty space next to it. **Total pending count**: the badge next to the "Tasks" page header (`{tasks.filter(t => !t.isComplete).length} pending` — was counting every task in the workspace) now reads `{pendingMine.length + pendingTheirs.length} pending`, scoped to just the two columns the current user actually sees. **Sidebar behavior unchanged**: the "Assistant's Queue" sidebar item still only renders for principals (driven by `assistant`, not `secondColumnMember`) — semantically the queue concept is RBK's email-triage queue managed by Emily; Emily herself doesn't need a sidebar link to "her own queue". **Gotchas**: (a) `secondColumnMember` prefers `assistant` over `principal` when both happen to be set (which shouldn't normally happen but the FK doesn't prevent it); (b) the project assignee toggle and Add Project modal still gate on `hasAssistant` (now `hasSecondColumn`-equivalent), so Emily can also use them to flip a project's assignee between herself and RBK — which is arguably more useful than the principal-only behavior; (c) the auto-session-migration effect from yesterday's hotfix still fires on first mount for cookies pre-dating Phase B / this change, so Emily and RBK both get the new `principal` field populated automatically on next page load without re-login.
- **Phase B hotfix: inverted assistant lookup + missing column guard + admin UI (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00463-2h2`. Three production bugs found right after Phase B shipped. **Bug 1 — inverted assistant lookup**: `workspace_members.assistant_to` is set on the **assistant's** row pointing back at the principal (Emily's `assistant_to = RBK.id` means "Emily assists RBK"). My Phase B session POST + AuthProvider impersonation code read `memberRow.assistant_to` as if it were a forward pointer, which meant RBK's page got `assistant=null` (his own `assistant_to` is empty) and Emily's page incorrectly resolved RBK as her assistant. Fixed in both code paths to use the reverse-lookup pattern: `SELECT * FROM workspace_members WHERE assistant_to = <my id>`. The session POST query now uses `.eq('assistant_to', memberRow.id).maybeSingle()`; AuthProvider impersonation uses `allMembers.find(m => m.assistant_to === impMember.id)`. **Bug 2 — column rendered without an assistant**: the "Emily Column" JSX in `Dashboard.tsx` (line ~5689) was never wrapped in a `hasAssistant` guard, so Becca and others with no assistant saw an empty second column. Wrapped the whole block in `{hasAssistant && (...)}`. Also fixed the stale hardcoded "EG" initials chip (line ~5789) to slice `theirAssigneeKey`. **Bug 3 — stale sessions don't auto-refresh**: users logged in with cookies from before Phase B had `current_member=null` and `assistant=null` in their session. Added a one-shot session migration effect in `AuthProvider`: on mount, if a user is authenticated but `workspace.currentMember` is null, call `refreshSession(user)` once (guarded by a `didMigrateSession` flag to prevent infinite loops for genuinely-no-member accounts). **New admin UI — Assistant picker**: `app/api/admin/workspace-members/[id]/route.ts` PATCH now accepts `assistant_to` (string id or null, with self-reference rejection). `Member` interface in `app/admin/permissions/page.tsx` extended with `assignee_key` + `assistant_to`. On each user row in the Users tab, a new pill-style "Assistant: [name ▾]" sits next to the divisions checkboxes; click to open a dropdown of other members with `assignee_key` set, plus "(none)" to clear. Selecting a new assistant runs `setMemberAssistant(principal, newAssistantId)` which does a two-step PATCH: clears any current assistant's `assistant_to`, then sets the new assistant's `assistant_to = principal.id`. Optimistic local update + rollback on failure. The pill turns indigo when there's an assistant set, slate when not. **Gotchas**: (a) the schema doesn't have a uniqueness constraint on `assistant_to`, so technically multiple rows could point at the same principal — the session lookup uses `.maybeSingle()` which will throw if that happens; if you need multiple assistants per principal in the future, switch to `.limit(1)`; (b) the auto-refresh effect runs once per Firebase user load — if someone has no workspace_members row at all, it'll fire the refresh once and then quiet down via `didMigrateSession`, no infinite loop; (c) the dropdown candidates filter requires `assignee_key !== null`, so members without a Tasks-column key won't appear as selectable assistants — that's intentional since they couldn't be DM'd via Slack or appear in tasks lookups.
- **Phase B: Tasks page generalized to dynamic currentMember + assistant (2026-05-23)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00460-5lc`. Replaces the legacy hardcoded RBK / Emily columns and labels with data-driven identity pulled from `workspace_members`. **Session pipeline**: `app/api/auth/session/route.ts` POST now queries the active member row + (if present) the assistant row by `assistant_to` FK and embeds both into the `__session` cookie as `current_member` and `assistant`. `app/api/auth/session/workspace/route.ts` GET returns the same fields; `lib/auth.ts` `AuthSession` interface extended; `app/api/admin/workspace-members/route.ts` GET now also returns `assignee_key`, `slack_user_id`, `assistant_to` for the impersonation path. **AuthProvider**: `WorkspaceContextType` gains `currentMember` and `assistant` (typed `CurrentMember` / `AssistantMember`). Impersonation override fetches `/api/admin/workspace-members` and rebuilds both fields from the impersonated member's row + their `assistant_to` lookup — so when Becca impersonates RBK, the Tasks page shows RBK's column + Emily's column with their display names. **Dashboard.tsx refactor** (the big surface): `useWorkspace` destructure extended; helper consts `myAssigneeKey`, `theirAssigneeKey`, `myAssigneeKeyLower`, `theirAssigneeKeyLower`, `myDisplayName`, `theirDisplayName`, `hasAssistant` introduced. `pendingRbk`/`completedRbk`/`pendingEmily`/`completedEmily` renamed to `pendingMine`/`completedMine`/`pendingTheirs`/`completedTheirs` and filtered by `myAssigneeKeyLower`/`theirAssigneeKeyLower`. `showCompletedEmily` → `showCompletedTheirs`. `handleTaskDrop` columnId is now the lowercase assigneeKey instead of literal `'rbk'`/`'emily'`. Column header `<h2>RBK</h2>` → `{myDisplayName}`; `<h2>Emily</h2>` → `{theirDisplayName ?? 'Assistant'}`. The "From Development" / "From Admissions" sourced-task filters now compare `t.assigned_to?.toLowerCase() === myAssigneeKeyLower` (was `=== 'RBK'`). The drag-target columnIds inside the column JSX now pass `myAssigneeKeyLower` / `theirAssigneeKeyLower`. The home view's "my tasks" filters (`t.assignee === 'rbk'`) all use `myAssigneeKeyLower`. The agenda-note assignee badges (`note.assignee === 'emily' ? 'Emily' : 'RBK'`) became case-insensitive and use `theirDisplayName`/`myDisplayName`. The greeting (`'kraussb@…' ? 'Good morning, Bini' : …`) is now fully dynamic: time-of-day + `currentMember.displayName.split(' ')[0]`. The project card avatar chips + assignee toggle in the project side panel use the dynamic color/label pair and disable the toggle when `hasAssistant === false`. The "Add Project" modal assignee buttons are also dynamic, showing only the current member when no assistant is configured. The task-creation modal "Assign to" buttons same pattern. The internal `task.assignee` field stays lowercase for cross-source consistency (emailTasks synthesizes lowercase from `[@RBK]/[@EMILY]` text tags; noteTasks downcase on construction since agenda_notes API now returns Capitalized). The `Project.assignee` type widened from `'rbk' | 'emily'` to `string`. **Sidebar.tsx**: new `assistant` prop typed `{ displayName: string | null } | null`; "Emily's Queue" nav item now reads ``${assistant.displayName}'s Queue`` and is hidden entirely when `!isViewer && assistant?.displayName` is falsy (most members have no assistant, so the nav item disappears for them). The prop is passed through from Dashboard.tsx, `app/home/page.tsx`, and `app/admin/permissions/page.tsx`. **TodayTasksCard**: removed the hardcoded `EMAIL_TO_ASSIGNEE` map; now consumes `useWorkspace().currentMember.assigneeKey` and filters case-insensitively. The `effectiveEmail` prop is kept on the type for backward compatibility but ignored. **DB migration**: `projects.assignee` lowercase → Capitalized (UPDATE applied via `phase_b_unify_projects_assignee_case` migration; 7 'emily'→'Emily', 6 'rbk'→'RBK'), matching the unified pattern from Phase A on `tasks.assigned_to` and `agenda_notes.assignee`. No CHECK constraint exists on `projects.assignee` so no constraint swap needed. **Shim removal**: the Phase A `toLegacyLowercase` shim on `/api/agenda-notes` GET/POST/PATCH responses has been deleted — the API now returns Capitalized `assignee` values natively (`lib/assignees.ts` `toLegacyLowercase` export is no longer referenced and can be removed in a future cleanup). All downstream callers handle the Capitalized form either via explicit lowercase normalization at construction or via case-insensitive comparison. **Out-of-scope (deferred to later phases per the assessment):** `app/components/EmailDashboard.tsx` was confirmed to be dead code (not imported anywhere) and was not touched; the `rbk_action` / `eg_action` priority enum values stay as-is (Phase E renaming); `[@RBK]` / `[@EMILY]` text tags embedded in `emails.meeting_notes` body content stay as-is (data migration would be needed); `kraussb@saracademy.org` is still the hardcoded OWNER_EMAIL / from-address / signature in `app/api/emails/...` (Phase C); the "RBK Command Center" page title + RBK social-media footer in Dashboard.tsx stay (Phase E branding). **Audit grep** of remaining `'rbk'`/`'emily'`/`'RBK'`/`'Emily'` literals in Dashboard / Sidebar / TodayTasksCard confirmed all surviving hits are intentional: `ASSIGNEE_OPTIONS` (5-user dropdown for task assignment — kept by design per the prompt), `VALID_NAV_IDS` containing `'emily'` (internal nav id key for the assistant queue — label is dynamic), `emailCategory` state values `'rbk'`/`'emily'` (UI category keys mapping to the unchanged `rbk_action`/`eg_action` priority enum), the email-text `[@RBK]`/`[@EMILY]` tag parser at line 2317, and a few useState initial defaults for new-task forms that don't affect display.
- **Phase A2: `ls` → `academy`, divisions UI, Admin tabs (2026-05-22)** — Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00458-bx4`. **Rename**: the institutional division formerly tracked as `'ls'` is now `'academy'` (SAR Academy = ELC + Lower School + Middle School, one building; `'hs'` = SAR High School, separate building, same institution). DB migrated via `array_replace(divisions, 'ls', 'academy')` on workspace_members; all 8 affected rows now show `['academy']` (Becca has `['academy','hs']`). In `lib/divisions.ts`: `DIVISION_LS`→`DIVISION_ACADEMY`, `LS_GRADE_NUMBERS`→`ACADEMY_GRADE_NUMBERS`, plus the institutional comment block at the top. Stale comment references in `app/api/absences/route.ts` and `app/api/admissions/route.ts` updated. **User Management additions**: Add User form now has a "Title" text input and a "Divisions" checkbox group (Academy / High School); per-row inline-edit affordances added — click the italic title to edit (Enter/blur commits, Escape cancels), click an Academy/HS pill to toggle that division (optimistic update + rollback). Both `POST /api/admin/workspace-members` and `PATCH /api/admin/workspace-members/[id]` accept `divisions: string[]` and `title: string`; GET includes them in the response. **Admin page restructured into tabs**: page header renamed "User Management" → "Admin". Three tabs: **Users** (default — all existing functionality + title/divisions), **School Settings** (workspace name inline-editable via new `PATCH /api/admin/workspace`, divisions-in-use summary derived from union of all members' divisions, "Branding — coming soon" placeholder), **Integrations** (6-card grid for Veracross / Gmail / Slack / Lever / Rise Vision / Google Calendar — each card shows "Connected" badge with system-configured label and a disabled "Configure" button with "Coming soon" tooltip; this is the Phase F SaaS self-service shell). New endpoint: `app/api/admin/workspace/route.ts` — GET returns `{id, name}` for a workspace; PATCH updates `workspaces.name`. ADMIN_EMAIL gated like the workspace-members routes. **Gotchas**: (a) the Integrations tab shows everything as "Connected" because all 6 are configured at the system level via env vars/secrets — when Phase F adds per-workspace API keys, the status will flip to be data-driven; (b) the workspaceDivisions summary on School Settings is derived from members on-the-fly (no DB column for "workspace divisions" — that's by design since divisions are member-scoped); (c) `INTEGRATION_CARDS` SVG icons are inline (no Lucide dep) so they stay terse — replace with the proper library if any other page needs icons.
- **Phase A foundations refactor (2026-05-21)** — Schema + lookup-table cleanup ahead of HS expansion (revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00456-2mt`). Six new columns added to `workspace_members`: `assistant_to uuid` (self-FK), `assignee_key text`, `slack_user_id text`, `divisions text[] DEFAULT '{}'`, `title text`, `email_identity_html text`. **Tasks/agenda_notes case unified to Capitalized** — `agenda_notes.assignee` data migrated (88 'rbk'→'RBK', 15 'emily'→'Emily') and CHECK constraint replaced; both tables now CHECK against `'RBK'|'Emily'|'Sara'|'Leora'|'Becca'`. Extracted `lib/assignees.ts` with `normalizeToCapitalized` (used by all writes) and `toLegacyLowercase` — the latter is a **backward-compat shim on `/api/agenda-notes` GET/POST/PATCH responses** that downcases assignee so Dashboard.tsx + TodayTasksCard continue to read lowercase unchanged; Phase B will remove it when the UI is refactored. Members backfilled: kraussb (RBK, Principal, ['ls']), egray (Emily, Executive Assistant, ['ls'], `assistant_to`→kraussb), sara.hasson (Sara, ['ls']), leora.miller (Leora, ['ls']), rglassberg (Becca, Director of Technology, ['ls','hs']); plus divisions-only for deis/edaniel/ahyman (['ls']). **debra@saracademy.org left at `divisions=[]` intentionally** — she wasn't in the Phase A backfill list; she's exec director and may need ['ls','hs'] in a follow-up. New helper `lib/getWorkspaceMemberByAssignee.ts` (`getWorkspaceMembers`, `getMemberByAssigneeKey`, `getMemberByEmail`) replaces the 4 hardcoded Slack/mention rosters — `lib/slackNotifications.ts` no longer has the `TASK_USERS` map; `sendTaskSlack` signature changed to `(workspaceId, assignedTo, task)` with all 6 callers (tasks×2, agenda-notes×2, donor-notes, simchas) updated; `app/api/tasks/due-today/route.ts` now builds a per-workspace lookup map; `app/api/development/donor-notes/route.ts` server-side `MENTIONABLE_USERS` replaced with `getWorkspaceMembers()` (client-side `MENTIONABLE_USERS` in `DonorAnnotations.tsx` left alone for Phase B — no UI changes this phase); `app/api/simchas/send-note/route.ts` `EMILY_SLACK_ID` constant replaced with `getMemberByAssigneeKey`. New `lib/divisions.ts` (constants `DIVISION_LS`/`DIVISION_HS`, `HS_GRADE_NUMBERS`, `LS_GRADE_NUMBERS`, `HS_GRADING_PERIOD=29`; helpers `memberHasDivision`, `getGradeFilterForMember`, `shouldExcludeHs`). The 4 user-context HS-exclusion sites are now data-driven: `app/api/absences/route.ts` (3 query sites guarded by `excludeHs`), `app/api/admissions/route.ts`, `app/api/admissions/reenrollment/route.ts`, `app/api/lever/route.ts`. The 2 non-user-context sites (`absences/sync`, `webhook/lever`) intentionally left hardcoded — they're cache-population/webhook jobs without a current-user. Behavior identical to pre-change because all current task-assignable members have `divisions=['ls']` and the helper's `shouldExcludeHs` returns true for that and for empty divisions. **Gotchas for Phase B**: (a) DB DEFAULT for `divisions` is `'{}'` which `shouldExcludeHs` treats as "exclude HS" — fine for LS-only workspace, but when HS members are added in Phase D the column default should change to `'{ls}'` or new HS rows explicitly set `['hs']`; (b) the lowercase shim in `/api/agenda-notes` must be removed when Dashboard.tsx's lowercase reads are refactored; (c) `simchas/send-note` direct `supabaseAdmin.from('agenda_notes').insert` was updated to write Capitalized `'Emily'` to satisfy the new CHECK.
- **Cloud Run min-instances=1 to kill cold starts (2026-05-21)** — Homepage was loading slowly because Cloud Run was scaling to zero after ~15 min idle, costing 3-8s of Node/Next.js boot on the next hit. Set `--min-instances=1` on the `ssrrbkcmdcenter` service (applied live via `gcloud run services update`, deployed as revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00454-p7m`) and added the flag to the `gcloud run services update` step in `deploy.sh` so it persists across redeploys. One container is now always warm; first request post-idle should respond from Next.js immediately. Approx cost: $5-15/mo for a small always-on instance. Other slow-load contributors (sequential auth handshake before any paint, three separate fetches from home cards, third-party iframe in ThisWeekCard) were identified but deferred — try this single change first, revisit only if homepage still feels slow.
### Archive (2026-05-14 and earlier)

These entries pre-date the Phase A–F refactor session. Kept verbatim for blame/history. Anything from this window that's still load-bearing has been re-summarized in the structured section above.

- **Performance pass: lazy loading, shimmer skeletons, decoupled stat cards, PWA (2026-05-14)** — (1) **Lazy loading:** Simchas and Gemara data fetches now only fire when user navigates to those sections (useRef flag prevents refetch). Absences, admissions, lever, communications were already lazy. (2) **Shimmer skeletons:** New `app/components/ui/Shimmer.tsx` with `ShimmerBlock`, `ShimmerStatCards`, `ShimmerTableRows`, `ShimmerCards`. `@keyframes shimmer` added to globals.css. Replaced all `animate-pulse`/spinner loading states in CooperFundTab, IsraelFundTab, GuardianCirclePage, WeeklyGiftsTab, CampaignsTab, Communications section. (3) **Decoupled stat cards:** Guardian Circle API now supports `?view=summary` — fast aggregation query returning `{totalRaised, outstandingTotal, donorCount}` without building the full constituent list. GuardianCirclePage does two-phase fetch: summary first (stat cards render immediately), then full data (table populates). (4) **PWA:** Updated `manifest.json` (name: "Command Center by Lhasa", start_url: "/home", theme_color: "#1B3A6B"). Generated placeholder icons (192/512px navy squares). Layout theme-color updated to #1B3A6B.
- **Clickable Waitlist badges in Enrollment Projection (2026-05-14)** — Made Waitlist count badges in the By Grade table clickable (cursor-pointer, hover, stopPropagation). Clicking opens a `projection_waitlist` drilldown panel titled "[Grade] – Waitlist" showing applicants where `APPLICATION_STATUS_GROUPS.Waitlisted.includes(application_status)` (codes 6, 17, 18, 19) for that grade. Each card shows applicant name, Veracross link, status badge (e.g. "Waitlisted (Academy)"), and "New Family" badge. Lazy name-fetch added for the new drilldown type. Badges with count 0 show "—" (no hover, no click). Same pattern as Pending/Pisgah/Leaving drilldowns.
- **Donor History: confirmed all-years query (2026-05-04)** — Verified `/api/development/donor-history` already returns ALL gifts for a constituent (no date filter on gifts_cache query). Fiscal year grouping uses Jul 1–Jun 30 logic (month >= 6 → current year start, else previous year). Fiscal years sorted descending. Lifetime total sums all years. Dedup logic unchanged. TypeScript + build clean.
- **Weekly Gifts: cached fetch + parallel pagination (2026-04-23)** — Veracross gifts fetch (10k+ records) now cached via `unstable_cache` with 5-min TTL and `weekly-gifts` tag. Parallel pagination: fetches page 1 to read `X-Total-Count` header, then fetches remaining pages in parallel batches of 5 via `Promise.all`. Falls back to sequential if no total count header. Logs fetch duration. Filter/dedup/response logic unchanged. First load ~40s → subsequent loads <1s for 5 minutes.
- **Cloud Function: sync-gifts-internal URL → Cloud Run direct (2026-04-29)** — Changed the gifts sync Cloud Function URL from `rbk-cmd-center.web.app` (Firebase Hosting) to `ssrrbkcmdcenter-429508710310.us-east1.run.app` (Cloud Run direct). Bypasses Firebase Hosting's 60s timeout for the sync request.
- **Middleware: exclude sync routes from session check (2026-04-29)** — Added `api/development/sync-gifts-internal` and `api/absences/sync` to the middleware matcher exclusion list. These routes use Bearer token or internal secret auth (no session cookie) and were being blocked with "Not authenticated" by the middleware before reaching their own auth handlers.
- **Weekly Gifts: pending online gifts banner (2026-05-05)** — Added subtle amber info banner above the gifts table linking to Veracross pending online gifts view (`/results/1470528`). Hidden in print.
- **Donor panel: Veracross gift detail link (2026-05-05)** — Added "View full giving history in Veracross →" link at the bottom of the donor side panel giving history, linking to `/detail/development-constituent/{id}/4028-gift-detail`. Opens in new tab, separated by a border-t divider.
- **Development: backfill-gifts endpoint (2026-05-04)** — New `POST /api/development/backfill-gifts?years=5` endpoint for one-time historical gift data load. Auth via `X-Internal-Secret` + `X-Workspace-Id` headers. Returns 202 immediately, runs backfill in background via `after()`. Fetches all gifts from Veracross (paginated, up to 50 pages), upserts into `gifts_cache` in 500-row batches. Updates `gifts_sync_meta` on completion. Added to middleware exclusion list.
- **Development: Veracross links fix + Campaigns tab + Donor panel + default sort (2026-05-03)** — (1) Fixed Veracross Axiom links to use `/development-constituent/{id}/5011-general` for persons and `/organization-constituent/{id}/5011-general` for orgs (was `/constituent/{id}/273-general`). (2) New "Campaigns" tab on Development page: groups fiscal year gifts by event, shows total amount + count per campaign, sorted by amount desc, clickable to expand individual gifts. API: `?view=campaigns` param on weekly-gifts route. New `CampaignsTab.tsx` component. (3) Donor side panel: clicking a donor name opens a panel (same pattern as recruiting). Shows lifetime giving total, giving history grouped by fiscal year with subtotals, Veracross external link. New `/api/development/donor-history?constituentId={id}` endpoint. (4) Default sort changed from amount desc to date desc (most recent first).
- **Recruiting: clickable stale cards + badge rename + text color fix (2026-05-03)** — (1) Stale candidate cards (14+/30+/90+ Days) are now clickable filters. Selecting one filters the candidate list to that staleness tier. Mutually exclusive with stage cards — selecting stale clears stage and vice versa. Active stale card fills with its color + white text. (2) Header badge renamed from "New This Week" to "New Applicants This Week". (3) Fixed stage card text contrast: selected cards now explicitly set label to `rgba(255,255,255,0.85)` and count to `white` instead of relying on color inheritance. Same fix for ALL card and stale cards when active. New state: `leverStaleFilter` ('14' | '30' | '90' | null).
- **Recruiting: pipeline overview redesign + stale candidates + header badges (2026-05-03)** — (1) Summary metrics moved to page header as inline badges ("44 Open Positions" + "25 New This Week") next to Refresh button. Removed "Active Pipeline" and "Total 1yr" pills. (2) Stage cards redesigned as "Pipeline Overview" with SAR brand colors: 2px colored borders, hover fills with color + white text, selected stays filled, responsive grid (no horizontal scroll), custom tooltips with descriptions. Color palette cycles: deep blue, orange, teal, green, blue, magenta, slate, amber. (3) "Needs Attention" stale candidate tier cards: 14+ days (amber), 30+ days (orange), 90+ days (red) — computed from `lastInteractionAt` field on opportunities. Show count, grayed out with 0. Respects active filters. Custom tooltips. (4) Stage counts still reflect filtered state (team + position + search).
- **Recruiting: cascading filters + moved summary pills + dynamic stage counts (2026-05-01)** — (1) Cascading filters: Position dropdown only shows positions from the selected team's postings. Changing team resets position to "All". (2) Stage pill counts now reflect the filtered state (team + position + search applied BEFORE stage counting). "ALL" card shows `preStageFiltered.length`. (3) Summary pills (Open Roles, New This Week, Active Pipeline, Total) moved from main content area to a 2x2 grid above the Open Roles card in the right column. Always show full unfiltered totals. Styled smaller (text-xs, rounded-lg). (4) Filter pipeline: team+position+search → preStageFiltered → stage pills count from this → stage selection → final display.
- **Recruiting: remove stage dropdown, widen search, show all candidates (2026-05-01)** — (1) Removed Stage dropdown from filter row — stage filtering now exclusively via the clickable pipeline stage cards (which were already functional). (2) Search bar widened to `md:flex-[2]` (~50% of row). (3) Removed `.slice(0, 30/50)` display limit — all filtered candidates now show. (4) Lever API route (`/api/lever`) now paginates all opportunities using `hasNext`/`next` offset loop (was limited to first 100). Logs total fetched count. (5) Candidate list header changed from "Recent Applications" to "All Candidates" when no stage selected.
- **Recruiting: dropdown filters redesign (2026-05-01)** — Replaced the collapsible pill-based filter sections with clean inline dropdown selects. Layout: search bar (flex-1) + Team dropdown + Stage dropdown + Position dropdown in one horizontal row (stacks on mobile). Active dropdowns get `bg-blue-50 border-blue-300` tint. "Showing X of Y candidates" count + "Clear all filters" link below the row. Removed `leverFilterExpanded` state — no longer needed.
- **Recruiting: collapsible filters + position filter + Lever notes (2026-05-01)** — (1) Team, Stage, and new Position filter pills are now in collapsible sections with chevron toggles (collapsed by default). Active filter shown as badge when collapsed. (2) New "Filter by Position" section with dynamically generated pills from unique posting titles. AND logic with team, stage, and search. (3) New `/api/recruiting/notes` route (GET + POST): fetches/posts notes via Lever API `opportunities/{id}/notes`. POST supports `perform_as` for user attribution using `LEVER_USER_MAP` (RBK + Emily Gray mapped). (4) Candidate detail panel now shows Notes section: lazy-loaded when panel opens, reverse-chronological list with author + relative timestamp, textarea + "Post Note" button for adding notes. State: `leverNotes`, `leverNotesLoading`, `leverNoteText`, `leverNotePosting`, `leverPositionFilter`, `leverFilterExpanded`.
- **Home page SAR brand redesign (2026-04-30)** — Full visual refresh of /home inspired by SAR Academy brand colors (deep blue #1B3A6B, orange #E87722, teal #00A5B5, green #7AB648, magenta #E91E8C). Changes: (1) WelcomeHeader: 4px rainbow accent bar at top (SAR brand gradient), greeting in bold deep blue 36px Source Serif, larger time-of-day icons with warm colors, date below. (2) WeekCalendarPeek: white rounded-2xl card, orange left accent on header, today highlighted with orange border + badge, hover effects on rows. (3) DashboardsGrid: tiles with colored top borders rotating through brand colors, bold icon circles with matching accent colors, rounded-2xl with shadow + hover lift. (4) TodayHero: deep blue left accent bar, section label in deep blue with orange dot. (5) MyProjectsList: heading in deep blue, cards rounded-2xl with subtle shadow, warm empty state with emoji. (6) Page background: bg-stone-50 (warm gray) instead of pure white.
- **Home page: "This Week at SAR" + greeting icon (2026-04-30)** — Rebuilt `WeekCalendarPeek` from a 4-card grid to a day-by-day grouped list inside a white card. Events grouped by date (Mon-Fri), today highlighted with blue left border + "Today" badge. Each event shows time + title. Max height 320px with scroll. Moved from bottom of /home to between TodayHero and MyProjectsList. Added time-of-day icon to `WelcomeHeader`: sun (morning), coffee (afternoon), sunset (evening), moon (night) — inline SVG icons. Added diagnostic logs to `/api/home/week-events` to debug why iCal feed returns empty for current week.

(2) Stage filter pills — dynamically generated from pipeline stage cards, replaces the old stage card click interaction with consistent pill styling. AND logic with team filter. (3) Search bar — filters candidate name, position title, and tags. Case-insensitive partial match. Placeholder "Search candidates, positions...". Summary pills always show full unfiltered counts. State: `leverTeamFilter`, `leverSearchTerm`. Candidate list uses `filteredDisplayOpps` which applies team + stage + search filters.
- **Absences charts layout refresh (2026-04-30)** — Updated the Attendance Overview charts layout: changed from equal `grid-cols-3` to flex layout with fixed donut (220px), flex-1 bar chart, fixed top 10 (320px). Wrapped all three in a single white card with `rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6`. Inner cards simplified to `border-slate-100` without shadow. Present color changed from `#22c55e` to `#10b981` (emerald). Stacks vertically on mobile. No API changes — charts data was already working after the pagination fix.
- **Admissions Overview: search bar (2026-04-30)** — Added search bar at top of Admissions Overview tab, between summary pills and the two-column layout. Filters grade rows by applicant name (from `applicantNames` cache), grade label, status label, and city (from `admissionsCities`). Case-insensitive partial match. Summary pills always show full unfiltered counts. Search clears when switching tabs. Empty state shows "No matching applicants found" when search yields no results. State: `admissionsSearchTerm`.
- **Weekly Gifts: hard_credit_gift_id dedup + constituent type links (2026-04-30)** — (1) Soft credit dedup now uses `hard_credit_gift_id` linkage: groups gifts by root gift ID (soft credits reference their hard credit via `hard_credit_gift_id`). Within each group, prefers `soft_credit_type === 1` (primary individual soft credit), then `gift_type === 1` (hard credit). Correctly collapses org→individual soft credit pairs (e.g. "Geller Family Giving Account" hard credit + "Geller, Lea" soft credit). Fallback secondary dedup by `constituent_id|date|amount|event/fund` for gifts without linkage. (2) Added `constituentType` field ('person' | 'organization') to display records, derived from `raw_data.constituent_record_type`. Veracross link now uses `/constituent/` for persons and `/organization-constituent/` for organizations.
- **Weekly Gifts: search bar (2026-04-29)** — Added search bar below date preset pills, above summary cards. Filters table rows by donor name, fund, event, amount, or note text (case-insensitive partial match). Summary cards always show full unfiltered totals. Clear button (×) when text entered. "No matching gifts found" when search yields no results. Hidden in print.
- **Fix: soft credit dedup key includes constituent_id (2026-04-29)** — The dedup grouping key was `date|amount|event/fund` which incorrectly collapsed different donors who gave the same amount on the same date to the same fund. Fixed: key is now `constituent_id|date|amount|event/fund`. Only hard credit (type 1) and soft credit (type 3) rows for the SAME donor are collapsed. Also removed all `[WG DIAG]` and `[WEEKLY-GIFTS DEBUG]` diagnostic logs.
- **Online gifts diagnostic route (2026-04-29)** — TEMPORARY route at `app/api/development/online-gifts-diagnostic/route.ts`. Becca-only. Fetches Veracross dev token with additional scopes `development.online_gifts:list development.online_gifts:read`. Tries three endpoint paths (`/v3/development/online_gifts`, `/v3/development/online-gifts`, `/v3/online_gifts`). Logs field names, sample records, unique statuses/gift_types. Returns field list only in HTTP response (no PII). Will be deleted after confirming endpoint shape.
- **Enrollment by City donut chart (2026-05-06)** — Collapsible "Enrollment by City" section below the by-grade table. Recharts `PieChart` donut (innerRadius=60, outerRadius=100) with SAR palette colors. Total student count centered in donut. Small cities (<2%) grouped into "Other" slice. Hover tooltip shows city, count, percentage. Click a slice or legend item to open a side panel (same card width, `lg:w-1/2`). Side panel has: city name + count header, search bar, grade filter pills, student cards with Veracross links, source badges (New Family/Returning), status labels. Legend below donut as 2-col grid, clickable. Data includes ALL students from the by-grade table: re-enrollment students + new accepted applicants. Students without city data grouped as "Unknown". New applicant fallback name: "New Applicant" (not raw ID). State: `projectionCityExpanded`, `projectionCityDrilldown`, `projectionCityGradeFilter`, `projectionCitySearch`.
- **Editable Budgeted column in By Grade table (2026-05-06)** — New `enrollment_budget` Supabase table (`workspace_id`, `grade_code`, `budgeted_count`, `school_year`, UNIQUE on `workspace_id,grade_code,school_year`). API: `GET/PATCH /api/admissions/budget` (session auth, viewer role blocked from PATCH). UI: "Budgeted" column after "25-26" in the By Grade table. Cells are inline-editable for owners/assistants (click to edit, Enter/blur to save, Escape to cancel). Shows comparison: green checkmark if Registered >= Budgeted, red gap number if below. Empty cells show "—" with pencil icon on hover. TOTALS row includes budgeted sum. State: `enrollmentBudget`, `budgetEditingGrade`, `budgetEditValue`, `budgetSavedGrade`.
- **Pisgah toggle (2026-05-11)** — `is_pisgah` boolean column added to `enrollment_grade_overrides`. API accepts `isPisgah` in PATCH (can flag Pisgah without changing grade). Drilldown panel shows amber "Pisgah" pill button (toggle on/off). By-grade Pisgah count uses `r.pisgah || gradeOverrides[id]?.is_pisgah`. Removing Pisgah on a record with no grade override deletes the row. `togglePisgah()` function handles both cases.
- **Tasks: Create panel, realtime sync, source fix (2026-05-12)** — (1) "Add" button opens task side panel in create mode (`taskPanelMode: 'edit' | 'create'`). Create panel has: auto-focused title input, assignee toggle (RBK/Emily), due date picker, urgent toggle, "Manual" source badge, Create Task/Cancel buttons. Optimistic add on save. (2) Source badges are correct: `email_id ? 'email' : agenda_item_id ? 'agenda' : 'manual'`. "Budget and Finance" was a topic name from the agenda — correct behavior, not a bug. (3) Supabase Realtime subscription on `agenda_notes` table (`postgres_changes`, filter `workspace_id=eq.${workspaceId}`). Silent re-fetch on any INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE. Green "Live" dot in Tasks header when subscribed. Channel cleanup on unmount. Also syncs to To-Do Today section.
- **Tasks inline Add button (2026-05-11)** — Replaced floating FAB with inline `h-7 px-3 text-xs` blue button in Tasks page header.
- **Grade override system (2026-05-11)** — `enrollment_grade_overrides` table (`workspace_id`, `student_id`, `student_name`, `original_grade`, `override_grade`, `school_year`, `reason`, `updated_by`, UNIQUE on `workspace_id,student_id,school_year`). API: `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/admissions/grade-overrides`. Applied in Dashboard.tsx AFTER projectionRows are built but BEFORE grouping — overrides each student's `gradeKey`. Drilldown panel shows "Move to grade" dropdown for users with `edit_enrollment_data` sub-permission (or owners). "Moved from [grade]" amber badge on overridden students. "Revert" button to remove override. By-grade table shows pencil icon on grade rows with overrides. Overrides also applied to drilldown grade filter via `effectiveGrade()` helper. City donut chart uses overridden grades too (via projectionRows). State: `gradeOverrides`, `overrideDropdownId`.
- **Enrollment By Grade totals row + Waitlist fix (2026-05-06)** — Added TOTALS row in `<tfoot>` of the By Grade table: sums Registered, Incomplete, Total, Pisgah, Pending, Waitlist, Leaving, and 25-26 across all grades. Bold font, bg-slate-50, border-t-2. Fixed Waitlist column: was filtering `application_decision_response === 6` (wrong field), now correctly uses `APPLICATION_STATUS_GROUPS.Waitlisted.includes(application_status)` (codes 6, 17, 18, 19). Added diagnostic `console.log` in admissions API for unique `application_status` values.
- **Clickable Incomplete badge in Enrollment Projection (2026-04-28)** — Made the Incomplete count badge in the By Grade table clickable (cursor-pointer, hover:opacity-80, stopPropagation). Opens drilldown panel titled "[Grade] – Registration Incomplete" showing both new applicants (response codes 4/9/1) and re-enrollments (statuses 2/3/4/6) for that grade, with source badges (New Family/Returning) and Veracross links. New drilldown type `projection_incomplete` added to the union type and to the lazy name-fetch useEffect. Matches the existing Pending/Pisgah/Leaving drilldown pattern.
- **Permissions page: save feedback (2026-04-28)** — Toggle saves now show: (1) typed toast — green "Saved" (2s) on success, red error (3s) on failure. (2) Inline indicator next to the toggle — blue pulsing dot while saving, green checkmark for 1.5s after save. Uses `savingKey`/`savedKey` state keyed by `memberId:moduleKey`. No layout shift — indicators positioned absolutely in a fixed-width slot.
- **Fix: attendance sync column mismatch (2026-04-28)** — The sync route mapped Veracross `person` field directly as a column name, but the `attendance_cache` table column is `name`. Every upsert silently failed with "Could not find the 'person' column". Fixed: `fetchAttendanceForDate()` now explicitly maps `r.person → name` in the row object. Also fixed the absences API `topAbsentees` query which selected `person` column — changed to `name`. Updated CLAUDE_CONTEXT.md table schema.
- **Attendance sync: background backfill via after() (2026-04-28)** — The `?days=N` backfill mode now returns 202 immediately and runs the backfill in the background using Next.js `after()` hook. Previously the endpoint blocked for the full backfill duration (~5+ min for 60 days), hitting Firebase Hosting's 60s first-byte timeout. Backfill progress logged per-day as `[ABSENCES BACKFILL] Date YYYY-MM-DD rows: N`. Daily mode and full backfill mode unchanged (still synchronous — they're fast enough).
- **Attendance sync: auth fix + days param (2026-04-28)** — Auth now checks Bearer token first (Cloud Function), then falls back to session check for Becca-only browser access. Previously failed silently when `SYNC_SECRET` env var was not set on Cloud Run. Added `?days=N` query param: backfills past N days (weekdays only, 300ms delay). Existing `?mode=daily` and `?mode=backfill` unchanged. Diagnostic log on auth failure. **Env var required:** `SYNC_SECRET=rbk-sync-2026` must be set on Cloud Run.
- **Fix: Absences charts blank — Supabase 1000-row limit (2026-04-28)** — The "Absences This Month" and "Most Absent Students YTD" charts were blank because their Supabase queries hit the default 1000-row limit (attendance_cache has 93,685 rows). Both queries now use while-loop pagination with `.range()` in 1000-row chunks, same pattern as the gifts_cache fix. The donut chart was unaffected because it uses live Veracross data, not the cache.
- **Fix: Refresh button spinner stuck + Cloud Run timeout (2026-04-28)** — Refresh handler rewritten with `AbortController` (90s client-side timeout), `finally` block that always clears spinner state, and diagnostic `[REFRESH]` console.logs at each step. Cloud Run timeout bumped from 60s to 300s (`gcloud run services update --timeout=300`) — the 60s default was killing sync requests that took 25-45s on spikes. **(Superseded 2026-09-03: this manual bump did NOT persist — the Firebase hosting step resets the SSR service to the 60s frameworks default on every deploy, and the service was found back at 60s. `--timeout=300` is now pinned in `deploy.sh` instead. See the September restart entry.)** AbortError shows specific "took too long" toast vs generic error toast.
- **Fix: Sidebar module visibility during impersonation (2026-04-28)** — Two bugs: (1) Operations section was hidden entirely for viewers (`!(role === 'viewer' && allowedModules)`), preventing Sara from seeing Development/Projects even with those modules enabled. Fixed: Operations section now shows if the viewer has any operations module (projects/development/lever) in effectiveModules. Added `moduleKey` to Projects and Lever nav items so they filter like Development. Emily's Queue hidden for viewers. (2) Admin "Permissions" link showed during impersonation because `isAdmin` checked real user email. Split into `isRealAdmin` (for impersonation dropdown — stays visible for Becca always) and `isAdmin` (for Permissions link — hidden during impersonation). Diagnostic log added: `[SIDEBAR DIAG]` showing effective role, allowedModules, effectiveModules, impersonating email.
- **Fix: Projects tile navigating to Admissions for viewers (2026-04-28)** — Race condition between URL cleanup effect and viewer redirect effect. The `useState` initializer for `activeNav` returns `'dashboard'` during SSR (no `window`), and React hydration reuses that value. The viewer redirect effect saw `activeNav='dashboard'` and redirected to the first allowed module (admissions) before the URL cleanup effect could set `activeNav='projects'` from `?nav=`. Fix: viewer redirect now checks `window.location.search` for a `?nav=` param and skips if present, letting the URL cleanup effect handle navigation.
- **Auth: case-insensitive email lookup on both checks (2026-04-28)** — The initial auth check already used `.ilike()` but the fallback workspace_members email lookup (for placeholder user_id replacement) still used `.eq('email', email)` — exact match. Changed to `.ilike('email', email.trim())`. Also improved the auth check to use `{ count: 'exact', head: true }` for efficiency. The placeholder user_id replacement (PLACEHOLDER → real Firebase UID) is already handled in the fallback path at lines 91-98.
- **Scheduled gifts sync Cloud Functions (2026-04-27)** — Two new scheduled functions in `functions/src/index.ts`: `syncGiftsHourlyWeekdays` (hourly Mon-Fri ET) and `syncGiftsDailyWeekends` (6 AM Sat-Sun ET). Both call `runGiftsSyncForAllWorkspaces()` which queries all workspaces with `modules.development === true`, then calls `POST /api/development/sync-gifts-internal` for each. New internal endpoint `app/api/development/sync-gifts-internal/route.ts` authenticates via `X-Internal-Secret` header (shared secret, no user session) + `X-Workspace-Id` header. Calls `syncGiftsForWorkspace()` from `lib/syncGifts.ts`. **Env var required:** `INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET` must be set on both Cloud Run and in `functions/.env`.
- **Weekly Gifts: paginated Supabase query + Refresh wired to sync (2026-04-27)** — Replaced single `.range(0, 49999)` query with a while-loop that paginates in chunks of 1000 to bypass Supabase's server-side row limit. Refresh button now calls `POST /api/development/sync-gifts` (Veracross → gifts_cache), then refetches from cache. Shows spinner during the ~25s sync, toast "Synced N gifts" on success, error toast on failure.
- **Weekly Gifts: Supabase row limit fix + order (2026-04-27)** — gifts_cache query changed to `select('*')` with `.order('date', { ascending: false }).range(0, 49999)`. Previously Supabase's default 1000-row limit was silently capping the result set, causing 0 gifts after date filtering (only oldest 1000 rows returned, none in the past 7 days). Diagnostic logs retained to confirm fix.
- **Weekly Gifts reads from gifts_cache (2026-04-27)** — `/api/development/weekly-gifts` now reads from Supabase `gifts_cache` table instead of fetching from Veracross directly. Removed all Veracross OAuth/fetch/cache logic from this route (that now lives in `lib/syncGifts.ts` called by `/api/development/sync-gifts`). All transformation logic preserved (date filter, gift type filter, in-kind exclusion, soft credit dedup, display fields, flags, sorting, summary, notes). Added `syncStatus` field to response from `gifts_sync_meta`. Removed `?refresh=true` param (refresh now uses the separate sync endpoint). Response is now instant (~100ms) since it reads from Supabase.
- **Gifts sync helper + on-demand sync endpoint (2026-04-27)** — New `lib/syncGifts.ts` with `syncGiftsForWorkspace(workspaceId)`: fetches all gifts from Veracross (OAuth + parallel pagination), upserts into `gifts_cache` table in batches of 500 (`onConflict: 'id'`), updates `gifts_sync_meta` with status/count/error. New `POST /api/development/sync-gifts` endpoint: auth + development module gated, calls `syncGiftsForWorkspace`, returns `{ success, count, last_sync_at }`. Existing `/api/development/weekly-gifts` still reads directly from Veracross (will switch to cache in next prompt). **SQL required:** `CREATE TABLE gifts_cache (id integer PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id uuid NOT NULL, gift_type integer, constituent_id integer, constituent_name text, date text, amount numeric, fund text, event text, fundraising_activity text, apply_to_pledge boolean, anonymous boolean, in_kind_gift_description text, soft_credit_type integer, hard_credit_gift_id integer, raw_data jsonb, synced_at timestamptz); CREATE TABLE gifts_sync_meta (workspace_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES workspaces(id), last_sync_at timestamptz, last_sync_status text, last_sync_count integer, last_sync_error text);`
- **Weekly Gifts: refresh button + last-synced indicator (2026-04-27)** — API: `?refresh=true` param bypasses `unstable_cache` and forces fresh Veracross fetch. Added `fetchedAt` (ISO) to response reflecting actual fetch time. Core fetch extracted into `fetchAllGiftsUncached()` returning `{ gifts, fetchedAt }`, with `fetchAllGiftsCached` as the `unstable_cache` wrapper. UI: Refresh button with spinner (animate-spin while loading) + "Last synced X min ago" label next to Print button. Relative time auto-updates every 30s via setInterval. Date preset pills (7/14/30) use normal cached fetch; Refresh button forces `?refresh=true`.
- **Removed generic Refresh button + Live indicator from Dashboard header (2026-04-27)** — Removed the "Refresh" button (called `refreshEmails`) and the green pulsing "Live" dot (`isConnected`) from the welcome header. Each page has its own page-specific refresh. The `useRealtimeEmails` hook and Supabase realtime subscription are unchanged — only the visual indicator was removed.
- **Auth: case-insensitive email lookup for workspace_members (2026-04-27)** — Sara Hasson couldn't sign in because `workspace_members` lookup used `.eq('email', email)` (exact match). Changed to `.ilike('email', email.trim())` for case-insensitive + whitespace-trimmed comparison. Added `console.log('[AUTH] Access denied for email:', email, 'matched rows: 0')` before 403 for debugging. Uses `supabaseAdmin` (service role) so RLS doesn't block the lookup.
- **Weekly Gifts: print layout + clickable card filters + column alignment (2026-04-23)** — (1) Print: sidebar gets `print:hidden`, Dashboard header gets `print:hidden`, Type and Note columns hidden via `print:hidden`. Row tints/shadows cleared for print. Table border cleaned up. (2) Summary cards are now clickable filters: "New Gifts" filters to non-pledge, "Pledge Payments" filters to pledge, "Total" clears filter. Active card gets `ring-2 ring-slate-900 ring-offset-2`. Summary amounts always show full totals. (3) Column alignment: replaced `<table>` with flexbox rows sharing a `COL` width map (donor flex-3, amount 100px, date 80px, event flex-3.5, type 140px, note 48px). Header and data rows use identical widths. Event/Fund column truncates properly.
- **Permissions page: Development column (2026-04-23)** — Added "Development" column to admin permissions grid, positioned after Projects. Same toggle pattern as other module columns. Toggles `development` key in `allowed_modules`.
- **Development page UI + Weekly Gifts tab (2026-04-23)** — New `app/components/development/DevelopmentPage.tsx` with tab structure (Weekly Gifts first/only tab). `WeeklyGiftsTab.tsx` fetches from `/api/development/weekly-gifts?days={7|14|30}` via apiFetch. Summary cards (Total/New/Pledge), sortable table with donor Veracross links, type badges (PLEDGE/SC/ANON/REFUND), inline note popover (save/delete/cancel). Top 3 by amount get gold tint, refunds get red tint. Date preset pills (7/14/30 days). Print button with `print:hidden` on non-table elements. Sidebar: "Development" nav item added to Operations group with dollar-sign icon, module-gated to `development`. Dashboard.tsx: `'development'` added to VALID_NAV_IDS, renders DevelopmentPage when active. `/api/home` DASHBOARD_META: added development tile (green tone). API: `?days=` param added to weekly-gifts route (valid: 7/14/30, default 7), included in dateRange response. New `lib/format.ts` with `formatMoney()` helper.
- **Weekly Gifts API + gift notes (2026-04-23)** — New `/api/development/weekly-gifts` GET route and `/api/development/gift-notes` POST/DELETE route. Weekly gifts fetches all gifts from Veracross `/v3/development/gifts` (paginated, no server-side date filter — Veracross rejects query params), filters client-side to past 7 days (ET timezone), cash gift types only (Donation, Donation Soft-Credit, Pledge Installment, Plg Soft-Credit), excludes in-kind. Soft credit deduplication groups by date+amount+event/fund and keeps the soft credit row. Display fields: displayName from constituent_name, displayEvent from event||fund. Attaches notes from Supabase `gift_notes` table. Module-gated to `development`. Gift notes route upserts on `gift_id+workspace_id`, always records real author email (not impersonated). Diagnostic route deleted.
- **Impersonation fix: /api/home uses impersonated identity (2026-04-22)** — `/api/home` now uses the impersonated user's email, workspace_id, display_name, and allowed_modules when impersonation is active. New `getEffectiveMembership()` helper in `lib/impersonate.ts` reads both `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` and `X-Impersonated-Email` headers, queries `workspace_members` for the target's full membership row. `lib/apiFetch.ts` now sends `X-Impersonated-Email` header alongside the workspace header. Projects query, firstName derivation, and dashboards list all use the impersonated identity. WelcomeHeader confirmed to read from prop (resolves automatically).
- **Viewer sidebar + navigation fixes (2026-04-22)** — (1) Added "Home" nav item at top of sidebar for viewer role (home icon, highlighted when pathname === '/home'). Hidden for owner/assistant. (2) Sidebar nav clicks are now route-aware: if current pathname !== '/', clicks use `router.push('/?nav={id}')` instead of `setActiveNav()` directly, fixing navigation from /home to Dashboard pages. Uses `usePathname()` from next/navigation. (3) `app/page.tsx` viewer redirect now checks for `?nav=` or `?projectPanel=` search params — if present, skips redirect and lets Dashboard process the params. This fixes dashboard tile clicks from /home bouncing back to /home for viewers.
- **Enrollment Projection: informational zone visual treatment (2026-04-21)** — De-emphasized the informational columns (Pisgah, Pending, Waitlist, Leaving, 25-26) in the By Grade table. Added: (1) "FOR REFERENCE" super-header row spanning all 5 informational columns (text-[10px] slate-400 tracking-wider uppercase). (2) Vertical 1px border-l border-slate-200 divider between Progress and Pisgah columns (header + all data rows). (3) All informational column headers now slate-400 italic (previously mixed colors). (4) Data cells in informational zone at opacity 0.75, with clickable badges at full opacity and hover restoring parent cell to opacity 1.0. No calculation or logic changes.
- **Clickable Pending + Pisgah badges in Enrollment Projection (2026-04-21)** — Made Pending Review and Pisgah count badges in the By Grade table clickable (cursor-pointer, hover:opacity-80, stopPropagation). Pending click opens drilldown filtered to `application_status === 1` for that grade, titled "[Grade] – Pending Review". Pisgah click opens drilldown filtered to `student_group_applying_for === 1`, titled "[Grade] – Pisgah". Both use the same slide-in panel pattern as the existing Leaving drilldown. New drilldown types: `projection_pending` and `projection_pisgah` added to the union type and to the lazy name-fetch useEffect. Badges with count 0 show "—" dash (no hover, no click).
- **Admissions Overview drilldown filter pills (2026-04-21)** — Added filter pills to the Admissions Overview grade drilldown panel: "All", "Enrollment Complete (N)", "Pending (N)", "Declined Offer (N)". Filters: enrolled = `application_decision_response === 2`, pending = `application_status === 1`, declined = `application_decision_response === 3`. Same pill style as Enrollment Projection drilldown (rounded-full, blue-50 active state). New state: `overviewDrilldownFilter` ('all' | 'enrolled' | 'pending' | 'declined'), resets on drilldown change. Pills only shown when `admissionsDrilldown.type === 'grade'`.
- **Dashboard welcome header uses workspace displayName (2026-04-21)** — Changed "Welcome back, {name}" from `user?.displayName` (Firebase auth, always the real user) to `wsDisplayName` from `useWorkspace().displayName` (returns impersonated name when active). Falls back to email prefix if null. Now shows impersonated user's name during impersonation.
- **Impersonation fix: context propagation + banner + modules (2026-04-21)** — Fixed impersonation not being functional. Root causes: (1) Initial workspace state had `impersonating: null` and no-op callbacks — now reads from `readImpersonation()` helper on init so first render has correct values. (2) `ImpersonationBanner` now reads localStorage directly as fallback for first render (context may not propagate until `loadWorkspaceFromSession` completes). (3) When impersonating, AuthProvider now fetches the impersonated member's `allowed_modules` from `/api/admin/workspace-members` and uses them for `getEffectiveModules()` — so Debra's viewer restrictions are applied correctly. (4) Debug console.log in Sidebar when impersonation is active showing `impersonating`, `role`, `effectiveModules`, `allowedModules`, `workspaceId`.
- **Admin impersonation + permissions workspace column + sidebar entry (2026-04-21)** — (1) **Permissions page**: added Workspace column after Name & Role, sort primary by workspace_name then display_name. (2) **Admin sidebar**: "Admin" section with "Permissions" nav item shown only for `rglassberg@saracademy.org`, uses key icon, navigates to `/admin/permissions`. (3) **Impersonation system**: Added `impersonating`, `startImpersonation`, `stopImpersonation` to `WorkspaceContext` in AuthProvider.tsx. Client-side only — no cookie/server change. Persisted in localStorage as `impersonation`, cleared on sign-out. When impersonating, context returns impersonated `workspaceId`, `role`, `displayName`. Firebase user stays unchanged. Admin sidebar shows impersonation dropdown listing all workspace members (fetched from `/api/admin/workspace-members`), with "No impersonation (myself)" option. Non-admin users see unchanged workspace switcher. (4) **ImpersonationBanner.tsx**: amber banner at top of every page when impersonating, showing target name/email/role/workspace + "Exit impersonation" button. Rendered from `layout.tsx` inside AuthProvider. (5) **lib/apiFetch.ts**: fetch wrapper that reads impersonation from localStorage and adds `X-Impersonated-Workspace-Id` header. (6) **lib/impersonate.ts**: `getEffectiveWorkspaceId()` server helper — reads impersonation header, only honors it if authenticated user is admin email. Updated in `/api/home/route.ts` and `/api/projects/route.ts` GET.
- **Admin permissions page (2026-04-21)** — New `/admin/permissions` page at `app/admin/permissions/page.tsx`. Admin-only: client-side guard checks `user.email === rglassberg@saracademy.org`, redirects non-admins to `/home`. Table shows all workspace members alphabetically with toggle checkboxes for 6 dashboard modules (admissions, absences, lever, faculty_absences, simchas, projects). Owners/assistants show disabled grayed-out checkmarks with tooltip. Viewers get interactive toggles that update `allowed_modules` JSONB on `workspace_members`. Optimistic updates with error revert + toast. New API routes: `GET /api/admin/workspace-members` (fetches all members with workspace name join, admin email check), `PATCH /api/admin/workspace-members/[id]` (updates `allowed_modules`, admin email check). Both return 403 for non-admin. No sidebar nav item — accessed directly via URL.
- **Home page wired to real data + navigation + viewer routing (2026-04-21)** — (1) New `app/api/home/week-events/route.ts`: fetches SAR school calendar iCal feed (`accalendars@saracademy.org`), parses VEVENT blocks (same pattern as simchas route), filters to current week Mon-Sun, returns up to 8 events sorted by start time. 5-min cache. Non-fatal on failure. (2) `app/home/page.tsx` now fetches `/api/home` and `/api/home/week-events` in parallel on mount, replaces all placeholder data. Loading skeleton shown while fetching, red retry banner on error. Calendar events transformed for `WeekCalendarPeek` (date/title/time format). (3) Dashboard tile click navigates via `router.push('/?nav={id}')`. Project row/card click navigates via `router.push('/?nav=projects&projectPanel={id}')`. (4) `Dashboard.tsx` reads `?nav=` and `?projectPanel=` URL search params on mount: sets `activeNav` from `?nav=`, stores `?projectPanel=` in `pendingProjectPanelRef` and opens the project side panel when projects finish loading. Cleans URL with `replaceState` after reading. (5) `TodayHero.tsx` now accepts `onOpenProject` prop instead of hardcoded `console.log`. (6) Viewer routing: `app/page.tsx` checks `session.role === 'viewer'` after auth and redirects to `/home`. Owners/assistants stay on `/`. No auth gate on `/home` itself (anyone can visit it).
- **Home page full-width layout (2026-04-21)** — Removed max-w-[1400px] cap from /home content wrapper. Content now fills available width with `px-8 lg:px-12 2xl:pr-16` padding. DashboardsGrid scales to 6 columns on 2xl: `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6`.
- **Home page layout widened + type scaled (2026-04-21)** — Widened /home content area from max-w-[1080px] to max-w-[1400px], reduced px-16 to px-10. WelcomeHeader greeting 26→32px, date 13→14px. DashboardsGrid grid-cols-4→xl:grid-cols-5, tile padding 14/16/16→16/18/18, name 13.5→14.5px, description 12→12.5px. MyProjectsList heading 18→20px, row padding 14/20→16/24, title 14→15px. TodayHero card padding 20/22→24/26, title 15→16.5px, description 12.5→13px. Pure layout/typography, no logic changes.
- **Home page scaffolded + Sidebar extracted (2026-04-21)** — New `/home` page at `app/home/page.tsx` for non-RBK users. Components in `app/components/home/`: `WelcomeHeader.tsx` (greeting by time of day + date, Source Serif 4 font), `TodayHero.tsx` (due-this-week / recently-updated projects, max 2 cards with ProgressRing), `MyProjectsList.tsx` (grouped by In progress / On hold / Complete, collapsible with localStorage persist as `homeProjectGroupExpanded`), `DashboardsGrid.tsx` (tinted tile grid of available modules), `WeekCalendarPeek.tsx` (4-card calendar strip), `ProgressRing.tsx` (reusable SVG ring). **Sidebar refactor:** Extracted sidebar JSX from Dashboard.tsx into `app/components/Sidebar.tsx` (accepts props: user, activeNav, setActiveNav, role, allowedModules, effectiveModules, workspaceId, workspaces, switchWorkspace, signOut, unreadCount, emilyQueueCount, onCompose, mounted). Dashboard.tsx imports and uses the new Sidebar component — no behavior change. Home page also uses Sidebar with navigation redirecting to `/#nav` for Dashboard pages. Source Serif 4 font added to `app/layout.tsx` via next/font/google (CSS var `--font-source-serif`). Page uses placeholder data — real API wiring comes next. Design tokens: page bg #fbfbfa, accent oklch(0.58 0.12 235), slate-150 #e8edf3. Loading skeleton and error banner included.
- **GET /api/home endpoint (2026-04-21)** — New standalone API route at `app/api/home/route.ts` powering the upcoming /home landing page for non-RBK users. Returns `{ firstName, projects, dashboards }`. Fetches display_name from `workspace_members` to derive firstName. Projects query: `workspace_id` scoped, excludes archived, filtered by `assignee_email = currentEmail OR currentEmail = ANY(team_emails)`, ordered by created_at desc. Each project includes `isMine` boolean. Dashboards built from effective modules via `getEffectiveModules()` mapped to static metadata (admissions/blue, absences/amber, lever/violet, faculty_absences/teal, projects/slate). Projects dashboard always included. Auth required (401 without session).
- **Projects: assignee_email + team_emails columns (2026-04-21)** — Added `assignee_email` (text, nullable) and `team_emails` (text[], default `'{}'`) columns to `projects` table. API route updated: POST accepts optional `assignee_email` and `team_emails`; PATCH allows updating both fields; GET returns them via `select('*')`. `Project` TypeScript interface in Dashboard.tsx updated with optional fields. Existing `assignee` field ('rbk'|'emily') unchanged for backward compatibility. Data-layer prep for user-level project assignment on the upcoming /home page.
- **Drafts to Approve section on Tasks page (2026-04-20)** — New collapsible "Drafts to Approve" section pinned to the top of the RBK column on the Tasks page. Collapsed by default, state persisted to localStorage as `draftsToApproveCollapsed`. Shows emails where `draft_status === 'draft_ready' && status !== 'done' && status !== 'junk'` (same core filter as `draftsReady` but independent). Section header: chevron icon, bold blue title, blue count badge (matches RBK column theme). Each card shows sender name, subject (line-clamp-1), summary or draft preview (line-clamp-2), time ago. Clicking a card expands the `ExpandedEmailPanel` inline below the card (only one at a time, `expandedDraftApproveId` state) with full draft review: thread view, editable draft textarea (saves on blur), Send button, Discard button, Request Revision button. After send/discard/revision, the inline panel auto-collapses (`expandedDraftApproveId` cleared in `sendEmail`, `discardDraft`, and `requestRevision` handlers). Count badge updates live. Section only shown in RBK column. No changes to All Emails page, Dashboard, API routes, or existing Drafts Ready popup.
- **Floating bug report button (2026-04-14)** — New `app/components/BugReportButton.tsx` (dynamically imported, SSR disabled). Floating bug icon button fixed bottom-right (z-50, slate-700). On click: captures screenshot via `html2canvas`, opens modal with thumbnail, feedback textarea, and submit button. POSTs to `/api/bug-reports` which inserts into Supabase `bug_reports` table (workspace_id, reported_by, page, feedback, screenshot_data, created_at). **SQL required:** `CREATE TABLE bug_reports (id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), workspace_id uuid, reported_by text, page text, feedback text, screenshot_data text, created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now());` Green toast confirmation on submit. Rendered at bottom of Dashboard.tsx return, outside all nav conditionals.
- **Admissions data caching + last-synced timestamp (2026-04-14)** — Added `admissionsLastFetched` state (Date | null), set on successful fetch. Data persists in React state across tab switches (Overview/Projection/New Enrollment) — no re-fetch. Re-fetches only on initial page load (`activeNav === 'admissions' && !admissionsData`) or manual refresh button click. Header shows "Last synced X min ago" / "Last synced just now" / "Never synced" next to the refresh button. Refresh button has `shadow-sm` and spinning icon while loading.
- **totalStudents fix: use gradeMap not cache query (2026-04-14)** — `totalStudents` was calculated from attendance_cache which inflated the count. Changed to count entries in `gradeMap` (built from Veracross students fetch) where `grade_level_id` is in valid non-HS set `[40,35,30,25,20,1-8]`. Removed the Supabase cache query for total students. gradeMap already contains all fetched students keyed by person_id → grade_level_id.
- **Absences page: 3 attendance overview charts (2026-04-14)** — Added recharts (npm install). Three charts at top of Student Absences page in "Attendance Overview" section: (1) **Donut chart** — Today's attendance breakdown (Present green, Absent red, Tardy amber, Early Dismissal blue). Shows percentage present in center. `totalStudents` from API = count of all students on latest cached attendance date (excludes HS). (2) **Bar chart** — Absences per day this month. `monthlyTrend` from API queries attendance_cache for attendance_category=1 grouped by date, current month. X-axis: date, Y-axis: count, red bars. (3) **Horizontal bar chart** — Top 10 most absent students YTD. `topAbsentees` from API queries attendance_cache from 2025-09-01, grouped by person_id, top 10 by count. Amber bars, name on Y-axis. All 3 queries in `/api/absences/route.ts` server-side with supabaseAdmin, wrapped in try/catch (non-fatal). `AbsencesData` interface updated with optional `totalStudents`, `monthlyTrend`, `topAbsentees` fields. Charts render only when data loaded. Layout: 3-col grid on wide, 1-col on narrow, 220px height each.
- **Tooltips on column label hover, no "?" icon (2026-04-14)** — Removed "?" icon from all By Grade table column header tooltips. Tooltip now appears when hovering the column label text itself. `group` class moved to the `<span>` wrapper around the label, tooltip popup unchanged. Added `font-normal not-italic` to tooltip text to prevent inheriting italic from Pending column header.
- **HS grade exclusion confirmed + diagnostic logs removed (2026-04-14)** — Re-enrollment filter already uses explicit allowlist `RE_ENROLLMENT_GRADE_LEVELS = [40, 35, 30, 25, 20, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]` — grade 12 (HS) was never included in the filtered `reEnrollments` array. The diagnostic logs that showed grade 12 were logging from the unfiltered `allStudents` array before the filter. Removed all `[GRADE_APPLY DIAG]` diagnostic console.log statements.
- **Pisgah column moved + Waitlist tooltip fix (2026-04-14)** — Moved Pisgah column from between Registered/Incomplete to the informational zone after Progress bar (before Pending Review). Column order now: Grade, Registered, Incomplete, Total, Progress, Pisgah, Pending, Waitlist, Leaving, 25-26. Pisgah does NOT affect Total. Waitlist tooltip updated to "New applicants placed on the waitlist by admissions" (was generic). Waitlist data already sourced from new applicants (`application_decision_response === 6`) — no data change needed.
- **Clickable Leaving badges + sort fixes (2026-04-14)** — (1) Leaving count badges in By Grade table are now clickable (cursor-pointer, hover:opacity-80, stopPropagation). Clicking opens a `projection_leaving` drilldown panel titled "[Grade] – Leaving" showing only enrollment_status===8 students for that grade, sorted alphabetically, with Veracross links. (2) "Families Declined Offer" collapsible list now sorted by grade (PROJECTION_GRADE_ORDER) then alphabetically by last name. (3) "Not Re-Enrolling" collapsible list now sorted by next_grade (PROJECTION_GRADE_ORDER) then alphabetically by last name.
- **Merged Registration Incomplete column (2026-04-14)** — Replaced three separate columns (In Process, Likely to Register, Contract Pending) with a single "Registration Incomplete" column in the By Grade table, stat cards, and funnel bar. Count = sum of all three categories per grade. Blue badge style. Stat cards reduced from 4 to 3 (Registered, Registration Incomplete, Pipeline Total). Funnel bar has 2 segments (Registered green, Incomplete blue). Progress bar per row also 2 segments. Tooltip: "Enrollment contract not yet complete. Includes families who have started the contract, indicated intent, or have a contract pending signature." Drilldown category 2 updated to include response codes [4,9,1] and enrollment statuses [2,3,4,6]. Total column math unchanged (Registered + Incomplete = Total).
- **Re-enrollment grade_applying_for diagnostic (2026-04-14)** — Added `grade_applying_for: number | null` to `ReEnrollmentStudent` interface (both API route and Dashboard.tsx). API route now checks multiple possible Veracross field names (`grade_applying_for`, `next_grade_level`, `future_grade_level`) for the target grade. If present and non-null, uses it directly as `next_grade` (no NEXT_GRADE_MAP needed — handles repeaters correctly). Falls back to `NEXT_GRADE_MAP[grade_level]` if null. Diagnostic logs first 5 students + Bareli (166424) with all candidate field names. **Check logs after page load to confirm correct field name.**
- **Auth loading guard — prevent stale data flash (2026-04-14)** — Added early return in Dashboard.tsx: if `authLoading` (from `useAuth()`) is true OR `workspaceId` is null, renders a full-screen white spinner instead of the dashboard. Prevents previous user's email/data from briefly flashing on page refresh before auth resolves. Destructured `loading` as `authLoading` from `useAuth()`. All data-fetching useEffects that depend on auth (projects, gemara, docs, admissions, lever) will fire after the guard clears since they depend on state that only populates after workspace loads. `useRealtimeEmails` already guards on `workspaceId` internally.
- **Tooltip visibility fix (2026-04-14)** — Tooltips on By Grade table headers were clipped by `overflow-x-auto` on the parent wrapper div. Fix: changed wrapper to `overflow-visible`, added `overflow-visible` to `th` elements, inline `style={{ overflow: 'visible' }}` on `table`, `thead`, `tr`. Tooltip classes changed from `hidden group-hover:block` to `invisible group-hover:visible opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-150` with `z-[9999]`. Icon wrapper changed to `relative inline-block group`.
- **Enrollment Projection: column header tooltips (2026-04-14)** — Added hover tooltips to all 9 data columns in the By Grade table header. Each column has a small "?" icon that shows a tooltip on hover (bg-slate-800 text-white, positioned above with caret). Built with Tailwind group/group-hover pattern — no libraries. Tooltips: Registered="Enrollment contract signed and completed", In Process="Accepted offer, enrollment contract not yet complete", Likely="Family has indicated strong intent to register", Contract="Contract sent, awaiting family signature", Pisgah="Applicants to the Pisgah early childhood program", Pending="Application received, awaiting admissions committee decision", Waitlist="Applicant placed on waitlist", Leaving="Re-enrolling student whose family has indicated they are leaving", 25-26="Current enrollment headcount for the 2025-26 school year".
- **Enrollment Projection Pass 2: full UI overhaul (2026-04-14)** — Major UI changes to Enrollment Projection tab: (1) Renamed columns: Enrolled→Registered, Accepted→In Process, Considering→Likely to Register, Pending→Contract Pending. Stat cards, funnel bar, and table all use new labels. (2) Five new table columns: Pisgah (purple badge, `student_group_applying_for === 1`), Pending Review (italic slate, `application_status === 1`), Waitlisted (amber, `application_decision_response === 6`), Leaving (red, `enrollment_status === 8`), 25-26 (slate, from `currentYearCounts`). Pending Review/Waitlisted/Leaving/25-26 are NOT included in Total. (3) Three school-level summary cards above pipeline cards: ELC (blue-400 left border, I/T through K), Lower School (green-400, 1st-5th), Middle School (purple-400, 6th-8th). Each shows registered count and is clickable → opens drilldown filtered to that school level. New `projection_category` values: 10=ELC, 11=LS, 12=MS. (4) `currentYearCounts` state added, fetched from API response, cleared on refresh. `GRADE_LABEL_TO_NEXT_NUMBER` reverse map for looking up current-year counts by grade label. (5) City chart legend count fix already done. (6) Admissions Overview search bar deferred to separate task.
- **Fix: 8th Grade row showing 173 students (2026-04-14)** — Current 8th graders (grade_level=8) go to 9th grade (HS) next year and should be excluded from re-enrollment projection. Split `VALID_GRADE_LEVELS` into two constants: `RE_ENROLLMENT_GRADE_LEVELS` [40,35,30,25,20,1-7] (excludes grade 8) for re-enrollment filtering, and `CURRENT_YEAR_GRADE_LEVELS` [40,35,30,25,20,1-8] (includes grade 8) for current year headcounts. Also added exit_date filter: students with `exit_date` in the past are excluded from both re-enrollments and current year counts. 8th Grade row now shows only new applicants applying for 8th grade, not current 8th graders going to HS.
- **Admissions API: richer data for Enrollment Projection (2026-04-14)** — `/api/admissions/route.ts` changes: (1) Include Pending Review applicants — filter now only excludes `application_status === 0`. (2) Added `student_group_applying_for` to `AdmissionApplication` — Pisgah students have value 1. (3) **CONFIRMED: Veracross `grade_level` = CURRENT year grade** (diagnostic: Aiden grade_level=5 = 5th Grade, currently in 5th). `NEXT_GRADE_MAP` restored and `next_grade` field kept on `ReEnrollmentStudent` — maps current→next (5→6, 20→1, etc.).
