Aiden now correctly appears in 6th Grade row. For students not in NEXT_GRADE_MAP (e.g. grade 8 repeaters), `next_grade` falls back to `grade_level`. (4) Added `currentYearCounts: Record<number, number>` — counts ALL students per grade (no enrollment_status filter). `VALID_GRADE_LEVELS` expanded to include grade 8. (5) Added `student_group: string | null` placeholder to `ReEnrollmentStudent`. (6) Diagnostic logs removed after confirming grade values.
- **CRITICAL: Grade numbering fix for re-enrollments (2026-04-13)** — Diagnostic logs revealed Veracross students API uses the SAME grade numbering as admissions (40=I/T, 35=2YN, 30=3YN, 25=4YN, 20=K, 1-8=1st-8th, 9-12=HS), NOT sequential -3 to 8. Previous code assumed sequential numbering, causing: (1) filter `grade_level >= -3 && <= 7` missed ALL ELC/K students (grades 20-40), only catching 675 of ~1000+ students; (2) `next_grade = grade_level + 1` was wrong (K=20 would give 21, not 1). Fix in `/api/admissions/route.ts`: added `NEXT_GRADE_MAP` lookup (40→35, 35→30, 30→25, 25→20, 20→1, 1→2, ..., 7→8) and `VALID_GRADE_LEVELS` array [40,35,30,25,20,1-7]. Filter now uses `VALID_GRADE_LEVELS.includes()`. Also fixed `campus` field which Veracross returns as object `{id, description}` not number. Dashboard.tsx: removed `NEXT_GRADE_LABELS` and `CURRENT_GRADE_LABELS` — since next_grade now uses admissions-style numbering, `ADMISSIONS_GRADE_LABELS` works for everything. `getCanonicalGradeKey` simplified to always use `ADMISSIONS_GRADE_LABELS`. "Not re-enrolling" section now uses `ADMISSIONS_GRADE_LABELS` for both current and next grade display.
- **Fixed viewer module gating and assistant sidebar regression (2026-04-14)** — `getEffectiveModules()` rewritten with explicit null check — assistants/owners with null `allowedModules` now correctly see all workspace modules (was breaking because truthy coercion treated edge cases incorrectly). Added `admissions: true` to workspace modules in Supabase (was missing, causing Debra's `allowedModules` intersection to return empty). Auth fix: session route now allows sign-in for any email in `workspace_members` table, not just hardcoded `ALLOWED_EMAILS` env var. Viewer sidebar correctly hides workspace switcher and Dashboard nav item. Confirmed working: Debra (`deis@saracademy.org`) sees Admissions & Enrollment + Student Absences only. Emily Daniel (`edaniel@saracademy.org`) sees admissions only. Amy Hyman (`ahyman@saracademy.org`) sees recruiting + faculty_absences (faculty absences page not yet built).
- **Viewer role + member-level module restrictions (2026-04-13)** — Added `allowed_modules` jsonb column to `workspace_members` (NULL = no restriction, sees all workspace modules). New `lib/modules.ts`: `getEffectiveModules()` helper computes intersection of workspace modules and member allowed_modules. Auth session, switch-workspace, and workspace routes all fetch and pass through `allowed_modules`. Dashboard sidebar and page gating use `effectiveModules` (not raw `modules`) — owner/assistant behavior completely unchanged. Viewers auto-redirect to first allowed module on login instead of Dashboard (priority: admissions → absences → recruiting → simchas → projects). Daily section (Dashboard, Inbox, Agenda, Tasks, Compose) and Operations section (Projects, Recruiting, Emily's Queue) hidden for viewers with `allowedModules` set. Four viewer accounts in RBK workspace: `deis@saracademy.org` (Debra Eis: admissions + absences), `edaniel@saracademy.org` (Emily Daniel: admissions only), `ahyman@saracademy.org` (Amy Hyman: recruiting + faculty_absences), `shasson@saracademy.org` (Sara Hasson: development + projects). **SQL run in Supabase SQL Editor.** Future: department head viewers scoped to projects module planned.
- **Student ID fix + clickable stat cards (2026-04-14)** — (1) Fixed Veracross links showing `undefined`: diagnostic confirmed Veracross students API returns `id` not `person_id`. Changed API route mapping from `s.person_id` to `s.id`. (2) Stat cards (Enrollment Complete, Accepted Offer, Considering, Pipeline Total) are now clickable — open the same drilldown panel used for grade rows but filtered by category across all grades. New `projection_category` drilldown type with `value`: 0=pipeline total, 1=enrolled, 2=accepted, 3=considering. Cards have cursor-pointer + hover:shadow-md + blue ring when active. Drilldown panel handler unified: both `projection_combined` (grade row) and `projection_category` (stat card) share the same panel code with conditional grade vs category filtering. Name fetch useEffect also handles `projection_category`.
- **Drilldown panel: Veracross link always rendered + personId type fix (2026-04-13)** — Changed `personId` type in `CombinedRow` from `number | undefined` to `number` since both new applicants (`applicant_id`) and re-enrollments (`id`) always provide a numeric ID. Removed the `personId != null` guard so Veracross links always render for all rows. Verified "not re-enrolling" and "declined" sections already had Veracross links. Filter pills and search logic confirmed correct — `sourceFiltered` applies pill filter, then `displayRows` applies search on top.
- **Admissions drilldown panel: search, filter, sort, link fix (2026-04-13)** — (1) Fixed Veracross links showing `undefined` in projection_combined drilldown: changed `id` field to `personId` with `number | undefined` type, added null guard (`personId != null`) before rendering link. For new applicants uses `applicant_id`, for re-enrollments uses `id` (person_id). (2) All drilldown panels now sort alphabetically by last name then first name. Projection combined panel stores `firstName`/`lastName` separately. Overview panel sorts using `applicantNames` lookup. (3) Search bar added to ALL drilldown panels (projection + overview): text input with "Search by name..." placeholder, clear button (×), filters list in real time. State: `admissionsDrilldownSearch` (resets on drilldown change). (4) Filter pills added to projection_combined drilldown only: "All" / "New Families (N)" / "Returning (N)". State: `admissionsDrilldownFilter` ('all' | 'new' | 're', resets on drilldown change). Filters and search work together. Pill style: rounded-full border, blue-50 active state.
- **City pie chart legend: missing counts in right column (2026-04-13)** — The "Applicants by City" pie chart legend on the Admissions Overview tab split items into two columns (even indices left, odd indices right). The right column was missing the `<span>` showing the count. Added `<span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 ml-1">{sl.count}</span>` to match the left column.
- **Veracross link URL format fix (2026-04-13)** — Updated all Veracross person record links on the admissions page from `https://app.veracross.com/sar/{id}/273/general` to `https://axiom.veracross.com/sar/#/detail/student-ls/{id}/273-general`. Four locations updated: projection_combined drilldown panel, admissions overview drilldown panel, declined families section, not re-enrolling section.
- **Enrollment Projection: grade label alignment fix (2026-04-13)** — (1) Fixed "Pre-K" → "4 Year Nursery" in ADMISSIONS_GRADE_LABELS (grade 25). (2) Fixed NEXT_GRADE_LABELS off-by-one: Veracross student grade_level 0=K, 1=1st, ..., 5=5th, so next_grade 1=1st (not K), next_grade 6=6th (not 5th). Aiden (grade_level=5, 5th grade) now correctly appears in "6th Grade" row. Updated mapping: next_grade 0=Kindergarten, 1=1st Grade, ..., 8=8th Grade. CURRENT_GRADE_LABELS also corrected to match (0=Kindergarten, 1=1st, ..., 7=7th). (3) Verified all re-enrollment grouping uses `s.next_grade` (not `s.grade_level`) in projection rows, drilldown panel, and name fetch. (4) New applicants correctly use `grade_applying_for` via `getCanonicalGradeKey('applicant', ...)` — no `current_grade` field is used.
- **Enrollment Projection: grade label shift + canonical grade normalization (2026-04-13)** — (1) Fixed NEXT_GRADE_LABELS shift-by-one: labels now represent the grade a student is ENTERING (next_grade 1 = Kindergarten, not 4 Year Nursery). Mapping: -2=2YN, -1=3YN, 0=4YN, 1=K, 2=1st, ..., 9=8th. Added CURRENT_GRADE_LABELS for display of current grade (used in "not re-enrolling" section). (2) Grade normalization: new `getCanonicalGradeKey(source, grade)` function converts both admissions grades (40/35/30/25/20/1-8) and re-enrollment next_grades (-2 through 9) to canonical string labels (e.g. "Kindergarten", "5th Grade") via their respective label maps (ADMISSIONS_GRADE_LABELS for applicants, NEXT_GRADE_LABELS for re-enrollments). Projection table now groups by canonical string key, so applicants for Kindergarten (grade_applying_for=20) and re-enrollments entering Kindergarten (next_grade=1) merge into one row. Sort order: PROJECTION_GRADE_ORDER array of string labels. Drilldown `projection_combined` now matches by label string instead of numeric value. Veracross numbering: students API grade_level -3=I/T, -2=2YN, -1=3YN, 0=4YN, 1=K, 2=1st, ..., 7=6th. Admissions grade_applying_for 40=I/T, 35=2YN, 30=3YN, 25=Pre-K, 20=K, 1=1st, ..., 8=8th.
- **Enrollment Projection: combined new families + re-enrollment data (2026-04-13)** — `/api/admissions/route.ts` now fetches both admissions applications AND current students for re-enrollment in a single GET. New `getStudentsToken()` requests `students:list students:read` scopes (same `VERACROSS_ADMISSIONS_CLIENT_ID`/`SECRET` credentials). Students endpoint paginated (1000/page, same loop pattern). Filters students to `grade_level` -3 to 7 (ELC through 7th — excludes 8th who go to HS) and `enrollment_status` in [2,3,4,5,6,8]. Returns `{ applications: AdmissionApplication[], reEnrollments: ReEnrollmentStudent[] }`. `ReEnrollmentStudent` interface: `{ id, first_name, last_name, grade_level, next_grade (grade_level+1), enrollment_status, campus }`. Enrollment status labels: 2=Pending Re-Enrollment, 3=Likely to Re-Enroll, 4=Re-Enrollment on Hold, 5=Re-Enrolled, 6=Likely to Re-Enroll, 8=Not Re-Enrolling. Students fetch is non-fatal — if it fails, returns empty `reEnrollments` with valid `applications`. Dashboard.tsx: `reEnrollmentsData` state stores the array. Projection tab now combines both sources: Enrollment Complete = re-enrolled (status 5) + new enrolled (response 2); Accepted Offer = new (response 4); Considering = likely to re-enroll (status 3,6) + new considering (response 9); Pending = pending/on-hold re-enrollment (status 2,4) + new pending (response 1). Stat cards, funnel bar, and By Grade table all use combined counts. Grade rows keyed by `next_grade` (for re-enrollments) and `grade_applying_for` (for new applicants). `NEXT_GRADE_LABELS` map for projected next-year grades (-2 through 9). `PROJECTION_GRADE_SORT` for ordering. Grade row drilldown uses `projection_combined` type showing both sources with "New Family" (green) / "Re-Enrollment" (blue) source badges + status badge + Veracross link. Two collapsible bottom sections: "N families declined offer" (new applicants, response 3) and "N students not re-enrolling" (status 8) with name, grade arrow, Veracross link. Amber "new families only" banner removed.
- **Admissions & Enrollment page + Enrollment Projection tab (2026-04-13)** — (1) Renamed page from "Admissions" to "Admissions & Enrollment" (page title + sidebar nav). (2) Added third tab "Enrollment Projection" between "Admissions Overview" and "New Enrollment". Tab state type expanded to `'overview' | 'projection' | 'enrollment'`. (3) Removed "Denied" summary pill from Admissions Overview tab (kept Accepted, Waitlisted, Withdrawn). (4) Drilldown panel: each applicant row now has a Veracross external link icon (`https://app.veracross.com/sar/{applicant_id}/273/general`, opens in new tab). (5) No changes to existing Overview or Enrollment tab content.
- **CRITICAL: Email workspace isolation hardened (2026-04-13)** — Full audit of every email fetch path. Fixed all paths that could leak data between workspaces. Changes: (1) `app/page.tsx` `getEmails()` — now returns `[]` immediately if `workspaceId` is null/undefined instead of fetching unfiltered; `.eq('workspace_id', workspaceId)` is always applied, never conditional. (2) `app/hooks/useRealtimeEmails.ts` realtime subscription — now returns early with `setIsConnected(false)` if `workspaceId` is missing; filter is always `workspace_id=eq.${workspaceId}`, never omitted. Channel name changed to `emails-${workspaceId}` so each workspace gets its own channel. (3) `useRealtimeEmails.ts` `refreshEmails()` — returns early if no `workspaceId`; `.eq('workspace_id', workspaceId)` always applied. (4) `Dashboard.tsx` popup email fetch — now requires `workspaceId` in the guard (`popupEmailId && workspaceId`); removed `|| ''` fallback. (5) `Dashboard.tsx` thread fetch in ExpandedEmailPanel — now requires `workspaceId` in the guard; `.eq('workspace_id', workspaceId)` always applied, never conditional. All API routes (emails/status, flag, draft, send, archive, send-batch, process-email, trash-email) were already filtering correctly. RLS via `set_current_user_id()` is defense-in-depth but does not protect realtime subscriptions — explicit workspace_id filters are the primary isolation mechanism.
- **End-of-day summary: Step 6 complete (2026-04-05)** — Module flags, branding, and workspace switching all working correctly. Module gating: API routes for absences, simchas, gemara now return 403 when module is disabled; sidebar already had gating from Phase 4. Branding: sidebar shows "Command Center / by Lhasa" with dynamic workspace name below; page title updated to "Command Center by Lhasa". Compose: moved from header icon to dedicated nav item in DAILY section. Sidebar polish: section labels, spacing, breathing room, bottom border separator, right border edge. Workspace switching fully fixed: switch-workspace route now writes complete session (modules, module_config, role, display_name) on every switch; labels, email counts, and sidebar items all update correctly on workspace change. **Known remaining:** workspace switch requires 2-3 clicks sometimes before data fully refreshes — likely a timing issue on reload, not blocking.
- **Switch-workspace cookie completeness fix (2026-04-05)** — `auth/switch-workspace/route.ts` was not fetching `display_name` or `role` from the database when switching workspaces — it relied on the session's stale `workspaces[]` array which only has `{ id, name, role }`. Now also queries `workspace_members` for the target workspace's `role` and `display_name`, so the cookie is fully updated with all fields that `session/route.ts` would set: `workspace_id`, `role`, `display_name`, `modules`, `module_config`. Priority chain for role: DB member row > session workspaces array > existing session value. After switching: inbox labels reflect the new workspace's `module_config.inbox`, emails are filtered by the new `workspace_id` (via `page.tsx getEmails(session.workspaceId)`), and sidebar modules respect the new workspace's `modules` flags.
- **Sidebar style refresh (2026-04-05)** — Professional SaaS-style sidebar polish. All existing colors preserved. Changes: (1) Compose pen icon removed from header, added as standalone "Compose" nav item at bottom of Daily section with pencil-line icon. (2) Section labels: `text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold`, more top margin (`mt-1`), consistent `px-3 mb-2`. (3) Nav items: `py-2` padding (was `py-3`), `gap-3.5` icon spacing, `size-5` icons consistently, `space-y-0.5` between items (tighter). Active/hover states unchanged. (4) Workspace switcher: `rounded-lg` (was `rounded-md`). (5) Bottom user section: `border-t border-slate-700` (more visible separator), `px-3 py-4`, avatar `w-9 h-9` (slightly smaller). (6) Sidebar: `border-r border-slate-700/50` right edge, `px-3` nav padding (was `p-4`).
- **Branding update: "Command Center by Lhasa" (2026-04-05)** — Sidebar app title changed from "RBK Command Center" to two lines: "Command Center" (text-lg font-semibold white) and "by Lhasa" (text-xs text-slate-400). Below that, the active workspace name is shown dynamically from the `workspaces` array (text-xs text-slate-500, truncated, wrapped in `mounted` guard for SSR safety). Date line moved below workspace name. Page `<title>` and Apple Web App title in `app/layout.tsx` updated from "RBK Command Center" to "Command Center by Lhasa". Description updated to "Operations dashboard by Lhasa".
- **API route module gating (2026-04-05)** — Added module flag checks to API routes so disabled modules return 403 instead of serving data. Routes gated: `/api/absences` (checks `modules?.absences === false`), `/api/simchas` (checks `modules?.simchas === false`), `/api/gemara` GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE (checks `modules?.gemara === false`). Uses the same fail-open pattern as the sidebar — only blocks when explicitly set to `false`, never when `modules` is `null`. Returns `{ error: 'Module not enabled' }` with status 403. Dashboard.tsx sidebar gating was already in place from Phase 4 — nav items filtered by `modules?.[moduleKey] !== false`, Gemara card gated, page content shows "not enabled" message. No changes needed in Dashboard.tsx.
- **Dev environment fully operational (2026-04-05)** — As of today, the full multi-workspace dev environment is live: Becca's dev workspace is isolated and showing only its own emails, her Gmail is flowing in via the Cloud Function, workspace switching works correctly, and inbox labels are configurable per workspace. Ready for product iteration on real data.
- **Initial email fetch workspace_id filter (2026-04-05)** — `app/page.tsx` `getEmails()` was fetching all 500 emails without a `workspace_id` filter, causing both workspaces to show the same emails. Root cause: the server-side fetch uses the `supabase` anon client with no RLS user context set (server components can't call `set_current_user_id`), so RLS couldn't scope the query. Fix: `getEmails()` now accepts `workspaceId` param from the session and adds `.eq('workspace_id', workspaceId)` when present. The realtime hook (`useRealtimeEmails.ts`) was already filtering correctly — both the subscription filter (line 81) and manual refresh (line 116-118) use `workspace_id`.
- **Workspace context caching fix (2026-04-05)** — The workspace switcher and configurable inbox labels were not reaching the client because the `GET /api/auth/session/workspace` response was being cached. Three fixes: (1) Added `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` to `auth/session/workspace/route.ts` to prevent Next.js from caching the response. (2) Added `cache: 'no-store'` and a `?t=${Date.now()}` cache-buster to the client-side fetch in `AuthProvider.tsx loadWorkspaceFromSession()`. (3) `refreshSession()` (10-min periodic ID token refresh) now calls `loadWorkspaceFromSession()` after updating the cookie, so the client context stays in sync with server-side cookie changes. Reordered function declarations in AuthProvider to resolve the circular dependency (`loadWorkspaceFromSession` now defined before `refreshSession`).
- **Configurable inbox labels per workspace (2026-04-05)** — Inbox priority section display labels ("RBK Action", "Emily Action") are now dynamic, read from `moduleConfig?.inbox?.owner_label` and `moduleConfig?.inbox?.assistant_label` with fallbacks to "Owner" / "Assistant". Dashboard.tsx destructures `moduleConfig` from `useWorkspace()`, derives `ownerLabel` and `assistantLabel` at the top of the component, and uses them in: (1) the category `<select>` dropdown options, (2) the "Action Emails" section header on the Dashboard. Database priority values (`rbk_action`, `eg_action`) are unchanged — only UI display strings were replaced. To configure per workspace, set `module_config` in the `workspaces` table with `{"inbox": {"owner_label": "RBK", "assistant_label": "Emily"}}`. RBK's workspace uses "RBK"/"Emily"; Becca's dev workspace uses "Becca"/"Assistant".
- **Becca's dev workspace live (2026-04-05)** — New workspace row in Supabase: id `b2c3d4e5-f6a7-8901-bcde-f12345678901`, name "Becca — Dev", owner_email `rglassberg@saracademy.org`. Gmail refresh token saved via OAuth consent flow. Becca now has two `workspace_members` rows: assistant on RBK's workspace, owner on her dev workspace. `triageGmail` Cloud Function successfully processing her emails into the dev workspace. Workspace switcher in sidebar lets her toggle between workspaces. **Pending:** Inbox priority labels ("RBK Action", "Emily Action") are hardcoded in Dashboard.tsx — need to be configurable per workspace via `workspaces.module_config` (suggested keys: `inbox.owner_label`, `inbox.assistant_label`). **Known issue:** "Decoding Firebase session cookie failed" log noise in Cloud Run — not blocking (all routes returning 200), will self-resolve as users get fresh cookies within 24 hours.
- **Session cookie size fix (2026-04-05)** — The `__session` cookie was exceeding the 4KB browser limit after the workspaces array was added, causing browsers to silently drop/truncate the cookie. Every API route then got null from `getAuthSession()` and returned 401/500. Root cause: `modules` and `module_config` objects were duplicated in every entry of the `workspaces[]` array AND at the top level, plus the `idToken` (~1500 chars) was stored but never read back. Fix: (1) Removed `modules` and `module_config` from the `workspaces[]` array entries — they're only stored at the top level for the active workspace. `WorkspaceInfo` interface is now `{ id, name, role }` only. (2) Removed `idToken` from the cookie payload — it's only used during the initial POST to `auth/session`, never read back. (3) `switch-workspace/route.ts` now fetches `modules`/`module_config` from Supabase when switching (since they're no longer in the workspaces array). (4) `session/workspace/route.ts` simplified — reads modules/module_config from top-level session fields only. (5) `AuthProvider.tsx` `WorkspaceInfo` interface updated, `loadWorkspaceFromSession` reads modules from top-level session data.
- **Cloud Functions multi-workspace loop (2026-04-05)** — Both `triageGmail` and `syncDraftsReady` Cloud Functions now query `SELECT id, owner_email, gmail_refresh_token FROM workspaces WHERE gmail_refresh_token IS NOT NULL` at the start of each run and loop over all results. Removed hardcoded `WORKSPACE_ID` env var dependency. Each workspace gets its own access token exchange, Gmail API calls, Supabase writes scoped to its `workspace_id`, and label cache. Per-workspace try/catch — if one workspace fails (e.g. expired refresh token), the error is logged and the loop continues to the next workspace. Log lines prefixed with `[owner_email]` for traceability. If no workspaces have refresh tokens, logs and exits cleanly. No changes to triage logic, OpenAI calls, Gmail API format, or webhook format.
- **Multi-workspace support + workspace switcher (2026-04-05)** — Users can now belong to multiple workspaces and switch between them. (1) `auth/session/route.ts` fetches ALL `workspace_members` rows for the user (not `.limit(1).single()`), batch-fetches workspace details, and includes a `workspaces[]` array in the session cookie. Default workspace prefers `owner` role. (2) `auth/session/workspace/route.ts` passes through the `workspaces[]` array to the client. (3) New `auth/switch-workspace/route.ts` POST endpoint: reads current `__session` cookie, verifies user is a member of the requested workspace (checks session `workspaces[]` first, falls back to Supabase `workspace_members` query), re-sets the `__session` cookie with the new active `workspace_id` + matching role/modules/module_config. (4) `AuthProvider.tsx` `useWorkspace()` exposes `workspaces` array and `switchWorkspace(id)` function. `switchWorkspace` saves to localStorage, POSTs to `/api/auth/switch-workspace` to update the server-side session cookie, then reloads the page. On load, checks `localStorage.selectedWorkspaceId` for a previously selected workspace, falls back to session default. Sign-out clears the selection. All localStorage reads happen inside `useCallback`/`useEffect` handlers (client-only). (5) `Dashboard.tsx` sidebar shows a workspace dropdown (slate-800 select, text-xs) above the user avatar/sign-out button when the user has 2+ workspaces. Shows workspace name and role. Single-workspace users see no UI change. **Hydration fix:** Switcher wrapped in `mounted` state guard so it only renders after client mount. No data fetching logic or existing data were changed.
- **Phase 2 + Phase 6 SQL migrations run (2026-04-05)** — Both `PHASE2_MIGRATION.sql` and `PHASE6_RLS.sql` successfully executed in Supabase SQL Editor. Phase 2: `workspaces` table created with RBK seed row (`a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890`, "SAR Academy — RBK", `kraussb@saracademy.org`). `workspace_members` table created with 3 rows: RBK (owner), Emily (assistant), Becca (assistant) — all with PLACEHOLDER user_ids that auto-replace with real Firebase UIDs on first sign-in via `auth/session/route.ts`. `workspace_id` column added to all 8 existing tables, all existing rows backfilled with RBK's workspace_id. Phase 6: `set_current_user_id()` RPC and `current_workspace_id()` helper functions created. RLS enabled on all 10 tables. Interim permissive "Allow authenticated access" policies applied first as a security fix, then dropped and replaced with proper `workspace_isolation` policies scoped to `current_workspace_id()`. All API routes use `supabaseAdmin` (service role, bypasses RLS). Dashboard.tsx calls `set_current_user_id` RPC on mount. **Pending verification:** confirm PLACEHOLDER user_ids in `workspace_members` get replaced with real Firebase UIDs after RBK, Emily, and Becca each sign in.
- **"Not Expected" attendance status** — Added support for Veracross status code 32 ("Not Expected"), which has `attendance_category === 0` (same as present) but `student_attendance_status === 32`. Widened the filter from `attendance_category > 0` to `attendance_category > 0 || student_attendance_status !== 0`. New `notExpected` array in API response. Dashboard shows a purple summary pill (only when count > 0) and a collapsible "Not Expected" section with grade grouping, matching the layout of other sections.
- **Attendance pagination fix** — The master_attendance endpoint is capped at 1000 records per page, but the school has ~1731 students. Previously only page 1 was fetched, so students whose records fell on page 2+ were silently missing from the Absences page. Replaced the single master_attendance fetch with a paginated loop using `X-Page-Number` header (same pattern the students endpoint already used). Also removed diagnostic console.log statements from the previous investigation session.
- **Admissions page — Applicants by City pie chart layout fix** — Restructured the Admissions Overview layout from a single-column stacked layout to a two-column grid with `items-start`. Left column contains the "Accepted: Family Response" card and the "Applicants by City" card stacked below it. Right column contains the "By Grade" card (tall, all grades). Inside the City card: legend (two-column grid of city entries with colored dots + counts) sits above a centered 280px SVG pie chart. Eliminated the previous full-width layout that was pushing the pie chart to the far right edge of the page with a large white space gap.
- **Deploy fix: `deploy.sh` script** — Firebase CLI `deploy --only hosting` fails at "finalizing version" with `409 ALREADY_EXISTS: Revision named 'ssrrbkcmdcenter-XXXXX' with different configuration already exists`. Root cause: the CLI updates the Cloud Function (creating a Cloud Run revision), then tries to PUT/replace the same Cloud Run service with hosting annotations using the same revision name — Cloud Run rejects it. Fix: `deploy.sh` runs the normal deploy (ignoring the expected error), then manually patches the unfinalized hosting version with the Cloud Run rewrite config via REST API, finalizes it, creates a release, and restores the `allUsers` IAM binding. The SSR function was also deleted and recreated to clear 12+ stale revisions. Site restored from 404 to working state.
- **Emily invitations → FYI** — Emails from `egray@saracademy.org` are excluded from `invitationEmails` and included in `fyiEmails` regardless of their triage priority. Prevents Emily's internal calendar/meeting invites from cluttering the Invitations section.
- **Compact Important Docs & Quick Links cards** — Both dashboard cards compacted: `py-1` padding, `text-xs` titles, `w-3 h-3` link icons, `truncate max-w-[200px]` on titles. Important Docs capped at 6 visible items; Quick Links shows 3 hardcoded + up to 3 dynamic items. Both show "View all (N)" link opening the Important Docs modal when items exceed the cap. Removed `max-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto` from Important Docs (no scroll needed with cap).
- **RBK Action visual hierarchy** — Left panel email list for `emailCategory === 'rbk'` now sorts urgent/unread emails to top, then read emails by date descending. Unread emails show a blue dot (`bg-blue-500`) instead of the priority dot, and both sender name and subject are `font-semibold`. A centered "Earlier" divider line appears between unread and read sections when both exist. Other email categories unchanged.
- **Dashboard draft cards navigate to All Emails** — Review Drafts cards in the Dashboard To-Do Today section now navigate to the All Emails page with the Drafts Ready popup open when clicked, instead of showing a minimal inline edit/approve/revision interface. This ensures RBK gets the full draft review experience (Send, Discard, full thread context) that the All Emails Drafts Ready popup provides. Cards are now clickable with hover shadow; the "View all N drafts" link also opens the popup.
- **Quick Links "+ Add" and dynamic items** — Quick Links dashboard card now has a "+ Add" button (same style as Important Docs) with an inline form (title + URL inputs, Save/Cancel). New links are saved via `POST /api/important-docs` (same table/API as Important Docs) and rendered below the 3 hardcoded links with hover-to-delete trash icons. State: `showAddQuickLink`, `newQuickLinkTitle`, `newQuickLinkUrl`. Dynamic items come from `importantDocs` array (shared data source).
- **B'nei Mitzvah Invitations section** — New collapsible section on the Simchas & Shivas page, above Shiva Emails. Filters emails where `priority === 'invitation'` and subject/body_text/summary contains "bar mitzvah" or "bat mitzvah" (case-insensitive), excluding done/junk. Collapsed by default (`expandedSections.bnbInvitations`). Each card shows subject, sender, summary, relative time, and "Add to Calendar" button (same handler as Shiva Emails — opens Create Event modal pre-filled).
- **Triage prompt tightened + no-action guard** — `triageGmail` OpenAI prompt now explicitly states `rbk_action` is ONLY for emails requiring RBK's personal reply/decision, that newsletters/notifications/receipts/system messages should be `fyi`, and that `action_needed` phrases like "no reply needed" must map to `fyi`. Post-parse code guard also catches contradictions: if `action_needed` contains "no reply needed", "no action required", etc. but priority is `rbk_action`, it downgrades to `fyi`.
- **Drafts Ready sent-mail detection** — `syncDraftsReady` now checks if a thread already has a SENT message before syncing the draft. For each thread with the "Drafts Ready" label, fetches thread metadata and checks for SENT in any message's labelIds. If found: skips draft sync, updates Supabase `draft_status` to `'sent'`, and swaps the label to "Drafts Synced". Dashboard `draftsReady` filter also excludes owner-sent emails as a safety net.
- **Owner sent-mail filter** — Emails sent by the workspace owner (kraussb@saracademy.org) no longer appear in any inbox section. Two-layer fix: (1) `triageGmail` Cloud Function now compares `fromEmail` against `owner_email` from the workspaces table and skips the message entirely (no Supabase insert, no Gmail label). (2) Dashboard.tsx adds `isOwnerEmail()` filter to all derived inbox sections (RBK Action, Emily Action, Important, Review, Invitations, FYI, Shiva, Untagged, Urgent) to catch any emails that already slipped through before the function fix.
- **Page refresh remembers last page** — `activeNav` state is now synced to `window.location.hash`. On mount, reads hash (e.g. `#inbox`, `#agenda`) and initializes `activeNav` if valid. On nav change, updates the hash. Dashboard clears the hash (no `#dashboard`). Valid nav IDs: `dashboard`, `inbox`, `agenda`, `tasks`, `gemara`, `communications`, `projects`, `absences`, `admissions`, `simchas`, `student-logs`, `lever`, `development`, `emily`. Uses `window.location.hash` directly — no Next.js router.
- **Auto-resolve placeholder workspace member UIDs** — `auth/session/route.ts` now looks up `workspace_members` by Firebase UID (`user_id`) first, then falls back to email if no row found. If the email-matched row has a placeholder `user_id` (contains 'PLACEHOLDER'), it auto-updates the row with the real Firebase UID from the session. Fixes Emily's login — her seeded row had `user_id: 'PLACEHOLDER_EMILY'` which never matched her real Firebase UID, so she got no workspace context. After her next sign-in, the placeholder is replaced and subsequent logins use the fast UID path.
- **Compose New Email** — "Compose" button at top of sidebar (blue, pencil icon, below app title and above nav items) opens a centered modal (`max-w-2xl`) with To, Subject, Body fields and Send/Cancel buttons. Posts to new `POST /api/emails/compose` route which builds an HTML MIME message with RBK's signature (same `EMAIL_SIGNATURE` as other send routes), sends via Gmail API `users/me/messages.send`. Send button is green, disabled until all fields filled, shows "Sending..." spinner. Form clears and modal closes on success; error displayed inline on failure. State: `composeOpen`, `composeTo`, `composeSubject`, `composeBody`, `composeSending`, `composeError`. Note: Subject line is NOT prefixed with "Re:" (unlike reply routes) since this is a new conversation.
- **triageGmail safety nets for empty subjects + bad priorities** — Emails with blank/empty subjects are now skipped (`continue`) with a log message instead of being sent to OpenAI (which returned unpredictable priority values). `VALID_PRIORITIES` constant hoisted to module level (matches `emails_priority_check` constraint). JSON.parse fallback on OpenAI response failure now defaults to `priority: 'fyi'` + `summary: 'Unable to triage'` (was `'review'` + `'Triage parsing failed'`). Priority validation unchanged — shiva maps to `fyi` + `action_status: 'shiva'`, any unrecognized priority defaults to `fyi`.
- **Quieter auth error handling** — Suppressed log noise from unauthenticated requests (Slack link previews, health checks, bots). `lib/auth.ts` `getAuthSession()` wraps the entire function (including `cookies()`) in try/catch, returning null silently on any failure. `middleware.ts` returns JSON 401 for API routes (instead of redirecting to `/login`) and catches cookie-read errors. `auth/session/route.ts` only logs unexpected errors — expected auth failures (expired tokens, invalid ID tokens, "Decoding Firebase session cookie failed") are suppressed. `auth/session/workspace/route.ts` wraps `cookies()` in try/catch. **Note:** The "Decoding Firebase session cookie failed" log from the Firebase framework adapter (SSR Cloud Run layer) is unavoidable — it occurs because `__session` stores plain JSON, not a Firebase session cookie JWT. These changes ensure our app code doesn't add noise on top of it.
- **Sync Gmail button + API route** — New "Sync Gmail" button on the All Emails (inbox) page next to the search bar. Triggers on-demand Gmail triage by calling `POST /api/gmail/sync`, which calls the `manualTriage` HTTP Cloud Function (`https://us-central1-rbk-cmd-center.cloudfunctions.net/manualTriage`). The Cloud Function publishes to the `firebase-schedule-triageGmail-us-central1` Pub/Sub topic internally, triggering the `triageGmail` scheduled function. Button shows loading spinner ("Syncing..."), green checkmark ("Synced!") for 3s on success, red X ("Failed") for 3s on error. After success, triggers `refreshEmails()` after 5s delay to pick up newly inserted emails. State: `gmailSyncStatus: 'idle' | 'syncing' | 'success' | 'error'`. Auth: requires signed-in user with workspaceId.
- **Phase 5: triageGmail Firebase Cloud Function** — New scheduled Cloud Function in `functions/src/index.ts` that replaces the GAS email triage pipeline. Runs every 15 minutes on weekdays (`*/15 * * * 1-5` ET). **Flow:** (1) Reads `gmail_refresh_token` and `owner_email` from `workspaces` table for `WORKSPACE_ID`. (2) Exchanges refresh token for fresh Gmail access token via `oauth2.googleapis.com/token`. (3) Fetches unprocessed emails from Gmail API: `in:inbox -label:RBK/Done -label:RBK -label:Emily -label:FYI -label:Review -label:Invitations -label:Shivas newer_than:1d`, max 20 messages. (4) For each message, fetches full MIME with `format=full`, parses body recursively (prefers text/plain, falls back to text/html stripped), parses From header. (5) Checks Supabase `emails` table for duplicate `message_id + workspace_id` — plain `.insert()`, no upsert/onConflict (emails table has no unique constraint on message_id+workspace_id). (6) Calls OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini` to triage each email → returns priority (`rbk_action|eg_action|important_no_action|review|invitation|fyi|shiva`), summary, action_needed, draft_reply as JSON. (7) Inserts into `emails` with full body (no truncation), `status: 'pending'`, `is_unread: true`, mapped `assigned_to`. (8) Applies matching Gmail label via `messages.modify` (RBK, Emily, Important No Action, Review, Invitations, FYI, Shivas). Creates labels if they don't exist. Label IDs cached in-memory per run. **Shiva handling:** When OpenAI returns `priority: 'shiva'`, the function maps it to `priority: 'fyi'` + `action_status: 'shiva'` (because `shiva` is not a valid value in the `emails_priority_check` constraint — it's an `action_status` in the Dashboard). Gmail label still applied as "Shivas". **Emails table constraints:** `emails_status_check` allows `pending|in_progress|done|archived` (not `active`). `emails_priority_check` allows `rbk_action|eg_action|important_no_action|review|invitation|fyi` (not `shiva`). Any unrecognized priority from OpenAI defaults to `fyi`. `to_email`, `category`, `processed_at` are all nullable — Cloud Function omits them safely. **Helpers:** `parseEmailBody()` uses `collectParts()` to recursively walk ALL MIME parts, concatenating every text/plain part (separated by `\n\n`) rather than stopping at the first one. If the concatenated plain text is a forwarding stub (under 100 chars or just "Begin forwarded message:" / "---------- Forwarded message" / "-------- Original Message"), it falls back to text/html parts stripped of tags. This fixes forwarded emails that previously returned only the forwarding header line. `parseFrom()` ("Name <email>" parsing), `getOrCreateLabel()` (Gmail label management). **Environment:** `functions/.env` with GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY, WORKSPACE_ID. Installed `@supabase/supabase-js` and `openai` in functions/. Deployed alongside existing `syncDailyAttendance`. **GAS pipeline stays running in parallel** — do not disable until this is proven end-to-end.
- **Gmail OAuth consent flow (Phase 5 prerequisite)** — New `/api/auth/gmail-consent` route: reads session to confirm signed-in user with workspaceId, builds Google OAuth2 authorization URL with `gmail.readonly + gmail.send + gmail.modify` scopes, `access_type: 'offline'` + `prompt: 'consent'` (forces refresh token), passes `workspaceId` as `state` param, redirects user to Google consent screen. New `/api/auth/gmail-callback` route: receives `code` and `state` (workspaceId) from Google redirect, exchanges code for tokens via POST to `oauth2.googleapis.com/token`, saves `refresh_token` to `workspaces.gmail_refresh_token` via `supabaseAdmin`, redirects to `/?gmailConnected=true` on success or `/?gmailConnected=false&error=...` on failure. Dashboard.tsx shows dismissible success/error banner when `gmailConnected` query param is present, then cleans URL with `replaceState`. `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=https://rbk-cmd-center.web.app` added to `.env.local`. `GMAIL_OAUTH_SETUP.md` created with setup steps: add `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` + `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` to `.env.local`, add redirect URI to Google Cloud Console, enable Gmail API, trigger consent flow for RBK. **Note:** `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` and `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` are NOT yet in `.env.local` — Becca must add them from Google Cloud Console credentials.
- **SaaS Phase 6: Row Level Security (RLS)** — `PHASE6_RLS.sql` created and **run in Supabase SQL Editor (2026-04-05)**. SQL creates: (1) `set_current_user_id(user_id text)` RPC function that calls `set_config('app.current_user_id', user_id, true)` — called by client-side code to set the Firebase UID for RLS resolution. (2) `current_workspace_id()` helper function that looks up `workspace_id` from `workspace_members` where `user_id` matches the current setting. (3) Enables RLS on all 10 tables (emails, agenda_notes, agenda_items, projects, gemara_items, important_docs, recurring_topics, attendance_cache, workspaces, workspace_members). (4) Creates `workspace_isolation` policy on each table using `workspace_id = current_workspace_id()`. Interim permissive policies applied first as security fix, then replaced with proper workspace-scoped policies. All API routes use `supabaseAdmin` (service role key) which bypasses RLS entirely. Dashboard.tsx calls `supabase.rpc('set_current_user_id', { user_id: user.uid })` on mount.
- **SaaS Phase 4: Module flags gate sidebar nav + page content** — Module flags from `useWorkspace()` now control visibility of optional sidebar nav items and their page content. Dashboard.tsx destructures `modules` from `useWorkspace()` alongside existing `workspaceId`. **Sidebar gating:** Community section nav items (Student Absences, Simchas & Shivas) have a `moduleKey` property; the array is `.filter()`'d by `modules?.[moduleKey] !== false` so items only hide when explicitly set to `false`. The entire Community section header hides when all its items are disabled. The Gemara dashboard card is wrapped with `modules?.gemara !== false &&`. Core items (Dashboard, All Emails, Meeting Agenda, Tasks, Projects, Emily's Queue) are never gated. **Page content gating:** When `activeNav === 'absences'` or `'simchas'` and the corresponding module flag is `false`, a centered "This module is not enabled for your workspace" message renders instead of the page content. **Fail-open:** When `modules` is `null` (workspace context not loaded, or no workspace), all items are visible — `modules?.key !== false` evaluates to `true` when modules is null. No API routes, data fetching, or styling changed.
- **SaaS Phase 3: Workspace-scoped API routes + client queries** — All API routes and client-side Supabase queries now filter by `workspace_id`. Routes that already used `getAuthSession()` (flag, draft, send, archive, send-batch, process-email, trash-email, calendar/*) now extract `session.workspaceId` and return 401 if missing, and add `.eq('workspace_id', workspaceId)` to all Supabase reads/writes. Routes that had no auth (emails/status, agenda-notes, projects, agenda-items, agenda-items/topics, important-docs, gemara) now import and call `getAuthSession()` with full workspace check. External API routes (absences, simchas) verify workspace membership but don't filter Supabase (they call Veracross/iCal). `useRealtimeEmails` hook now accepts optional `workspaceId` param — filters both the realtime subscription and manual refresh queries. Dashboard.tsx imports `useWorkspace()`, passes `workspaceId` to `useRealtimeEmails`, and filters popup email fetch and thread fetch by workspace_id. Webhook/email route unchanged (already stamps workspace_id from Phase 2). **No breaking changes** — works identically before and after the Phase 2 SQL migration is run (workspaceId will be null until migration, and routes return 401 only if workspaceId is explicitly missing from an authenticated session).
- **SaaS Phase 2: Multi-tenant foundation** — (1) `PHASE2_MIGRATION.sql` created and **run in Supabase SQL Editor (2026-04-05)**. Creates `workspaces` and `workspace_members` tables, inserts RBK seed workspace (`a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890`), adds `workspace_id` column to all 8 existing tables, backfills all existing rows with the seed workspace ID, inserts RBK/Emily/Becca as members with placeholder user_ids. (2) `auth/session/route.ts` now queries `workspace_members` by email on sign-in to load `workspace_id`, `role`, `display_name`, then fetches `modules` and `module_config` from `workspaces` table — all included in `__session` cookie. Falls back gracefully if tables don't exist. (3) `lib/auth.ts` `getAuthSession()` now returns `workspaceId`, `role`, `modules`, `moduleConfig` alongside existing `user`/`accessToken`. Additive — existing callers unaffected. (4) `AuthProvider.tsx` exports new `useWorkspace()` hook (workspaceId, role, modules, moduleConfig, displayName) via `WorkspaceContext`. Populated on auth via `GET /api/auth/session/workspace` (new lightweight endpoint). `useAuth()` unchanged. (5) `webhook/email/route.ts` stamps `workspace_id: 'a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890'` on every new email insert. **No breaking changes** — app works identically before and after the SQL migration is run.
- **Auth prompt reverted from consent to normal (2026-04-20)** — The temporary `prompt: 'consent'` (which forced every returning user through the full Google permissions screen) has been reverted now that all users have re-consented for `gmail.readonly`. Login page (`login/page.tsx`) now uses `prompt: 'select_account'` (account picker, no permissions re-grant). Silent token refresh (`refreshGoogleToken()` in Dashboard.tsx) now uses `prompt: 'none'` (fully silent, no popup). The Gmail OAuth consent flow for workspace owners (`/api/auth/gmail-consent`) intentionally keeps `prompt: 'consent'` because it needs `access_type: 'offline'` to obtain a refresh token.
- **Gmail thread fetch (SaaS Phase 1)** — New `/api/gmail/thread/[threadId]` route fetches full raw Gmail thread on demand using the signed-in user's access token. Returns structured JSON with decoded MIME body (prefers HTML, falls back to text/plain, handles multipart recursively). `ExpandedEmailPanel` now fetches full Gmail thread when an email is opened (via `email.message_id`), renders HTML bodies with `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` + `prose prose-sm`, falls back silently to existing Supabase `body_text` on failure. Shows "fetching..." label while loading. `GmailThreadMessage` interface added to Dashboard.tsx. `gmail.readonly` scope added to OAuth flow in both `login/page.tsx` and `refreshGoogleToken()` — users need to sign out and back in once to grant it.
- **Sidebar nav grouped into sections** — Nav items grouped into 3 collapsible sections: **Daily** (Dashboard, All Emails, Meeting Agenda, Tasks — always expanded, no toggle), **Community** (Student Absences, Simchas & Shivas — collapsed by default), **Operations** (Projects, Emily's Queue — expanded by default). Each group has a small uppercase section label (`text-xs text-slate-400 font-semibold tracking-wider`). Community and Operations have chevron toggles. Collapsed state persisted to localStorage as `sidebarCollapsed` (`Record<string, boolean>` keyed by group name). Badge counts (unread emails on All Emails, Emily's Queue count) remain on their respective items.
- **Forwarded email body fix** — Added `extractForwardedBody()` helper that detects forwarded message dividers (`---------- Forwarded message`, `-------- Original Message`, `Begin forwarded message:`, `--- Forwarded by`) and extracts the forwarded content. If the outer wrapper before the divider is just a signature/greeting (≤2 non-empty lines or no line >40 chars), only the forwarded content is shown. If the wrapper has meaningful content, both are shown. Applied in both `ExpandedEmailPanel` thread messages (via `stripSignature` which now calls `extractForwardedBody` first) and the task side panel email body preview (replaced raw regex cleanup with `stripSignature`).
- **Bar/Bat Mitzvah "Attending" tag** — Each Bar/Bat Mitzvah event on the Simchas & Shivas page now has an attending toggle. Design A: expanding a pill reveals "Mark Attending" button; attending pills turn green with "ATTENDING" label. Design B: each card has an "Attending?" pill on the right; toggled shows "✓ ATTENDING" in green. Persisted to localStorage as `simchasAttending` (`Record<string, boolean>` keyed by event UID). Not yet wired to Google Calendar API.
- **Shiva emails moved to Simchas & Shivas page** — Removed the collapsible "Shivas" section from All Emails inbox. Added a new "Shiva Emails" collapsible section at the bottom of the Simchas & Shivas page (`activeNav === 'simchas'`). Collapsed by default. Each card shows subject, sender, summary, time, and an "Add to Calendar" button that opens the Create Event modal pre-filled with subject as title and summary as description. Shiva emails remain excluded from all inbox priority sections via `action_status !== 'shiva'` filter.
- **Past-due tasks auto-urgent** — The Urgent summary card on the dashboard now includes tasks with a past-due date (not just manually toggled urgent tasks). The `urgentTasks` derivation checks `taskDueDates[tid].date < todayET` in addition to `taskUrgent[tid]`, using ET timezone. Completed tasks are excluded. This was already working in the To-Do Today sorting and Tasks page — the fix brings the Urgent card count and list into consistency.
- **Webhook draft_reply fix** — The 23505 duplicate handler in `webhook/email/route.ts` now updates `draft_reply` alongside `draft_status` when a duplicate email is re-synced. Previously only `draft_status` was updated, so Emily's draft content from "Drafts Ready" syncs never made it into Supabase. Now both fields are included in the update, so draft content appears in the Command Center's Drafts Ready section.
- **Student Absences Phase 2 — Cache, YTD, Consecutive, Follow-Up Alerts** — (1) New `/api/absences/sync` route with `?mode=daily` and `?mode=backfill`, protected by `SYNC_SECRET` auth header. Fetches Veracross attendance and upserts ALL records (including present) into Supabase `attendance_cache` table. Backfill mode loops from 2025-09-02 to yesterday with 300ms delay. (2) New Firebase scheduled function `syncDailyAttendance` runs weekdays at 6 PM ET via Cloud Scheduler. Created `functions/` directory with firebase-functions v5 setup. (3) Main absences route now queries `attendance_cache` for `ytd_absences` (count of `attendance_category=1` records per student) and `consecutive_absences` (walks backwards through cached school days counting streak). Wrapped in try/catch, defaults to 0 if cache is empty. (4) "Needs Follow-Up" amber alert box at top of absences page shows students with 3+ consecutive absences or 15+ YTD absences. Hidden when no flags. (5) YTD badge `(N this year)` shown on each absent student row after status badge.
- **Student Absences page (Phase 1)** — New `activeNav === 'absences'` page in left sidebar (users icon, after Simchas). Fetches today's absences live from Veracross API via new `/api/absences` route (OAuth2 client credentials, pagination support). Excludes HS students and present students. Shows three summary count pills (Absent/Tardy/Early Dismissal) and three collapsible sections with color-coded status badges, notes, arrival/dismissal times. Lazy-loads on first navigation. Refresh button re-fetches. Loading skeleton and error states. Env vars: `VERACROSS_CLIENT_ID`, `VERACROSS_CLIENT_SECRET`, `VERACROSS_SCHOOL_ROUTE`.
- **Simchas & Shivas fixes** — (1) Removed all fake sample/seed events — empty state now shows "None this week". (2) Shiva/funeral events now pulled from new `/api/calendar/week` endpoint (fetches full Mon–Sun week from Google Calendar) instead of today-only `calendarEvents` prop, so past/future events in the week actually appear. (3) Also matches "levaya" in title. (4) Fixed React hooks violation crash (Error #310) — moved useState/useEffect from conditional IIFE to component top level.
- **Simchas & Shivas page** — New `activeNav === 'simchas'` page in left sidebar (star icon, after Projects). Shows two categories of events for the current week (Mon–Sun): (1) Bar/Bat Mitzvahs from the SAR public iCal feed via new `/api/simchas` route (server-side fetch + manual VEVENT parsing, no npm deps, 5-min cache). (2) Shivas & Funerals filtered from `/api/calendar/week` (title contains "shiva", "funeral", or "levaya"). Two design prototypes displayed for RBK to compare: Design A = 7-column week grid with colored event pills, Design B = two side-by-side cards listing events by date. Page header shows week date range.
- **TBD notes + Agenda TBD section** — (1) TBD popup modal has a notes textarea per email, saved to Supabase (`tbd_notes` column) on blur — syncs across devices. (2) "TBD" collapsible section at bottom of Agenda page (above Available Tags), expanded by default with amber header. Clicking any TBD item opens the shared TBD popup modal (same one used on All Emails page) with full functionality: notes textarea, Emily's suggestion, Move to Action, Mark Done.
- **Agenda Add Item fix** — The Save button on the "+ Add Item" form did nothing because `agenda-items/route.ts`, `agenda-notes/route.ts`, and `agenda-items/topics/route.ts` all used `supabase` (anon key) instead of `supabaseAdmin` (service role key). With RLS enabled, the anon client silently failed on INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE. All three routes now use `supabaseAdmin`, matching every other API route. Added console.log debugging and error handling to the save handler in Dashboard.tsx so silent failures are logged.
- **Shivas section** — New collapsible "Shivas" section on the All Emails page between FYI and Untagged. Filters emails where `action_status === 'shiva'`. Added `'shiva'` to valid action statuses in `emails/status/route.ts`. All other section filters exclude shiva emails (`e.action_status !== 'shiva'`). Collapsed by default, included in search auto-expand.
- **Untagged emails feature** — Emails that bypass the triage system (no known priority value) now appear in a new "Untagged" collapsible section in the Inbox, filtered to last 14 days only. Each card has a "Move to..." dropdown to reassign to any priority section (Action, Emily's Queue, Important, Review, Invitation, FYI) or mark as Junk. "All Junk" button in header marks all untagged emails as junk with confirm dialog. Junk sets `status: 'junk'` and calls `/api/gmail/trash-email` (fire-and-forget Gmail trash). Junk emails are excluded from all sections, search, Emily's queue, and urgent alerts. Email fetch limit increased from 100 to 500 in both `page.tsx` and `useRealtimeEmails.ts`. New API route: `gmail/trash-email/route.ts`. Added `'junk'` to valid statuses in `emails/status/route.ts`.
- **Kanban card HTML strip** — Added `stripHtml()` helper (regex tag stripping) to clean HTML from project descriptions on kanban card previews. Description is now stored as HTML from Tiptap, so card preview uses `stripHtml(project.description)` while the side panel renders it natively via TiptapEditor.
- **Project side panel Tiptap editors** — Replaced plain textareas in the project side panel with Tiptap rich text editors. (1) Description: Tiptap editor with toolbar (Bold, Italic, Underline, Strikethrough, Bullet list, Ordered list, Link), saves HTML on blur. (2) Updates: Same Tiptap editor, Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to post, editor clears after posting via key remount. Saved updates render as HTML with `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` and `prose prose-sm`. (3) Removed separate Links section — links now inserted inline via Tiptap link extension. (4) New `TiptapEditor.tsx` component, dynamically imported with `next/dynamic` (SSR disabled). (5) Installed `@tiptap/react`, `@tiptap/pm`, `@tiptap/starter-kit`, `@tiptap/extension-link`, `@tiptap/extension-underline`, `@tiptap/extension-placeholder`, and `@tailwindcss/typography`. (6) Layout: Description → Also Involves → Assignee+Due Date → Updates.
- **Task notes preview multi-line** — Notes preview on task cards (Tasks page RBK/Emily columns + Dashboard To-Do Today) changed from `truncate` (single-line ellipsis) to `whitespace-pre-wrap line-clamp-3` so line breaks and bullet points render properly, capped at 3 lines.
- **Urgent unified across dashboard** — The dashboard Urgent card and To-Do Today section now reflect manually marked urgent tasks (from `taskUrgent` localStorage state) alongside urgent emails. (1) Urgent card: shows both urgent emails and urgent tasks; tasks display with an amber "Task" label to distinguish from emails; count includes both; "All clear" only shows when zero of each. (2) To-Do Today: urgent tasks sort to top of the task list, get `bg-rose-50` background + amber left border + red "Urgent" badge — matching the Tasks page treatment. (3) Consistent state: `taskUrgent` is React state (initialized from localStorage), so toggling urgent on the Tasks page immediately reflects on the Dashboard without any page refresh.
- **View Email popup fix (agenda)** — The "View Email" button on agenda items silently failed when the linked email was older than the 100-email local state limit. Root cause: `popupEmail = emails.find(e => e.id === popupEmailId)` returned null for emails beyond the limit, so the popup never rendered. Fixed by adding an on-demand Supabase fetch: when `popupEmailId` is set but the email isn't in local state, it fetches directly from the `emails` table, adds it to the state array, and sets the draft text. Also imported `supabase` client directly into Dashboard.tsx.
- **Projects panel & card improvements** — (1) Priority editable from side panel: added 3-way pill toggle (High/Medium/Low) matching the add modal style, saves immediately on click. Also added inline priority toggle on card hover (replaces static badge with clickable pill row). (2) Progress bar colors use department accent: `departmentColors` now includes a `fill` property (e.g. `bg-blue-400`), used for card progress bars and a new visual progress bar in the side panel below the range slider. Replaces old green/blue/slate threshold logic. (3) Updates support paragraphs and bullets: replaced single-line input with textarea (min 3 rows, auto-expands), added "• Bullet list" toolbar button that inserts `•` at cursor position, changed Enter to create newlines (Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to post), saved updates render with `whitespace-pre-wrap` for proper line breaks and bullet formatting.
- **Tasks page improvements** — (1) Removed filter pills (All/Urgent/From Email/From Agenda) — all tasks now shown, urgent tasks visually distinguished instead. (2) Added urgent toggle to task cards — exclamation triangle icon appears on hover, toggles `taskUrgent` localStorage state. Urgent tasks get rose-50 background, amber left border, and red "Urgent" badge. Works on both RBK and Emily columns. (3) Fixed source tags — cards now correctly show "From email" (blue), "From agenda" (violet), or "Manual" (slate) based on actual task source. Previously everything showed "From agenda" incorrectly. (4) Side panel updated with urgent toggle button and correct source tags.
- **Agenda page overhaul** — (1) Fixed View Email button (`e.stopPropagation()`, uses `popupEmailId` for popup modal). (2) Added "+ Add Item" button at top of agenda list with inline form (title input + email search/link). Creates `item_type='manual'` agenda items. (3) Added "+ project" button on each agenda item — inline dropdown with title + department selector, POSTs to `/api/projects`. (4) Reworked standing topics as tags — topics are now tags applied to agenda items via `tags text[]` column, shown as removable chips, with "+ tag" dropdown. Standing topics panel becomes tag management area. (5) Simplified notes to single chronological stream — removed tab filter (All/Notes/Decisions/Actions), removed type selector on new notes (defaults to 'note'), removed inline type/assignee toggles on existing notes. SQL: `ALTER TABLE agenda_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tags text[] DEFAULT '{}'; ALTER TABLE agenda_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS title text;`
- **TBD email feature** — Full holding area for emails RBK is unsure about. TBD toggle icon on email cards, amber TBD button with count badge in top action bar, TBD popup modal for reviewing/acting on parked emails, "Needs Your Input" section in Emily's Queue where Emily can add suggestions via TbdInputCard, teal notification dot when suggestions exist. `action_status='tbd'` removes emails from priority sections. New `tbd_suggestion` column on emails table. SQL: `ALTER TABLE emails ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tbd_suggestion TEXT;`
- **Task card previews (notes + due date)** — Task cards on both the Dashboard To-Do Today section and the Tasks page now show inline previews of notes (text-xs italic truncated, only if notes exist) and due dates (calendar icon + date, with bell icon if time is set). Due date coloring: red if overdue, amber if due today, slate otherwise. Layout order on card: title → source label → notes preview → due date. Also added today-aware amber coloring to the existing due date badges on Tasks page cards (previously only had overdue red).
- **Dashboard cards redesign + Gemara edit + column DnD fix** — (1) All four dashboard summary cards (Urgent, Quick Links, Important Docs, Gemara) now use consistent visual design: same card dimensions, header style (uppercase label), item list layout with link icons and `text-sm font-semibold` titles, `text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline` link styling. Removed large number displays from Urgent and Important Docs. Urgent card shows "All clear" or compact list of urgent email subjects. (2) Important Docs card now has inline "+ Add" button and add form (title + URL inputs, Save/Cancel) directly in the card — matches Gemara's add flow. Docs list has hover-to-delete trash icons. Old Important Docs popup modal still works separately. (3) Gemara edit pen icon is now functional: clicking opens inline edit form with pre-filled title/url/body, saves via PATCH `/api/gemara`. Added PATCH handler to `app/api/gemara/route.ts`. (4) Fixed Projects kanban column drag-and-drop: card `onDrop` was unconditionally calling `e.stopPropagation()` which blocked column reorder drops — now early-returns when `draggingProjectId` is not set. Added `e.dataTransfer.setData()` to grip handle and card `onDragStart` for Firefox compat. Made grip icon always visible (`text-slate-400`, no `opacity-0`).
- **Project links, drag-and-drop, multi-department tags** — Cards are now draggable between department columns (HTML5 native DnD, updates `department` via PATCH). Department columns are reorderable via grip handle in header (persisted to localStorage as `projectColumnOrder`). Projects support `links` (JSONB array of `{title, url}`) shown as blue pills on cards and editable in side panel. Projects support `tags` (text[] of secondary departments) shown as department chips on cards and editable in side panel via "Also involves" section with dropdown. Supabase SQL: `ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN links JSONB DEFAULT '[]'; ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tags text[] DEFAULT '{}';`
- **Gemara Save button fix** — Fixed `addGemaraItem` to properly handle and log API errors (previously silently ignored non-200 responses). Added explicit `type="button"` to Save/Cancel buttons. Extracted title trim to local variable for consistency. Destructured API response defensively.
- **Projects kanban board** — New "Projects" nav item with full kanban board: 11 department columns (horizontal scroll), project cards with priority/progress/assignee/due date, slide-in side panel (status toggle, progress slider, description, assignee, due date, updates feed), floating + button with centered add modal, `/api/projects` CRUD route with soft-archive delete. Supabase `projects` table.
- **Gemara resource card** — Replaced "Meeting Agenda" summary card on dashboard with Gemara card. Emily adds links and notes for RBK's Gemara class. Inline add form with Link/Note type toggle, scrollable item list with type-specific styling, delete on hover. `/api/gemara` route, Supabase `gemara_items` table.
- **Dashboard tasks clickable** — To-Do Today task cards now open the same task side panel as the Tasks page. Panel moved to top-level render (outside `activeNav` conditionals) so it works from any view. Shared `taskPanelId` state. Completion circles work for both email and agenda note tasks from dashboard. Active card shows blue ring highlight.
- **Standalone tasks** — Add Task button POSTs to Supabase `agenda_notes` with `type='action'` and no `email_id`. Required `ALTER TABLE agenda_notes ALTER COLUMN email_id DROP NOT NULL` and `ALTER TABLE agenda_notes ADD COLUMN completed boolean DEFAULT false` in Supabase SQL Editor.
- **Task completion** — Agenda note tasks toggle `completed` field via PATCH to `/api/agenda-notes`. Email tasks toggle `[DONE]` marker in `meeting_notes`.
- **Tasks page redesign** — 3D floating cards with purposeful color coding (blue=RBK, teal=Emily, amber=urgent, green=completed), slide-in side panel with editable title/due date/time/source/assignee/notes, drag-and-drop, filter pills, inline editing
- **Agenda items system** — Replaced simple email flagging with `agenda_items` + `recurring_topics` tables. API supports email and topic items, bulk reorder, auto-sync of flagged emails.
- **Email pipeline fix** — Webhook URL updated from Vercel to Firebase; recovery function for missed emails
- **Calendar auth fix** — Session route now preserves access token across periodic ID token refreshes; cookie extended to 24h
- **Agenda phase 2** — Inline note type pills, assignee toggles, tab filters, threaded notes per agenda item
- **Campaigns tab → "Campaign Giving by Fund"** (2026-05-13) — Old per-event grouping replaced. New tab now groups FY26 gifts by `fund` (filtered to `OP:%`), shows Donations + Outstanding Pledges per fund, and displays a $6.2M annual fundraising goal progress bar at the top. Pledge-payment donations (`apply_to_pledge=true`) are excluded from "Donations" to avoid double-counting (the pledge row already represents those dollars). Funds sorted by totalRaised desc; each card expands to an inline accordion with a constituent table (Donor | Total pledge | Paid | Outstanding | Status). Status badges: gray "Paid in full" / green "In progress" / amber "Pledge pending". Sort within drilldown: Pledge pending + In progress first, then Paid in full; within each by outstanding desc. New route: `/api/development/fundraising-goals`.
- **Guardian Circle dedicated tab** (2026-05-13) — New top-level sidebar nav item under Operations (`activeNav = 'guardian-circle'`), gated to `development` module. Three stat cards (Total raised, Donors, Outstanding pledges), donor name search, CSV export, and full constituent table sorted by outstanding desc. New route: `/api/development/guardian-circle`. The Guardian Circle card on the Campaign Giving by Fund tab shows a "View tab →" link instead of an inline accordion (clicking sets `activeNav` to `guardian-circle`). `GuardianCirclePage.tsx` and a shared `ConstituentTable.tsx` were added under `app/components/development/`.
- **`pledge_balance` column on `gifts_cache`** (2026-05-13) — Added `numeric DEFAULT 0` via migration. Backfilled from existing `raw_data->'pledge'->>'balance'` for all gift_type=2 rows (556 pledge rows backfilled). Veracross actually returns pledge data nested as `raw_data.pledge.{balance, total_received, total_writeoff, increment, start_date, payment_frequency}` — not a flat field; `lib/syncGifts.ts` mapping now extracts `g.pledge?.balance ?? 0` into the new column on every sync. Veracross query reference: #62098.
- **Active campaign filter on fundraising-goals + guardian-circle routes** (2026-05-13) — Both routes now filter `fundraising_activity = 'Operating 2025-2026'` only (no `date >=` clause). This mirrors Veracross query #62098's "Campaign in (Active_Campaign)" filter and excludes prior-year pledge payments tagged Operating 2024-2025 (~$579K of Guardian Circle payments that hit during FY26 but applied to FY25 pledges) and forward-dated Operating 2026-2027 pledges that were leaking into FY26 totals. **The previous `date >= '2025-07-01'` clause was removed**: a campaign-tagged gift can legitimately carry a pre-FY26 date (e.g. retro-tagged pledges) and Veracross still counts it.
- **Guardian Circle formula fix: `totalRaised = donations + pledge_balance`** (2026-05-14) — `app/api/development/fundraising-goals/route.ts` rewritten with the Veracross "Donation & Pledge Balance" formula: each row's contribution is `amount` for donations (gift_type=1, including pledge payments), `pledge_balance` for pledges (gift_type=2). This replaces the old "exclude apply_to_pledge=true donations + use full pledge amount" rule. Conservation (`amount = balance + total_received + total_writeoff`) makes the two formulas mathematically equivalent on a clean dataset, so the Guardian Circle total stays at $3,680,963.95 — but the new formula matches Veracross's bookkeeping mental model and produces cleaner per-donor `paid`/`outstanding` splits. Constituent type now also includes `lastGiftAmount`, `thankYouLetterDate`, `paymentFrequency`, `primaryDevelopmentRole`. **Known residual $28,671.93 gap from Veracross #62098 persists** — three specific records Sara identified (Shmidman $25K 4/17/23, Siegel $1,897 3/13/25, Levine $1,774 4/10/25) are not present in `gifts_cache` even after a fresh re-sync; the sync pulls every row Veracross returns and no client-side filter excludes them, so the data isn't being returned by the API. Donor count also moved: 169 (old formula) → 246 (new formula, includes pledge-payment-only donors); Veracross shows 175 — neither matches, and the discrepancy is the same upstream-data issue.
- **`gifts_cache`: 3 new columns** (2026-05-14) — `thank_you_letter_date date`, `payment_frequency text`, `primary_development_role text`. Backfilled from raw_data. `payment_frequency` is mapped from Veracross's pledge.payment_frequency numeric code to a human label via `PAYMENT_FREQUENCY_LABELS` in `lib/syncGifts.ts` (code 8 → null since it represents "Custom/None" at SAR). `primary_development_role` is NOT returned by `/v3/development/gifts`; we derive a coarse role (`Household` / `Organization`) from `constituent_record_type` until the constituents endpoint is wired up. The granular Parent/Alumni classification the user wanted is a future enhancement.
- **`ConstituentTable` columns: Frequency + Thank you** (2026-05-14) — Added after Outstanding. Frequency renders as a muted slate pill when present, `—` when null. Thank you renders a green checkmark + formatted date when `thank_you_letter_date` is set, `—` otherwise. Veracross link icon moved to a per-row action column alongside an envelope icon (visible on row hover) that opens the new ThankYouModal.
- **Guardian Circle role filter pills** (2026-05-14) — Above the search bar. Default `All`; clicking a pill (e.g. `Household`, `Organization`) filters the constituent table to that `primaryDevelopmentRole`. Pills are derived from the unique role values present in the loaded constituents.
- **Thank-you note generator** (2026-05-14) — New `/api/development/draft-thank-you` route calls Anthropic (claude-sonnet-4-6) with prompt caching on the system prompt; falls back to a static template if `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is not set. The `ThankYouModal` (`app/components/development/ThankYouModal.tsx`) shows the editable draft, Copy button, and "Send via Email" button (opens mailto: with the body pre-filled — donor email is not in `gifts_cache` so the `to:` field is empty). **`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is NOT yet configured** — in production it must be set via the `gcloud run services update` step in `deploy.sh` (or via Firebase Functions secrets). Until it's set, the modal returns a template draft and shows an amber note indicating template fallback.
- **Cooper Fund: "Money In · By Event" chart** (2026-05-14) — `/api/development/cooper-fund` now returns a `moneyIn` array, grouped from `gifts_cache` where `fund='Cooper' AND fundraising_activity='Cooper'`. Events are deprefixed (`APL: `) and `null`/`'None'` events bucket as `General / Undesignated`. CooperFundTab renders a horizontal Recharts bar (green `#375623`, distinct from blue disbursements chart) with a custom tooltip showing dollar total + gift count. Bars sorted desc; $0 events filtered out.
- **Israel Fund: "Money In · By Event" collapsible** (2026-05-14) — `/api/development/israel-fund` returns a `moneyIn` array. The user's stated SQL filter (`NOT LIKE 'OP:%' AND NOT LIKE 'Operating%' AND != 'Cooper'`) would have included `Big Bold Future` ($17M capital campaign) which is unrelated to Israel and would dominate the visualization, so the implemented filter is narrowed to `fundraising_activity = 'External Funds General'` — the natural Israel/external bucket (~36 distinct events). Events deprefixed (`APL: ` / `DEV: `). IsraelFundTab renders a collapsible accordion (collapsed by default) with a searchable table: Event / Raised / Outstanding Pledges, sorted by raised desc.
- **Israel Fund initiatives re-seeded** (2026-05-14) — `israel_fund_summary` row replaced with 35 initiatives (was 6) from SAR Israel Support Management Report as of 2026-04-14. Summary fields: total_raised $1,797,242; pledges_not_paid $11,900; grants_paid $1,454,892; balance_at_sar $250,764; funds_not_claimed $4,000.
- **IsraelFundTab UI: search + scroll** (2026-05-14) — Initiatives table now has a header search input that filters by name and a `max-height: 600px` scroll container so the long list doesn't push the page down. Same pattern applied to the Money In table inside the accordion (`max-height: 480px`). Sticky table headers within scroll containers.
- **Admissions Pisgah tag no longer re-buckets students** (2026-05-14) — The Pisgah toggle on enrollment projection drilldowns writes a `grade_overrides` record with `override_grade === original_grade` as a flag carrier. Previously the projection-rows loop treated *any* override row as a grade move, which yanked Pisgah-tagged students into the "Registered" / override grade row and surfaced a "Moved from [grade]" badge. Three call sites in `Dashboard.tsx` now ignore overrides where `override_grade === original_grade`: (1) the `projectionRows.forEach` that mutates `r.gradeKey`, (2) the `overridesByGrade` indicator count, and (3) the per-row `effectiveGrade()` helper used by the drilldown panel filters. The "Moved from" badge JSX is also gated on the same condition. The grade-row `gPisgah` and footer `tPisgah` counts are unchanged — they read from the now-correct `gradeKey` grouping and naturally count Pisgah students on their original grade row.
- **Pisgah drilldown panel now reads from projection-equivalent set** (2026-05-14) — Previously the `projection_pisgah` panel filtered `admissionsData` by `student_group_applying_for === 1` AND `getCanonicalGradeKey('applicant', a.grade_applying_for) === gradeLabel`. After the Pisgah-doesn't-rebucket fix, that comparison used the original Veracross grade, but the table's clickable Pisgah count was computed against the effective `gradeKey`, so the two diverged and the panel showed 0 students for every grade. The drilldown now rebuilds a projection-row-equivalent set locally: includes both new applicants (Pisgah from Veracross `student_group_applying_for === 1` OR a manual `gradeOverrides[id].is_pisgah`) and re-enrollments (manual toggle only — Veracross has no Pisgah field on re-enrollments). Effective grade respects the same Pisgah-only-doesn't-rebucket rule via a local `effectiveGradeFor()` helper. Each row renders name, status pill, source pill (New Family / Returning), and Veracross link — matching the visual pattern of the `projection_incomplete` drilldown.
- **iOS Safari crash fix: shimmer animation rewritten with `animate-pulse`** (2026-05-14) — `app/components/ui/Shimmer.tsx` previously used inline `background: linear-gradient(...)` + `backgroundSize: '200% 100%'` + a custom `@keyframes shimmer` animating `background-position`. Composing many of these (table rows + stat cards on the same view) was crashing iOS Safari's GPU compositor and surfacing a generic "Application error: a client-side exception has occurred." Skeletons now use Tailwind's built-in `animate-pulse` (opacity 0.5 → 1 → 0.5). The unused `@keyframes shimmer` rule was removed from `globals.css`. Grid styling on `ShimmerStatCards` also moved off inline `display: grid` onto `grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3` so the skeleton itself follows the mobile-first standard.
- **User Management: Add User display_name field + inline name edit** (2026-05-21) — Add User form grid widened from 2-col to 3-col (Email · Display name · Role); display name is optional and only sent in the POST payload when non-empty. Each member row's name is now inline-editable: clicking the name swaps it for a controlled `<input>` that commits on Enter or blur via `PATCH /api/admin/workspace-members/[id]` with `{ display_name }` (Escape cancels). A small pencil icon fades in on hover next to the name to advertise editability. Empty submission saves as `null` so the fallback `email.split('@')[0]` re-takes over. Optimistic UI with rollback on PATCH failure and a "Name updated" toast on success.
- **`agenda_notes` and `tasks` added to `supabase_realtime` publication** (2026-05-21) — Per the previous turn's discovery, the `tasks-sync` channel in Dashboard.tsx had been subscribing cleanly but never receiving broadcasts because neither table was in the publication. Migration `realtime_publication_agenda_notes_tasks` ran `ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE agenda_notes; ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE tasks;`. Also added a permissive `anon SELECT USING (true)` policy on `agenda_notes` — the only existing policy used `current_workspace_id()` which returns NULL for anon clients in this Firebase-Auth codebase, so Realtime would have silently filtered every event. `tasks` already had a permissive `USING (true)` SELECT policy so no extra policy was needed. **End state**: 4 tables now in the realtime publication — `emails`, `project_updates`, `agenda_notes`, `tasks`. The Live indicator on the Tasks view (`tasksLive`) now reflects functioning cross-device sync instead of being cosmetic. No code changes — purely a database fix.
- **Project updates: localStorage → Supabase + real-time sync** (2026-05-21) — New `project_updates` table (id uuid PK, workspace_id FK, project_id FK, text, author, created_at) with indexes on workspace_id and project_id. **Two RLS policies**: the spec's strict `workspace_members WHERE user_id = auth.uid()::text` policy (kept for documentation / future Supabase-Auth migration; currently evaluates FALSE for all client requests because this app uses Firebase Auth and `auth.uid()` is always null), PLUS a permissive `anon SELECT USING (true)` policy that's necessary for Realtime broadcasts to actually deliver — without it the supabase_realtime publication filters every event.
