Security stays at the API layer (`/api/project-updates` uses `supabaseAdmin`, validates session + workspace). **Important discovery while building this**: the existing `tasks-sync` channel that subscribes to `agenda_notes` has never actually been delivering cross-device updates — only the `emails` table was in the `supabase_realtime` publication. `project_updates` was added via `ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE project_updates`, so this is the first table in the app with end-to-end working Realtime. New `/api/project-updates` route (GET `?project_id=`, POST, DELETE `?id=`). **Dashboard.tsx refactor**: `projectUpdates` state now starts empty (no localStorage seed), bulk-fetched once on workspace ready, kept in sync via a `project-updates-sync` Realtime channel handling INSERT and DELETE events (filtered by workspace_id, dedup-by-id on INSERT so optimistic local entries don't double up when the broadcast echoes back). `addProjectUpdate` rewritten async: temp-id optimistic insert → POST → replace temp with server row (rollback on error). New `deleteProjectUpdate` mirrors. **One-time localStorage migration** on mount: reads `localStorage.projectUpdates`, POSTs each entry oldest-first so the newest stays at the top, then `localStorage.removeItem('projectUpdates')` so re-mounts don't double-post. The card-recency indicator + panel feed continue to use the `timestamp` field on each entry — kept backward-compatible.
- **User Management page extended** (2026-05-21) — `/admin/permissions` already existed (admin-gated by `ADMIN_EMAIL = 'rglassberg@saracademy.org'`, sidebar-linked) with a member table + per-module toggles + PATCH-driven module updates. Three things were missing per the user-management spec: **Add User**, **Remove**, and a **dynamic module list**. Now: (1) new `POST /api/admin/workspace-members` inserts a row with `user_id = 'PLACEHOLDER-<email>'` — the existing session route at `app/api/auth/session/route.ts:91` already swaps that for the real Firebase UID on first login, so no client-side magic-link or invite flow is needed; the user just signs in via Google. Endpoint is idempotent: re-adding an existing email returns the existing row with `already_existed: true` instead of failing the unique key. (2) New `DELETE /api/admin/workspace-members/[id]` — refuses to delete the admin row to prevent UI lockout; access ends on the user's next session refresh. (3) `PATCH /api/admin/workspace-members/[id]` extended to accept `role` (validated against `'owner' | 'assistant' | 'viewer'`) and `display_name`, in addition to the existing `allowed_modules`. UI changes: top-right "+ Add User" button toggles an inline form with email field + role dropdown (3 actual DB roles) + 13-module checkbox grid (`ALL_MODULE_OPTIONS` — superset of `DASHBOARD_COLUMNS`, includes `gemara`/`communications`/`calendar`/etc. that the table columns omit). Module checkboxes disable + dim when role is owner/assistant (those see everything regardless). Per-row Remove (trash icon, far right of each row), with a `window.confirm` guard and optimistic rollback. Role badge is now inline-clickable — clicking opens a 3-option `<select>` that saves on change. Optimistic UI + 2-3s toast for success/error. The page title was also renamed from "Permissions" to "User Management" to match the spec, and the subheader updated. **Spec-vs-reality note**: the spec asked for `role = 'admin'` gating but the codebase has never had an `admin` role — `workspace_members.role` CHECK is `owner|assistant|viewer`, and "admin" gating is `ADMIN_EMAIL` email equality. The page keeps the existing `ADMIN_EMAIL` check (Becca-only access) — the role dropdown in the UI exposes the three actual DB roles.
- **Project kanban cards: updates indicator + count badge** (2026-05-19) — Each card now renders a tiny row directly below the project title showing total updates count (e.g. `3 updates`, muted slate-400) and, when any update's `timestamp` is within the last 7 days, a small blue dot (`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-500`) prefacing the count to flag fresh activity. The row is only rendered when at least one update exists; cards with zero updates look identical to before. Implementation note: updates aren't a field on the `project` object — they live in `projectUpdates` (a `Record<projectId, Array<{text, timestamp}>>` state map persisted to localStorage). The card pulls from `projectUpdates[project.id]` and computes recency inline (`Number.isFinite(t) && t >= Date.now() - 7*24*60*60*1000`).
- **Thank-you Gmail draft Bcc fix** (2026-05-19) — The previous `buildRawMime` in `/api/development/draft-thank-you/route.ts` always emitted a `To:` header line, even when the donor email was unknown (`To: \r\n`). RFC 5322 doesn't allow an empty field-body, and Gmail's MIME parser was silently dropping the subsequent headers (including `Bcc:`) on some accounts when it hit the malformed line. Rewrote header construction to build a `headerLines` array, conditionally appending `To:` only when a non-empty donor email is provided; `Bcc:`, `Subject:`, MIME headers always appended. Body separated by the canonical `\r\n\r\n`. **UI heads-up for verifying**: Gmail's compose window hides the Bcc field by default — click the "Bcc" link next to the To row to expand it. The recipient `sar.tracking@mail.veracross.com` will receive the message on send regardless of UI visibility; opening the sent-items entry after sending also lists the Bcc recipients.
- **Eight-fix pass: task panel, home layout, assignee dropdown, project tasks, mentions, thank-you Gmail draft** (2026-05-19) —
  - **Mark Complete removed from task side panel** (`Dashboard.tsx` task panel edit-mode footer). Completing now happens only via the circle on the task card. Footer was tightened to a single Close button + `pb-20` so the FAB at `fixed bottom-8 right-8` no longer covers it. Same `pb-20` added to the create-task panel footer.
  - **Home layout** (`app/home/page.tsx`) — top row collapsed to `grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4` (This Week + Today's Schedule). Today's Tasks renders full-width below for power users (previously a 3rd column on lg). No Projects column. `ThisWeekCard` constrained to `max-w-2xl` to stop the calendar oversizing on laptops.
  - **Assignee dropdown** — new module-level `ASSIGNEE_OPTIONS = [{value:'rbk',label:'RBK'},…(5 users)]` in `Dashboard.tsx`. Inline add-task pills, create-task panel pills, and the project-panel task form all map over it. State types widened from `'rbk' | 'emily'` → `string`. **Schema**: dropped + recreated `agenda_notes_assignee_check` to accept all 5 lowercase keys (`rbk|emily|sara|leora|becca|NULL`). Tasks-view UI in Dashboard still only renders RBK + Emily columns; tasks assigned to Sara/Leora/Becca via this dropdown get Slack DM'd correctly but won't appear in those legacy columns until those columns are extended.
  - **Tasks in Projects** — new `tasks.project_id uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` column + index. `/api/tasks` GET accepts `?project_id=`; POST/PATCH accept `project_id` field. Server-side `normalizeAssignedTo()` capitalizes incoming lowercase assignee values so the `tasks_assigned_to_check` constraint (`'RBK'|'Emily'|'Sara'|'Leora'|'Becca'`) is satisfied regardless of caller form — agenda-notes-style lowercase and tasks-style Capitalized both work. Project side panel got a new Tasks section between Updates and Footer: list of `tasks` rows for that project (circle to toggle done, assignee pill, line-through when complete) + inline add form (title input + 5-assignee pill row + "+ Add Task" button). Loads on panel open via `useEffect` keyed on `selectedProject.id`.
  - **@mention creates task for every mentioned user** — `/api/development/donor-notes/route.ts` previously only created a task on `@RBK`. Now loops over every user matched by `parseMentions(noteText)` and inserts one task per user with `assigned_to=user.name`. Each gets the Slack assignment DM in addition to the mention DM (two-DM design carried over).
  - **Thank-you note → Gmail draft with auto-BCC** — `/api/development/draft-thank-you/route.ts` now accepts an optional `donorEmail`, generates the Claude/template draft text, then (if `session.accessToken` exists) calls `POST https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/drafts` with a MIME message including `Bcc: sar.tracking@mail.veracross.com` and the same RBK signature as `/api/emails/compose/route.ts`. Response now includes `draftId` + `draftUrl` (Gmail drafts deep link via thread id). `ThankYouModal` button switches from `mailto:` to "Open Draft in Gmail" when a `draftUrl` is returned (opens in new tab); falls back to the legacy `mailto:` (no BCC) if Gmail draft creation fails. Both paths preserve the user's ability to review/edit before sending.
- **`SLACK_BOT_TOKEN` added to env config — Slack DMs now live** (2026-05-19) — Token was missing from `.env.local`, `.env`, AND Cloud Run, which is why every `[sendTaskSlack]` log path with the Slack fetch was unreachable (`if (!slackToken) return;` short-circuited). User added `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN=xoxb-...` to `.env.local` (line 36). `deploy.sh` `--update-env-vars` clause appended `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN=$SLACK_BOT_TOKEN` (relies on the `source .env.local` block at the top of deploy.sh to export it). Verified live on Cloud Run revision `ssrrbkcmdcenter-00441-pxj` via `gcloud run services describe ... | grep -i slack` → `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN` present. All Slack DM paths (Lever webhook, donor notes mention DM, Simchas Condolence Note Request, `sendTaskSlack` on task assignment, daily 8am ET due-today reminder) now functional end-to-end. **Lessons captured for future env vars:** any new secret must be added in three places — `.env.local` (local dev + the source target for deploy), `deploy.sh` `--update-env-vars` clause (otherwise Cloud Run won't see it), and verified with the gcloud-describe grep after deploy.
- **`sendTaskSlack` extracted to `lib/slackNotifications.ts`, wired into all 4 task-creating routes** (2026-05-19) — Root issue was that the Tasks "+ Add" UI flow (`Dashboard.tsx:5209` / `:5230`) POSTs to `/api/agenda-notes` with a lowercase assignee, not to `/api/tasks` where `sendTaskSlack` lived. Manual UI task creation never triggered any DM. New file `lib/slackNotifications.ts` exports `TASK_USERS`, `sendTaskSlack`, and types. Lookup is **case-insensitive** so the helper resolves both `'Emily'` (tasks-table CHECK constraint form) and `'emily'` (agenda_notes legacy form) to the same user. Imported and called from: (1) `/api/tasks/route.ts` POST + PATCH (POST on creation, PATCH only when `assigned_to` actually changes — prior value read first); (2) `/api/agenda-notes/route.ts` POST + PATCH (POST when row is `type=action` with assignee; PATCH only on genuine reassignment — prior value read first); (3) `/api/simchas/send-note/route.ts` after the tasks-table mirror insert; (4) `/api/development/donor-notes/route.ts` when `@RBK` auto-task is created. **Two-DM design choice**: Simchas Send Note still fires its bespoke `:clipboard: Condolence Note Request` DM (Emily gets two DMs per condolence event — context + generic), and donor-note `@RBK` mentions fire both the mention DM and the generic assignment DM (RBK gets two DMs per `@RBK` mention). This matches user spec; if it becomes noisy we can gate generic DM behind a "not already DM'd by this route" flag. Diagnostic logs from prior fix are preserved (`[sendTaskSlack] called with assignedTo`, `resolved user`, `Slack API response`).
- **Root cause: `tasks_assigned_to_check` rejected all new inserts** (2026-05-19) — The `tasks` table had a pre-existing CHECK constraint `CHECK (assigned_to = ANY (ARRAY['rbk', 'emily']))` (lowercase only, no Sara/Leora/Becca). Every insert from the three routes I added — `/api/development/donor-notes` (`'RBK'`), `/api/simchas/send-note` (`'Emily'`), `/api/tasks` POST (`'Emily'`/`'RBK'`) — silently failed this constraint, so the table stayed empty. The `(error: taskErr)` log statements in those routes did fire on Cloud Run, but only `[SIMCHAS SEND NOTE] tasks insert failed (non-fatal)` style messages, and nobody was watching the logs. That also explains why the assignment Slack DM never fired: the POST never reached `sendTaskSlack` because the insert errored out before it. Constraint replaced via migration `tasks_assigned_to_allow_all_5_users` to accept all 5 `TASK_USERS` keys: `CHECK (assigned_to = ANY (ARRAY['RBK', 'Emily', 'Sara', 'Leora', 'Becca']))`. Verified live by a service-role test insert of `'Emily'`, which succeeded. Also added diagnostic `console.log` statements per spec (`[sendTaskSlack] called with assignedTo`, `[sendTaskSlack] resolved user`, `[sendTaskSlack] Slack API response`, `[tasks POST] assigned_to value`) so future failures surface immediately in Cloud Run logs.
- **Task assignment Slack DM + daily 8am ET due-date reminder** (2026-05-19) — `/api/tasks/route.ts` now has a `TASK_USERS` map (5 users keyed by display name → `{ slackId, fullName }`) and a `sendTaskSlack` helper. New `POST` handler creates a task and fires the assignment DM if `assigned_to` is set; existing `PATCH` handler was extended to support `assigned_to`, `due_date`, `title`, `description` changes (not just `status`), and reads the prior `assigned_to` value before updating so the DM only fires when the assignee actually changes. The DM text is `:pushpin: New task assigned to you` + title + optional From/Due lines + Command Center link. Daily reminder: new endpoint `POST /api/tasks/due-today` (auth via `X-Internal-Secret`, same pattern as `sync-gifts-internal`) computes ET day bounds, queries tasks where `due_date` is in `[start_of_ET_day, start_of_next_ET_day)` and status is not done/completed and assigned_to is set, then DMs each assignee. **Important deviation from spec**: `due_date` is `timestamptz`, not `date` — a plain `.eq('due_date', '2026-05-19')` (as the spec literally wrote) would compare against UTC midnight and miss any task due during ET morning hours; using a UTC-aware range query instead. Cloud Function `dailyTaskDueReminder` (`functions/src/index.ts`) is scheduler-only — `functions.pubsub.schedule('0 8 * * *').timeZone('America/New_York').onRun(...)` — POSTing to the Next.js endpoint with `X-Internal-Secret`. This matches the existing `syncGiftsHourlyWeekdays` pattern; **deviated from the spec's `runWith({ secrets: [...] })` form** because this project uses 1st-gen `firebase-functions` v5 with plain `process.env` access throughout (`SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `INTERNAL_SYNC_SECRET`). `@supabase/supabase-js` is already in `functions/package.json` and was not bumped — the function never queries Supabase directly. Verified live: scheduler `firebase-schedule-dailyTaskDueReminder-us-central1` is `ENABLED` with `0 8 * * *`.
- **Simchas "Send Note" → confirmation modal + Slack DM + dual-write task** (2026-05-15) — Replaced the fire-and-forget Send Note button on Shiva email cards with `SimchasSendNoteModal` (`app/components/SimchasSendNoteModal.tsx`). Modal shows: header "Send Condolence Note — [Family Name]", read-only email summary card (uses `email.summary`, dated by `received_at`), optional "Add a note for Emily" textarea with the spec's placeholder, and Send-to-Emily / Cancel buttons. New endpoint `POST /api/simchas/send-note` does three things atomically: (1) writes to `agenda_notes` with `assignee='emily'` and the full title+summary+RBK-note body so Emily's existing Tasks column keeps showing the item without any UI changes; (2) inserts into the `tasks` table with `assigned_to='Emily'`, `source='simchas'`, `source_ref=<familyName>`, `title="Draft condolence note — <familyName>"`, and `description` containing the email summary + RBK's optional note — for source-tracked consumers / future "From Simchas" rollup (failure here is logged but doesn't fail the request); (3) Slack DMs Emily (`U05M5KT86GK`) via `chat.postMessage` with the spec's clipboard-emoji format including a Command Center link to `/?nav=tasks`. On success the modal closes, the Send Note button turns into a green-emerald "Sent" pill with a checkmark, and a 3-second bottom-right toast says "Sent to Emily". Cancel closes the modal with no DB writes. Removed the now-unused `shivaNoteSending` state.
- **ConstituentTable mobile card layout** (2026-05-15) — Below `md`, ConstituentTable renders a `divide-y` card list instead of the desktop table. Each card shows donor name + role/frequency line + tag pills on the left, and total committed (paid + outstanding) + "X owed" amber amount on the right. Tapping a card toggles the same `expandedId` state the desktop table uses, so `DonorAnnotations` + Draft thank you + Veracross link render in an inline expansion below the card. Desktop table wrapped in `hidden md:block`. The shared empty-state remains hoisted. Field-name translation note: the spec used snake_case (constituent_name, primary_development_role, etc.) but the actual `Constituent` interface uses camelCase (donorName, primaryDevelopmentRole, paymentFrequency, donorId, outstanding) — translated accordingly. "totalRaised" in the spec doesn't exist on the type; rendered as `paid + outstanding` (total committed) to mirror the per-donor contribution to the fund's totalRaised in the new formula.
- **Guardian Circle moved from sidebar into Development tabs** (2026-05-15) — Pure navigational restructure, no UI/data/functionality changes inside `GuardianCirclePage`. **Removed**: the `'guardian-circle'` sidebar nav item in `Sidebar.tsx`, `'guardian-circle'` from `VALID_NAV_IDS` in `Dashboard.tsx`, the top-level `activeNav === 'guardian-circle' && <GuardianCirclePage />` render, and the Dashboard-side `GuardianCirclePage` import. **Added**: Guardian Circle as the 3rd tab in `DevelopmentPage` (`Weekly Gifts → Campaign Giving by Fund → Guardian Circle → Cooper Fund → Israel Fund`). `DevelopmentPage` exports `DevelopmentTabKey` and now reads a `?tab=` URL param on mount to support deep links — falls back to `weekly-gifts`. **Rewired callers**: `CampaignsTab`'s "View tab →" link on the Guardian Circle row now takes an optional `onSwitchTab` prop and switches the parent tab instead of changing top-level nav (legacy `onNavigate` fallback still routes to Development if `onSwitchTab` isn't wired). The `donor-notes` route's Slack DM link changed from `?nav=guardian-circle` to `?nav=development&tab=guardian-circle` — clicking it opens Dashboard, which sets `activeNav='development'`; DevelopmentPage then mounts, reads `?tab=guardian-circle`, and pre-selects the tab (and cleans the `tab` param from the URL after first read). Lazy-load behavior remains the conditional-render pattern (`{activeTab === 'guardian-circle' && <GuardianCirclePage />}`) — the component mounts only when the tab is active, fetches its summary + full payload via the existing two-phase flow, and unmounts when the user switches away. The tabs row also got `flex-wrap` so 5 tabs don't overflow on narrow viewports.
- **Weekly Gifts mobile card list + SAR Academy filter** (2026-05-15) — Below `md`, the gifts feed now renders a card list (`md:hidden flex flex-col divide-y`) instead of the desktop flex-table, since the table's column widths squished each cell to 1-2 characters on a 390px viewport. Each card shows donor name + event/fund on the left and amount + date on the right, with PLEDGE / REFUND chips inline next to the event. Tapping a card opens the donor side panel (same as clicking the donor-name link on desktop). The desktop block is unchanged and wrapped in `hidden md:block`. The shared empty-state branch (`No gifts in this range` / `No gifts recorded today` / `No matching gifts found`) is hoisted above both views. Also: `/api/development/weekly-gifts/route.ts` now does `.neq('constituent_name', 'SAR Academy')` on the `gifts_cache` query to exclude SAR's own internal journal entries (e.g. the $900K operating transfer) from the feed.
- **DonorAnnotations now client-only via `dynamic(..., { ssr: false })`** (2026-05-15) — iOS Safari was crashing with "Application error: a client-side exception has occurred" after the donor-notes/@mention deploy. `ConstituentTable.tsx` and `WeeklyGiftsTab.tsx` now import `DonorAnnotations` via `next/dynamic` with `{ ssr: false }` so the component never renders server-side and never participates in hydration. Type imports (`type DonorTag`) stay static — they're erased at compile time. `GuardianCirclePage.tsx` already used a type-only import, no change needed. Verified by curl: the served HTML for `/login` contains zero references to DonorAnnotations / TagPills / MentionText / NotesList / MENTION_PATTERN — the module is in its own client chunk that loads only when GuardianCircle or WeeklyGifts mounts. **Additionally fixed a subtle module-shared regex bug**: the previous `MentionText` had a module-level `MENTION_PILL_REGEX = new RegExp(..., 'g')` and mutated `lastIndex` inside its `useMemo`. Under React's concurrent rendering or Strict Mode double-render, two `MentionText` instances iterating the same g-flagged regex could clobber each other's `lastIndex` and (we suspect) trigger an infinite loop on iOS Safari's stricter scheduling. The fix builds a fresh `RegExp(MENTION_PATTERN, 'g')` per call. `requestAnimationFrame` in `insertMention` is now guarded with `typeof window !== 'undefined'` as defense-in-depth (the handler only fires client-side anyway). API routes (`donor-notes`, `donor-tags`) audited and contain no browser-only references.
- **WeeklyGiftsTab per-gift note popover replaced with DonorAnnotations** (2026-05-14) — The note icon on each gift row used to open a small popover with a "Notes for Rabbi…" textarea backed by the `gift_notes` table. That popover is now replaced with the shared `DonorAnnotations` component keyed on the gift's donor (`gift.displayName` / `gift.constituent_id`), so it gets @mention autocomplete, Slack DMs, and `@RBK` auto-task creation for free — same behavior as Guardian Circle. Implementation: added `inlineTags` state + a fetch effect that pulls the donor's tags when `expandedNoteGiftId` changes; popover JSX swapped from textarea/save/delete buttons to `<DonorAnnotations tags={inlineTags} onTagsChange={setInlineTags} />` + a Close button. Removed the now-dead `noteText`, `noteSaving`, `saveNote`, `deleteNote` state and handlers. **Semantics shift:** the underlying data moved from per-gift (`gift_notes`) to per-donor (`donor_notes`) — existing rows in `gift_notes` remain in the database but are no longer editable from the UI. The amber-dot indicator on the icon (`gift.note ? text-amber-600 : ...`) still highlights gifts that have legacy per-gift notes, but opening the popover now shows that donor's donor-level notes instead. The `/api/development/gift-notes` route is preserved for any future re-introduction. Mention any of RBK / Emily / Sara / Leora / Becca from either Guardian Circle OR a Weekly Gifts donor row and they'll get the same Slack DM.
- **RBK email correction + Slack IDs for all 5 mentionable users** (2026-05-14) — Fixed `krauss@saracademy.org` → `kraussb@saracademy.org` and filled in real `slackId` values for Sara (`U04NB3YP3`), Leora (`U05M4L1RY6Q`), and Becca (`U04PVHXSD`). All 5 users (RBK, Emily, Sara, Leora, Becca) now have non-null Slack IDs, so `@mention` notifications fire DMs end-to-end. Synced in both copies of `MENTIONABLE_USERS` (`app/components/development/DonorAnnotations.tsx` and `app/api/development/donor-notes/route.ts`).
- **Donor note @mention autocomplete** (2026-05-14) — `DonorAnnotations.tsx` exports a `MENTIONABLE_USERS` constant (5 users: RBK, Emily, Sara, Leora, Becca, each with `name`, `fullName`, `email`, `slackId`). The notes input now detects `@` followed by alphanumerics adjacent to the caret and renders a small white-card dropdown above the input filtered by name/fullName. Click or Enter inserts `@<Name> ` and refocuses. Escape closes. Notes are still stored as plain text. The display side parses tokens via `MENTION_PILL_REGEX` (matches only the 5 known names with a word-boundary after) and renders each as an inline blue pill via the new `MentionText` component — used both in the donor row notes lists and anywhere DonorAnnotations is mounted. **Note**: `MENTIONABLE_USERS` is duplicated in `app/api/development/donor-notes/route.ts` because the route needs the same list for server-side mention parsing without coupling client + server bundle. Keep both lists in sync.
- **Slack DM on @mention + RBK auto-tasks** (2026-05-14) — `POST /api/development/donor-notes` now parses the saved note for `@<Name>` tokens against `MENTIONABLE_USERS`. For each mentioned user with a `slackId`, it fires a Slack DM via `chat.postMessage` (`SLACK_BOT_TOKEN` env var, same shape as the Lever webhook DM at `app/api/webhook/lever/route.ts:52`). DM body: `📋 *Donor Note* — you were mentioned by [author full name]` + donor name + full note text + a Command Center link. Failures are logged and never block the note insert. **Sara, Leora, Becca have no Slack ID** and are silently skipped — adding their IDs to `MENTIONABLE_USERS` (both files) will enable them. Additionally: if `@RBK` appears in the note, the route also inserts a row into the `tasks` table with `source='development'`, `source_ref=<constituentName>`, `assigned_to='RBK'`, `status='todo'`, and a title formatted as `[Development] <Donor> — <first 80 chars of note>…`.
- **`tasks` table source columns + workspace_id** (2026-05-14) — Added `source text DEFAULT 'manual'`, `source_ref text`, and `workspace_id uuid REFERENCES workspaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE`. Backfilled existing rows to `source = 'manual'` so legacy data slots into "My Tasks". Index on `(workspace_id, source)`.
- **New `/api/tasks` route + Tasks UI "From Development" section** (2026-05-14) — `GET /api/tasks?source=development` (or no filter) returns workspace-scoped rows from the `tasks` table; `PATCH /api/tasks` flips `status` and writes `completed_at`. Dashboard.tsx fetches once on first visit to the Tasks view (`loadedSourcedTasksRef`) and renders a new **From Development** collapsible group at the top of the RBK column (above Drafts to Approve), showing pending count in a green pill and donor name as a subtitle under each task. A parallel **From Admissions** group is rendered with the same scaffolding but only when admissions tasks exist. Completed tasks in each group collapse to a "Show completed (n)" toggle. **Pragmatic scope note**: this is layered ON TOP of the existing two-column RBK/Emily UI (which still derives tasks from emails+meeting_notes and `agenda_notes`). The user's "My Tasks / From Development / From Admissions" three-section split wasn't a full rewrite — the existing column structure is unchanged, and the new groups slot into RBK's column since auto-created tasks are `assigned_to='RBK'`.
- **Viewer accounts granted dev + Guardian Circle access** (2026-05-14) — `sara.hasson@saracademy.org` and `leora.miller@saracademy.org` are now `viewer`-role members on the SAR workspace with `allowed_modules = {"development": true, "guardian-circle": true}`. Sara already had a row (Firebase UID `XDGTACXXb6PDm0WTSLDmRZiVSe83`) so the UPDATE overwrote her prior `{"projects": true, "development": true}` — re-add `"projects": true` if she still needs Projects access. Leora hadn't signed in yet, so the row uses the `PLACEHOLDER-leora.miller@saracademy.org` `user_id` convention; the session route at `app/api/auth/session/route.ts:91` will swap it for her real Firebase UID on first login (case-insensitive email match). **Schema note for future inserts:** `workspace_members.allowed_modules` is `jsonb` storing `Record<string, boolean>` (object), NOT an array — `lib/modules.ts:24` iterates it via `Object.entries`. The unique constraint is `(workspace_id, user_id)`, not `(workspace_id, email)`.
- **Donor notes + donor tags tables** (2026-05-14) — Two new tables: `donor_notes` (workspace_id, constituent_name, constituent_id, note, created_by, created_at, updated_at) and `donor_tags` (workspace_id, constituent_name, constituent_id, tag, color, created_by, created_at). Both indexed on `(workspace_id, constituent_name)`; `donor_tags` also has a unique index on `(workspace_id, constituent_name, tag)` so applying the same tag twice is a no-op upsert. Both have RLS policies — `auth.jwt()->>'email' IN workspace_members` — but those are **defense-in-depth only** because this codebase authenticates with Firebase Auth (not Supabase Auth), so `auth.jwt()` is NULL for all real requests. All actual reads/writes go through `supabaseAdmin` (service role key, bypasses RLS).
- **Donor notes + tags API routes** (2026-05-14) — `GET/POST/DELETE /api/development/donor-notes` and `/api/development/donor-tags`. Both routes are workspace-scoped via `getEffectiveWorkspaceId`, gated on the `development` module. GET accepts optional `?constituent_name=X` for per-donor fetch or no filter for a workspace-wide bulk fetch (avoids N+1 on list pages). POST creates; DELETE `?id=X` removes. The tags route enforces a server-side allowlist (`TAG_DEFS`) so only the 5 predefined tag labels can be persisted, and assigns the matching color server-side rather than trusting the client's value.
- **`Today` filter on Weekly Gifts** (2026-05-14) — `app/api/development/weekly-gifts/route.ts` allowed `days` list extended from `[7, 14, 30]` to `[0, 7, 14, 30]`. `days=0` resolves to `weekAgo = today`, so the existing date filter (`giftDate >= weekAgoET && giftDate <= todayET`) returns only today's gifts in ET. `WeeklyGiftsTab.tsx` period pills replaced with a labelled list `{ d: 0, label: 'Today' } | ... | { d: 30, label: '30 days' }` (Today is the first pill). Empty state branches: search-active → "No matching gifts found"; `days === 0` → "No gifts recorded today"; otherwise → "No gifts in this range".
- **Donor annotations (notes + tags) UI** (2026-05-14) — Shared component at `app/components/development/DonorAnnotations.tsx` exports `default DonorAnnotations`, `TagPills`, `NotesList`, `TAG_DEFS` (5 predefined tags with colors), and types `DonorTag` / `DonorNote`. `ConstituentTable` extended with optional `tagsByDonor: Map<string, DonorTag[]>`, `onTagsChange`, and `enableAnnotations` props. When `enableAnnotations` is true, rows become clickable: tag pills render inline next to the donor name (always visible, color-coded), and clicking the row expands a slate-50 row containing `<DonorAnnotations>` with tag picker + notes list + add input. Tags are bulk-fetched once at the parent level (`GuardianCirclePage` issues a single `GET /api/development/donor-tags`) to avoid 246 separate requests on row render. **Pattern note**: `CampaignsTab` still uses `<ConstituentTable compact />` without `enableAnnotations`, so its accordion drilldowns stay non-expandable — switch the flag on there if you want notes/tags in those fund drilldowns too. On `WeeklyGiftsTab`, `DonorAnnotations` is injected at the top of the existing donor side panel; tags are fetched per-donor when the panel opens (no bulk fetch since this page only shows one donor's annotations at a time).
- **/home page mobile drawer wiring** (2026-05-14) — Critical miss from the prior mobile pass: `/home/page.tsx` renders its **own** `<Sidebar>` instance (it's a separate route from `/` which uses `Dashboard.tsx`). The previous pass only wrapped the Dashboard.tsx sidebar in the mobile drawer; /home was still rendering the sidebar inline, eating 256px of a 390px viewport and giving the appearance that mobile changes hadn't shipped at all (no hamburger, no X — the sidebar was just sitting there always). Same drawer pattern now applied to `/home`: `sidebarOpen` state, mobile backdrop (`md:hidden fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/40`), fixed-overlay drawer wrapper that becomes `md:static md:translate-x-0` on desktop, mobile sticky header inside `<main>` with hamburger + "Home" label, and `onNavClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}` on the Sidebar. Content padding also mobile-tightened: `px-8 lg:px-12 2xl:pr-16 pt-14 pb-24` → `px-4 md:px-8 lg:px-12 2xl:pr-16 pt-4 md:pt-14 pb-24` (less wasted horizontal space, less wasted top padding now that the sticky header sits above).
- **Mobile sidebar close affordance** (2026-05-14) — Added an explicit X close button inside the `Sidebar` `<aside>`, positioned `absolute top-3 right-3` with `md:hidden` so it only renders on mobile. The button calls `onNavClick?.()` (same callback the wrapper uses to close on nav tap) — they share the prop because both actions resolve to "close the drawer." `<aside>` got `relative h-full` added: `relative` so the absolute X positions correctly; `h-full` so the aside fills the `fixed inset-y-0` drawer wrapper on mobile (without it the bottom of the drawer showed the backdrop bleeding through past the aside's content height). Touch target is 44×44. The backdrop from the prior pass (`md:hidden fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/40` with `onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}`) was verified still in place and functioning — users now have three ways to dismiss the drawer: the X button, tapping the backdrop, or tapping any nav item.
- **Mobile sidebar drawer + responsive layout pass** (2026-05-14) — `Sidebar` now accepts an optional `onNavClick` prop and fires it whenever any nav item is tapped (covers `handleNav`, the Home button, Daily Announcements, Admin Permissions, and Compose; Today's Folder external link intentionally does not close the drawer because it opens in a new tab). `Dashboard.tsx` wraps Sidebar in a `fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40` drawer that translates in/out on mobile and switches to `md:static md:translate-x-0` on desktop. A semi-transparent backdrop (`md:hidden fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/40`) renders behind the drawer when open and closes it on tap. Below `md`, a sticky header bar (`md:hidden sticky top-0 z-20`) renders inside `<main>` with a hamburger button (44×44 touch target), the current page name (`activeNav.replace(/-/g, ' ')` with `capitalize`), and a 9px spacer. Viewport accessibility fix: removed `maximumScale: 1` and `userScalable: false` from `viewport` (WCAG 2.1 1.4.4). Removed the redundant manual `<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable/-status-bar-style">` tags from `<head>` since Next.js's `metadata.appleWebApp` emits them — the `<link rel="apple-touch-icon">` stays because we use a non-default icon path. Stat card grids on `CooperFundTab`, `IsraelFundTab`, `GuardianCirclePage`, and the enrollment Pipeline section in `Dashboard.tsx` were changed from `grid-cols-3` to `grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3`. Cooper table wrapped in `overflow-x-auto` with `min-w-[480px]`; Israel initiatives + money-in tables switched from `overflowY: 'auto'` to `overflow: 'auto'` with `min-w-[560px]` / `min-w-[520px]` so columns scroll horizontally instead of clipping. Existing side panels (`fixed top-0 right-0 bottom-0 w-full max-w-[420px]`) already resolve to full-width on a ≤420px viewport and were left as-is. **Not audited in this pass:** the inbox / agenda / tasks / projects views inside `Dashboard.tsx` still contain several `grid grid-cols-2` and similar non-responsive layouts; lower-priority pages (recruiting, emily, communications) also weren't touched. Apply the same `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2` pattern when next editing those views.
